# Garuda-purana 3

## Chapter 1

## \|\| 1.1 \|\|

*śrīgaṇeśāyanamaḥ \|*  
*śrīlakṣmīnṛsiṃhāya namaḥ \|*  
*śrīdattātreyāya namaḥ \|*  
*śrīvedavyāsāya namaḥ \|*  
*śrīhayagrīvāya namaḥ \|*  
*atha gāruḍe brahmakāṇḍastṛtīya ārabhyate \|*  
*oṃ mallānāmaśanirnṛṇāṃ naravaraḥ strīṇāṃ smaro mūrtimān gopānāṃ svajano 'satāṃ kṣitibhṛtāṃ śāstā svapitroḥ śiśuḥ \|*  
*mṛtyurbhojapatervidhātṛvihita stattvaṃ paraṃ yogināṃ vṛṣṇīnāṃ ca patiḥ sadaiva śuśubhe raṅge 'cyutaḥ sāgrajaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. The lord of Yadavas, accompanied by his elder brother Baiarama, looked splendid in the battle-field-the lord who was a thunderbolt to the enemy, the best and the noblest among men, cupid incarnate to the women, kith and kin of the cowherds, chastiser of the wicked kings, a loving child to his parents, Yama to the lord of Bhojas as ordained by fate, the transcendental soul for meditation on the part of Yogins.

## \|\| 1.2 \|\|

*namo nārāyaṇāyeti tasmai vai mūlarūpiṇe \|*  
*namaskṛtya pravakṣyāmi nārāyaṇakathāmimām \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Salutation to lord Narayana, the source of the universe. After paying due homage to the lord, I shall narrate his story.

## \|\| 1.3 \|\|

*śaunakādyā mahātmāno hyṛṣayo brahmavādinaḥ \|*  
*naimiṣākhye mahāpuṇye tapastepurmahattaram \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. The great sages, Йаипака and others preached spirituality and practised penance in the holy region of Naimisa.

## \|\| 1.4 \|\|

*jitendriyā jitāhārāḥ saṃtaḥ satyaparāyaṇāḥ \|*  
*yajantaḥ parayā bhaktyā viṣṇumādyaṃ jagadgurum \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. They had conquered their senses, regulated their diet, were devoted to truth. They worshipped Visnu, the primeval preceptor of the universe with supreme devotion.

## \|\| 1.5 \|\|

*gṛṇantaḥ paramaṃ brahma jagaccakṣurmahaujasaḥ \|*  
*sarvaśāstrārthatattvajñāstepurnaimiṣa kānane \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Versed in the knowledge of sastras, invested with supreme glory they meditated on Brahman — the eye of the universe and practised penance in the Naimisa region.

## \|\| 1.6 \|\|

*yajñairyajñapatiṃ kecijjñānairjñānātmakaṃ param \|*  
*kecitparamayā bhaktyā nārāyaṇamapūjayan \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Some worshipped the lord of sacrifices with sacrifices. Some worshipped the lord incarnate of knowledge through knowledge. Some worshipped him with supreme devotion.

## \|\| 1.7 \|\|

*ekadā tu mahātmānaḥ samājaṃ cakruruttamāḥ \|*  
*dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāmupāyaṃ jñātumicchavaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Once upon a time, the sages held a council with a desire to ascertain the means of Dharma, Artha, Kama and Moksa.

## \|\| 1.8 \|\|

*ṣadviṃśatisahasrāṇi munīnāmūrdhvaretasām \|*  
*teṣāṃ śiṣyapraśiṣyāṇāṃ saṃkhyā vaktuṃ na śaṅkyate \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Twenty-six thousands of sages reputed for their self-control gathered there. They were accompanied by their disciples and disciples of disciples who could not be counted in number.

## \|\| 1.9 \|\|

*munayo bhāvitātmāno militāste mahojasaḥ \|*  
*lokānugrahakartāro vītarāgā vimatsarāḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. The pure-souled sages of great lustre who were devoid of malice and attachment assembled there to devise ways for the welfare of the people.

## \|\| 1.10 \|\|

*kathaṃ harau manuṣyāṇāṃ bhaktiravyabhicāriṇī \|*  
*kena sidhyettu sakalaṃ karma trividhamātmanaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. How can we create the people's firm devotion for lord Hari? How could our threefold activity bear fruit for the welfare of the world?

## \|\| 1.11 \|\|

*ityevaṃ praṣṭumātmānamudyatānprekṣya śaunakaḥ \|*  
*sāṃja lirvākyamāha sma vinayāvanataḥ sudhīḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. The sages were eager to put up such queries to Saunaka. He, the wise man, could very well guess their anxiety. With hands joined in reverence and head bent as a token of modesty he said in reply.

## \|\| 1.12 \|\|

*śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*āste siddhāśrame puṇye sūtaḥ paurāṇikottamaḥ \|*  
*sa etadakhilaṃ vetti vyāsaśiṣyo yatīśvaraḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. In the holy hermitage of the Siddhas there abides Suta who is well versed in the Puranic lore. He, the best of the selfcontrolled, is the disciple of Vyasa. He will answer the queries to your entire satisfaction.

## \|\| 1.13 \|\|

*tasmāttameva pṛcchāma ityevaṃ śaunako muniḥ \|*  
*atha te ṛṣayo jagmuḥ puṇyaṃ siddhāśramaṃ tataḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Let us put our queries to him. Thus, баипака, accompanied by the sages, went to the hermitage of the Siddhas.

## \|\| 1.14 \|\|

*papracchuste sukhāsīnaṃ naimiṣāraṇyavāsinaḥ \|*  
*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*vayaṃ tvatithayaḥ prāptāstvātitheyosi suvrata \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Shaunaka and the Naimisa sages approached Suta and put their queries to him when they found him in proper mood. О you of good vow, please know that we have come to you as guests and we expect that you will receive us as such.

## \|\| 1.15 \|\|

*snānadānopacāreṇa pūjayitvā yathāvidhi \|*  
*kena viṣṇuḥ prasannaḥ syātsa kathaṃ pūjyate naraiḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. By what means and in what manner with sacred ablution, gifts, etc. shall lord Visnu be worshipped so that he may be gratified thereby.

## \|\| 1.16 \|\|

*muktisādhanabhūtaṃ ca brūhi tattvavinirṇayam \|*  
*sūta uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇudhvamṛṣyaḥ sarve hariṃ tattvavinirṇayam \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Please tell us your conclusive, answers to our query so that they may serve as the means of our final release.

## \|\| 1.17 \|\|

*natvā viṣṇuṃ śriyaṃ vāyuṃ bhāratīṃ śeṣasaṃjñakam \|*  
*dvaipāyanaṃ guruṃ kṛṣṇaṃ pravakṣyāmi yathāmati \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. О sages, listen, I am going to tell you my conclusive answers to your queries.

Having paid homage to lord Visnu, LaksmI, Vayu, Bharata, Sesa the preceptor Vyasa and lord Krsna I shall answer your queries as far as I understand.

## \|\| 1.18 \|\|

*nāsti nārāyaṇasamaṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati \|*  
*etena satyavākyena sarvārthānsādhayāmyaham \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. There is nothing equal to Narayana, nor has it been nor would it be. In this faith, 1 accomplish my task, achieving purpose.

## \|\| 1.19 \|\|

*śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*kimarthaṃ namanaṃ viṣṇorgranthādau munisattama \|*  
*kartavyaṃ brūhi me brahmankṛpayā mama suvrata \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. О noble Suta, please tell us why homage is paid to lord Visnu at the very beginning. О you of good vow, do also tell us about the proper procedure of reciting a Purana

## \|\| 1.20 \|\|

*tataḥ śriyaṃ tato vāyuṃ bhāratīṃ ca tataḥ param \|*  
*ante vyāsaṃ kimarthaṃ ca tvaṃ namaskṛtavānasi \|*  
*sūtasūta mahābhāga brūhi kāraṇamatra ca \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. You have paid homage to lord Visnu, LaksmI, Vayu, Bharat! and Vyasa. Why this order in paying homage? О Sets of blessed virtue, let us know the reason thereof.

## \|\| 1.21 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ādau vandyaḥ sarvavedaikavedyo vede śāstre setihāse purāṇe \|*  
*sattāṃ prāyo viṣṇurevaika eva prakāśate 'to namya eko harirhi \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Visnu shall be worshipped at the outset-He who is knowable through the Vedas, Shastras and Puranas. He alone is the source of energy and lumination. He alone is worthy of our praise.

## \|\| 1.22 \|\|

*sarvatra mukhyastvadhikonyatopi sa eva namyo na ca śaṅkarādyāḥ \|*  
*namanti ye 'vinayācchaṅkaraṃ tu vināyakaṃ caṇḍikāṃ reṇukāṃ ca \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. He is the supreme deity, superior to the gods. He alone is worthy of adora on, not Shiva or others. The arrogant people ... by pass Visnu and pay homage to Ganesa, Candika, Renuka, Surya, Bhairava, Vayu, Sarasvati, Parvati and Vaisnavas, i.e. averse to the worship o\* t. The sages have proclaimed thus.

## \|\| 1.24-25 \|\|

*na pārthikyānnamanaṃ kāryameva prīṇanti naitā devatāḥ pūjanena \|*  
*pūjāṃ gṛhītvā devatāścaiva sarvāḥ kiñciddatvā phaladānena tāṃśca \|\| 24 \|\|*

*saṃtarpya tuṣṭaiḥ svamanonu sārāttaiḥ kāritāṃ kāmyapūjāṃ tathaiva \|*  
*nivedayitvā paradevatāyāṃ viṣṇau harau śrīpuruṣādivandye \|\| 25 \|\|*

24-25. Hence, one should not bow to each deity separately, for they are not satiated by individual worship. Whatever worship is rendered unto them they reciprocate partially and at the same time dedicate that worship to the supreme lord Visnu adored by LaksmI and others.

## \|\| 1.26 \|\|

*ihāparatrāpi sukhetarāṇi dāsyanti paścādadharaṃ vai tamaśca \|*  
*ato hyete naiva pūjyā na namyā mokṣecchubhirbrāhmaṇādyairdvijendra \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. О lord of birds, these deities should neither be worshipped nor saluted by Brahmins and other castes desirous of salvation, for they are the source of trouble here and hereafter. They can grant the worshipper the dark, inferior regions.

## \|\| 1.27-28 \|\|

*tathaiva sarvāśramibhiśca nityaṃ mahāvipattāvapi vipravaryāḥ \|*  
*śrīkāmya yā ye tu bhajanti nityaṃ śrībrahmarudredrayamādidevān \|\| 27 \|\|*

*iheva bhuñjanti mahacca duḥ khaṃ mahāpadaḥ kuṣṭhabhagandarādīn \|*  
*namanti ye 'vaiṣṇavānbrahmarudravāyu pratīkānnaiva te viṣṇubhaktāḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

27-28. People in any stage of life should not worship them even in adverse circumstances. Those who for the accomplishment of desire worship LaksmI, Brahma, Siva, Indra, Yama, undergo great hardships, great calamities, foul diseases such as leprosy, fistula. Those who vow to Brahma, Rudra, V ayu and other gods, excluding Visnu are called-Vaisnavas.

## \|\| 1.29 \|\|

*abhiprāyaṃ tvatra vakṣye munīndrāḥ paraṃ gopyaṃ hṛdi dhāryaṃ hi taddhi \|*  
*vāyoḥ pratīkaṃ pūjyameveha viprā na brahmarudrādipratīkameva \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. О great sages, I shall tell you an important secret which you should keep close to the heart and not divulge to any other. О Brahmins, you should worship the image of Vayu and not the image of Brahma, Rodra or other deity.

## \|\| 1.30-32 \|\|

*pūjākāle devadevasya viṣṇorvāyoḥ pratīkaṃ yogyabhāge nidhāya \|*  
*antargataṃ tasya vāyorhariṃ ca lakṣmīpatiṃ pūjayitvā hi samyak \|\| 30 \|\|*

*paścādvāyoḥ supratīkaṃ ca samyaṅ nirmālyaśeṣeṇa hareḥ samarcayet \|*  
*pṛthakca sragdhūpavilepanādipūjāṃ prakurvanti ca ye vimūḍhaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

*teṣāṃ duḥ khamiha loke paratra bhaviṣyate nātra vicāryamasti \|*  
*prāyaścittaṃ svasti viprāḥ kathañcittatkurvantu smaraṇaṃ nāma viṣṇoḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

30-32. When at worship, you should set up an image of Vayu, the symbol of Visnu at an appropriate place. You should worship Visnu, the lord of LaksmI, in the appropriate manner. You should thereafter worship the image of Vayu with the remains of gifts. The stupid people who worship Vayu and the rest with freh garlands, incense and sandal but not with the remains considerably suffer here and hereafter. There is no doubt in this. As an expiation, they should recite Svasti mantras and meditate upon Visnu.

## \|\| 1.33 \|\|

*pāṣaṇḍarudrādikasaṃ pratiṣṭhitānharervāyoḥ śaṅkarasya pratīkān \|*  
*namanti ye phalabuddhyā vibhūḍhāsteṣāṃ phalaṃ śāśvataṃ duḥ khameva \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. The stupid who bow to the images of Siva, Vayu and others set up by the hypocrites, the followers of Tamasa god Rudra for the attainment of desire suffer eternally.

## \|\| 1.34 \|\|

*vāyoḥ pratīkaṃ yadi vipravaryaiḥ pratiṣṭhitaṃ cennamanaṃ hi kāryam \|*  
*naivedyaśeṣeṇa hareśca viṣṇoḥ pūjā kṛtā cenna hi doṣaleśaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. If the best of Brahmins have set up an image of Vayu,one should pay homage to the same. If with the remains of offering made to Visnu, Vayu is worshipped, there is not in the least any fault.

## \|\| 1.35 \|\|

*gururhi mukhyo hanumajjanirmahānrāmāṅghribhakto hanumānsadaiva \|*  
*evaṃ viditvā paramaṃ hariṃ ca putraṃ punarmukhyadevasya vāyoḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Vayu, the father of Hanuman, is the should pay homage to Visnu as well as Hanuman, the offspring of the great god Vayu.

## \|\| 1.36-37 \|\|

*namaskāro nānyathā vipravaryā ādhīyatāṃ hṛdi sarvai rahasyamam \|*  
*ye vaiṣṇavā vaiṣṇa vadāsabhṛtyāḥ sarvepi te sarvadā viṣṇumeva \|\| 36 \|\|*

*namanti ye vai pratipādayanti tathaiva puṇyāni ca sāttvikāni \|*  
*namanti ye vāsudevaṃ hariṃ ca samyak svaśaktyā pratipādayanti \|\| 37 \|\|*

36-37. There is no other way of paying homage, О best of Brahmins, you all should keep this as a guarded secret.

The devotees of Visnu or the followers of the sect should pay homage to Visnu and Visnu alone. By doing so, in an appropriate manner they can acquire virtue.

## \|\| 1.38 \|\|

*pravṛttimārgeṇa na pūjayanti hyāpatkāle paradaivaṃ tadanyam \|*  
*te vaiṣṇavā vaiṣṇavadāsabhṛtyā anye ca sarve 'vaiṣṇavamātrakāḥ smṛtāḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Those who do not worship, even in adversity, any god other than Visnu are the true devotees of the lord. Those whose minds are diverted to other gods are not the real devotees in any sense. You should not entertain any doubt in this respect.

## \|\| 1.39 \|\|

*upakramairupasaṃhārasya liṅgairhariṃ guruṃ hyantareṇaiva yānti \|*  
*tānevāhuḥ satpurāṇāni viprāḥ kalau yuge nābhyasūyanti sarve \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. The learned declare those Puranas to be I sacred or worth hearing which preach the cult of Visnu from beginning to the end. In the iron age, such Puranas are welcome to attain popularity among the masses,'

## \|\| 1.40 \|\|

*yato hitānye pratipādayanti pravṛttidharmānsvasvavarṇānurūpān \|*  
*ato hyasūyanti sadā vimūḍhāḥ kalau hi viprāḥ pracurā hi tepi \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. There are a number of Puranas, which preach respective dharms to the four castes. Stupid people accept their verdict and feel jealous of one another.

## \|\| 1.41 \|\|

*na cāsti viṣṇoḥ sadṛśaṃ ca daivataṃ na cāsti vāyoḥ sadṛśo guruśca \|*  
*na cāsti tīrthaṃ sadṛśaṃ viṣṇupadyāḥ na viṣṇubhaktena samosti bhaktaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. There is no deity equal to Visnu, no preceptor equal to Vayu, no place of pilgrimage holier than the bank of the Ganges, no devotee of gods equal to the devotee of Visnu

## \|\| 1.42 \|\|

*anyāni viṣṇoḥ pratipādakāni sarvāṇi te sāttvikānīti cāhuḥ \|*  
*śrāvyāṇi tānyeva manuṣyaloke śrāvyāṇi nānyāni ca duḥ khadāni \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. The Puranas devoted to Visnu are called sattvikas. These should be preached to the people but not others which cause suffering.

## \|\| 1.43 \|\|

*kalau yuge sarva purāṇamadhye trīṇyeva mukhyāni haripriyāṇi \|*  
*mukhyaṃ purāṇaṃ hi kalau nṛṇāṃ ca śreyaskaraṃ bhāgavataṃ purāṇam \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. In the Kali age, only three principal puranas are devoted to Visnu. Among these the Bhagavata Purana renders more good to the people.

## \|\| 1.44 \|\|

*pūrvaṃ hi sṛṣṭiḥ pratipādyate tra yato hyato bhāgavataṃ paraṃ smṛtam \|*  
*yasminpurāṇe kathayanti sṛṣṭiṃ hyādau viṣṇorbrahmarudrādikānām \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. The Bhagavata Purana opens with the description of the origin of the universe; Visnu, Brahma, Rudra and others.

## \|\| 1.45 \|\|

*nānārthamevaṃ kathayanti vipra nīcoccarūpaṃ jñānamāhurmahāntaḥ \|*  
*tenaiva siddhaṃ pravadanti sarvaṃ hyataḥ paraṃ bhāgavataṃ purāṇam \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. The wise declare knowledge to be manifold, consisting of various grades — high, low and middling. All that knowledge is found in the Bhagavata Purana. Hence, Bhagavata is the highest of all Puranas.

## \|\| 1.46 \|\|

*tataḥ paraṃ viṣṇupurāṇamāhustataḥ paraṃ gāruḍasaṃjñakaṃ ca \|*  
*trīṇyeva mukhyā ni kalau nṛṇāṃ tu tathā viśeṣo gāruḍe kiñcidasti \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. The Visnu Purana comes next then comes Garuda. The three are principal Puranas in the Kali age. Garuda contains some additional matter.

## \|\| 1.47-48 \|\|

*śṛṇudhvaṃ vai taṃ viśeṣaṃ ca viprāstryaṃśairyuktaṃ gāruḍākhyaṃ purāṇam \|*  
*ādyāṃśaṃ vai karmakāṇḍaṃ vadanti dvitīyāṃśaṃ dharmakāṇḍaṃ tamāhuḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

*tṛtīyāṃśaṃ brahmakāṇḍaṃ vadanti teṣāṃ madhye tvantimoyaṃ variṣṭhaḥ \|*  
*tṛtīyāṃśaśravaṇātpuṇyamāhustulyaṃ puṇyaṃ bhāgavatasya viprāḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

47-48. О Brahmanas, listen to that speciality the Garuda Purana is divided into three parts. The first part is called Karma Kanda, the second Dharma Kanda and the third Brahma Kanda. Among these the third part is the best Listening to this part grants virtue which is equal to the virtue of listening to complete Bhagavata.

## \|\| 1.49 \|\|

*tṛtīyāṃśe paṭhite vedatulyaṃ phalaṃ bhavennātra vicāryamasti \|*  
*tṛtīyāṃśaśravaṇādeva viprāḥ phalaṃ proktaṃ paṭhatopyarthamevam \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. When the third part is recited one obtains merit equal to the merit of reciting the Vedas. There can be no second thought in this respect.

## \|\| 1.50 \|\|

*tṛtīyāṃśaśravaṇādarthataśca puṇyaṃ cāhuḥ paṭhato vai daśāṃśam \|*  
*tato varaṃ matsyapurāṇamāhustato varaṃ kūrmapūrāṇamāhuḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. О Brahmins by listening to the third part of the Purana one obtains merit equal to that one obtains by reciting and understanding the same. Mere recitation awards one-tenth of the merit which accrues to the person who listens to and understands the same.

## \|\| 1.51 \|\|

*tatrāpi kiñcidveditavyaṃ bhavecca purāṇaṣaṭke sattvarūpe munīndrāḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Next in order of merit is the Matsya Purana, then comes Kurma Purana, then Vayu. These three are also Sattvika Puranas. О best of sages, in the sixfold series of Sattvika Puranas, there is lot of knowledge to be discovered.

## \|\| 1.52 \|\|

*sattvādhame mātsyakaurme tathāhurvāyu cāhuḥ sāttvikaṃ madhyamaṃ ca \|*  
*viṣṇoḥ purāṇaṃ bhāgavataṃ purāṇaṃ sattvottamaṃ gāruḍaṃ cāhurāryāḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Among the Sattvika Puranas, Matsya and Kurma are inferior, Vayu is middling. Visnu and Bhagavata are superior.

## \|\| 1.53 \|\|

*skāndaṃ pādmaṃ vāmanaṃ vai varāhaṃ tathāgreyaṃ bhaviṣyaṃ parvasṛṣṭau \|*  
*etānyāhū rājasānīti viprāstatraikadeśaḥ sāttvikastāmasaśca \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. Skanda, Padma, Vamana, Varaha, Agni and Bhavisya are Rajasa Puranas. Each of these contains sections on the Rajasa and Tamasa material.

## \|\| 1.54 \|\|

*rajaḥ prācuryādrājasānīti ca huḥ śrāvyāṇi naitāni mumukṣubhiḥ sadā \|*  
*teṣāṃ madhye sāttvikāṃśāśca saṃti teṣāṃ śrutergāruḍīyaṃ phalaṃ ca \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. They are Rajasa, Rajas being prominent in them. Those seeking for release should not listen to these sections. Only the Sattvika part among these Puranas should be listened tothat provides merit equal to that of listening to Garuda.

## \|\| 1.55 \|\|

*brahmāṇḍalaiṅgye brahmavaivartakaṃ vai mārkaṃṇḍeyaṃ brāhmamādityakaṃ ca \|*  
*etānyā hustāmasānīti viprāstatraikadeśaḥ sāttviko rājasaśca \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Brahma, Brahmanda, Brahmavaivarta, Linga, Markandeya and Aditya are the Tamasa Puranas. Parts of these Puranas are Sattvika and Rajasa.

## \|\| 1.56 \|\|

*śrāvyāṇi naitāni manuṣyaloke tattvecchubhistāmasānītyato hi \|*  
*teṣu sthitāḥ sāttvikāṃśā munīndrāsteṣāṃ śrutirgāruḍaikāṅghritulyā \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. They should not be listened toby the seekers of truth, for they are Tamasa. О best of sages, there are Sattvika portions in them, listening to which accords merit equal to the merit of listening to Garuda (in part).

## \|\| 1.57-59 \|\|

*alpānyupapurāṇāni vadantyaṣṭādaśāni ca \|*  
*viṣṇudharmotaraṃ caiva tantraṃ bhāgavataṃ tathā \|\| 57 \|\|*

*tattvasāraṃ nārasiṃhaṃ vāyuproktaṃ tathaiva ca \|*  
*tathā haṃsapurāṇaṃ ca ṣaḍetāni munīśvarāḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

*sāttvikānyeva jānīdhvaṃ prāyaśo nātra saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*eteṣāṃ śravaṇādeva gāruḍārdhaphalaṃ śrutam \|\| 59 \|\|*

57-59. Smaller in size are the Upapuranas which are eighteen in number. Among these, V isnudharmottara, Bhagavata, T attvasara, Nrsimha, Vayu and Hamsa are Sattvika, undoubtedly. By listening to these one secures half of the merit one would receive by listening to Garuda.

## \|\| 1.60-63 \|\|

*bhaviṣyottaranāmānaṃ bṛhannāradameva ca \|*  
*yamanāradasaṃvādaṃ laghunāradameva ca \|\| 60 \|\|*

*vināyakapurāṇaṃ ca bṛhadbrahmāṇḍameva ca \|*  
*etāni rājasānyāhuḥ śravaṇādbhuktaruttamā \|\| 61 \|\|*

*gāruḍātpādatulyaṃ ca phalaṃ cāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ \|*  
*purāṇaṃ bhāgavataṃ śaivaṃ nandiproktaṃ tathaiva ca \|\| 62 \|\|*

*pāśupatyaṃ raiṇukaṃ ca bhairavaṃ ca tathaiva ca \|*  
*etāni tāmasānyāhurharitattvārthavedinaḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

60-63. Bhavisya, Brhannarada and Laghunarada consisting of a dialogue between Yama and Narada, Karttika Purana and Brhad Brahmanda are the Rajasa Puranas. By listening to these one gels full enjoyment. One gets one-fourth of the merit one would obtain by listening to Garuda. The wise have declared thus. Bhagavata, Shiva, Nandi, Pasupata, Rainuka, Bhairava are Tamasa Puranas — so declare the wise who are conversant with the cult of Visnu.

## \|\| 1.64 \|\|

*eteṣāṃ śravaṇādviprāgāruḍāṅghyardhmeva ca \|*  
*sarveṣvapi purāṇeṣu śreṣṭhaṃ bhāgavataṃ smṛtam \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. Listening to these Puranas imparts merit equal to of the merit accruing from listening to Garuda. Bhagavata is the best of all Puranas.

## \|\| 1.65 \|\|

*vedaistulya sama pāṭhe śravaṇe ca tadardhakam \|*  
*arthataḥ śravaṇe cāsya puṇyaṃ daśaguṇaṃ smṛtam \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. By reciting the Purana one gets merit equal to that one would get by reciting the Vedas. One who hears the Purana recited gets half of the merit one gets by hearing the Vedas. If one hears the Purana recited and understands what he hears gets tenfold the merit of the one who hears the Vedas and understands them.

## \|\| 1.66-67 \|\|

*vaktuḥ syāddviguṇaṃ puṇyaṃ vyākhyātuśca tathādhikam \|*  
*anantavedaiḥsāmyamāhurmahāntaḥ bhārānmahattvādbhāratasyāpi viprāḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

*vedobhyosya tvarthataścādhikatvaṃ vadanti bai viṣṇurahasyavedinaḥ \|\| 67 \|\|*

66-67. The reciter (who understands the meaning) gets twice that merit and the commentator or expositor gets even more.

The wise declare that the Purana is equal to the Vedas in the weightiness of purpose and the greatness of glory. It is more valuable than the Vedas so far as the meaning is concerned. This is declared by those who understand the mystery of Visnu.

## \|\| 1.68 \|\|

*tatra śreṣṭhāṃ gītikāmāhurāryāstathaiva viṣṇornāmasāhasraka ca \|*  
*tayostatra śravaṇādbhāratasya daśādhikaṃ phalamāhurmahāntaḥ \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. The wise declare that the praise of Visnu and the thousand appellations of the deity recorded in this Purana are the best among those who can bestow merit. Listening to the two obtains tenfold or more merit one earns by hearing the Bharata.

## \|\| 1.69 \|\|

*daityāḥ sarva viprakuleṣu bhūtvā kṛte yuge bhārate ṣaṭsahasryām \|*  
*niṣkāsya kāṃścinnavanirmitānāṃ niveśanaṃ tatra kurvanti nityam \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. In Bharatavarsa, in the golden age (Satya yuga), the daityas are born in the Brahmin families. They take some Verses out of the Bharata consisting of 6000 verses and replace them by new ones.

## \|\| 1.70 \|\|

*matvā hariṃ bhagavānvyāsarūpī cakre tadā bhāgavataṃ purāṇam \|*  
*tathā samākhyāya ca vaiṣṇavaṃ tattataḥ paraṃ gāruḍākhyaṃ sa cakre \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. The sage Vyasa (the author of this Purana) paid homage to Visnu and composed among the Puranas the Bhagavata Purana, at first, in the glorification of Lord Visnu. Thereafter he composed Garuda.

## \|\| 1.71 \|\|

*ato hi gāruḍaṃ mukhyaṃ purāṇaṃ śāstrasaṃmatam \|*  
*gāruḍena samaṃ nāsti viṣṇudharmapradarśane \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. Garuda is one of the principal Puranas as declared in the Shastras. There is no Purana equal to Garuda in the exposition, of Vaisnava cult.

## \|\| 1.72 \|\|

*yathā surāṇāṃ pravaro janārdano yathāyudhānāṃ pravaraḥ sudarśanam \|*  
*yathāśvamedhaḥ pravaraḥ kratūnāṃ chinneṣu bhakteṣu tathaiva rudraḥ \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. As Visnu is the best of Gods, as Sudarsana is the best of missiles, as the horse sacrifice is the best of sacrifices, as Rudra is the best among the inferior deities.

## \|\| 1.73 \|\|

*nadīṣu gaṅgā jalajeṣu padmamacchinnabhakteṣu tathaiva vāyuḥ \|*  
*tathā purāṇeṣu ca gāruḍaṃ ca mukhyaṃ tadāhurharitattvadarśane \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. As the Ganges is the best among the rivers, lotus among the water-plants, Vayu among the superior deities, so Garuda is the best of all Puranas devoted to the cult of Visnu

## \|\| 1.74 \|\|

*ato harirnamaskāryo gamyo yogyo hariḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. In the Garuda Purana, Visnu is the principal deity. Being worthy of praise and easy to approach he should be honoured and worshipped at first.

## \|\| 1.75-76 \|\|

*bhāgyātmakatvācchrīdevyā namanaṃ nadanu smṛtam \|*  
*paro narottamo vā sa sādhakeśopi ca smṛtaḥ \|\| 75 \|\|*

*ato namyo vāyurapi purāṇādau dvijottamāḥ \|*  
*bhāratī vākyarūpatvānnamyā vāyoranantaram \|\| 76 \|\|*

75-76. After Visnu comes the blissful LaksmI who is equally worthy of honour.?.nd worship.

О best of Brahmins, then comes Vayu, the noblest and the best among the Brahmins and the lord of aspirants of Visnu. He should be worshipped when the recitation of a Purana begins.

Next, the Goddess Bharat! should be worshipped as she represents the Goddess speech itself.

## \|\| 1.77 \|\|

*upasādhako naraḥ prokto yatotastadanantaram \|*  
*namya ityacyate sadbhistāratamyena sarvadā \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. Next, among men, the sage Vyasa, the author of the Puranas should be worshipped. Being the devotee of Visnu he is entitled to worship.

## \|\| 1.78-79 \|\|

*ato vyāsaṃ namaskuryādgranthakartṛtvahetutaḥ \|*  
*śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*vyāsasya namanaṃ hyante kathaṃ kāryaṃ mahātmanaḥ \|\| 78 \|\|*

*ante ca vandane tasya kāraṇaṃ brūhi suvrata \|*  
*sūta uvāca \|*  
*viṣṇoranantaraṃ vyāsanamanaṃ mukhyameva hi \|\| 79 \|\|*

78-79. How is it that the Sage Vyasa comes last in the list of those who are worthy of worship. О you of good vows, tell us the reason thereof.

## \|\| 1.80 \|\|

*harireva yato vyāso vācyacakrasvarūpakaḥ \|*  
*vyāso naiva samatvena prokto bhagavato hareḥ \|\| 80 \|\|*

80\. Homage should be paid to Vyasa at the end while homage is rendered to Visnu at the beginning. Visnu is the principal deity in the Puranas and Vyasa the incarnation of speech- words and their meaning. Vyasa cannot be equated with Visnu

## \|\| 1.81 \|\|

*tatrāpi kāraṇaṃ vakṣye sādareṇa munīśvarāḥ \|*  
*vyāsastu kaścana ṛṣiḥ purāṇe tāmase smṛtaḥ \|\| 81 \|\|*

81\. О best of sages, out of regard for you, 1 shall tell you the reason thereof. In the Puranas dominated by Tamoguna, Vyasa is eulogized as a great sage.

## \|\| 1.82 \|\|

*iti jñānā valaṃbena daityā daityānugaiḥ samāḥ \|*  
*praviśanti hyandhatama iti tvante namaskṛtaḥ \|\| 82 \|\|*

82\. Enticed by the knowledge of this fact the asuras arid their followers who worship Vyasa at first in preference to Visnu emer the i region of pitch darkness. Hence, Vyasa is worshipped at the end This knowledge is the secret of secrets which should be kept close to the heart.

## \|\| 1.83-84 \|\|

*yadidaṃ paramaṃ gopyaṃ hṛdi dhāryaṃ na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*parāṇāṃ namyamevoktaṃ pratipādyaṃ yatotra hi \|\| 83 \|\|*

*samāsavyāsabhāvāddhi parāṇāṃ tatpratīyate \|*  
*vāstavaṃ taṃ na jānīyurupajīvyo yato hariḥ \|\| 84 \|\|*

83-84. Whoever is said to be worthy of homage, should not be accepted as such, for Visnu alone is worthy of worship.

## \|\| 1.85 \|\|

*harirvyāsastveka eva vyāsastu harivatsmṛtaḥ \|*  
*upajīvyatadīśatve tayoreva na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 85 \|\|*

85\. Visnu and Vyasa are identical. The Smrtis declare that Vyasa resembles Visnu in many respects.

## \|\| 1.86 \|\|

*īśakoṭipraviṣṭatvācchriyaḥ svāmitvamīritam \|*  
*trayāṇāmupajīvyatvātsevyatvātsvāmitā smṛtāḥ \|\| 86 \|\|*

86\. The three — Vayu, etc., are declared to be worthy of worship.

## \|\| 1.87 \|\|

*vāyvādīnāṃ trayāṇāṃ ca sevyatvātsevyatā smṛtā \|*  
*bhūbhāraharaṇe viṣṇoḥ pradhānāṅgaṃ hi mārutiḥ \|\| 87 \|\|*

87\. In lightening the burden of the earth, Hartman the son of Maruti is the principal organ of Visnu,

## \|\| 1.88 \|\|

*vākyarūpā bhāratī tu dvitīyāṅgaṃ hi sā smṛtā \|*  
*tṛtīyāṅga hareḥ śeṣo na namyāḥ sāmyato hareḥ \|\| 88 \|\|*

88\. Bharati (also called Sarasvati), identical with speech, is the second organ. &esa is the third organ. The trio is not treated on equal' terms with Visnu.

## \|\| 1.89 \|\|

*pratipādyā mukhyatayā namyā eva samīritāḥ \|*  
*avāntarāśca vāyvādyā na namyāstena te smṛtāḥ \|\| 89 \|\|*

89\. They who are declared to be principal deities are worthy of worship. Vayu and the rest are secondary, hence they are not to be worshipped (on equal terms with Visnu).

## \|\| 1.90 \|\|

*bhīṣmadroṇādināmāni bhīmādiṣveva mukhyataḥ \|*  
*vācakāni yato nityaṃ tannamyāste munīśvarāḥ \|\| 90 \|\|*

90\. Among Bhlma and others BhTsma and Drona are me principal. The noblest of men they are worthy of worship. Thus I have told

## Chapter 2

## \|\| 2.1 \|\|

*śrīśaunaka uvāca \|*  
*kathaṃ sasarja bhagavāṃstattattattvābhimāninaḥ \|*  
*sṛṣṭikramaṃ na jānāmi devānāṃ hyantaraṃ mune \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Shaunaka said: — please tell us how the lord created gods out of the constituents. О sage, I am not aware of the serial order of creation and their traits distinguishing one another.

## \|\| 2.2 \|\|

*śaunakenaiva muktastu sūto vacanamabravīt \|*  
*śūta uvāca \|*  
*samyagvyavasitā buddhistava brahmarṣisattama \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Thus spoken to by Shaunaka, Suta said in reply.

## \|\| 2.3 \|\|

*evameva kṛtaḥ praśro harau tu garuḍena vai \|*  
*yaduktavānharistasmaitadvakṣyāmi tavānagha \|*  
*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*sṛṣṭiṃ vrūhi mahābhāga saccidānandavigraha \|\| 3 \|\|*

Suta said: — О best of Brahmanic sages, you have put a pertinent question to me.

3\. Garuda had put the same question to Visnu. I shall tell you, О pious one, what Visnu had proclaimed to Garuda.

## \|\| 2.4 \|\|

*sṛṣṭau jñāte tavotkarṣo jñātaprāyo bhaviṣyati \|*  
*brahmādīnāṃ tāratamyajñānaṃ mama bhaviṣyati \|\| 4 \|\|*

Garuda said: — О blessed one, of the nature of existence, consciousness and bliss, please tell us how this universe came into being?

4\. When the process of creation is revealed, your excellence is manifested. I shall like to know the relative superiority of the gods, Brahma, Visnu and others.

## \|\| 2.5 \|\|

*mokṣopāyamyaḥ sa vokta mitatarattasya sādhanam \|*  
*garuḍenaiva muktastu kṛṣṇo vacanamabravīt \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. What are the means whereby one can attain moksa. Thus questioned by Garuda lord Krsna spoke in reply.

## \|\| 2.6 \|\|

*śṛkṛṣṇa uvāca mūlarūpe hyato jñeyo viṣṇutvādviṣṇuravyayaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Sri Krsna said: — The eternal, indeclinable Visnu is of the basic fohai. Because he is all- pervasive, his incarnation as Krsna is known as complete.

## \|\| 2.7 \|\|

*avatāramidaṃ proktaṃ pūrṇatvādeva suvrata \|*  
*aneko hyekatāṃ prāpya saṃśete pralayāya vai \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Manifested in multiforms he becomes one at the time of dissolution.

## \|\| 2.8 \|\|

*tatrāpi ca viśeṣosti jñātavyaṃ tatvameva saḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. There too, there is a demarcating line. He, the supreme reality, should be known by all means.

## \|\| 2.9 \|\|

*bhedena darśanādvāpi bhedābhedena darśanāt \|*  
*viṣṇorguṇānāṃ rūpāṇāṃ tadaṅgānāṃ sukhādinām \|*  
*tatraiva daśanādvāpi kṣiprameva tamo vrajet \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. By knowing him as many, in different forms or fmany and-one or by observing his different traits, forms or components such as existence, consciousness and bliss, one is absolved of lamas immediately.

## \|\| 2.10-13 \|\|

*puruṣāntaramārabhya kalpitā ye dvijottama \|*  
*harirūpāstu te jñeyā ekībhūtā hi tena te \|\| 10 \|\|*

*praleya samanuprāpte jīvāḥ sa yānti māmakāḥ \|*  
*virājṛpe hareḥ saṃti tadā te ca hyanekadhā \|\| 11 \|\|*

*ekībhāvaṃ prāpnuvanti mūlena pralaye dvija \|*  
*biṃbena tu svayaṃ viṣṇurekībhāvaṃ vrajedyadi \|\| 12 \|\|*

*pratibiṃbaḥ kathaṃ jīvo bhavennārāyaṇasya ca \|*  
*tadadhīnastatsadṛśo harerjīvo na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

10-13. When the hour of dissolution arrives, О best of birds, the forms of Visnu that were manifest in different individuals assemble in the all-embracing body of Visnu. О bird, they are united with the root.

Visnu becomes one with his reflection jlva, then how can Jlva remain dependent on Visnu.

## \|\| 2.14 \|\|

*pratibiṃbasya śabdārtho hyayamevamudāhṛtaḥ \|*  
*tasmācca biṃbarūpāṇāmekībhāvaṃ na cintayet \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Thus they explain the meaning of pratibimba. The difference between the two and reflection is obvious therefore.

## \|\| 2.15 \|\|

*kṛṣṇarāmādivaccaiva tvekī bhāvo vivakṣitaḥ \|*  
*biṃbānāṃ mūlarūpasya bhedo nātra vivakṣitaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Krsna, Rama and others of equal cadre are the emanations of basic reality. Though they manifest in different forms, they are not to be thought'of as separate.

## \|\| 2.16-17 \|\|

*tatrāpi ca viśeṣosti jñātavyastattvamicchubhiḥ \|*  
*ekāṃśena tu biṃbaistu caikībhāvaṃ vrajanti te \|\| 16 \|\|*

*ekāṃśena tu jīvatve saṃsthitā nātra saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*biṃbamūlaṃ na jānanti te janā hyasurāḥ smṛtāḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

16-17. Those who are in search of reality should note a special point. Jivas arc related to the root only partially. They stand as Jivas distinct from the root.

## \|\| 2.18 \|\|

*eka eva hariḥ pūrvaṃ hyavidyāvaśataḥ svayam \|*  
*aneko bhavati hyārādādarśapratirbibavat \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. The knowledge of the root is essential. Those who are not aware of the root are asuras. They think that by illusion, the nondual entity shows many forms as reflections in the mirror.

## \|\| 2.19 \|\|

*evaṃ vadanti ye mūḍhā stepi yāntyadharaṃ tamaḥ \|*  
*upādhirdvividhaḥ proktaḥ svarūpo bāhya eva ca \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. But this sort of thinking takes them to the region of pitch darkness. The marks that distinguish Self from Supreme self are twofold: internal and external.

## \|\| 2.20 \|\|

*bāhyopādhirlaye yāti muktāvanyasya saṃśthitiḥ \|*  
*sarvopādhivi nāśe hi pratibiṃbaḥ kathaṃ bhavet \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. The external marks disappear as the material dissolves. The internal difference remains even in the state of release. If both the internal and external differences disappear (there being non-dua! brahman in existence), how can there be a difference due to reflection and the thing reflected?

## \|\| 2.21 \|\|

*cidrūpākhyo hyupādhistu mokṣe yepyadhikāriṇaḥ \|*  
*duḥ kharūpo hyupādhistu tamaso yedhikāriṇaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. The distinguishing mark of the self going to be released is consciousness or awareness (cit). The distinguishing mark of the Self to hell (region of darkness) is suffering.

## \|\| 2.22 \|\|

*miśrarūpo hyupādhistu nityasaṃsāriṇāṃ mataḥ \|*  
*bāhyopādhirliṅgadehaḥ sarveṣāṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. For the jiva who is prone to migrate,the distinguishing mark is mixed (cidrupa and duhkharilpa). The grass body is an external appendage for the seeker.

## \|\| 2.23 \|\|

*daityāḥ duḥ khāyate yasmāttasmāduḥ khī hariḥ svayam \|*  
*tattadduḥ khasvarūpatvāddaityānāṃ biṃbarūpakaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. As the evil-minded asuras of ten cause distress, Visnu feels distressed on that account. Thus the nature of the lord being none other than distress itself, the lord acquires nature of reflection.

## \|\| 2.24 \|\|

*daityasthitānāṃ biṃbānāṃ mūlarūpasya vai prabhoḥ \|*  
*parasparaṃ tathā bhedaṃ hyantaraṃ vā na cintayet \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. One should note the distinguishingmark of the lord's reflection in the daityas.

## \|\| 2.25 \|\|

*śrībhūdurgādirūpāṇāṃ tathā sītādirūpiṇām \|*  
*anyonyaṃ nāṇumātraṃ ca bhedo bāhyāntarepi ca \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Among the divine forms as that of Surya or of human forms as that of SIta, there is not in the least any difference internal or external.

## \|\| 2.26 \|\|

*cintanīyaḥ kathamapi jñātvā yāntyadharaṃ tamaḥ \|*  
*pratibiṃbasthito biṃbaḥ strīrūpo hyasti sarvadā \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. He who ponders over the difference goes to hell. The image of an object or a person reflected in the mirror is always akin to the object or person reflected.

## \|\| 2.27 \|\|

*pralaye samanuprāpte lakṣmyā saha khagottama \|*  
*ekībhāvaṃ nāpnuvanti viṃbena saha saṃsthitāḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. At the hour of dissolution, О best of birds, the was in the form of lord's reflection do not merge into lord.

## \|\| 2.28 \|\|

*biṃvasthitānāṃ rūpāṇāṃ lakṣmyāśca vinatāsuta \|*  
*bhedastu nāṇumātraṃ ca śaṅkanīyaḥ kathañcana \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Not even the slightest doubt be entertained, О son of Vinata, about the nondifference between reflection and the object reflected.

## \|\| 2.29-30 \|\|

*yadā hi śete pralayārṇave vibhurjīvāṃśca sarvānudare niveśya \|*  
*muktāṃśca brahmendramarudgaṇādīnprātpavyamuktīṃśca sutau? ca saṃsthitān \|\| 29 \|\|*

*prāptāndhakūpādisamastajīvāṃstathaiva prāptavyakalīnathāparān \|*  
*tathaiva nityaṃ sṛtisaṃsthitāñjanānacetanānṛkṣarūpādijīvān \|\| 30 \|\|*

29-30. When the hour of dissolution arrives the lord sleeps in the ocean keeping jivas in his belly, also the released souls, Brahma, Maruts, etc, as well as those going to be liberated, those present in hell, those who are likely to fall, those who are stationary, those who rotate on the wheel of life and death, those who are wild beasts, bears, etc. Keeping all these in the belly the lord sleeps in waters for duration of a Kalpa.

## \|\| 2.31 \|\|

*evaṃ janāñjaṭhare saṃnidhāya samyakūśete hyaṃbhasi vai sa kalpe \|*  
*lakṣmīstu sā sarvavedātmikā ca bhaktyā harau nityasaṃvardhitāpi \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Laksmi corresponds knowledge embodied in the Vedas, She is nourished by devotion to Visnu. She exhibits devotion to Visnu by the movements of her body and offers homage to the lord.

## \|\| 2.32 \|\|

*atyādaraṃ darśayatīva sā tu īḍe viṣṇuṃ bhaktisaṃvardhitāpi \|*  
*ceṣṭādirūpeṇa tadā na kiñcidāsīdvinā viṣṇumatha śriyaṃ ca \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. There was nothing but Visnu and Laksmi as the creation came into being.

## \|\| 2.33 \|\|

*paryaṅkarūpeṇa vabhūva devī vāsasvarūpeṇa ramā vireje \|*  
*sarvaṃ ramā saiva tadaiva cāsītsaikā devī bahurūpā babhāṣe \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. The goddess Laksmi served as a couch to the lord. She was her lord's abode. Among the females she was the first who praised the lord thus.

## \|\| 2.34 \|\|

*tvamutkṛṣṭaḥ sarvadevottamatvānna tvatsamaḥ kaścidevādhiko vā \|*  
*tvaṃ brahma eko na caturmukhaśca nāhaṃ rudro na bṛhaspatiśca \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. In your excellence, you exceed all. None is comparable to you, none excels you. You alone are the non-dual Brahma. The term is applicable neither to Brahma nor to Laksmi nor to Rudra nor to Brhaspati.

## \|\| 2.35 \|\|

*viṣṇāveva brahmaśabdo hi mukhyo hyanyeṣvamukhyo brahmarudrādikeṣu \|*  
*anantaguṇapūrṇatvādbrahmeti harirucyate \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. The term is applicable primarily to Visnu. It is secondarily applicable to Brahma, Rudra and others. Being the store-house of endless merits Visnu is caged Brahma.

## \|\| 2.36 \|\|

*guṇādipūrṇatābhāvānnānye brahmetyudāhṛtāḥ \|*  
*deśānantyaṃ guṇataḥ kālato vā nāstyānantyaṃ kvāpi deśe ca kāle \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Other gods contain no fulness of merits, hence they are not called Brahma.

## \|\| 2.37-38 \|\|

*yadā nantyaṃ kimu vaktavyamatra guṇānantyaṃ nāsti brahmādikeṣu \|*  
*yadyapyahaṃ deśataḥ kālataśca samastadā vāsudevena sārdham \|\| 37 \|\|*

*tathāpi me guṇato nāstyanantaṃ tato dharmā guṇatonantataśca \|*  
*saṃti śrutāvaviruddhāśca deve cintyā hyacintyā bahudhā te hyanantāḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

37-38. They are non-eternal in relation to space or time. No problem couid have arisen, were they found eternal in all respects. But the problem stands, for Brahma and other gods are not eternal due to the shortage of their merits.

Though I am comparable to lord Vasudeva, in respect of etemality in relation to time and space, I am not equal to him in the fulness of merit. The Vedas declare your manifold merits which in all authoritative texts remain uncontradicted. Your merits are endless, some being manifest and some hidden.

## \|\| 2.39-43 \|\|

*ato guṇāṃstava devasya viṣṇo stotuṃ sadā smo na hareḥ kadāpi \|*  
*nāhaṃ na keśau na ca gīrna rudro na dakṣakanyā na ca menakāsutā \|\| 39 \|\|*

*na vai biḍaujā na ca vā pulomajā na cedhmavāho na yamo na cānyaḥ \|*  
*na nārado nāpi bhṛgurvasiṣṭho na vighnapo nāpi balyādayaśca \|\| 40 \|\|*

*na vai virāṭo nāpi bhīmaḥ śaniśca na puṣkaro na kaśerustathaiva \|*  
*na kinnarāḥ pitaro naiva devā gandharvamukhyā nāpi vā tuṣyasaṃjñāḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

*na vai kṣitīśā na ca mānuṣāśca viṣṇorna jānanti kimatra cānye \|*  
*mattodhamaḥ kociguṇena brahmā samo hi tasya brahmaṇo mātariśva \|\| 42 \|\|*

*tau vai virāge haribhaktibhāve dhṛtistitiprāṇabaleṣuyoge \|*  
*buddhau samānau saṃsṛtau mokṣakāle parasparādhārasamanvitau ca \|\| 43 \|\|*

39-43. Therefore, we are unable to evaluate your merits. Neither I, nor Sarasvati, Shiva, Rudra, Satl, the daughter of Daksa, Parvatl the daughter of Menaka or Mena, Indra, IndranI, Agni, Yama, Narada, Bhrgu, Vasistha, Ganesa, Bali, Virata, Bhuman, Saml, Kaseru, Kinnara, manes, gods Gandharvas, Tusyas, kings and their proteges can praise your Virtue..

Brahma is inferior to me by crones of merits. Vayu being equal to Brahma is inferior to me by the same number.

The two are equal to each other in the matter of detachment, in their devotion to Visnu, in their courage, stability, life strength, restraint and intellect from the origin to the dissolution of the universe. The two are inseparable in the exercise of their power or support.

## \|\| 2.44 \|\|

*annābhimānaṃ brahma cāhurmurāriṃ jīvābhimānaṃ vāyumāhurmahāntaḥ \|*  
*na śaktosau brahmadevo vivastuṃ vāyuṃ vinā saṃsṛtāveva nityam \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. The Vedas equate Visnu with Brahma in support of the universe. They declare Vayu as JIva the life principle of the universe. Brahma cannot function as the creator without the support of Vayu.

## \|\| 2.45-46 \|\|

*na taṃ vinā mātariśvā ca vastumanyonyamāptiḥ kālato nyūnatā ca \|*  
*yadā mahattattvani yāmakobhūdbrahmāṇḍāntasthūlasṛṣṭau mahātmā \|\| 45 \|\|*

*tadā vāyurnāśakadvai mahātmā bāhye sṛṣṭau kālabhedena cāsti \|*  
*sarasvatī bhāratī brahmaṇastu saṃvatsarānantaraṃ saṃbabhūva \|\| 46 \|\|*

45-46. Vayu cannot function without the support of Brahma. They support each other in activating the universe. Their mutual superiority can very well be evaluated by taking recourse to time.

When lord Brahma controlled the principle of intellect from activating material creation lying dormant in the universal egg, Vayu was unable to set it in motion. The evolution of the universe was the event of later date.

## \|\| 2.47 \|\|

*yadā daśābdāḥ samatītā mahātmā tadā vāyuḥ samabhūllokapūjyaḥ \|*  
*kiñcinnyūnatvaṃ sthūlasṛṣṭau mahātmannaitāvatā vānayoḥ saumyahāniḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. The honourable Sarasvati was born after Brahma had completed his one year. When ten years were completed, the honourable Vayu came into being. In the material world, Vayu is inferior to Visnu. But, their coordination does not suffer by this inferiority.

## \|\| 2.48 \|\|

*sarasvatī vatsarātsaṃbabhūva hyanantaraṃ brahmaṇo janmakālāt \|*  
*giraḥ sakāśātkālato nyūnatāsti vāyostadā hyadhamattve kṣatiḥ kā \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Sarasvati was born after Brahma completed a year. Sarasvati is therefore inferior to Visnu in respect of time. Vayu is much more inferior in the same respect.

## \|\| 2.49 \|\|

*vāyoranantaraṃ vāṇī hyabhūtsaṃvatsarātparam \|*  
*yāvatpaścājjanistāvatpūrvadehakṣayo bhavet \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. After Vayu had completed a year Vak was born. As it was born after, it perished early.

## \|\| 2.50 \|\|

*śeṣastvindro rudra ete trayaśca samā hyete jñānabalādikeṣvapi tathāpi teṣāṃ kālato nyūnatāsti kālopi teṣāṃ dvivyesahasravarṣam \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Sesa, the lord of snakes, Indra, and Rudra- the three arc equal in knowledge, strength and spirit. Still they are inferior to each other in respect of time. They last for two thousand divine years.

## \|\| 2.51 \|\|

*anantarudro brahmavāyū yathā vā tathā jñeyo naiva hāniḥ svarūpe \|*  
*sthūlasya sṛṣṭau bāhyasṛṣṭau mahātmankālānnyūnatvaṃ sa mayā naiva cintyaḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Sesa, Rudra, Brahma and Vayu are of equal cadre and equal efficacy in the process of evolving the material creation. They are inferior to one another only in respect of time. But this inferiority should be totally ignored.

## \|\| 2.52 \|\|

*teṣāṃ sakāśādvāruṇī pārvatī ca sauparṇīnāmnī tistra etā mahātman \|*  
*daśābdebhyonantaraṃ saṃbabhūvuḥ sarasvatī bhāratīvacca bodhyā \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Varuni, Parvatl and Sauparna — the three were born after ten years. They should be treated at par with Sarasvatl and Bharati.

## \|\| 2.53 \|\|

*indro varo rudrabhāryādikebhya evaṃ jñānaṃ sarvadā dehyamandam \|*  
*evaṃ jñānaṃ yasya bhavecca loke sa vai jñānī vedavedyaḥ sa eva \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. Indra is superior to Rudrani (wife of Rudra). This knowledge is always gainful. Possessed of this knowledge one is called the knower. He alone has the knowledge of the Vedas,

## \|\| 2.54 \|\|

*na vai jñānītyantaraṃ yo na veda sa vedavādī na ca vedapāṭhakaḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. One who is ignorant of different traits of gods is not the knower of the Vedas. He is merely a Veda-vadin, not a Veda-pathaka.

## \|\| 2.55 \|\|

*vedākṣarāṇi yāvanti paṭhitāni dvijātibhiḥ \|*  
*tāvanti harināmāni priyāṇi ca hareḥsadā \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Whatever syllables of the Vedas are repeated by men of three castes, constitute the very names of Hari which are ever dear to him.

## \|\| 2.56 \|\|

*mama svāmī harirnityaṃ dāsohaṃ sarvadā hareḥ \|*  
*brahmādyā devatāḥ sarvā guravo me yathākramam \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. Hari is my master. I am a life-long slave of Hari. Brahma and other gods are my life- preceptors.

## \|\| 2.57 \|\|

*eteṣāṃ ca hariḥ svāmī vede sarvatra gīyate \|*  
*evaṃ jānaṃstu yo vedānsaṃpaṭhetsa dvijottamaḥ \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. The Vedas declare Hari as the lord of all. He who learns the Vedas with this knowledge is the best of the twice-born.

## \|\| 2.58 \|\|

*sa vedapāṭhako jñeyastadanye vedavādinaḥ \|*  
*vedabhārabharākrāntaḥ sa vai brāhmaṇagardabhaḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. He alone is the knower of the Vedas. Other than him is only a Veda-vadin who carries the burden of the Vedas on his shoulders.

## \|\| 2.59-60 \|\|

*jñānābhimānī vedamānī ubhau tu parasparaṃ hyūcatuḥ sarvadaiva \|*  
*jalaṃ vedo yatra vāso murārerācāryāṇāṃ saṃgadoṣāddvijānām \|\| 59 \|\|*

*mahāparādhāḥ saṃti loke mahātmansahasraśaḥ śataśaḥ koṭiśaśca \|*  
*hariśca tānkṣamate sarvadaiva nāmatrayasmaraṇādvai kupāluḥ \|\| 60 \|\|*

59-60. Men possessing knowledge of Brahman (Supreme reality) and that of the supreme text (viz., the Vedas) speak to each other thus Veda is water wherein abides Visnu. Men of teaching and other professions incur countless sins which out of compassion, he condones if they utter his three names.

## \|\| 2.61 \|\|

*sarvāparādhādrahitaṃ dānamānairyuktaṃ sadā tāratamyācca hīnam \|*  
*dṛṣṭvāparādhaṃ tasya viṣṇurmahātmā hāhākāraṃ kurute krodhabuddhyā \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. The Supreme lord, when he observes that a guiltless person, with an honourable place in society, has committed a sinful deed, is extremely irritated and howls at him.

## \|\| 2.62 \|\|

*uttiṣṭha govinda suvedavedya sovyātkṛtākhyo mayi samyak prasīda \|*  
*bho keśavottiṣṭha sukhasvarūpa sṛṣṭau vyaye vartayituṃ samarthaḥ \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. О Govinda, you can be realized only by means of true knowledge, not by any other means. You are of the nature of bliss. Rise, О Kesava and be favourable to me. О lord, you have the power to create as well as dissolve the Universe.

## \|\| 2.63 \|\|

*sṛṣṭvā brahmāṇaṃ prerayetpūjyasṛṣṭau sṛṣṭvā rudraṃ prerayetsaṃhṛtau ca \|*  
*prāptavyayogyānbrahmaśeṣādidevāndṛṣṭvādṛṣṭvā dehi mokṣaṃ ca samyak \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. You generate Brahma and urge him to create the Universe. You generate Rudra and urge him to dissolve it. You observe god Brahma, &esa and the rest who are worthy of approach.

## \|\| 2.64 \|\|

*hare murāre svāpahīnādya tiṣṭha kalpā dikānantarajñāna (raṃ buddhi) (jāna) hīnāt \|*  
*samyag dṛṣṭvā karmadṛṣṭyā mahātmallaṃbdhaṃ tamo dāhi duḥ khasvarūpam \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. Rise up, О Hari, that are ever watchful. Deprived of true knowledge and engrossed in worldly affairs from kalpa to kalpa, I undergo tortuous pains of unbearable suffering, О lord.

## \|\| 2.65 \|\|

*daityādikānduḥ khamatīnha yasmāttamasyandhesarvadā citsvarūpī \|*  
*tasmādāhurduḥ svarūpī haristvaṃ duḥ khasvarūpāttvaṃ ca duḥ khī hare tvam \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. О Hari, you are of the nature of consciousness (cit sakti). You throw sinful daityas and evil minded persons in the dungeon full of intense darkness. They say you are of the nature of suffering, О Hari, since you are distressed by that act of yours.

## \|\| 2.66 \|\|

*uttiṣṭha nārāyaṇa vāsudeva hyuttiṣṭha kṛṣṇācyuta mādhaveti \|*  
*uttiṣṭha vaikuṇṭha dayārdramūrte uttiṣṭha lakṣmīśa namonamaste \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. Rise, О Narayana, Vasudeva, Krsna, Acyuta, Madhava, Vaikuntha, lord of Laksmi, of compassionate nature. Homage to you.

## \|\| 2.67 \|\|

*uttiṣṭha madhveśa sarasvatīśa uttiṣṭha rudreśa tathāṃbikeśa \|*  
*uttiṣṭha candreśa tathā śacīśa vipreśa bhakteśa gaveśa nityam \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. Rise, О lord of Madhu, lord of Sarasvatl, Lord of Rudra, lord of Ambika, lord of the moon, lord of SacI, lord of the Brahmins, lord of devotion, lord of cows. Rise, О lord.

## \|\| 2.68 \|\|

*śāstrapriyottiṣṭha ṛci priyastvaṃ yajuḥ priyottiṣṭha nidānamūrte \|*  
*sāmapriyastvaṃ ca tathā murāre atharvavedapriya sarvadā tvam \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. You that are fond of sastras, rise up. You that are fond of Rk hymns rise up. You that are fond of Yajus rise up. You that are a primary cause and fond of Saman, rise up. You that are the enemy of Mura and fond of Atharva lore, rise up.

## \|\| 2.69 \|\|

*gadyapriyastvaṃ ca purāṇamūrte stutipriyottiṣṭha vicitramūrte \|*  
*sugāyanaprītikarastvameva hyutiṣṭha śīghraṃ kamalā patistvam \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. You that are of primeval form, you that are fond of prose, you that are of variegated form, you that are fond of praise, you that are lord of Laksmi we please you with melodious song. Rise, О lord, rise immediately.

## Chapter 3

## \|\| 3.1 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*babhūvecchā mama devasya viṣṇoḥ sraṣṭuṃ sṛjyānmokṣayogyāṃśca moktum \|*  
*icchāśaktiḥ sarvadaivāsti viṣṇostathāpi tadvyāharaṇaṃ ca laukikam \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. God Visnu had a desire to create beings worthy of creation, to release beings worthy of release. Visnu ever cherishes such desires, though the expression of such desires is expressedly material.

## \|\| 3.2 \|\|

*tadā harirjagṛhe laukikaṃ ca tamaḥ pānaṃ tena rūpeṇa cakre \|*  
*tadrūpamāhuḥ prākṛtaṃ vai tadajñā hyandhaṃ tamaḥ praviśantyeva sarve \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Hari assumed a material form and swallowed darkness. That form of the lord they declare to be material. Those who do not know this enter the pitch dark region.

## \|\| 3.3 \|\|

*avatārā mahāviṣṇoḥ sarve pūrṇāḥ prakīrtitāḥ \|*  
*pūrṇaṃ ca tatparaṃ rūpaṃ pūrṇātpūrṇāḥ samudgatāḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. The incarnations of lord Visnu are perfect. Perfect is that supreme form. Perfection begets perfection.

## \|\| 3.4 \|\|

*parāvaratvaṃ teṣāṃ tu vyaktimātraviśeṣataḥ \|*  
*na deśakālasāmarthyātpārāvaryaṃ kathañcana \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Superiority and inferiority rest on personality, not on the facilities of Space and Time.

## \|\| 3.5 \|\|

*pūrvarūpaṃ ca pūrṇaṃ ca pūrṇaṃ padavitāragam? \|*  
*rūpaṃ tadātmanyādāya pūrṇamevāvaśiṣyate \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The Supreme lord is full. The Superimposed universe is full. When the Superimposed full is taken off that which remains is also full.

## \|\| 3.6 \|\|

*laukikavyavahāroyaṃ bhūbhārakṣapaṇādikaḥ \|*  
*tasya dṛrṣṭi vinā nānyo layaḥ kṛṣṇādinā kvacit \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. It has become a custom to say that he incarnates in the person of Krsna for lightening the burden of the earth. There can be no dissolution of the world without his wishes.

## \|\| 3.7 \|\|

*tattve pīḍā na kartavyā tayā duḥ khāni vindati \|*  
*atyantapīḍanāttasya rogastasya na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Too much effort should not be made for realizing Self. Too much effort made for self- realization occasions trouble. Too much effort leads to certain disease, it is certain.

## \|\| 3.8 \|\|

*jñātavyāṃśe tu pīḍā tu kartavyā guruṇā saha \|*  
*tamantevāsinaṃ cāhuḥ sa eva cu guruḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. The teacher and the disciple should mcke common effort to know what it is possible to know. The teacher and the disciple who work together in that direction can rightly be called by those designations.

## \|\| 3.9 \|\|

*ye kurvanti harestattvavicāraṃ tu parasparam \|*  
*tāveva guruśiṣyau tu vinatānandasaṃyuta \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. О son of Vinata, those who think upon the lord as Supreme Reality are the teacher and the taught in the right sense of the word.

## \|\| 3.10 \|\|

*guruṇāpi samaṃ hāsyaṃ kartavyaṃ kuṭilaṃ vinā \|*  
*harṣāmarṣayutaḥ śiṣyo guruḥ kauṭilyasaṃyutaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. They say there is no harm to cut jokes with the teacher, if the joke is simple enough.

## \|\| 3.11 \|\|

*ubhau tau nirayaṃ yāto yāvadācandratārakam \|*  
*sākṣāddhariḥ puruṣaḥ piṅgalākṣaḥ svamāyāyāṃ guṇamayyāṃ mahātmā \|*  
*svapauruṣeṇaiva sumaṅgalena adhāttu vīryaṃ bhagavānvīryavāṃśca \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. If the disciple cuts joke with the teacher out of ridicule or rage, if the teacher makes an ironical remark-the disciple and the teacher go to hell where they stay as long as the moon and the stars shine in the sky.

The High-Souled red-eyed Hari, possessed of strong semen, deposited his energy in Maya consisting of three gunas with an auspicious force.

## \|\| 3.12 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*vīryasvarūpaṃ brūhi me vāsudeva vīrye tvadīye saṃśayo me vibhāti \|*  
*kiṃ vīryamīśasya svarūpabhūtaṃ kiṃ vā vibhinnaṃ vada sādhu vetsi \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Garuda said: — О Hari, please tell us about the nature of your energy and its constituents. Is that energy the very nature of yours or is it a separate entity

## \|\| 3.13 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*yadvīryamādhatta hariḥ svayaṃ prabhurmāyābhidhāyāṃ vinatātanūja \|*  
*tadvīryamāhurnṛhareḥ svarūpaṃ vipaścito niścitatattvadarśinaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Shri Krsna said: — О son of Vinata, the energy which lord Hari deposited in Maya is the very nature of the lord. The wise who know the truth have declared thus.

## \|\| 3.14 \|\|

*bhinnaṃ tadāhuḥ prākṛtameva cāhuḥ svanābhipadmādikavacca bodhyam \|*  
*naitāvatā jñānarūpasya viṣṇorna vīryahāniriti cintanīyam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. They have also declared that the energy being material is a separate entity, like the lotus in the navel of Visnu. This fact is not detrimental to the concept of the lord being full of energy, as the lord is the very form of knowledge.

## \|\| 3.15 \|\|

*vīryasvarūpī bhagavānvā sudevaḥ sarvatra deśepi ca sarvakāle \|*  
*sarvārthavānyadi na syātkhagendra tarhīśvaraḥ puruṣo naiva sa syāt \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Lord Vasudeva is of the nature of energy everywhere and in all times. О lord of birds, if he were not possessed of energy he would not be both Isvara and Purusa.

## \|\| 3.16 \|\|

*acintyavīryaiścintyavīryairdvirūpaḥ strīrūpamekaṃ puruṣaṃ tathā param \|*  
*ubhe rūpe vīryavatī khagendra tayorabhedaścintanīyo hi samyakū \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The people in general regard him as twofold: of the form of male and of the form of female. The two should not be considered to be separate entities, О lord of birds.

## \|\| 3.17 \|\|

*strīrūpavānyadi na syātkhargedrastrīṇāṃ kathaṃ pratibiṃbatvameva \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. If the lord were separate from the female form, О lord of birds, how could the woman be his reflection.

## \|\| 3.18 \|\|

*strīrūpamasmādbrahmajaṃ (dvāstavaṃ) cintanīyaṃ svarūpametannānyathā cintanīyam \|*  
*strīrūpavannaiva vicintanīyaṃ napuṃsakaṃ tbasya janyaṃ hi viddhi \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Hence, the female is inseparable from' the male form. The two forms constitute the very nature of the lord. This should not be taken otherwise.

## \|\| 3.19 \|\|

*napuṃsakaṃ navaiva svarūpabhūtamato harau nāsti vicintanīyam \|*  
*strībiṃbabhūte harirūpe khagendra śrīrūpamastīti vicintanīyam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. The neutral form is alien to his nature. It is the effect and not his real nature. It is not present in Hari, О lord of birds. Know

I that in the form of Hari there is reflected the form of LaksmI.

## \|\| 3.20-21 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*striyā striyaśca saṃyogaṃ vyarthamahurmanīṣiṇaḥ \|*  
*strīrūpabhūte viṃbe tu strīrūpāḥ santi sarvadā \|\| 20 \|\|*

*sthitau tatra nimittaṃ ca brūhi kṛṣṇa mama prabho \|\| 21 \|\|*

20-21. Garuda said: — The contact of a woman with a woman is futile. So say the wise. The female form being the mirror, how can there be reflected another female form? Mow could the female form abide in the female form. What is the reason there of. Tell me, О lord.

## \|\| 3.22-23 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*strībiṃbabhūtastrīrūpe lakṣmīrna syātkhageśvara \|*  
*nityāviyoginī devī kathaṃ syātparamātmanaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

*hareranantarūpāṇāṃ strīrūpāṇāṃ khageśvara \|*  
*anantānantarūpeṇa nityaṃ śuśrūṣaṇe ratā \|\| 23 \|\|*

22-23. Lord Visnu assumes different female forms, О lord of birds. How can it be possible that LaksmI (a female form herself) may not be reflected therein, since she, the eternal one, being inseparable from the lord is always at his service in her many forms.

## \|\| 3.24 \|\|

*ato lakṣmyā viyogastu śaṅkanīyaḥ kathañcana \|*  
*nārāyaṇo nāma hariḥ svatantraḥ śriyā vinā nāsti kadāpi tārkṣya \|*  
*harermukundasya padāravinde śuśrūṣamāṇā paramādareṇa \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Hence, it is the separation from her lord (and not her union with him) that can be the object of doubt on the part of the disciple.

О Garuda, lord Visnu can never remain without LaksmI who is ever devoted to his lotus-feet.

## \|\| 3.25 \|\|

*hariṃ vinā śrīrapi deśakāle nāstīti mokṣecchubhireva vedyam \|*  
*yasyāmadhādvīryamanukṣaṇaṃ ca sā māmikā cendrajālā tmiketi \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Those seeking for release should know that LaksmI can never remain without Visnu in any space or time.

## \|\| 3.26 \|\|

*vadanti ye asurā mūḍharūpā adhaṃmataḥ praviśantyeva sarve \|*  
*māyā nāma prakṛtistvemāhuḥ susūkṣmarūpā na tu cendrajālikā \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Lord Visnu deposits energy inher to further growth of creation. The asuras hold that energy is the magical power of the lord. But they are fools who ignore reality and as a result enter the region of pitch darkness.

In fact what we call Maya is nothing but Prakrti (the primordial nature) invested with subtle form that is different from magic.

## \|\| 3.27 \|\|

*tasyābhimānaḥ śrīriti veditavyo vīryādhānaṃ tatra teṣāṃ ca melaḥ \|*  
*kāryonmukhaṃ melanaṃ cāhurāryā ito rūpaṃ nāhurāyyāśca viṣṇoḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. They should know that LaksmI is the very Self of Lord Visnu wlio deposits energy in her. The union of the two is not without a purpose.

## \|\| 3.28 \|\|

*sānādi nityā satyarūpā ca viṣṇormithyā rūpā sā kathaṃ syātkhagendra \|*  
*satyā tanuḥ prakṛtestannigūḍhā satyatvamāhurvyavahārārtharūpam \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. She is beginningless, eternal and truthful. How can she be a fake, О lord of birds? Prakrti is eternal truth though not manifest in distinct form.

## \|\| 3.29 \|\|

*vyavahārarūpā satyatā cetprakṛtyāstadā kathaṃ syādyadanādibhūtā \|*  
*anādinityā yadi na syātkhagendra suśūkṣmarūpeṇa na kāraṇaṃ syāt \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. But if she be an empirical reality, how can she be eternal and if she be non-eternal, О lord of birds, how can she be the cause of creativity in her subtle form?

## \|\| 3.30 \|\|

*sūkṣmeṇa rūpeṇa ca kāraṇaṃ syāt tarhi prapañcasya ca kāraṇaṃ vada \|*  
*avidyāyā vaśato viṣṇureva nānārūpairdṛśyate viṣṇureva \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. If she be the cause of creativity in the subtle form, who can be the cause of creativity in the gross form?

## \|\| 3.31 \|\|

*śāstrajñānānnāśameti hyavidyā na saṃśayo hariṇā caikyameti \|*  
*evaṃ brūṣe yadi vādatkhagendra vakṣyehaṃ te tatra yuktiṃ śṛṇu tvam \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. If you say: By the power of illusion, Visnu manifests himself in many forms. But when illusion disappears by knowledge, the universe (of various forms) is absorbed in Visnu. О lord of birds, hear, I shall produce an argument.

## \|\| 3.32 \|\|

*sarvajñarūpasya hi me murāreḥ kathaṃ harerghaṭate hyajñatā ca \|*  
*sūrye yathā tamo nāsti tathā nārāyaṇe harau \|*  
*ajñānaṃ nāsti pakṣīdra kathaṃ tatvaṃ bravīṣyaho \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Hari, being omniscient, how can ignorance abide with him? As darkness cannot co-exist with the sun, there can be no ignorance co-existing with the lord.

## \|\| 3.33 \|\|

*ato nāhaṃ brāhmaṇastvādikālādupādhisaṃbandhavaśādajñatācet \|*  
*sarvajñosau kutra pakṣīndra viṣṇuralpajñajīvo jñānaśūnyaśca kutra \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Hence, the lord is not associated with Maya. If that were so, О lord of birds, how could we explain the difference between the omniscient lord and the lord of limited knowledge.

## \|\| 3.34 \|\|

*viruddhayoścānayoḥ sarvadaiva kathaṃ caikyaṃ saṃvādiṣyanti vedāḥ \|*  
*deśe kāle sarvadā dduḥ khahīno jagatkartā pūrṇaśaktiḥ sadaiva \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. How could the Vedas declare the identity of the two who are of contrary nature. Not circumscribed by space and time, the creator of the universe is omnipotent and is above all sorrows.

## \|\| 3.35 \|\|

*jīvaḥ sadā svalpakartāsti pūrṇaḥ saṃsārarūpe duḥ kharūpe ca nityam \|*  
*viruddhayoścānayoraikyamāhurīśasya māyāvaśato māyinaśca \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. The individual soul, on the other hand, is the creator of a small world, being prone to sorrows and afflictions of births..The two are contradictory in nature and appear to be distinct. Such is the power of illusion of the magician lord.

## \|\| 3.36 \|\|

*ye vaiṣṇavā vaiṣṇavadāsavaśyāsteṣāṃ drohaṃ sarvadā saṃcaredyaḥ \|*  
*hariprītistena bhavenna nityamānandavṛddhistena bhavenna muktau \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. The devotees of Visnu and their followers are exempt from censure and reproach. He who bears malice to them cannot receive the pleasure of Visnu. Even in the state of release he cannot enjoy perfect bliss.

## \|\| 3.37 \|\|

*māyī sadā māyibhṛtyastathāpi bhedajñānānnindyate kāryate ca \|*  
*tenāpi teṣāṃ duḥ khavṛddhirbhavecca hyadhaṃ tamaḥ punarāvṛttihīnam \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Those who perceive a difference between the Supreme and the subservient Self become subject to reproach and are overpowered by suffering and afflicted by affliction. They enter the region of pitch darkness whence there can be no return.

## \|\| 3.38 \|\|

*khagendrātaḥ prakṛtiḥ sūkṣmarūpā sā nityā sā satyabhūtā sadaiva \|*  
*evaṃ svayaṃ kālavāyvādikānāṃ parā (ramā)ṇavaḥ satyarūpāśca santi \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. О lord of birds, the primordial nature (Prakrti) is subtle in form. It has the nature of eternal truth as are the infinitesimal particles of space, time, air and other elements.

## \|\| 3.39-40 \|\|

*parā (māṇū)nāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ veditavyaṃ jñānecchubhirnānyathā veditavyam \|*  
*padārthānāṃ pārthivānāṃ khagendra viśeṣāṇāṃ caramākhyo viśeṣaḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

*sa evaḥ syātparamāṇurdvijendra yontyovi (va) śeṣovayavaśca sa smṛtaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

39-40. The seekers of eternal wisdom should know that the material objects consist of infinitesimal particles. You should know, О bird, that in the categories of the material objects there is a category caved Visesa of which the final Visesa is the paramanu.

## \|\| 3.41 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*he kṛṣṇa he mādhava sāttvatāṃ pate padārthānāṃ caramāṃśaḥ parāṇu \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Garuda said: — О Krsna, О Madhava, О lord of Satvatas, you say that the ultimate particle of a substance is paramanu.

## \|\| 3.42 \|\|

*iti proktaṃ tatra me saṃśayosti yontyo viśeṣaḥ sa tu nāṃśayuktaḥ \|*  
*yo hyaṃśayukto na tu soṃtyo viśeṣa evaṃ mamābhāti vacastu tathyam \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. It is still not clear to me. The ultimate particle is indivisible (the other particles are divisible). That which is divisible cannot be ultimate particle. This view is invariable, so it appears to me.

## \|\| 3.43 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*ya eva loke saṃsthitā mānuṣāstu viśeṣāṇāṃ darśane śaktiyuktāḥ \|*  
*tathāpi te yasya cāṃśitvameva viśeṣaṃ vai naiva draṣṭuṃ samarthāḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Sri Krsna said: — There are experts who can perceive the divisible particles of a substance but not the ultimate particle.

## \|\| 3.44 \|\|

*tamevāhuścaramāṃśaṃ viśeṣaṃ ye caivamāhurmunayastena cānye \|*  
*ye kāṇādā gautamādyāḥ khagendra niraṃśakaṃ paramāṇuṃ vadanti \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. The sages declare the ultimate particle to be Visesa. Following the view of ancient sages, the philosophers Kanada and Gautama.

О lord of birds, have indorsed that the ultimate Visesa called Paramanu is indivisible.

## \|\| 3.45 \|\|

*anantāṃśaiḥ saṃyutatvepi tāṃśca niraṃśino bhrāntidṛṣṭyā vadanti \|*  
*tasmātparā (ramā) ṇoḥ paramāṇutvamasti tadaṃśānāṃ vinatāgarbhajāta \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. The infinitesimal particles are divisible. Those who declare them indivisible are totally wrong in their view. Therefore, О son of Vinata, the particles are divisible in many parts.

## \|\| 3.46 \|\|

*parā (ramā) ṇūnāmekadeśe khagendra tanno saṃti prāṇināṃ rāśayaśca \|*  
*pratyekaśa saṃti rūpā hareśca hyataśca tatparamāṇoraṇīyaḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. О lord of birds, the infinitesimal particles are divisible in many parts. There are, in the same way, many forms of lord Hari. These forms are divisible while the lord himself is indivisible.

## \|\| 3.47 \|\|

*yo vā tvaṇīyānparamasya viṣṇoḥ sa eva rūpo mahato mahīyān \|*  
*teṣāmanyonyaṃ na viśeṣosti kaścidacintyarūpe ca vicintanīyaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. That which is the subtlest of the subtle forms is also the greatest of the great. No characteristic difference should be traced among the forms of the lord who is of inconceivable form.

## \|\| 3.48-49 \|\|

*kālakoṭivihīnatvaṃ kālānantyaṃ vidurbudhāḥ \|*  
*deśakoṭivihīnatvaṃ deśānantyaṃ vidurbudhāḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

*guṇānāmaprameyatve guṇānantyaṃ vidurbudhāḥ \|*  
*ānantyaṃ trividhaṃ nityaṃ harernānyasya kasyacit \|\| 49 \|\|*

48-49. None else but the lord is eternal in respect of time, space and quality. The wise define the eternity of time as time not circumscribed by time, the eternity of space as space not circumscribed by space, the eternity of attribute as an attribute not circumscribed by attributes

## \|\| 3.50 \|\|

*tasya sarvasvarūpeṣu cānantyaṃ tu trilakṣaṇam \|*  
*tathāpi deśatastasya paricchedopi yujyate \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Thus, the lord is characterized by his threefold eternity. Though omnifarious or all pervasive, he can still be circumscribed by space. His inconceivable and wondrous powers are manifest in the forms he takes for the welfare of the people.

## \|\| 3.51-53 \|\|

*paricchedastathā vyāpterekarūpepi yujyate \|*  
*tasyācintyādbhutaiśvaryaṃ vyavahārārthameva ca \|\| 51 \|\|*

*guṇataḥ kālataścaiva paricchedo na kutracit \|*  
*vyāptatvaṃ deśato hyasti sarvabhūteṣu yadyāpi \|\| 52 \|\|*

*na ca bhedaḥ kvacittasya hyaṇumātrepi yujyate \|*  
*tathāpi vidyateṇutvaṃ tasmādaiśvaryayogataḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

51-53. In respect of attributes and time, there is no difference in the forms of the lord. Being all- pervasive though circumscribed by space, there is no difference even in the minutest form of the lord. Still he is divisible even in subtle parts. Such is the virtue of his supreme power.

## \|\| 3.54 \|\|

*tasmādviddhyavatārārthaṃ vyāptatvaṃ cāpi bhaṇyate \|*  
*yattasya vyāpakaṃ rūpaṃ paraṃ nārāyaṇaṃ viduḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. Therefore, О Garuda, know that he incarnates in particular forms. His allpervasive form (which does not incarnate) they call Narayana.

## \|\| 3.55-58 \|\|

*ataśca paramāṇūnāṃ pārthivā'nantyavādinām \|*  
*bhedaḥ parasparaṃ jñeyastatheśasya mahātmanaḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

*jaḍeśayorjaḍānāṃ ca jīvānāṃ ca parasparam \|*  
*tathaiva jaḍajīvānāṃ nityaṃ bhedo jaḍeśayoḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

*pañca bhedā ime nityaṃ sarvāvasthāsu sarvaśaḥ \|*  
*etādṛśyāṃ tu māyāyāṃ vīryamādhatta vīryavān \|\| 57 \|\|*

*puruṣākhyo haristasmāttriguṇānasṛjatprabhuḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

55-58. Thus, in all conditions and in all entirety, there is a fivefold classification of the different forms of the lord, viz., the eternal and noneternal, the animate and inanimate and the lord circumscribed and not circumscribed by time, space and attributes. Lord Hari, the primeval Purusa of mighty power, deposited energy in the primordial nature called Maya and produced creation characterized by three gums, viz. sattva, rajas and tamas.

## Chapter 4

## \|\| 4.1 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*yathā sasarja bhagavāṃstrīn guṇānprakṛtestadā \|*  
*lakṣmīstrirūpā saṃbhūtā śrīrbhūrdurgoti saṃjñitā \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. When the lord created the three gunas, their composite form being Prakrti, there Fs rang up Laksmi in her threefold form, viz., Shri, Bhu and Durga.

## \|\| 4.2 \|\|

*sattvābhimāninī śrīstu bhūrdevī rajamāninī \|*  
*tamobhimāninī durgā hyevamāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Sri was characterized by sattva, Bhu by rajas and Durga by tamas. Thus say the wise.

## \|\| 4.3 \|\|

*antaraṃ na vijānīyādrūpāṇāṃ ca parasparam \|*  
*guṇānāṃ caiva saṃbandhāddurgādīnāṃ khageśvara \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. О lord of birds, one should not recognize any difference among Shri, Bhu, Durga — the three forms of Laksmi.

## \|\| 4.4 \|\|

*antaraṃ ye vijānanti te yāntyandhantamaḥ param \|*  
*puruṣastu trirūpobhūdviṣṇurbrahmā bhavetisaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Those who recognize difference among the three, by virtue of three distinct gunas are thrown in the pitch dark regions.

Purusa assumed three forms: Visnu, Brahma and Shiva, each containing the respective guna — sattva, rajas and tamas.

## \|\| 4.5 \|\|

*sattvena lokānvardhayituṃ viṣṇuḥ sākṣāddhariḥ svayam \|*  
*sṛṣṭiṃ kartuṃ ca rajasā brahmaṇi prāviśaddhariḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Visnu pervaded the universe to sustain people with sattva guna. He inspired Brahma to create beings with rajas guna.

## \|\| 4.6 \|\|

*ādyo brahmā sa vijñeyo na tu sākṣāddhariḥ svayam \|*  
*tamasāpi samānhantuṃ rudre ca prāviśaddhariḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Brahma created the universe with rajas guna. Hence, Brahma is the first creator and not Visnu.

Visnu inspired Rudra to annihilate the world with tamo guna.

## \|\| 4.7 \|\|

*rudre sthito rudrasaṃjño na rudrastu hariḥ svayam \|*  
*viṣṇureva hariḥ sākṣāttāvubhauna harī smṛtau \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Visnu, when he inspired Rudra, was called Rudra. But Rudra and Visnu are not identical. Visnu is not identifiable with Brahma either.

## \|\| 4.8 \|\|

*āviṣṭarūpau vijñeyau brahmarudrābhidhāyakau \|*  
*evaṃ jñātvā mokṣameti nānyathā tu kathañcana \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Brahma and Rudra are pervaded by Visnu. This very knowledge leads one to release. It is never otherwise.

## \|\| 4.9 \|\|

*viṣṇubrahmādirūpāṇāmaikyaṃ jānanti ye dvijāḥ \|*  
*te yānti narakaṃ ghoraṃ punarāvṛttivarjitam \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. They should recognize the respective functions of the three; Brahma, Visnu. and Rudra. Those who do not recognize the same go to terrific hells whence there is no return.

## \|\| 4.10 \|\|

*guṇatrayaṃ praviṣṭastu puruṣo hariravyayaḥ \|*  
*kāryonmukhaṃ yathā bhūyātkṣobhayāmāsa vai tathā \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. The eternal lord Visnu pervaded the' gums, and activated them for the purpose of creation.

## \|\| 4.11 \|\|

*jātakṣobhādbhagavato mahānāsīdguṇatrayāt \|*  
*guṇatraye vidyamānādbhāgādeva na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Mien the three gunas were activated there was evolved Mahat out of the constituents of the three gums.

## \|\| 4.12 \|\|

*mahato brahmavāyū ca jajñāte khābhimāninau \|*  
*tasya saṃvatsarātpaścādyamalau saṃbabhūvatuḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Out of Mahat were evolved Brahma and Vayu. They were born twins after the lapse of a year since Mahat came into being.

## \|\| 4.13 \|\|

*rajaḥ pradhānaṃ yattatvaṃ mahattattavamitīritam \|*  
*sargaṃ tvimaṃ vijānīyādguṇavaiṣamyanāmakam \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Mahat being activated by rajas, the creation was evolved out of the gunas in the state of imbalance.

## \|\| 4.14 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*mahattattavasvarūpasya jñānārthaṃ devakīsuta \|*  
*tvayoktā guṇavaiṣamyanāmikā sṛṣṭiruttamā \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Garuda said: — О son of DevakI, you have explained to me the nature of creation evolved out of the gunas. I have gained by your grace the correct knowledge of the principle of Mahat in imbalance.

## \|\| 4.15-20 \|\|

*guṇavaiṣamyaśabdārthaṃ mama brūhi mahāprabho \|*  
*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*guṇavaiṣamyaśabdārthajñāpanāya khageśvara \|\| 15 \|\|*

*apikṣitaṃ ca tatrādau guṇasāmyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*samyagjñāpayituṃ tatra khādau tāvatsvageśvara \|\| 16 \|\|*

*rāśibhūtaṃ guṇānāṃ tu darśayiṣye sthitiṃ ca vai \|*  
*rāśībhūtasya tasamaḥ sakāśādvinatāsuta \|\| 17 \|\|*

*rāśībhūtaṃ rajo jñeyandviguṇaṃ tattu nānyathā \|*  
*rāśībhūtasya rajasaḥ sakāśādvinatāsuta \|\| 18 \|\|*

*rāśībhūtaṃ tathā sattvaṃ dviguṇaṃ samudāhṛtam \|*  
*mūlaprakṛtijā hyete na mūlā prakṛtiḥ smṛtā \|\| 19 \|\|*

*yataḥ prakṛtirūpāṇāṃ paricchedo na vidyate \|*  
*ataḥ prakṛtijā jñeyā na mūlāste khageśvara \|\| 20 \|\|*

15-20. О lord, now explain to me the significance of the gunas in imbalance.

## \|\| 4.21 \|\|

*evaṃ tava guṇānāñca parimāṇaṃ khageśvara \|*  
*uktaṃ svarūpaṃ teṣāṃ tu tava samyak khageśvara \|\| 21 \|\|*

Lord Krsna said: — О lord of birds, before I explain to you the gunas in imbalance, I shall explain to you, first of all, what gunas in equilibrium would signify and before I do that О lord of birds, I shall explain to you the portion of the gunas in their material forms, ether, etc.

With the aggregate of tamas, О lord of birds, were joined the particles of rajoguna twice in proportion. With the aggregate of rajas, О son of Vinata, were joined the particles of sattva guna twice in proportion.

The gunas are evolved out of the primordial nature, they are not primary. Since forms of Prakrti are many and not categorizabic, the gunas are secondary, not primary.

21\. Tims I have explained to you, О lord of birds, the nature of the gunas, their number and the way how they function.

## \|\| 4.22 \|\|

*tatra rāśitraye sattvaṃ kevalaṃ samudāhṛtam \|*  
*rajastamobhyāṃ garuḍa hyavimiśraṃ hyatastu tat \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Of the three gunas, the sattva alone is pure. О Garuda, it is not mixed with rajas guna or tamas guna.

## \|\| 4.23 \|\|

*kevalaṃ sattvamityuktaṃ na tu śreṣṭhatvataḥ prabho \|*  
*sṛṣṭikāle kevalaṃ syātpralaye miśritaṃ bhavet \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. It is called kevala sattva, not that it is superior to other gunas but because it was the only existing guna at the beginning of Creation which got mixed with the rest at the time of dissolution.

## \|\| 4.24-25 \|\|

*sarvadāpyavimiśraṃ ca sattvarāśiṃ khageśvara \|*  
*sarvadāpi vimiśraṃ ca sattvarāśiṃ dvijottama \|\| 24 \|\|*

*ye vijānanti te sarve viśanti hyadharaṃ tamaḥ \|*  
*rajastamoguṇau vīndra itarābhyāṃ vimiśritau \|\| 25 \|\|*

24-25. О lord of birds, the sattva remains pure at all times (except at dissolution). Those who think contrarily enter the lower regions of pitch darkness. О lord of birds, only the rajas and lamas are mixed gunas.

## \|\| 4.26 \|\|

*sṛṣṭau pralayakālepi miśrāveva khageśvara \|*  
*rāśibhūtepi rajasi rajobhāgācchatādhikam \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. О lord of birds, they are mixed at the beginning and at the end of creation. With the aggregate of rajas, more than a hundred part of Sattva is mixed, О best of birds, it is not otherwise

## \|\| 4.27-28 \|\|

*sattvaṃ ca miśritaṃ jñeyaṃ nānyathā pakṣisattama \|*  
*rajasaḥ śatabhāgānāṃ madhye tu vinatāsuta \|\| 27 \|\|*

*ya eko bhāga uddiṣṭastāvatparimitaṃ tamaḥ \|*  
*rāśibhūtepi rajasi miśritaṃ parikīrtitam \|\| 28 \|\|*

27-28. О son of Vinata, of the aggregated hundred parts of rajas one part is lamas. It is mixed with the aggregated part of rajas.

## \|\| 4.29 \|\|

*rajorāśisthitistvevaṃ tāta vyāptaṃ tamoguṇaiḥ \|*  
*rāśibhūtepi tamasi sattvaṃ ca vinatāsuta \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. О dear, such is the position of the aggregated rajas mixed with lamas. In the aggregated lamas, there is mixed sattva, О son of Vinata.

## \|\| 4.30 \|\|

*tamaḥ sakāśādgaruḍa daśabhāgādhikena ca \|*  
*miśritaṃ bhavatītyevaṃ jñātavyaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. О Garuda, sattva is mixed with lamas approximately more than the tenth part of tames.

## \|\| 4.31 \|\|

*tamaso daśabhāgānāṃ madhye tu vinatāsuta \|*  
*ya eko bhāga uddiṣṭastāvatparimitaṃ rajaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. О son of Vinata, with the ten aggregated parts of tamas is mixed rajas in one tenth part.

## \|\| 4.32 \|\|

*rāśibhūtepi tamasi miśritaṃ bhavati dhruvam \|*  
*tamorāśisthitistvevaṃ jñātavyā pakṣisattama \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. О best of birds, this is the position of the aggregated tamas.

## \|\| 4.33-37 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*raśibhūtepi rajasi rāśibhūte tamasyapi \|*  
*sattvāṃśā hyadhikāḥ saṃtītyevamuktaṃ mayānagha \|\| 33 \|\|*

*tatra me saṃśayo hyasti śṛṇu tvaṃ sāttvatāṃ pate \|*  
*yadrāśyāṃ yadrā śibhāgā hyadhikāḥ saṃti yāvatā \|\| 34 \|\|*

*tāvatā vyavahāraḥ syātkṣīranīramiva prabho \|*  
*śrutvā sa garuḍenoktaṃ bhagavānpuruṣottamaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

*uvāca para maprītyā saṃstuvan garuḍaṃ hariḥ \|*  
*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*rajorāśyā tamorāśyā sattvarāśyadhikā sadā \|\| 36 \|\|*

*miśritaṃ cāpi pakṣīndra na sattavamiti kīrtyate \|*  
*rajorāśistamorāśirityevaṃ vibudhā viduḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

33-37. Garuda said: — О lord of Satvatas, I have strong doubts in regard to that.

In an aggregated guna, whatever parts of another aggregated guna are found in greater or smaller proportion, the guna acquires that name, like the milk in water, How is that? О lord!

On hearing the query of Garuda, the lord spoke with great affection, appreciating the query of his devotee.

## \|\| 4.38 \|\|

*viṣaṃ tu carudugdhasthaṃ viṣamityucyate yathā \|*  
*evaṃ mayoktā garuḍa guṇānāṃ nijasaṃsthitiḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

The Lord said: — О lord of birds, the aggregated sattva is greater than the aggregated rajas or aggregated tamas.

Sattva is not minted either with rajas or with tamas. The learned declare the aggregated rajas or tamas by those very names and not by any other.

38\. Poison mixed with milk or sacrificial oblation is nothing but poison, the same is the position of gunas, О bird.

## \|\| 4.39-43 \|\|

*sāmyāvasthāṃ guṇānāṃ ca śṛṇvidānīṃ khageśvara \|*  
*rāśīkṛtācca rajasaḥ janyaṃ yacca kageśvara \|\| 39 \|\|*

*mahattattve praviṣṭaṃ ca yadrajaḥ parikīrtitam \|*  
*pralaye samanuprāpte mahattattve sthitaṃ rajaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

*dvādaśāṃśena tu hyaddhā vibhaktaṃ bhavatiṃ prabho \|*  
*rāśībhūte hi sattve tu daśabhāgena miśritam \|\| 41 \|\|*

*samyak bhavati pakṣīndra tathaikāṃśena cāṇḍaja \|*  
*tamorāśyā miśritaṃ ca bhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

*anyenaikena bhāgena rajorāśyā khageśvara \|*  
*miśritaṃ bhavatītyevaṃ jñātavyaṃ nānyathā kvacit \|\| 43 \|\|*

39-43. О lord of birds, I shall now tell you of the state of equilibrium of the gunas.

Whatever is the product of aggregated rajas, whatever has entered in the mahat principle is also called rajas. When the hour of dissolution comes, rajas remains in mahat by twelve parts. In the aggregated sattva it remains by ten parts; in the aggregated tamas by one part, О bird.

A part of aggregated rajas remains in rajas by one part.

## \|\| 4.44 \|\|

*guṇatrayepi bhagavānmahattattvasya cāṇḍaja \|*  
*evaṃ layastu jñātavyo hṛdi tattvārthavodibhiḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Thus, О bird, the principle of mahat is dissolved in the three gunas.

## \|\| 4.45 \|\|

*evaṃ guṇatrayāṇāṃ ca miśritattvātkhageśvara \|*  
*guṇasāmyamiti prāhurevaṃ jānīhi vai khaga \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. О lord of birds, when the gums are mined in equal proportion, the learned declare it to be the equilibrium of gunas

## \|\| 4.46 \|\|

*anyathā ye vijānanti te yānti hyadharaṃ tamaḥ \|*  
*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*rāśīkṛtaguṇānāṃ ca trayāṇāṃ parameśvara \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. Those who think otherwise, are placed in the pitch dark regions.

## \|\| 4.47 \|\|

*viśālānāṃ paraṃ brahmanpralaye guṇasāmyatā \|*  
*kathaṃ brūhi mahābhāga etattattvaṃ samāsataḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. О lord, tell me, in brief, how the aggregated gunas stand in equilibrium, at the time of final dissolution (pralaya).

## \|\| 4.48 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*rāśībhūtaguṇānāṃ tu trayaṇāmapi sattama \|*  
*tadā vimiśritatvena hyavasthānaṃ vidurbudhāḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. The Lord said: — The learned declare that the aggregated gunas stand in equilibrium, being mixed together.

## \|\| 4.49-53 \|\|

*idānīṃ guṇavaiṣamyaṃ śṛṇu samyaṅ mama priya \|*  
*sṛṣṭikāle tu saṃprāpte yatpūrvaṃ pralaye khaga \|\| 49 \|\|*

*daśabhāgaiśca sattve tu miśritaṃ yadra jastathā \|*  
*tamasyapyekabhāgena praviṣṭaṃ yattu tadrajaḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

*rajasyapyekabhāgena praviṣṭaṃ yacca tadrajaḥ \|*  
*evaṃ dvādaśabhāgaiśca praviṣṭaṃ sarvaśo rajaḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*
