*sattvastairdaśabhāgaiśca tathaikena rajoṃśinā \|*  
*evamekādaśairbhāgaistamasthāṃśena vai vdija \|\| 52 \|\|*

*miśritaṃ bhavati hyaddhā mahattattvaṃ tadā smṛtam \|*  
*etadanyo viśeṣaśca mantadhyo vinatāsuta \|\| 53 \|\|*

49-53. О dear, now, I shall tell you about the imbalance of gunas.

The rajas which remained mixed by ten parts with sattva, by one part with tamas, by one part with rajas at the time of dissolution, remains mixed by ten parts with sattva, by one part with rajas and by one part with tamas. Thus the twelve parts stand conjoined and constitute mahat.

О son of Vinata, there is another speciality in this context.

## \|\| 4.54 \|\|

*ekāṃśastāmaso jñeyo mahattattve na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*evaṃ trayodaśairbhāgairmiśritaṃ tacca sattama \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. In the principle of mahat, one part is tamas. Thus the mahat principle is mixed with all gunas.

## \|\| 4.55-56 \|\|

*evametadvijānīyānnānyathā tu kathañcana \|*  
*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*caturmukhācchrutaṃ pūrvaṃ bhagavansāttvatāṃ pate \|\| 55 \|\|*

*caturbhāgātsamutpannaṃ mahattattvamiti prabho \|*  
*tatraikāṃśastamaḥ proktaḥ tribhāgo raja eva ca \|\| 56 \|\|*

55-56. Garuda said: — О lord of Satvatas, I have heard, formerly from Brahma that the mahat principle was evolved out of four components of guess, i.e. one part of tamas and three parts of rajas.

## \|\| 4.57 \|\|

*tadāhurbrahmaṇo rūpaṃ guṇavaiṣamyanāmakam \|*  
*caturbhāgātmakaṃ proktaṃ mahattattvaṃ śrutaṃ mayā \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Thus, the principle of mahat consists of four parts, being the form of Brahma himself which the learned declare to be due to the imbalance of gunas.

## \|\| 4.58 \|\|

*trayodaśāṃśaiḥ saṃbhūtamiti proktaṃ tvayānagha \|*  
*tadetatsaṃśayaṃ chindhi kṛpālo bhaktavatsala \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. It is evolved out of thirteen parts, О fond of devotees and merciful one, please clear my doubts regarding this point.

## \|\| 4.59-62 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*brahmoktamya mayoktasya vivādo nāsti sarvathā \|*  
*mūlasattve miśritaṃ ca daśabhāgena yadrajaḥ \|\| 59 \|\|*

*tatsarvaṃ ca militvaiva tveko bhāgastu kīrtitaḥ \|*  
*mūle rajasi yaccokto rajobhāgaḥ khageśvara \|\| 60 \|\|*

*bhāge dvitīye vijñeyastadrajo nātra saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*mūle tamasi yaccokto rajobhāgastathaiva ca \|\| 61 \|\|*

*tṛtīyabhāgo vijñeyo nātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|*  
*tathā mūle ca tamasi hyeko bhāgastamaḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 62 \|\|*

59-62. Sri Krsna said: — What Brahma had stated to you and what I have said just now is not open to doubt. Rajas is mixed with primary sattva by ten parts, the total is but one constituent.

The second constituent consists of rajas extent in the primary rajas. The third constituent consists of rajas present in the primary tamas. The fourth constituent consists of one part of rajas present in the primary lamas being designated as tamas.

## \|\| 4.63 \|\|

*evaṃ tribhāgo rajasaḥ ekāṃśastamasaḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*tadāhurbrahmaṇo dehaṃ guṇavaiṣamyanāmakam \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. Thus, the three parts of rajas and one part of tamas constitute the body of Brahma due to the imbalance of guess.

## \|\| 4.64 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*mahattattvasya catvāro bhāgāsteṣu rajastrayaḥ \|*  
*tamasastveka eveti tvayoktaṃ garuḍadhvaja \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. Garuda said: — Mahat consists of four parts, of which the three are rajas, one is tamas-this is what you have declared to me.

## \|\| 4.65 \|\|

*rajobhāgātmako dehoḥ brahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ \|*  
*iti pratīyate brahmanvacanāttava mādhava \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. О lord, it appears from your statement that thebody of Brahma is constituted of rajas.

## \|\| 4.66 \|\|

*śuddhasattvātmako deho brahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ \|*  
*evaṃ hi śrūyate kṛṣṇa saṃśayo metra bādhate \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. There is a doubt, О lord, on this point. The body of Brahma is constituted of pure sattva, they say. How could it be formed of rajas.

## \|\| 4.67-68 \|\|

*tamevaṃ saṃśayaṃ chindhi yaddhi tacchrotumarhati \|*  
*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*tribhāgabhūte rajasi tathā dvādaśadhāpi ca \|\| 67 \|\|*

*rajasopekṣayā sattvaṃ daśāṃśādhikameva ca \|*  
*praviṣṭamastīti khaga jñātavyaṃ tacchaṇu dvija \|\| 68 \|\|*

67-68. In the threefold rajas of twelvefold aggregate there exists sattva tenfold more in proportion to rajas.

## \|\| 4.69 \|\|

*tamasopekṣayā sattvaṃ daśāṃśādhikameva vai \|*  
*praviṣṭamastīti khaga vaktavyaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. In the threefold rajas of twelvefold aggregate, there exists sattva tenfold more in proportion to tamas. In the threefold rajas of twelvefold aggregate there exists tamas elevenfold more in proportion to tamas.

## \|\| 4.70 \|\|

*tamasopekṣayā tatra tama ekādaśaṃ smṛtam \|*  
*ekāṃśastu rajo jñeyamevamāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ \|*  
*evaṃ ca militānbhāgānvakṣye śṛṇu mahāmate \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. In the threefold rajas of twelvefold aggregate there exists rajas one-fold more in proportion to tamas.

Now, I shall tell you about the mixed parts, hear, О Wise one.

## \|\| 4.71-72 \|\|

*mahattattvasamutpattā upādānaṃ khageśvara \|*  
*trayodaśāṃśā vijñeyā dvādaśāśaṃ rajaḥ smṛtam \|\| 71 \|\|*

*ekāṃśastamaso jñeyastatra bhāgāñchṛṇu dvijā \|*  
*ādau tu dvādaśāṃśeṣu bhāgānvakṣyāmi tacchṛṇu \|\| 72 \|\|*

71-72. The material that constitutes mahat, О lord of birds, consists of thirteen parts of gunas of which rajas contributes twelve portions ay tamas one. I shall tell you about their sub-division. First of all, I shall tell you about the parts of gunas.

## \|\| 4.73 \|\|

*ekāṃśastamaso jñeyastaddaśāṃ śādhikaṃ rajaḥ \|*  
*tacchatāṃśādhikaṃ sattvamevamāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. Parts of rajas: One part of tamas, ten parts of rajas, one part of sattva — twelve parts in all.

## \|\| 4.74 \|\|

*ekāṃśatamasi hyevaṃ vibhāgāñchṛṇu sattama \|*  
*ekāṃśastu rajo jñeyastamo hyekā daśādhikam \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. Parts of tamas: One part of rajas, eleven parts of tamas.

## \|\| 4.75 \|\|

*tamobhāgāstu vijñeyāstaddaśāṃśādhikaḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*sattvabhāga iti jñeyo mahattattve khageśvara \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. Parts of sattva: Ten parts of sattva. Such is the position of sattva in the mahat principle.

## \|\| 4.76 \|\|

*sattvāṃśo bahulo yasmācchuddhasattvaṃ caturmukhaḥ \|*  
*utpattirmahataścoktā evaṃ ca vinatāsuta \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. Since Brahma is constituted of more sattva parts than any other deity he is called Buddha sattva i.e. consisting of pure sattvika quality.

## \|\| 4.77 \|\|

*tajjñānānmokṣamāpnoti nānyathā tu kathañcana \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. I have thus told you about the constituents of mahat, О son of Vinata, the knowledge whereof leads one to release from the cycle of birth.

## Chapter 5

## \|\| 5.1 \|\|

*etādṛśe mahattattve lakṣmyā saha hariḥ svayam \|*  
*praviveśa mahābhāga kṣobhayāmāsa vai hariḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. In the mahat principle, as described above, lord Visnu entered together with Laksmi and activa ted the same.

## \|\| 5.2-3 \|\|

*ahantattvamabhūttasmājjñānadravyakriyātmakam \|*  
*ahaṅkārasamutpattāvekāṃśastamasi smṛtaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

*taddaśāṃśādhikarajastaddaśāṃśādhikaṃ prabho \|*  
*sattvamityucyate sadbhirhyetadātmā tvahaṃ smṛtam \|\| 3 \|\|*

2-3. From mahat ego was born, being continued of knowledge, substance and activity. In the constitution of ego tamas contributed one part, rajas ten and sattva contributed more. Lord become his atman.

## \|\| 5.4 \|\|

*ahantattvābhimānī tu ādau śeṣo babhūvaha \|*  
*sahasrābdācca paścāttau jātau khagaharau dvija \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Characterized by the principle of ego, Sesa was evolved at thebeginning of creation. After the lapse of a thousand years, were born Shiva and Garuda.

## \|\| 5.5 \|\|

*ahantattve khaga hyeṣu praviṣṭo hariravyayaḥ \|*  
*kṣobhayāmāsa bhagavāllaṅkṣmyā saha hariḥ svayam \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The eternal Visnu entered the ego. Lord Visnu and LaksmI activated the ego.

## \|\| 5.6 \|\|

*vaikārikastāmasaśca taijasaścetyahaṃ tridhā \|*  
*tridhā babhūva rudropi yatasteṣāṃ niyāmakaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. I am threefold: of modified (i.e. secondary) form, born of tamas and of tejas. Lord Rudra is the controller of the three.

## \|\| 5.7-10 \|\|

*vaikārikasthito rudro vaikārika iti smṛtaḥ \|*  
*tāmase tu sthito rudrastāmaso hyabhidhīyate \|\| 7 \|\|*

*taijase tu sthito rudro loke vai taijasaḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*taijase tu hyahantattve lakṣmyā saha hariḥ svayam \|\| 8 \|\|*

*viśitvā kṣobhayāmāsa tadāsau daśadhā tvabhūt \|*  
*śrotraṃ cakṣuḥ sparśanaṃ ca rasanaṃ ghrāṇameva ca \|\| 9 \|\|*

*vākpāṇipādaṃ pāyuśca upastheti daśa smṛtāḥ \|*  
*vaikārike hyahantattve praviśya kṣobhayaddhariḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

7-10. Lord Rudra controls all secondary objects. Therefore, he is called secondary. Controlling taijasa forms he is called taijasa.

Lord Visnu and LaksmI entered the ego which the lord set to motion. He, the lord, evolved five organs of intellect: cars, eyes, touch, taste, smell, and five organs of action, viz., tongue, hands, feet, anus and phallus.

## \|\| 5.11 \|\|

*mahattattvādimā adāvindriyāṇāṃ ca devatāḥ \|*  
*ekādaśavidhā āsankrameṇa tu khageśvara \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. О lord of birds, from the mahat were evolved the presiding deities of eleven organs of sense.

## \|\| 5.12 \|\|

*manobhimāni nī hyādau vāruṇī tvabhavattadā \|*  
*anantaraṃ ca sauparṇī gaurojāpi tathaiva ca \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. In the beginning was born VarunI, the presiding deity of the mind and then Gaurija.

## \|\| 5.13 \|\|

*śeṣādanantarāstāsāṃ daśavarṣādanaṃram \|*  
*utpattiriti vijñeyaṃ krameṇa tu khageśvara \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. These were evolved from Shesa one after the other, within ten years serially.

## \|\| 5.14 \|\|

*manobhimānināvanyāvindrakāmau prajajñatuḥ \|*  
*tārkṣya hyanantarau jñeyau muktau saṃsāra eva ca \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Then were evolved Indra and Kamathe presiding deities of the mind. О Tarksya, bondage and release came afterwards.

## \|\| 5.15-17 \|\|

*tatastvagātmā hyabhavatsohaṃ kārika īritaḥ \|*  
*tataḥ pāṇyātmakāścaiva jajñire pakṣisattama \|\| 15 \|\|*

*śacī ratiścāniruddhastathā svāyaṃbhuvo manuḥ \|*  
*bṛhaspatistathā dakṣa ete pāṇyātmakāḥ smṛtāḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

*dakṣasyānantaraṃ jajñe pravāho nāma cāṇḍaja \|*  
*sa evoktaścātiṃvāho yāpayatyātmacoditaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

15-17. Then I was evolved in the bodily form constituted of skin and called a creator. Then, О best of birds, creatures possessed of hands came into being. Shau, Rati Aniruddha, Manu the son of self-born Brahma, Brhaspatl and Daksa were born in human form.

О bird, after Daksa was born Pravaha, also called Ativaha who by self-urge causes objects to move.

## \|\| 5.18 \|\|

*hastādanantaraṃ jñeyo na tu śacyādivatsmṛtaḥ \|*  
*tatobhavanmahābhāga cakṣuridriyamātmanaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Thereafter, the organ of perception came into being.

## \|\| 5.19 \|\|

*svāyaṃbhuvamanorbhāryā śatarūpā yamastathā \|*  
*candrasūryau tu cattvāraścakṣurindriyamāninaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Satarupa, wife of Svayambhuva Manu, Yama, Moon and Sun-the four presiding deities of their respective organs came into being.

## \|\| 5.20 \|\|

*candraḥ śrotrābhimānīti tathā jñeyaḥ khageśvara \|*  
*jihvendriyātmā varuṇaḥ sūryasyānantarobhavat \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. О lord of birds, the moon is the presiding deity of ears. Varuna, the presiding deity of the tongue came after them Sun.

## \|\| 5.21-23 \|\|

*vāgindriyābhimāninyo hyabhavanvaruṇādanu \|*  
*dakṣapatnī prasūtiśca bhṛguragnistarthava ca \|\| 21 \|\|*

*tatra vaite mahātmāno vāgindriyaniyāmakāḥ \|*  
*ye kravyādādayaścoktāstenantattvaniyāmakāḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

*sāmyatvācca tathaivoktirna tu tattvābhimānitaḥ \|*  
*upasthamānino vīndra babhūvustadanantaram \|\| 23 \|\|*

21-23. After Varuna were born the wives of Daksa, their offsprings: Bhrgu and Agni the presiding deities of the tongue. These high-souied ones control the organ of speech Kravyada and others, of which mention has been made before, do not control any principle. They are grouped in a separate category, for they are not the presiding deities of any principle of life.

After that, О lord of birds, were born the presiding deities of the generating organ. They are

## \|\| 5.24 \|\|

*viśvāmitro vasiṣṭotrirmarīciḥ pulahaḥ kratuḥ \|*  
*pulastyoṅgirasaścaiva tathā vaivasvato manuḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Visvamitra, Vasistha, Atri, Marstci, Pulaha, Kratu, Pulastya, Angiras and Vaivasvata Manu. Mann and others of endless number are the presiding deities of the generating organ.

## \|\| 5.25 \|\|

*manvādayonantasaṃkhyā upasthātmāna īritāḥ \|*  
*pāyośca mānino vīndra jajñire tadanantaram \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. О lord of birds, thereafter were born the presiding deities of anus. They are:

## \|\| 5.26-28 \|\|

*sūryeṣu dvādaśasveko mitrastārā guroḥ priyā \|*  
*koṇādhipo nirṛtiśca pravahapriyā \|\| 26 \|\|*

*cattvāra ete pakṣīndra vāyutattvābhimāninaḥ \|*  
*ghrāṇābhimāninaḥ sarve jajñire dvijasattama \|\| 27 \|\|*

*viṣvavaseno vāyuputrau hyaśvinau gaṇapastathā \|*  
*vittapaḥ sapta vasava ukto hyāgnistathāṣṭamaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

26-28. Mitra — one of the twelve Suns, Tara the wife of Brhaspati the presiding deity of the quarters, Nirrti, Pravaha-the consort of Pravaha. These four, О lord of birds, are the presiding deities of the principle of Vayu.

О lord of birds, the following are the presiding deities of smell-Visvaksena, A svins, sons of Vayu, lord of ganas and wealth, seven Vasus and Agni the eighth.

## \|\| 5.29 \|\|

*satyānāṃ śṛṇu nāmāni droṇaḥ prāṇo dhruvastathā \|*  
*arke doṣastathā vaskaḥ saptamastu vibhāvasuḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Now, I shall tell you the names of seven Vasus: Drona, Prana, Dhruva, Arka, Dosa, Vasu, Vibhavasu being the seventh,

## \|\| 5.30 \|\|

*daśarudrāstathā jñeyā mūlarudro bhavaḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*daśa rudrasya nāmāni śṛṇuṣva dvijasattama \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Rudras are ten in number. The primary Rudra is caved Bhava. О best of birds, now I shay tell you the ten names of Rudra.

## \|\| 5.31 \|\|

*raivanteyastathā bhīmo vāmadevo vṛṣākapiḥ \|*  
*ajaikapādahirvudhnyo bahurūpo mahāniti \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Raivanteya, Bhlma, Vamadeva, Vrsakapi, Ahirbudhnya, Bahurupa, Mahan.

## \|\| 5.32-33 \|\|

*daśa rudrā iti proktāḥ ṣaḍādityāñchṛṇu dvija \|*  
*urukramastathā śakro vivasvānvaruṇastathā \|\| 32 \|\|*

*parjanyotibāhureta uktāḥ pūrvaṃ dvijottama \|*  
*parjanyavyatiriktāstu pañcaivoktā na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

32-33. These are ten Rudras, О bird. Now listen to six Adityas, viz., Urukrama, Sakra, Vivasvan, Varuna, Parjanya, Atibahu. Except i Parjanya, they are mentioned before.

## \|\| 5.34-35 \|\|

*gaṅgāsamastu parjanya iti coktaḥ khageśvara \|*  
*savitā hyaryamā dhātā pūṣā tvaṣṭā tathā bhagaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

*catvāriṃśattathā sapta mahataḥ parikīrtitāḥ \|*  
*dvāvuktāviti vijñeyo pravahotivahastathā \|\| 35 \|\|*

34-35. О lord of birds, Parjanya is at par with Ganga, Savitrl, Aryaman, Dhatrl, Pusan, Tvastr and Bhaga. Mahat is forty-seven. Pravaha and Ativaha have been mentioned above.

## \|\| 5.36-39 \|\|

*tathā daśavidhā jñeyā viśvedevāḥ khageśvara \|*  
*śṛṇu nāmāni teṣāṃ tu purūravārdravasaṃjñakau \|\| 36 \|\|*

*dhūrilocanasaṃjñau dvau kratudakṣetisaṃjñakau \|*  
*dvau satyavasusaṃjñau ca kāmakālakasaṃjñakau \|\| 37 \|\|*

*evaṃ daśavidhā jñeyā viśvedevāḥ prakīrtitāḥ \|*  
*tathā ṛbhugaṇaścoktastathā ca pitarastrayaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

*dyāvā pṛthivyau vijñeyau ete ca ṣaḍaśītayaḥ \|*  
*devāḥ prajajñire sarve nāsikadriyamāninaḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

36-39. О lord of birds, Visvedevas are ten, viz., Pururavas, Adrava, Dhuri, Locana, Kratu, Daksa, Satya, Vasa, Kama and Kalaka.

Thus are the Rbhus, three groups of Pitrs, Dyuloka and Bhuloka.

Thus were born the eighty six deities, the presiding ones of the organ of smell.

## \|\| 5.40 \|\|

*ākāśasyābhimānī tu gaṇapaḥ sudāhṛtaḥ \|*  
*ubhayatrābhi mānīti jñeyaṃ tattvārthavedibhiḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. The lord of ganas is the presiding deity of ether and of the organ of smell.

## \|\| 5.41 \|\|

*viṣvaksenaṃ vinā sarve jayādyā viṣṇupārṣadāḥ \|*  
*abhavansamahīnāśca viṣvaksenādanantaram \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Excluding Visvaksena, Jaya and the rest are the councillors. In their mutual relationship some are equal and some unequal.

## \|\| 5.42 \|\|

*etepi nāsikāyāśca avāntaraniyāmakāḥ \|*  
*ataste tattvamānibhyo hyavarāste prakīrtitāḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. These are the secondary controllers of the organs of sense. Hence, they are inferior to the presiding deities of the tattvas.

## \|\| 5.43-44 \|\|

*sparśatattvābhimānī tu apānaścetyudāhṛtaḥ \|*  
*rūpābhimānī saṃjajñe vyāno nāma mahānprabho \|\| 43 \|\|*

*rasātmaka udānaśca samāno gandhanāmakaḥ \|*  
*apāṃ nāthāśca catvāro marutaḥ parikīrtitāḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

43-44Арапа is the presiding deity of the principle of touch, Vyana that of form, Udana that of taste and Samana that of smell. The four Maruts are mentioned as the lords of waters.

## \|\| 5.45 \|\|

*jayādyanantarānvakṣye samutpannānkhageśvara \|*  
*pradhānāgre prathamajaḥ pāvakaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. О lord of birds, I shall now tell you about Jaya etc., who came after Visvaksena. In the materialistic creation Agni stands first. Cyavana son of sage Bhrgu and Utathya son of Brhaspati come after Agni.

## \|\| 5.46-48 \|\|

*bhṛgormaharṣeḥ putraśca cyavanaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ \|*  
*bṛhaspateśca putrastu utathyaḥ parikīrtitaḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

*raivataścākṣuṣaścaiva tathā svārociṣaḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*uttamo brahmasāvarṇī rudrasāvarṇireva ca \|\| 47 \|\|*

*devasāvarṇisāvarṇirindrasāvarṇirevaca \|*  
*tathaiva dakṣasāvarṇirdharmabhāvarṇireva ca \|\| 48 \|\|*

46-48. Manus, eleven in number, were also created first viz, Raivata, Caksusa, Svarocisa, Uttama, Brahma-Savami, Rudra-Savarni, Deva-Savami, Savarni, Indra-Savarni, Daksa-Savarni and Dharma-Savami.

## \|\| 5.49 \|\|

*ekādaśavidhā hyevaṃ manavaḥ parikīrtitāḥ \|*  
*pitṝṇāṃ saptakaṃ caivetyādyāḥ saṃjajñire khaga \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Seven groups of manes were created thereafter, О lord of birds.

## \|\| 5.50-54 \|\|

*tadanantaramutpannāstebhyo nīcāḥ śṛṇu dvija \|*  
*varuṇasya patnī gaṅgā parjanyākhyo vibhāvasuḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

*yamabhāryā śyāmalā tu hyaniruddhapriyā virāṭ \|*  
*brahmāṇḍamāninī saiva hyuṣānāmnā suśabditā \|\| 51 \|\|*

*rohiṇī candrabhāryoktā sūryabhāryā tu saṃjñakā \|*  
*etā gaṅgādiṣaṭūsaṃkhyā jajñire vinatāsuta \|\| 52 \|\|*

*gaṅgādyanantaraṃ jajñe svāhā vai mantradevatā \|*  
*svāhānāmāgnibhāryoktā gaṅgādibhyodhamā śrutā \|\| 53 \|\|*

*svāhānantarajo jñeyo jñānātmā budhanāmakaḥ \|*  
*budhastu candraputro yaḥ svāhāyā adhamaḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

50-54. Born thereafter and inferior to these are the following six: Ganas the wife of Varuna, Vibhavasu the wife of Parjanya, Syamala the wife of Yama, Usa the wife of Aniruddha, RohinI the wife of the moon and Samhakira the wife of the sun.

After Ganga, Svaha, the presiding deity of the mantras was born. Svaha is the wife of Agni, being inferior to Gangs and others.

After Svaha was born Budha, the presiding deity of knowledge. Budha, the son of the moon, is inferior to Svaha.

## \|\| 5.55-56 \|\|

*uṣā nāma tathā jajñe budhasyānantaraṃ khaga \|*  
*uṣānāmā bhimānī tu hyaśvibhāryā prakīrtitā \|\| 55 \|\|*

*budhādhamā sā vijñeyā nātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|*  
*tataḥ śanaiścaro jajñe pṛthivyātmeti viśrutaḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

55-56. О bird, after Budha, Usa was born. Usa who attained the greatest fame by this name was the wife of Asvins.

She is inferior to Budha. Thereafter was born Sanaiscara the presiding deity of the principle of earth.

## \|\| 5.57 \|\|

*uṣādhamastu vijñeyastato jajñetha puṣkaraḥ \|*  
*karmābhimānī vijñeyaḥ śanaiścara itīritaḥ \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Inferior to Use was Puskara, the presiding deity of all activities known also as dTcdf^qilHHl ^T%cf

## \|\| 5.58 \|\|

*tattvābhimānino devānevaṃ sṛṣṭvā hariḥ svayam \|*  
*praviveśa sa deveśastattveṣu ramayā sahā \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. Thus having created the presiding deities, Visnu, the lord of gods, entered the principles together with Laksmi.

## Chapter 6

## \|\| 6.1-3 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*tatratatra sthitāstattve tattattattvābhimāninaḥ \|*  
*svesve hyāyatane svāṅge tadarthaṃ ca khageśvara \|\| 1 \|\|*

*hariṃ nārāyaṇaṃ samyak stotuṃ samupacakrire \|*  
*cintyācintyaguṇe viṣṇau viruddhāḥ saṃti sadguṇāḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

*ekaikaśohyanantāste tadguṇānāṃ stutau mama \|*  
*kva śaktiriti buddhyā sā vrīḍayāvanatābravīt \|\| 3 \|\|*

1-3. The Lord said: — О lord of birds, the presiding deities of the principles, stationed in their respective orbits, began to praise lord Visnu in the prescribed manner. In you of recognizable or unrecognizable qualities there exist contrary qualities. Severally, they are many. I have no power to enumerate them.

Thus feeling shy and bashful, LaksmI spoke to Visnu in the modest and humble tone.

## \|\| 6.4 \|\|

*śrīruvāca \|*  
*natāsmi te nātha padāravindaṃ na veda cānyaccaraṇādṛte tava \|*  
*tvayīśvare saṃti guṇāḥ śrutāstu tathāśrutāḥ saṃti ca devadeva \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Sri said:-0 lord, I bow to your lotusfeet. I have no resort except the shelter of your feet. Your lordship possesses Virtue. Some I have known, some I have not.

## \|\| 6.5 \|\|

*samyak sṛṣṭaṃ svāyatanaṃ ca datvā govinda dāmodara māṃ ca pāhi \|*  
*stutyā madīyaśca sukhakapūrṇaḥ priyo jano nāsti tathā tvadanyaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Please give me shelter. Protect me from dangers. There is none more delightful, none more endearing than your lordship.

## \|\| 6.6 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*lakṣmīpate sarvajagannivāsa tvaṃ jñānasiṃdhuḥ kva ca viśvamūrte \|*  
*ahaṃ kva cājñastava vai śaktirasti hyajñohaṃ vai hyalpaśaktirmamāsti \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Brahma said: — "'О lord of LaksmI, an abode of living beings, you are the ocean of knowledge and omniformed. Where am I with my limited knowledge and full of ignorance and where your lordship allpowerful and omniscient. I am ignorant with my circumscribed knowledge. So is Her Ladyship LaksmI.

## \|\| 6.7 \|\|

*lakṣmyāścaiva jñānavairāgyabhakti hyatyalpamaddhā mayi sarvadaiva \|*  
*tava prasādādasti jagannivāsa tatra svāmitvaṃ nāsti viṣṇo sadaiva \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. I have very little knowledge, very little devotion and very little detachment. And whatever little I possess is also due to your favour. There too, О Visnu, who are an abode of the universe, I have no ownership.

## \|\| 6.8 \|\|

*na dehi tvaṃ sarvadā me murāre ahaṃmamatvaṃ prāpyametāvadeva \|*  
*gamyajñānaṃ yogyaguṇe rameśa pramādo vā nāstināstyadya nitya \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. О lord, eschew my ego and attachment to worldly affairs. I desire nothing more. 1 deserve the attainment of right knowledge. I am not a negligent or an imprudent pupil.

## \|\| 6.9 \|\|

*tanme hṛṣīkāṇi patantyasatpathe padāravinde tu patantu sarvadā \|*  
*lakṣmyā hyahaṃ koṭiguṇena hīnaḥ stotuṃ sāmarthyaṃ nāsti me suprasīda \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Let my organs of senses that are inclined to move in wrong channels fall at your lotusfeet. I am inferior to LaksmI by millions of virtues. I have no capacity to sufficiently praise your merits. Be pleased, О lord."

## \|\| 6.10 \|\|

*iti stavaṃ viṣṇuguṇānvidhātā tārkṣyasthitaḥ prāñjalistasya cāgre \|*  
*tadā vāyurdevadevo mahātmā dṛṣṭvā viṣṇu bhaktisaṃvardhitātmā \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Thus with palms joined in reverence Brahma praised the lord in the presence of Garuda. Then, the god of gods, the noble Vayu was visibly moved to love and devotion at the sight of the lord.

## \|\| 6.11 \|\|

*snahottharāvaḥ skhalitākṣarastaṃ muñcankaṇānprāñjalirābabhāṣe \|*  
*vāyuruvāca \|*  
*ete hi devāstava bhṛtyabhūtāḥ padāravindaṃ paramaṃ sudurlabham \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. With eyes overflowing with tears and speech-characterized in faltering accents he began to praise the lord.

## \|\| 6.12 \|\|

*caturvidhānpuruṣārthānrameśa saṃprārthaye tacca sadāpi deva \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā hareḥ saiva māyaiva tāvatsukāraṇaṃ kiñcidanyanna cāsti \|\| 12 \|\|*

Vayu said: — 'These gods are your servants. Your lotus-like feet are inaccessible to us.

12\. О lord of Laksmi, I beg the favour of yours granting me the tour objects of human life, О lord.

The world is the creation of lord's Maya. 1 see no other cause for evolving the world. Let me not go astray, О lord. I am anxious to serve your lotus-feet.

## \|\| 6.13 \|\|

*ato nāhaṃ pradayopi bhūman bhavatpadāṃbhojaniṣavaṇotsukaḥ \|*  
*lokasya kṛṣṇādvimukhasya karmaṇā apuṇyaśīlasya suduḥ khitasya \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. О lord, you incarnate for rendering favour to the people who are averse to devotion, who are given to sinful acts and who are terribly suffering from (threefold) affliction. О lord, you have no other object of life.

## \|\| 6.14 \|\|

*anugrahārthaṃ ca tavāvatāro nānyaśca kiñcitpuruṣārthastaveśa \|*  
*gobhūsurāṇāṃ ca mahīruhāṇāṃ tathā surāṇāṃ pravarāvatāraiḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. О Vasudeva, out of mere sport, you incarnate in human form. You render welfare to the cows, Brahmins, gods and the world of vegetation, that being the sole purpose of your incarnation on this earth.

## \|\| 6.15 \|\|

*kṣemopakārāṇi ca vāsudeva krīḍanvidhatte na ca kiñcidanyat \|*  
*mano na tṛpyatyapi śaṃsatāṃ naḥ sukarmamauleścaritāmṛtāni \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. О lord, you are the foremost of pious activators. О lord of the mind-bom god, the consort of Hari, our mind, engaged in praise of your merit, fords no satiation whatsoever.

## \|\| 6.16 \|\|

*acchinnabhaktasya hi me mukunda sadā bhaktiṃ dehi pādāravinde \|*  
*sadā tadevāstu na kiñcidanyadyatra tvamāsīḥ puruṣe devadeva \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. О Mukunda, I am your steadfast devotee. Pray create devotion in me for your lotus-feet. This and nothing else do I crave of you.

## \|\| 6.17 \|\|

*ahaṃ ca tatrāsmi tava prasādādyatrāsmyahaṃ tatra bhavānmahāprabho \|*  
*vyaṃsirmameyaṃ ca śarīramadhye caturmukhaścaiva na caitatadanyaiḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. О god of gods, in whatever station you are present, let me also be there by your favour. Conversely, in whatever station I am present, let your presence be also there, О lord. This is my utmost desire. The four-faced Brahma alone is aware of it.

## \|\| 6.18 \|\|

*madīyanidrā tava vandanaṃ prabho madīyayāmācaraṇaṃ pradakṣiṇam \|*  
*madīyavyākhyāharaṇaṃ stutiḥ syādevaṃ viditvā ca samarpayāmi \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. О lord, let my subconscious mind be also at your worship; let my activities be your circumambulation, let my behavioural conduct also be your praise. О lord, I offer all these to you with my conscious mind.

## \|\| 6.19 \|\|

*madbrṛddhiyogyaṃ ca padārthajātaṃ dṛṣṭvā hareḥ pratimā eva tacca \|*  
*itthaṃ matvāhaṃ sarvadā devadeva tatrasthitānharirūpān bhajiṣye \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. The material substances intended for my use are nothing but different forms of Hari. О God of gods, I always regard these to be such, and worship the same with proper devotion.

## \|\| 6.20 \|\|

*yaccandanaṃ yattu puṣpaṃ ca dhūpaṃ vastraṃ ca yadbhakṣyabhojyādikaṃ ca \|*  
*etatsarvaṃ viṣṇuprītyarthamevetyetadvrataṃ sarvadā vai kariṣye \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. The Sandal paste, fragrant flowers, the incense, the clothes, the eatable food-stuff — all these are for the pleasure of Visnu. I undertake this vow for the gratification of Visnu.

## \|\| 6.21 \|\|

*avaiṣṇavāndūṣayiṣye sadāhaṃ sadvaiṣṇavānpā (llāṃ) layiṣye murāre \|*  
*viṣṇudruhāṃ chedayiṣye ca jihvāṃ tacchṛṇvatāṃ pūrayiṣye trapūlkāḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. I abhor the non Vaisnava people and love the Vaisnava ones. I cut off the tongue of the people who reproach lord Visnu. With the heated liquefied tin I fill the ears of those who hear his reproaches.

## \|\| 6.22 \|\|

*etādṛśī śaktirmamāsti deva tava prasādādbra linopi viṣṇo \|*  
*athāpi nāhaṃ stavane samarthaḥ lakṣmyā hyahaṃ koṭiguṇairvihīnaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. O God, by your favour I possess sufficient strength for the purpose. Still I lack in strength to offer sufficient prayers. I am inferior to Laksmi by millions of virtues.

## \|\| 6.23 \|\|

*etatstotraṃ hyarthayeccaiva yā naḥ tatra prītirhyakṣayā me sadā syāt \|*  
*stotraṃ hyetatpāṭhayantīha loke te vaiṣṇavāste ca haripriyāśca \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. I crave for eternal pleasure accruing from the company of the person who sings praises of Hari. Those who unitiate others to the same are Vaisnavas attached to Visnu.

## \|\| 6.24 \|\|

*kurvanti ye paṭhanaṃ nityameva samarpayiṣyati sadā harau ca \|*  
*teṣāṃ hariḥ prīyate keśavolaṃ harau prasanne kimalabhyamasti \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Lord Visnu is verity pleased with those who recite this praise. And when the lord is pleased there is nothing that remains inaccessible to the praiser."

## \|\| 6.25 \|\|

*evaṃ stutvā valadevo mahātmā tūṣṇīṃ sthitaḥ prāñjaliragrato hareḥ \|*  
*sarasvatyuvāca \|*  
*ko vā rasajño bhagavan murāre hare guṇastavanātkīrtanādvā \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Thus, the noble Baladeva eulogized lord. Hari and stood in front of him with palms joined in reverence.

"O lord, is there a man who can appreciate the excellence of your Virtues and be satisfied with the counting of your praise.

## \|\| 6.26 \|\|

*alaṃbuddhiṃ prāpnuyāddevadeva brahmādibhiḥ sarvadā stūyamāna \|*  
*yaḥ karṇanāḍīṃ puruṣasya yāto bhavapradāṃ deharatiṃ chinatti \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. O god of gods, you are praised by Brahma and other gods. Your merits entering the car-hole of a person destroy our attachment for the body that is the cause of our rebirth.

## \|\| 6.27 \|\|

*na kevalaṃ deharatiṃ chinattyasadgṛhakṣetrabhāryāsuteṣu nityam \|*  
*paśvādirūpeṣu dhanādikeṣu anarghyaratneṣu priyaṃ chinātti \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Your praise, О lord, destroys our attachment not only for the body but also for our home, wife, sons, etc., and for fields, animals, gems or other precious articles.

## \|\| 6.28 \|\|

*anaṃ tavedapratipāditopi lakṣmīrna vai veda tava svarūpam \|*  
*caturmukho naiva veda na vāyurasau na vettīti kimatra citram \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Though the Vedas have tried to explain your identity (they have not done it fully). Laksmi too. has failed to gauge your essence. Neither the four-faced Brahma nor Vayu knows your reality.

## \|\| 6.29 \|\|

*etādṛśasya stavane kvāsti śaktirmama prabho brahmavāyvoḥ sakāśāt \|*  
*śatairguṇaiḥ sarvadā nyūnatāsti ato hare dayayā māṃ ca pāhi \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. I cannot sufficiently praise you, О lord. I am inferior to Brahma and Vayu. Hence, be merciful and protect me, О lord!

## \|\| 6.30 \|\|

*evaṃ stutvā hariṃ sā tu tūṣṇīmāsa khagaśvara \|*  
*bhāratī tu tadā stotuṃ hariṃ samupacakrame \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. О lord of birds, thus Sarasvati praised Hari and became silent. Thereafter, Bharatl began to praise lord Hari.

## \|\| 6.31-32 \|\|

*bhāratyuvāca \|*  
*brahmeśa lakṣmīśa hare murāre guṇāṃstava śraddadhānasya nityam \|*  
*tathā stuvantosya vivardhamānāṃ matiṃ ca nityaṃ viṣayeṣvasatsu \|\| 31 \|\|*

*kurvanti vairāgyamamutra loke tataḥ paraṃ bhaktidṛḍhāṃ tathaiva \|*  
*tataḥ paraṃ caiva hareḥ prasannatāṃ kurvanti nityaṃ tava devadeva \|\| 32 \|\|*

31-32. "O lord of Brahma, О lord of Laksmi, О slayer of Mura, I have firm faith in your virtues, the praise whereof produces detachment of the people towards material objects and develops their firm devotion whereby you, О lord, are entirely pleased.

## \|\| 6.33 \|\|

*tenāparokṣaṃ ca bhavecca tasya ato guṇānāṃ stavane ca me ratiḥ \|*  
*sā tu prajātā puruṣasya nityaṃ saṃsāraduḥ khaṃ tu tadācchinatti \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. I have, therefore, strong desire to praise your virtues, О lord.

The act of your praise removes sorrows root and branch.

## \|\| 6.34 \|\|

*vicchinnaduḥ khasya tadādhikāriṇa ānandarūpākhyaphalaṃ dadāti \|*  
*harerguṇānastuvatāṃ ca pāpaṃ teṣāṃ hi puṇyaṃ ca tathā kṣiṇoti \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. When sorrows are removed, the deserving devotee enjoys fruits of pleasure. The absence of devotion, on the contrary, invites sin and destroys virtue.

## \|\| 6.35 \|\|

*evaṃ viditvā paramo gururmama vāyurdayālurmama vallabhaśca \|*  
*harerguṇānsarvaguṇaprasārānmamaiva yogyānsukhamukhyabhūtān \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Knowing all this, my revered preceptor Vayu who was very kind and affectionate to me, declared your qualities, the source of all pleasure and bliss, selecting them out of the many accruing from pious deeds.

## \|\| 6.36 \|\|

*uddhṛtya puṇyebhya ivārtabandhuḥ śivaśca no druhyati puṇyakīrtim \|*  
*tava prasādācca śriyaḥ prasādādvāyoḥ prasādācca mamāsti nityam \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Even lord Siva, the friend of the distressed, does not feel jealous of our holy fame. I derive bliss of pleasure by your favour, by the favour of Laksmi and Vayu.

## \|\| 6.37 \|\|

*yadyatkarotyeva sadaiva vāyustattatkarotyeva sadaiva nityam \|*  
*vāyorvirodhaṃ na karoti devaḥ sa tadvirodhaṃ ca karoti nityam \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Whatever act is enjoined by Vayu, the same is endorsed by you.

## \|\| 6.38 \|\|

*harervirodhaṃ na karoti vāyurvāyorvirodhaṃ na karoti viṣṇuḥ \|*  
*vāyoḥ prasādānmamanāsti kiñcidatānabhāvaśca tava prasādāt \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Your lordship does not contradict Vayu and Vayu does not contradict your lordship. I feel no inferiority or handicap in the accomplishment of my task.

## \|\| 6.39 \|\|

*yathaiva mūlaṃ ca tathāvatāre duḥ khādikaṃ nāsti samīraṇasya \|*  
*vāyustathānye ca ubhau mukundastathāvatāreṣu na duḥ kharūpau \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. As in the original form, so in the assumed form at the stage of incarnation, Vayu feels no suffering. Visnu, Vayu and other incarnations do not suffer even when they take human shape.

## \|\| 6.40 \|\|

*aśaktavaddṛśyate vāyudevaḥ yugānusārāṃllokadharmāṃstu rakṣan \|*  
*narāvatāre tatra deve murāre hyaśaktatā neti vicaṃ tanīyam \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Vayu appears to be powerless but he protects all customs current in different yugas. While he takes human forms he does not suffer from the pangs of birth.

О lord I and Vayu do not suffer on that account. О destroyer of Asura Madhu, thus situated, I am unable to sufficiently evaluate your merits, О lord.

## \|\| 6.41 \|\|

*avatārarūpe yamaduḥ khādikaṃ ca na cintanīyaṃ jñānibhirdevadeva \|*  
*ahaṃ kadācitsukhanāśapradeśe daityāṃstathā mārayituṃ gatosmi \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. In the process of incarnation, the wise should know, there are no tortures accruing from Yama. Know that once. I went to slay the demons in the hellish region. (But I did not suffer at the sight of the sinners being tortured there).

## \|\| 6.42 \|\|

*naitāvatā mama vāyośca nityaṃ duḥ khātanaṃ naiva saṃcitanīyam \|*  
*etādṛśohaṃ stavanenu kāsti śaktirguṇānāṃ madhusūdana prabho \|*  
*vāyoḥ sakāśācca guṇena hīnā saṃsārarūpe muktarūpe ca deva \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. О god, I am inferior to Vayu in both human and divine forms."

## \|\| 6.43 \|\|

*evaṃ stutvā bhāratī tu tūṣṇīmāsa khageśvara \|*  
*tadanantarajaḥ śeṣaḥ prāñjaliḥ prāha keśavam \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Thus, having rendered praise Bharat! was silent, О lord of birds.

Then Sesa who was younger in age to Bharati spoke to the lord with palms joined in reverence.

## \|\| 6.44 \|\|

*śeṣa uvāca \|*  
*nāhaṃ ca jāne tava pādamūlaṃ rudro na vetti garuḍo na veda \|*  
*ahaṃ vāṇyāḥ śataguṇāṃśahīno dattvā hyāyatanaṃ pāhi māṃ vāsudeva \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. "I do not know your worthy self nor does Rudra nor Garuda, I am inferior to Bharati in hundreds of pious deeds. О lord, give me your shelter and protection.

## \|\| 6.45 \|\|

*evaṃ stutvā saśeṣastu tūṣṇīmāsa khageśvara \|*  
*tadanantarajo vīśaḥ stotuṃ samupacakrame \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Thus having praised the lord, isesa remained silent. Thereafter, Garuda began to praise the lord in the following way.

## \|\| 6.46 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*tava padoḥ stutiṃ kiṃ karomyahaṃ mama padāṃbuje hyarpitaṃ manaḥ \|*  
*kathamahaṃ mukhe pakṣiyonijaḥ kathamevaṅguṇā nīḍituṃ kṣamaḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. Sesa said: — "O lord, how far can I praise your lotusfeet whereupon my heart is set. How can I, a viviparous being be able to enumerate your virtues?"

## \|\| 6.47 \|\|

*evaṃ stutvā tu garuḍastūṣṇīmāsa nayānvitaḥ \|*  
*tadanantarajo rudrastotuṃ samupacakrame \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Having praised Visnu, Garuda was silent. Thereafter Rudra began to praise him thus.

## \|\| 6.48 \|\|

*rudra uvāca \|*  
*yā vai taveśa bhagavanna vidāma bhūman bhaktirmamāstu śivapādasarojamūle \|*  
*channāpi sā nanu sadā na mamāsti deva tenādruhaṃ tava viruddhamataḥ karomi \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Rudra said: — "O lord, I have been ignorant of your pervading power. My devotion was centred on the lotus feet of lord Siva. I lost devotion for you and bore malice. I acted contrary to your desire.

## \|\| 6.49 \|\|

*sarvānna buddhisahitasya hare murāre kā śaktirasti vacane mama mūḍhabuddheḥ \|*  
*vāṇyā sadā śataguṇena vihīnamenaṃ māṃ pāhi ceśa mama cāyatanaṃ ca dattvā \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. О lord, I, being stupid and devoid of discretion am unable to eulogize your virtues. I am inferior to Bharati in hundreds of marks. О lord, grant me your shelter and protection."

## \|\| 6.50 \|\|

*evaṃ stutvā sa rudrastu tūṣṇīmāsa dvijottamaḥ \|*  
*śeṣānantarajā devī vāruṇī vākyamabravīt \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Having praised the lord thus, Rudra was silent. Thereupon, the goddess VarunI who was younger to Shesa spoke thus.

## \|\| 6.51 \|\|

*vāruṇyuvāca \|*  
*lakṣmīpate brahmapate manoḥ pategiraḥ pate rudrapate nṛṇāṃ pate \|*  
*guṇāṃstava stotumahaṃ samarthā na pārvatī nāpi suparṇapatnī \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Varuna said: — Lord of LaksmI, О lord of Brahma, О lord of Manu, О lord of speech, О lord of Rudra, О lord of men, I am unable to praise you. Neither Parvati nor Brahml the consort of Garuda can do that.

## \|\| 6.52 \|\|

*śeṣādahaṃ daśaguṇairvihīnā māṃ pāhi nityaṃ jagatāmadhīśa \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. I am inferior to gesa by ten marks. О lord of the universe, I pray you, grant me eternal protection."

## \|\| 6.53 \|\|

*evaṃ stutvā vāruṇī tu tūṣṇīmāsa khageśvara \|*  
*tadanantarajā brāhmī sauparṇī hyupacakrame \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. O lord of birds, having praised the lord thus, Varum became silent. Brahml, the wife of Garuda, who was inferior to her in age, spoke thus.

## \|\| 6.54 \|\|

*sauparṇyuvāca \|*  
*stotuṃ guṇāṃstava hare jagadī śavācā śrotuṃ hare tava kathāṃ śravaṇe na śaktiḥ \|*  
*yastattvanuṃ smarati deva tava svarūpaṃ ko vai nu veda bhuvi taṃ bhagavatpadārtham \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. Sauparna said: — "О Hari, О lord of the universe, I have no power to praise you in words nor the power to hear your praise. He who remembers your personality, О lord, is blessed indeed. Who else on earth can know the lord, the blissful one.

## \|\| 6.55 \|\|

*ato guṇastavane nāsti śaktirvīndrāhadaṃ daśaguṇairavarā ca nityam \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Therefore, I cannot sufficiently praise your virtues. О lord, I am inferior to Rudra in Virtue by ten marks."

## \|\| 6.56 \|\|

*evaṃ stutvā tu sauparṇī tūṣṇīmāsa khageśvara \|*  
*rudrānantarajā stotuṃ girijā tūpacakrame \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. О lord of birds, having praised thus, Brahml was silent. Then, Parvati who was younger to Rudra began her praise.

## \|\| 6.57 \|\|

*pārvatyuvāca govinda nārāyaṇa vāsudeva tvayā hi me kiñcidapi prayojanam \|*  
*nāstyeva svāminna ca nāma vācā saubhāgyarūpaḥ sarvadā eka eva \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. О Narayana, О Vasudeva, О lord, I have no selfish motive in praising you. О lord, you are unparallel in virtue.

## \|\| 6.58 \|\|

*nārāyaṇeti tava nāma ca ekameva vairāgyabhaktivibhave paramaṃ samarthām \|*  
*asaṃkhyabrahmādikahatyanāśāne gurvaṅganākoṭivināśane ca \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. Your appellation Narayana alone has the power to confer detachment from worldly affairs and devotion to the lord. It can wipe off the sin of slaying a Brahmana and of having illicit connection with the wife of the preceptor.

## \|\| 6.59 \|\|

*nāmādhikāriṇī cāhaṃ guṇānāṃ ca mahāprabho \|*  
*stavane nāsti me śaktī rudrāddaśaguṇairaham \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. I praise your name as the devotee of your lordship but cannot sufficiently praise your virtues. 1 am inferior to Rudra in virtues by ten marks. There can be no second thought in this matter." Thus saying, Parvati was silent, О lord of birds.

## Chapter 7

## \|\| 7.1 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*pārvatyānantarotpanna indro vacanamabravīt \|*  
*indra uvāca \|*  
*tava svarūpaṃ hṛdi saṃvijānan samutsukaḥ syātstavane yastu mūḍhaḥ \|*  
*ajānataḥ stavanaṃ devadeva tadevāhurhelanaṃ cakrapāṇe \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. India who was younger to Indra said "At first a person should realize the Self in his heart and then praise the lord. О lord of lords, if he offers praise without knowing him it becomes insulting to the lord, the holder of discus.

## \|\| 7.2 \|\|

*tathāpi tadvai tava nāma pūrvaṃ bhavettadā puṇyakaraṃ bhavediti \|*  
*rudrādi kānāṃ stavane nāsti śaktistadā vaktavyaṃ mama nāstīti kiṃ vā \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. But even the recital of your name confers merit to the reciter.

O lord, Rudra and others do not have sufficient power to render homage to you. How can I, an insignificant person, offer horltage to you.

## \|\| 7.3 \|\|

*guṇāṃśato daśabhī rudrato vai sadā nyūno matsamaḥ kāmadevaḥ \|*  
*jñāne bale samatā sarvadāsti tathāḥ kāmaḥ kiṃ ca dūtaḥ sadaiva \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. I am inferior to Rudra in virtue by ten marks. Lord Kama is equal to me in knowledge and strength (Neither Kama nor any other god can praise you sufficiently).

## \|\| 7.4 \|\|

*evaṃ stutvā devadevo hariṃ ca tūṣṇīṃ sthitaḥ prāñjalirnamrabhūrdhā \|*  
*tadanantarajo brahmā ahaṅkārika ūcivān \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Having praised the lord, the god of gods stood motionless with palms joined in reverence and forehead lowered in humility.

Thereafter, Brahma, the self-centered god spoke in the following words.

## \|\| 7.5 \|\|

*ahaṅkārika uvāca \|*  
*namaste gaṇapūrṇāya namaste jñānamūrtaye \|*  
*namo 'jñānavidūrāya brahmaṇenaṃ tamūrtaye \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Aharitkarika said: "Homage to the lord abounding in qualities. Homage to the lord, the image of knowledge. Homage to the lord, devoid of ignorance. Homage to Brahma, the all-pervasive lord.

## \|\| 7.6 \|\|

*indrādahaṃ daśaguṇaiḥ sarvadā nyūna ukto na jani tvāṃ sarvadā hyaprameya \|*  
*tathāpi māṃ pāhi jagadguro tvaṃ dattvā divyaṃ hyāyatanaṃ ca viṣṇo \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. I am inferior to India in merits by ten degrees. О inscrutable one, I do not ever know you. О lord of the universe, you should protect me, О Visnu, grant me divine abode."

## \|\| 7.7 \|\|

*āhaṅkārika evaṃ tu stutvā tūṣṇīṃbabhūva ha \|*  
*tadanantarajā stotuṃ śacī vacanamabravīt \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. The self-centered Brahma praised Hari in this manner and was silent.

Thereafter, Indram praised Hari in the following way.

## \|\| 7.8 \|\|

*śacyuvāca \|*  
*saṃcintayāmi aniśaṃ tava pādapadmaṃ vajrāṅkuśadhvajasaroruhalāñchanāḍhyam \|*  
*vāgīśvarairapi sadā manasāpi dhartuṃ no śakyamīśa tava pādarajaḥ smarāmi \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Saci said: — "Incessantly I dwell upon your lotus-feet that bear the marks of thunderbolt, goad, banner and lotus. О lord, I think upon the dust of your lotus-feet which even the wise people cannot carry by their mind.

## \|\| 7.9 \|\|

*āhaṅkārikaprāṇācca guṇaiśca daśabhiḥ sadā \|*  
*nyūnabhūtāṃ ca māṃ pāhi kṛpālo bhaktavatsala \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. I am inferior to the self-centered ВгаЬща in virtue by ten degrees. О compassionate lord who are fond of devotees, grant me your shelter and protection."

## \|\| 7.10 \|\|

*evaṃ stutvā śacī devī tūṣṇīṃ bhagavatī hyabhūt \|*  
*tadanantarajā stotuṃ ratiḥ samupacakrame \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Having praised the lord thus, goddess Indram! was silent. Thereafter Rati (wife of Kama — god of love) who was younger to Indram in age began to praise the lord.

## \|\| 7.11 \|\|

*ratiruvāca \|*  
*saṃcintayāmi nṛharervadanāravindaṃ bhṛtyānukaṃpitadhiyā hi gṛhītamūrtim \|*  
*yacchrīniketamajarudraramādikaiśca saṃlālitaṃ kuṭilaṅkuntalavṛndajuṣṭam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Rati said: — T think upon the lotus-face of Man-lion (Nrsimha) who had incarnated himself in half-human and half-animal form out of desire to render protection to his devotee, who (the lord), accompanied by LaksmI and attended by Brahma, Rudra and others looks splendid by curly tresses of hair.

## \|\| 7.12 \|\|

*etādṛśaṃ tava mukhaṃ nuvituṃ na śaktiḥ śacyā samāpi bhagavanparipāhi nityam \|*  
*kṛtvā stutiṃ ratiriyaṃ paramādareṇa tūṣṇīṃ sthitā bhagavataśca samīpa eva \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Though I am equal to Indrani in all respects I cannot sufficiently praise your virtues. О lord, I pray for your eternal protection."

Thus having rendered praise, with devotion, Rati stood silent by the lord.

## \|\| 7.13 \|\|

*ratyanantarajo dakṣaḥ stotuṃ samupacakrame \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Thereafter, Daksa who was junior in age to Rati began to praise the lord.

## \|\| 7.14 \|\|

*dakṣa uvāca \|*  
*saṃcintaye bhagavataścaraṇodatīrthaṃ bhaktyā hyajena pariṣiktamajādivandyam \|*  
*yacchaucaniḥ sṛtamajapravarāvatāraṃ gaṅgākhyatīrthamabhavatsaritāṃ variṣṭham \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Daksa said: — "I meditate upon the holy feet of the lord which out of devotion are water-washed and worshipped by Brahma, the ablutionary water being turned in Ganga, the holiest of holy Indian rivers.

## \|\| 7.15 \|\|

*rudropi tenava vidhṛtena jaṭākalāpapūtena pādarajasā hyaśivaḥ śivobhūt \|*  
*etādṛśaṃ te caraṇaṃ karuṇeśa viṣṇo stotuṃ śaktirmama nāsti kṛpāvatāra \|*  
*ratyā samaḥ śrutigato na gatosmi mokṣametādṛśaṃ ca paripāhi nidānamūrte \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. The dust from the lord's feet on the matted locks of hair made Shiva auspicious. О compassionate lord who have incarnated on earth for favouring your devotees, I do not possess sufficient strength to praise such power of yours. The Vedas declare that I am at par with Rati. I have not attained moksa. Please protect me as such, О primeval lord (from sensuous pleasures)."

## \|\| 7.16 \|\|

*evaṃ stutvā sa dakṣastu tūṣṇī meva babhūva ha \|*  
*tadanantarajaḥ stotuṃ bṛhaspatirupākramīt \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Having praised thus, Daksa was silent. Thereafter, Brhaspati, who was junior to him in age, spoke thus.

## \|\| 7.17 \|\|

*bṛhaspatiruvāca \|*  
*saṃcintayāmi satataṃ tava cānanābjaṃ tvaṃ dehi duṣṭaviṣayeṣu viraktimīśa \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. I meditate upon your lotus-face. О lord, you may kindly endow me with power to detach myself from vicious objects of pleasure.

## \|\| 7.18 \|\|

*eteṣu śaktiryadi vai sa jīvo kartā ca bhoktā ca sadā ca dātā \|*  
*yoṣāṃ ca putrasuhṛdau ca paśūṃśca sarvamevaṃ vinaśyati yato hi tadāśu chindhi \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. I, the individual soul, regard myself as the creator, enjoyer, and bestower of objects of pleasure. О lord, cut off my attachment immediately to my sons, wife, friends, cattle, for they are surely destructible.

## \|\| 7.19 \|\|

*saṃsāracakrabhramaṇenaiva deva saṃsāraduḥ khamanubhūyehāgatosmi \|*  
*śaktirna cāsti navane mama devadeva satyā samaṃ ca satataṃ paripāhi nityam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. О lord, rolling on the wheel of birth and undergoing pain in the process of that action, I have come up here. О overlord, I cannot praise you sufficiently. О lord, I pray you to grant me protection as you granted to Rati

## \|\| 7.20 \|\|

*evaṃ śrutvā ca paramaṃ tūṣṇīmeva sthito muniḥ \|*  
*tadanantarajastotuṃ hyaniruddhopacakrame \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Having praised the lord thus; Sage Brhaspati stood by him in complete silence. Aniruddha who was younger to him in age began to praise him thus

## \|\| 7.21 \|\|

*aniruddha uvāca \|*  
*evaṃ harestava kathāṃ rasikāṃ vihāya strīṇāṃ bhage ca vadane parimuhya nityam \|*  
*viṣṭhāntrapūritabile rasiko hi nityaṃ sthāyī ca sūkaravadeva vimūḍhabuddhiḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Aniruddha said: — О lord, a man of poor intellect is averse to hearing the sweet narrative of your lordship. Such a person indulges in sensuous pleasures, the kissing of a woman's lovely cheek or pressing his penis into their vagina filled with feces and intestines, like a pig fond of excrement.

## \|\| 7.22 \|\|

*majjāsthipittakapharaphalādipūrṇe carmāntraveṣṭitamukhe patitaṃ ha pītam \|*  
*āsvādane mama ca pāpagatermurāre māyābalaṃ tava vibho paramaṃ nimittam \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. I, indulging in sinful activity, am fallen in a pit filled "with marrow, bones, bile, phlegm, blood, filth, covered with skin and entrail, I feel pleasure thereto. О lord, the power (Shakti) that co-exists with you is the causte of creation.

## \|\| 7.23 \|\|

*saṃsāracakre bhramataśca nityaṃ suduḥ kharūpe sukhaleśavarjite \|*  
*malaṃ vamantaṃ navabhiśca dvāraiḥ śarīramāruhya sumūḍhabuddhiḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. I, of perverted intellect, roll on the wheel of creation which is of the nature of sorrows, being entirely devoid of pleasure and entittfng filth from the nine pores of the body frame.

## \|\| 7.24 \|\|

*namāmi nityaṃ tava tatkathāmṛtaṃ vihāyadeva śrutimūlanāśanam \|*  
*kuṭuṃbapoṣaṃ ca sadā ca kurvandānādyakurvannivasan gṛhe ca \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. O lord, I bow to you-I who have (unfortunately left tasting the nectar of your auspicious narrative and have completely gone over to the worldly affairs such as the maintenance of my family — a fact which destroys my Vaisnava dharma.

I have not abandoned my house (which as a Vana-prastha I should have done) nor have I been disposed to charitable deeds.

## \|\| 7.25 \|\|

*dūre ca saṃsāramalaṃ tvidaṃ kuru dehi hyado divyakathāmṛtaṃ sadā \|*  
*etādṛśohaṃ tava sadguṇaughaṃ stotuṃ samartho nāsmi śacīsamaśca \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. О lord, remove this filth off from me. Give me an ambrosial drink in the form of story of your divine life. Like the consort of Indra, I cannot praise your virtues, О lord.

## \|\| 7.26 \|\|

*evaṃ stutvāniruddhastu tūṣṇīmāsa khageśvara \|*  
*tadanantarajaḥ stotraṃ manaḥ svāyaṃbhuvobravīt \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. О lord of birds, thus having praised the lord, Aniruddha stood silent. Thereafter, Manu, the son of Brahma and who was junior to Aniruddha said thus:

## \|\| 7.27 \|\|

*svāyaṃbhuva uvāca \|*  
*stotuṃ hyanupraviśatopi na garbhaduḥ khaṃ tasmādahaṃ paramapūjyapadaṃ gataste \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Svyambhuva said: — "Though I am born of a womb, I have not suffered the embryonic pains. Hence, I have taken shelter with your worthy feet.

## \|\| 7.28 \|\|

*manorbhāryā mānavī ca yamaḥ saṃyaminīpatiḥ \|*  
*diśābhimānī candrastu sūryaścakṣurniyāmakaḥ \|*  
*parasparasamā hyete muktvā saṃsārameva ca \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Manavi — the wife of Manu, Yama-the lord of Samyamini, the Moon — the presiding deity of the eight quarters, the Sun — the presiding deity of the eye are equal to one another when they have set out on the path of release."

## \|\| 7.29 \|\|

*pravāhādviguṇonaścetyevaṃ jānīhi cāṇḍaja \|*  
*sūryānantarajaḥ stotuṃ varuṇaḥ saṃpracakrame \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Thereafter, Varuna, twice inferior to Vayu and junior to the Sun, began to praise the lord, О lord of birds.

## \|\| 7.30 \|\|

*varuṇa uvāca \|*  
*tvadvicchayā racite dehagehe puttre kalatrepi dhane dravyajātau \|*  
*mamāhamityalpadhiyā ca mūḍhā saṃsāraduḥ khe vinimajjanti sarve \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Varuna said: — "O lord, this abode in the form of my body is built by your choice. So are the son, wife, riches and other objects. The stupid, possessing very little knowledge of reality are attached to worldly affairs. They are drowned in the ocean of suffering.

## \|\| 7.31 \|\|

*ato hare tādṛśīṃ me kubuddhiṃ vināśya me dehi te pādadāsyam \|*  
*ahaṃ manoḥ pādapādārdhabhūtaguṇena hīnaḥ sarvadā vai murāre \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. О lord, undo this wrong notion of mine and favour me with the chance to serve at your feet. I am inferior to Mann by sixteen ath§as, О slayer of Mura.

## \|\| 7.32 \|\|

*evaṃ stutvā tu varuṇaḥ prāñjaliḥ samupasthitaḥ \|*  
*varuṇānantarotpanno nārado hyastuvaddharim \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Having praised thus, Varuna stood attentive with palms joined in reverence. Thereafter, Narada, who was junior to Varuna began to praise the lord,

## \|\| 7.33 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*yannāmadheyaśravaṇānukīrtanātsvādvanyatattvaṃ mama nāsti viṣṇo \|*  
*punīhyataścaiva parovarāyānyajjihvāgre vartate nāma tasya \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. "O lord, there is nothing more pleasing than hearing or uttering the praise of the lord. О lord, you purify those who utter your name, together with their ancestors and successors.

## \|\| 7.34 \|\|

*yajjihvāgre harināmaiva nāsti sa brāhmaṇo naiva sa eva gokharaḥ \|*  
*ahaṃ na jāne ca tava svarūpaṃ nyūno hyahaṃ varuṇātsarvadaiva \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. He is not a Brahmana but an ass who does not utter the name Hari. О lord, I do not know your actual form. I am always inferior to Varuna."

## \|\| 7.35 \|\|

*evaṃ stutvā nārado vai khagendrastūṣṇīmabhūddevadevasya cāgre \|*  
*yo nāradānantaraṃ saṃbabhūva bhṛgurmahātmā stotumupapracakrame \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Having praised the lord thus, О lord of birds, Narada stood silent before Hari. Thereafter, Bhrgu who was junior to Narada began to praise the lord.

## \|\| 7.36 \|\|

*bhṛguruvāca \|*  
*kimāsanaṃ te garuḍāsanāya kiṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ kaustubhabhūṣaṇāya \|*  
*lakṣmīkalatrāya kimasti deyaṃ vāgīśa kiṃ te vacanīyamasti \|*  
*ato na jāne tava sadguṇāṃśca hyahaṃ sadā varuṇā tpādahīnaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Bhrgu said: — О lord, what better Vehicle you need when you have Garuda as such. When you have Kaustubha gem for your ornament, what more ornament you need. What more riches do you need from a devotee when you have Laksml (the goddess of wealth) as your wife. О lord of speech, what more praise can I render unto you. I do not know your good qualities. О lord I am always inferior to, Varuna by full one quarter of merits."

## \|\| 7.37 \|\|

*evaṃ stutvā hariṃ devaṃ bhṛgustūṣṇīṃ babhūva ha \|*  
*tadanantarajo hyagnirastāvītpuruṣottamam \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Thus having praised Hari, Bhrgu stood in silence. Thereafter, Agni who was inferior to Bhrgu in age began to praise the lord\*-the Supreme primeval Purusa.

## \|\| 7.38 \|\|

*agniruvāca \|*  
*yattejasāhaṃ susamiddhatejā havyaṃ vahāmyadhvare ājyasiktam \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Agni said: — 'O lord, spirited by your energy, I carry oblations sprinkled over with ghee at the altar of sacrifice.

## \|\| 7.39 \|\|

*yattejasāhaṃ jaṭhare saṃpraviśya pacannannaṃ sarvadā pūrṇaśaktiḥ \|*  
*ato na jāne tava sadguṇāṃśca bhṛgorahaṃ sarvadaivaṃ samosmi \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Supported by your energy, I enter the belly of a person and cook the food therein. I derive my energy from yours, О lord. I am. equal to Bhrgu in every respect."

## \|\| 7.40-42 \|\|

*tadanantarajā stotuṃ prasūtirupacakrame \|\| 40 \|\|*

*prasūtiruvāca \|*  
*yannāmārthavicāraṇepimunayo muhyati vai sarvadā tvadbhītā api devatā hyavirataṃ strībhiḥ sahaiva sthitāḥ \|*  
*māndhātṛdhruvanāradāśca bhṛgavo vaivasvatādyākhilāḥ premṇā vai praṇamāmyahaṃ hitakṛte tasmai namo viṣṇave \|\| 41 \|\|*

*ato na jāne tava sadguṇānsadā evaṃ vidhā kā mama śaktirasti \|*  
*stutvā hyevaṃ prasūtistu tūṣṇīmāsītkhageśvara \|\| 42 \|\|*

40-42. "Even the Sages are perplexed in understanding the meaning of your name. The deities and their consorts are always engaged in your praise. So are Mandhatr, Dhruva, Narada, Bhrgu, Vaivasvata and the rest. I offer salutatioxto you, О lord, out of my devotion. Homage to you, О lord, who look after our well being. Hence, I do not know your benign aspect. I do not possess ability to offer praise." О lord of birds, having offered praise Prasuti remained silent.

## \|\| 7.43 \|\|

*agnirvāgātmako brahmaputro bhṛgu ṛṣistathā \|*  
*tadbhāryā vai prasūtistu traya ete samāḥ smṛtāḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Agni, sage Bhrgu son of Brahma and the embodiment of speech and his wife Prasuti — the three are equal in status.

## \|\| 7.44 \|\|

*varuṇātpādahīnāśca pravahādviguṇādhamāḥ \|*  
*dakṣācchatāvarā jñeyā mitrāttu dviguṇādhikāḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. The three are inferior to Varuna by one quarter, to Pravaha by half, to Daksa by one. hundred, superior to Mitra by twice that number.

## \|\| 7.45 \|\|

*prasūtyanantaraṃ jāto vasiṣṭho brahmanandanaḥ \|*  
*vinayāvanato bhūtvā stotuṃ samupacakrame \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Thereafter, Vasistha son of Brahma junior to Prasuti began to offer praise.

## \|\| 7.46 \|\|

*vasiṣṭha uvāca \|*  
*namostu tasmai puruṣāya vedhase namonamo 'sadvṛjinacchide namaḥ \|*  
*namonamo svāṅgabhavāya nityaṃ natosmi henātha tavāṅghripaṅkajam \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. Vasistha said: — "Homage to Brahma, with the wicked sinners О lord, I bow to your lotus-feet.

## \|\| 7.47 \|\|

*māṃ pāhi nityaṃ bhagavanvāsudeva hyagnerahaṃ sarvadā nyūna eva \|*  
*mitrādahaṃ sarvadā kiñcidūnaḥ stutvā deva sobhavattatra tūṣṇīm \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. О lord, son of Vasudeva, protect me ever. I am. always inferior to Agni, I am, always inferior to Mitts, in certain aspects."

Having praised the lord thus, Vasistha stood silent before the lord.’

## \|\| 7.48 \|\|

*yo vasiṣṭhānantarajo marīcirbrahmanandanaḥ \|*  
*harintuṣṭāva parayā bhaktyā nārāyaṇaṃ gurum \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Thereafter, Marfci, son of Brahma, junior to Vasistha praised Hari with great devotion.

## \|\| 7.49 \|\|

*marīciruvāca \|*  
*devena cāhaṃ hatadhīrbhavanaprasaṅgātsarvāśubhopagamanādvimukhedriyaśca \|*  
*kurve ca nityaṃ sukhaleśalavādinā tvaddaraṃ manastvaśubhakarma samācarīṣye \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Marlci said: — "My intellect is weakened. I have developed attachment to worldly affairs, I walk in wrong direction. My senses, are quite perverted. I hanker after scanty pleasures. I turn my mind to objects that are far off from you. I stoop to wicked acts, О lord.

## \|\| 7.50 \|\|

*etādṛśohaṃ bhagavānanantaḥ sadā vasiṣṭhasya samāna eva \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. О lord, characterized by such points, 1 am equal to Vasistha."

## \|\| 7.51 \|\|

*evaṃ stutvā marīcistu tūṣṇīmāsa tadā khaga \|*  
*tadatantarajohyatrirastāvītprāñjalirharim \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Having praised the lord thus, Marlci became silent, О bird. Atri, who was junior to him, praised Hari with hands joined in reverence.

## \|\| 7.52 \|\|

*āvirbhavajjagatprabhavāyāvatīrṇaṃ tadrakṣaṇārthamanavadyañca tathāvyayāya \|*  
*tattvārthamūlamavikāri tava svarūpaṃ hyānandasāramata eva vikāraśūnyam \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. "О lord, You have manifested yourself the self-born deity, one who abhors relations for creating, protecting and destroying the universe. Your form is truth itself eternal and unchanging. It is the essence of joy and unmodifiable.

## \|\| 7.53 \|\|

*traiguṇyaśūnyamakhileṣu ca saṃvibhaktaṃ tatra praviśya bhagavanna hi paśyatīva \|*  
*ato marārestava sadguṇāṃśca stotuṃ na śakromi marīcetulyaḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. Your real nature is devoid of sattva, rajas and tamas. It is apportioned in all objects wherein your existence remains unmanifest. Therefore, О lord, destroyer of Mura, I, like Marici am unable to praise your virtues."

## \|\| 7.54 \|\|

*evaṃ stutvā hyatrirapitūṣṇīmāsa tadā khaga \|*  
*tadanantarajaḥ stotumaṅgirā vākyamabravīt \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. Having praised thus, О bird, Atri stood silent. Thereafter Angiras began to praise the lord.

## \|\| 7.55 \|\|

*aṅgirā uvāca \|*  
*draṣṭuṃ na śakromi tava svarūpaṃ hyanantabāhūdaramastakaṃ ca \|*  
*anantasāhasrakirīṭajuṣṭaṃ mahārhanānābharaṇaiśca śobhitam \|*  
*etādṛśaṃ rūpamanantapāraṃ stotuṃ hyaśaktastu samosmi cātreḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Angira said: — "О lord, I do not see your body possessed of many arms, bellies and foreheads, adorned with thousands of diadems and other precious jewels. I am unable to praise your eternal form though 1 am equal to Atri in all aspects."

## \|\| 7.56 \|\|

*evaṃ stutvā hyaṅgirāśca tūṣṇīmāsa khageśvara \|*  
*tadanantarajaḥ stotuṃ pulastyo vākyamavravīt \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. О lord of birds, having spoken thus, Angiras stood silent before the lord. Pulastya who was junior to him, began to praise the lord.

## \|\| 7.57 \|\|

*pulastya uvāca \|*  
*yo vā haristu bhagavānsa (sva) upāsakānāṃ saṃdarśayedbhuvanamaṅgalamaṅgalaṃ ca \|*  
*(laśca) yasmai namo bhagavate purupāya tubhyaṃ yo vāvitā nirayabhāgagamaprasaṅge \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Pulastya said: — 'Lord Hari manifests himself to his devotees and confers suspiciousness. Homage to you, О lord, that savest the sinner from the suffering of hell

## \|\| 7.58 \|\|

*etādṛśāṃstava guṇānnavituṃ na śaktaṃ māṃ pāhi bhagavansadṛśo hyaṅgirasā ca \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. I am unable to praise your virtue, О lord. I am equal to Angiras in that aspect.

## \|\| 7.59 \|\|

*evaṃ stutvā pulastyopi stūṣṇīmeva vabhūva ha \|*  
*tadanantarajaḥ stotuṃ pulaho vākyamabravīt \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. Having praised thus, Pulastya stood in silence before the lord. Thereafter, Pulaha who was junior to Pulastya in age bcegan to praise the lord thus.

## \|\| 7.60 \|\|

*pulaha uvāca \|*  
*niṣkāmarūparihitasya samarpitaṃ ca snānāvarottamapayaḥ phalapuṣpabhojyam \|*  
*ārādhanaṃ bhagavatastava satkriyāśca vyarthaṃ bhavediti vadanti mahānubhāvāḥ \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. Pulaha said: — "O lord, you are devoid of form and desire. Still I offer you bath, cloth, water, fruits, flowers and foodstuff. О lord of Vaikuntha this worship rendered unto you is altogether fruitless. Thus say the noble sages.

## \|\| 7.61 \|\|

*tasmai sadā bhagavate praṇamāmi nityaṃ niṣkāmayā tava samarpaṇamātravuddhyā \|*  
*vaikuṇṭhanātha bhagavanstavane na śaktiḥ sohaṃ pulasatyasadṛśosmi na saṃśayotra \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. I, therefore, offer my worship unto you, О lord, with no desire for return but only with the intention'to place myself at your service. О lord of Vaikuntha, I cannot praise you enough. I am equal to Pulastya in my capacity. There is no doubt in this."

## \|\| 7.62 \|\|

*evaṃ stutvā tu pulahastūṣṇīmāsa tadā khaga \|*  
*tadanantarajaḥ stotuṃ kratuḥ samupacakrame \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. О bird, having praised thus, Pulaha became silent. Thereafter Kratu, who was junior to Pulaha began to praise the lord thus.

## \|\| 7.63 \|\|

*kraturuvāca \|*  
*prāṇaprayāṇasamaye bhagavaṃstavaiva nāmāni saṃsṛtijaduḥ khavināśakāni \|*  
*yenaikajanmaśamalaṃ sahasaiva hitvā saṃyāti muktimamalāṃ tamahaṃ prapadye \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. Your names, at the time of death, О lord, destroy all suffering accruing from birth. Your names, when recited remove, all of a sudden, the pains of birth and confer release. Of such, yourself, О lord, I place myself at disposal.

## \|\| 7.64 \|\|

*ye bhaktyā vivaśā viṣṇo nāmamātraikadajalpakāḥ \|*  
*tepi muktiṃ prayāntyāśu kimuta dhyāyinaḥ sadā \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. О lord Visnu, not to speak of those who meditate on you, even those who utter your name for devotion attain release."

## \|\| 7.65 \|\|

*evaṃ stutvā kraturapi tūṣṇīmāsa khageśvara \|*  
*tadanantarajaḥ stotuṃ manurvaivasvatobravīt \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. O lord of birds, having praised thus, Kratu stood silent. Thereafter Vaivasvata Manu who was junior to him began to praise the lord.

## \|\| 7.66 \|\|

*vaivalasvata uvāca \|*  
*sohaṃ hi karmakaraṇe nirataḥ sadaiva strīṇāṃ bhoge ca nirataśca gude pramattaḥ \|*  
*jihvendriye ca niratastava darśane ca samyagvirāgasahitaḥ paramo dareṇa \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. Vaivasvata said: — I am always engaged in worldly activities, in sensual enjoyment, occupied with the objects of senses, and averse to joys arising from your sight.

## \|\| 7.67 \|\|

*māṃsāsthimajjarudhiraiḥ sahite ca dehe bhaktiṃ sadaiva bhagavannapi taskare ca \|*  
*gurvagnibāḍabagavādiṣu satsu duḥ khātsamyagviraktimupayāmi sahasva nityam \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. I am attached to this thievish body-а heap of flesh, bones, marrow and blood. When I maintain a preceptor, holy fire sacred cow, etc. I cannot easily get away from worldly objects. О lord, remain merciful to me always.

## \|\| 7.68 \|\|

*lokānuvādaśravaṇe paramā ca śaktirnārāyaṇasya namane na ca mesti śaktiḥ \|*  
*lokānuyānakaraṇe paramā ca śaktiḥ kṣetrādimārgagamane paramā hyaśaktiḥ \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. I listen to the scandal of others. But I cannot sufficiently praise you, О lord. I walk over the path traversed by the common people but I do not follow the path of renunciation walked over by a few.

## \|\| 7.69 \|\|

*vaiśyādikeṣu dhanikeṣu parā ca śaktiḥ sadbrāhmaṇeṣvapi na śaktiraho murāre \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. I spend on prostitutes and roguish people. But I do not spend on Brahmins and saints, О destroyer of Mura.

## \|\| 7.70 \|\|

*vaivasvatamanurdevaṃ stutvā tūṣṇīṃ babhūva ha \|*  
*tadanantarajaḥ stotuṃ viśvāmitropacakrame \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. Having praised the lord thus, Vaivasvata Manu became silent. Thereafter, Visvamitra who was junior to Vaivasvata Manu began, to praise the lord.

## \|\| 7.71 \|\|

*viśvāmitra uvāca \|*  
*na dhyāte caraṇāṃbuje bhagavato saṃdhyāpi nānuṣṭhitā jñānadvārakapāṭapāṭanapaṭurdharmopinopārjitaḥ \|*  
*antarvyāphamalābhighātakaraṇe paṭvī śrutā te kathā no deva śravaṇena pāhi bhagavanmāmatritulyaṃ sadā \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. Visvamitra said: — "О lord, I have not meditated on your lotus-feet, nor performed sandhya nor adhered to Dharma that opens the gate of knowledge. I have not heard your narrative which could remove the impurity of my body. О lord, protect me, I am equal to Atri in all respects.

## \|\| 7.72 \|\|

*viśvāmitraṛṣistvevaṃ stutvā tūṣṇīṃ babhūva ha \|*  
*bhṛgunāradakṣāṃśca vihāya brahmaputrakāḥ \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. Thus having praised the lord, the sage - Visvamitra remained silent.

## \|\| 7.73 \|\|

*saptasaṃkhyā vasiṣṭhādyā viśvāmitrastathaiva ca \|*  
*vaivasvatamanustvete parasparasamāḥ smṛtāḥ \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. Excepting Bhrgu, Narada and Daksa, the seven sons of Brahma, Visvamitra and Vaivasvata Manu are equal to one another in this aspect.

## \|\| 7.74 \|\|

*vahnerapyavarā nityaṃ kiñcinmitrādguṇādhikāḥ \|*  
*tadanantajastotraṃ vakṣye śṛṇu khageśvara \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. Others are inferior to fire. Still others are more virtuous. О lord of birds, hear. I am going to tell you the praise of Hari by Mitra who was junior to Visvamitra in age.

## Chapter 8

## \|\| 8.1 \|\|

*kratoranantaraṃ jāto mitro (śro) nāma khageśvara \|*  
*nārāyaṇaṃ jagadyoniṃ stotuṃ samupacakrame \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. О lord of birds, Mitra who was junior to Visvamitra began to praise Narayana, the source of the universe.

## \|\| 8.2 \|\|

*mitra uvāca \|*  
*natosmyajñastvaccaraṇāravindaṃ bhavacchidaṃ svastyayanaṃ bhavacchide \|*  
*veda svayaṃ bhagavānvāsudevo nāhaṃ nāgnirna tridevā munīndrāḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Mitra said: — "I, who am ignorant of reality, bow to your lotus-feet for release from the meshes of worldly existence — the feet that stop the rotation of life and death and constitute an abode for the well-being of the world. Lord Vasudeva alone knows all this, not I, nor fire, nor Brahma nor Visnu nor diva, О lords of sages.

## \|\| 8.3 \|\|

*athāpare bhāgavatapradhānā yadā na jānīyurathāpare kutaḥ \|*  
*māṃ pāhi nityaṃ paratopyadhīśa viśvāmitrānnyūna eveti nityam \|*  
*ahaṃ parjanyārdviguṇa eva nityamato mama stavane nāsti śaktiḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Not to speak of others, even the chiefs among the devotees of Visnu do not know him. О lord, protect me ever from all others. I am inferior to Visvamitra, though I am twice superior to Parjanya. I cannot praise you enough, О lord.

## \|\| 8.4 \|\|

*evaṃ stutvā hariṃ mitrastūṣṇīmāsa tadā khaga \|*  
*tadanantarajā tārā stotuṃ samupacakrame \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Thus, having praised Hari, О lord, Mitra remained silent. Tara, who was junior to Mitra began to praise the lord.

## \|\| 8.5-6 \|\|

*tārovāca*  
*ananyena tu bhāvena bhaktiṃ kurvanti ye dṛḍhām \|*  
*tvatkṛte tyaktakarmāṇastyaktasvajanabāndhavāḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

*tvadāśrayāṃ kathāṃ śrutvā (dṛṣṭvā) śṛṇvanti kathayanti ca \|*  
*tathaite sādhavo viṣṇo sarvasaṃgavivarjitāḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

5-6. "There are people who are singularly devoted to you О lord. Having abandoned all activities together with their kith and kin they are engaged in hearing and reciting your narrative. О Visnu, these pious people are devoid of all attachments.

## \|\| 8.7 \|\|

*tanmadhye patitāṃ pāhi sadā mitrasamāṃ prabho \|*  
*tārānantarajaḥ prāha nirṛtiśca khageśvara \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. О lord, I who am equal to Mitra, have fallen in their midst." О lord of birds, thereafter, Nirrti who was junior to Tara said.

## \|\| 8.8 \|\|

*nirṛtiruvāca \|*  
*yogena tvayyarpitayā ca bhaktyā saṃyānti lokāḥ paramāṃ gatiṃ ca \|*  
*āsevayā sarvaguṇādhikānāṃ jñānena vairāgyayutenadave \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Niairrti said "People attain supreme position by Yoga and devotion directed to your lordship by rendering service to those who are superior to me in virtue.

## \|\| 8.9 \|\|

*cittasya nigraheṇaiva viṣṇoryānti paraṃ padam \|*  
*ato māṃ pāhi dayayā sadā tārāsamaṃ prabho \|*  
*tadanantarajā stotuṃ prāvahī taṃ pracakrame \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. By knowledge accompanied by detachment О lord, and by the control of mind, the devotees reach the highest ladder. Hence, protect me, О lord, who am equal to Tara in position.

Thereafter, Pravaha, who was junior to Nirrti began to praise the lord.

## \|\| 8.10 \|\|

*pravāhyuvāca \|*  
*sutāḥ prasaṃgena bhavanti vīryāttava prasādātparamāḥ sampadaśca \|*  
*yā hyuttamaślokarasāyanāḥ kathāstatsevanādāstvapavargavartmani \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Pravahi said: — "Children are born of semen by intercourse. Prosperity accrues by your favour. Your narrative, О lord, that serves as the elixir of life, can take a devotee to the path of salvation.

## \|\| 8.11 \|\|

*bhaktirbhavetsarvadā devadeva sadāpyahaṃ nirṛteḥ sāmyameva \|*  
*saharbhāṣyakomitraḥ tkayītāraḥ prakīrtitāḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. О lord of lords, let my devotipn remain, ever at the service of your feet. I am equal to Nirrti in status."

## \|\| 8.12 \|\|

*koṇādhipo nirṛtiśca prāvahī pravahapriyā \|*  
*catvāra ete parjanyāttriguṇāḥ parikīrtitāḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Lord of the inter-spaces of quarters, Nirrti, Pravahi and the beloved of Pravaha these four

## \|\| 8.13 \|\|

*tadanantarajānvakṣye tāñchṛṇu tvaṃ khageśvara \|*  
*pravāhabhāryānantarajo viṣvaksenothapārṣadaḥ \|*  
*vāyuputro mahābhāgaḥ hariṃ stotuṃ pracakrame \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Now, I shall tell you, О lord of birds, of the people who are junior to them.

First of all, the noble Visvaksena who was junior to the mistress of Pravaha and was a member of the assembly of Lord Visnu began to praise the lord.

## \|\| 8.14 \|\|

*viṣvaksena uvāca \|*  
*bhagavānmokṣadaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ pūrṇānando sadāyadi \|*  
*yadi syātparamā bhaktirhya parokṣatvasādhanā \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Visvatasena said: — "If all joyous lord Krsna be the conferer of salvation, — '

## \|\| 8.15-16 \|\|

*tathā svagurumārabhya brahmānteṣu ca sādhuṣu \|*  
*tadyogyatānusāreṇa bhaktirniṣkapaṭā yadi \|\| 15 \|\|*

*tulasyādiṣu jīveṣu yadi syātprītiraṇḍaja \|*  
*saṃsmṛtiśca tadā nāśī bhūyādeva na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

15-16. If there be guileless devotion towards preceptor, holy hermits, Brahma and others, О lord, if there be attachment for TulasI and other sacred plants, one attains release from the tangles of birth and death, there is no doubt in this."

## \|\| 8.17 \|\|

*evaṃ stuttvā mahābhāgo viṣvakse no mahāprabho \|*  
*tūṣṇīṃ babhūva garuḍa prāñjalirnamrakandharaḥ \|*  
*mitrādahaṃ nyūna eva nātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. О lord, having said thus, Visvaksena remained silent.

Thereafter, Garuda with hands joined in reverence and head bent low, said, "I am inferior to Mitra in status. There is no doubt in this."

## Chapter 9

## \|\| 9.1 \|\|

*śrīgaruḍa uvāca \|*  
*ajānajasvarūpaṃ ca brūhi kṛṣṇa mahāmate \|*  
*tadanyāṃśca krameṇeva vaktuṃ kṛṣṇa tvamarhasi \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Srl Garuda said: — О lord, of great wisdom, I shall like to know the nature of deities Ajana and others in the serial order of their position.

## \|\| 9.2-4 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*ajānākhyā devatāstu tattaddevakule bhavāḥ \|*  
*ajānadevatāstā hi tebhyogyāḥ karmadevatāḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

*virādhaścārudeṣṇaśca tathā citrarathastathā \|*  
*dhṛtarāṣṭraḥ kiśoraśca hūhūrhāhāstathaiva ca \|\| 3 \|\|*

*vidyādharaścograseno viśvāvasuparāvasū \|*  
*citrasenaśca gopālo balaḥ pañcadaśa smṛtāḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

2-4. Krsna said: — The deities Ajana and others are born in different families of gods. Thereafter come fifteen deities of action, viz, Viradha, Caru, Desna, Citraratha, Dhrtarastra, Kisora, Huhu, Haha, Vidyadhara, Visvavasu, Paravasu, Citrasena, Gopala and Bala.

## \|\| 9.5 \|\|

*evamādyaśca gandharvāḥ śatasaṃkhyāḥ khageśvara \|*  
*ajānajasamā jñeyā muktau saṃsāra eva ca \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. О lord of birds, such Gandharvas are hundred in number. They are equal in status to Ajanaja in the state of release and bondage.

## \|\| 9.6-7 \|\|

*ajñānajāstu me devāḥ karmajebhyaḥ śatāvarāḥ \|*  
*ghṛtācī menakā raṃbhā urvaśī ca tilottamā \|\| 6 \|\|*

*suketuḥ śabarī caiva mañjughoṣā ca piṅgalā \|*  
*ityādikaṃ yakṣaratnaṃ saha saṃparikīrtitam \|\| 7 \|\|*

6-7. Ajanaja deities who are hundred percent inferior to Karmaja deities axe GhrtacI Menaka, Rambha. UrvasI, Tilottama Suketu, Tabari, Manjughosa, Pingala and other prominent Yaksas (Yaksanis).

## \|\| 9.8-15 \|\|

*ajānajasamā hyete karmajebhyaḥ śatāvarāḥ \|*  
*viśvāmitro vasiṣṭhaśca nāradaścyavanastathā \|\| 8 \|\|*

*utathyaśca muniścaitāndrājapitvā khageśvara \|*  
*ṛṣayaśca mahātmāno hyajānajasamāḥ smṛtāḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

*śatarciḥ kaśyapo jñeyo madhyamaśca parāśaraḥ \|*  
*pāvamānyaḥ pragāthaśca kṣudrasūktaśca devalaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

*gṛtsamado hyāsuriśca bharadvājotha mudgalaḥ \|*  
*uddālako hyṛ śṛṅgaḥ śaṅkhaḥ satyavratastathā \|\| 11 \|\|*

*suyajñaścaiva bābhravyo māṇḍūkaścaiva bāṣkalaḥ \|*  
*dharmācāryastathāgastyo dālbhyo dārḍhyacyutastathā \|\| 12 \|\|*

*kavaṣo haritaḥ kaṇvo virūpo musalastathā \|*  
*viṣṇuvṛddhaśca ātreyaḥ śrīvatso vatsaletyapi \|\| 13 \|\|*

*bhārgavaścāpnavānaśca māṇḍūkeyastathaiva \|*  
*maṇḍkaścaiva jābacaliḥ vītihavyastathaiva ca \|\| 14 \|\|*

*gṛtsamadaḥ śaunakaśca ityādyā ṛṣayaḥ smṛtāḥ \|*  
*eteṣāṃ śravaṇādeva hariḥ prīṇāti sarvadā \|\| 15 \|\|*

8-15. Equal to Ajanaja and hundred per cent inferior to Karmaja are Visvamitra, Vasistha, Narada, Cyavana and Utathya.

The noble sages equal to Ajaneya are Kasyapa, Parasara the middle, Pavamanya, Pragatha, Ksudra-sukta Devala, Grtsamada, Asuri, Bharadvaja, Mudgala, Uddalaka Rsyagrnga, Shankha, Satyavrata, Suyajna, Babhravya, Mandukya, Baskala, Dharmacarya, Agastya, Dalbhya, Dardhya Acyuta. Kavasa, Harita, Kanva, Virupa, Musala, Visnuvrddha,

Atreya, Sr!vatsya, Vatsala, Bhargava. Apnavana Mankukeya, Mandukya, Javali, Vitihavya. Grtsamada, баипака. The very names of these sages (who are his devotees) are pleasing to Hari.

## \|\| 9.16 \|\|

*bruve dvyaṣṭasahasraṃ ca śṛṇu tārkṣya mama striyaḥ \|*  
*agniputrāstu yaddvyaṣṭasahasrañca mama striyaḥ \|*  
*ajānajasamā hyetā (te) nātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. I shall tell you, О Tarkhya, listen. I have sixteen thousand wives who have borne to me sixteen thousand sons who are equal to

## \|\| 9.17 \|\|

*tvaṣṭuḥ putrī kaśerūśca tāsāṃ madhye guṇādhikā \|*  
*tadanantarajānvakṣye śṛṇu samyak khageśvara \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Kaseru, the daughter of Tvastr is senior to these in merit. О lord of lords, now I shall tell you about those who are junior to these in age.

## \|\| 9.18 \|\|

*ājānebhyastu pitaraḥ saptabhyonye śatāvarāḥ \|*  
*tathādhikā hi pitara iti vedavidāṃ matam \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Pitrs are hundred per cent junior to seven Ajanajas. Conversely, the Vedic scholars declare them ito be hundred per cent senior to Ajanajas.

## \|\| 9.19 \|\|

*tadanantarājānvakṣye śṛṇu tvaṃ dvijasattama \|*  
*aṣṭābhyo devagandharvā aṣṭottaraśataṃ vinā \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. О best of birds, listen, I shall tell you about those who are junior to Pitrs in age.

Of the eight there sprang Deva Gandharvas. They are less in number to the Pitrs by hundred and eight.

## \|\| 9.20 \|\|

*tebhyaḥ śataguṇānandā devapreṣyāstu mukhyataḥ \|*  
*svamukeneva devaiśca ājñāpyāḥ sarvadā gaṇāḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Thereafter were born Anandas who were hundred per cent superior to Gandharvas in virtue. They were principally the attendants of Devas, who received direct orders from them.

## \|\| 9.21 \|\|

*ākhyātā devagandharvāstebhyaste ca śatāvarāḥ \|*  
*tebhyastu kṣitipā jñeyā avarāśca śatairguṇaiḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. These are hundred per cent inferior to gams. And hundred percent inferior to Deva Gandharvas are the Ksitipas.

## \|\| 9.22 \|\|

*tebhyaḥ śataguṇājñeyā mānuṣeṣūttamā gaṇāḥ \|*  
*evaṃ prāsaṃgikānuktvā prakṛtaṃ hyanusarāmyaham \|*  
*evaṃ brahmādayo devā lakṣmyādyā api sarvaśaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Hundred per cent superior to these are Uttama ganas among mankind. Having so far made incidental remarks, I shall now take up the matter in hand.

## \|\| 9.23 \|\|

*stutvā tūṣṇīṃ sthitāḥ sarve prāñjalīkṛtya bho dvija \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Thus the deities Brahma, LaksmI, etc., completed their praise and stood in silence with their palms-joined in reverence.

## \|\| 9.24 \|\|

*iti stutaśca deveśo bhagavān hariravyayaḥ \|*  
*teṣāmāyatanaṃ dātuṃ manasā samacintayat \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Thus praised, Visnu, the eternal lord of gods, thought of granting shelter to his devotees.

## \|\| 9.25 \|\|

*idaṃ pavitramārogyaṃ puṇyaṃ pāpapraṇāśanam \|*  
*hariprasādajanakaṃ svarūpasukhasādhanam \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. This pious praise is pleasing to the lord. It is sacred as well as destroyer of sin's. It is the means of imparting bliss of self-realization.

## \|\| 9.26 \|\|

*idaṃ tu stavanaṃ viprā na paṭhantīha mānavāḥ \|*  
*na śṛṇvanti ca ye nityaṃ te sarve caiva māyinaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Those who do not read or hear this praise of the lord are hypocrites.

## \|\| 9.27 \|\|

*nasmarantontaraṃ nityaṃ ye bhuñjanti narādhamāḥ \|*  
*tairbhuktā satataṃ viṣṭhā sadā krimiśatairyutā \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. The wicked people who do not differentiate the lord from the deities and at the same time enjoy the pleasures of life eat nothing but faeces abounding in hundreds of worms.

## Chapter 10

## \|\| 10.1 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*devairevaṃ stuto viṣṇurbhagavānsāttvatāṃ patiḥ \|*  
*kīdṛśaṃ hyāśrayaṃ dattvaiṣāṃ viveśa mahāprabhuḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Garuda said: — Thus praised by the gods, the lord of the Satvatas disappeared after granting them the favour of his shelter.

## \|\| 10.2 \|\|

*etadveditumicchāmi kṛṣṇakṛṣṇa mahāprabho \|*  
*samyag brūhi dayālo tvaṃ yadi macchrotramarhati \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. О lord Krsna, I wish to know what sort of shelter the overlord granted to his devotees. О compassionate lord, please tell me truly if your lordship thinks I am fit to hear.

## \|\| 10.3 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*teṣu tattveṣu bhagavānsa viveśa mahāprabhuḥ \|*  
*kṣobhayāmāsa bhagavān saṃbandhavidhuro hariḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. The supreme lord Visnu entered the related tattvas which he set to motion, with the desire to create the universe.

## \|\| 10.4 \|\|

*adau sasarja bhagavān brahmāṇḍaṃ kana kātmakam \|*  
*pañcāśatkoṭivistīrṇaṃ yojanānāṃ samantataḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. In the beginning the lord created the primordial egg constituted of gold and which was spread over fifty crores of yojanas around.

## \|\| 10.5 \|\|

*tadūrdhvamaṇvavayavastāvānkanakarūpakaḥ \|*  
*vartate tata ūrdhvaṃ tu pañcāśatkoṭibhūtalam \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Above it, there is a minute particle of golden hue of the same dimension as the egg itself. Above that, there is the earth as wide as the fifty crow yojanas.

## \|\| 10.6 \|\|

*evaṃ koṭiśataṃ tasyāvayavaḥ parikīrtitaḥ \|*  
*tataśca saptāvaraṇaiḥ samatātparidhīkṛtam \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Thus the dimension of the egg extends over hundred crow yojanas. The egg isencompassed by seven enclosures all around.

## \|\| 10.7 \|\|

*kabandhāvaraṇaṃ hyādyaṃ koṭyā daśasahasrakam \|*  
*dvitīyā varaṇaṃ jñeyaṃ pāvakasya mahātmanaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. The first enclosure consists of water extending over ten thousand crows of yojanas. The second enclosure consists of fire.

## \|\| 10.8-9 \|\|

*apāṃ daśaguṇairyuktaṃ samantātparidhī (khī) kṛtam \|*  
*tṛtīyāvaraṇaṃ jñeyaṃ harasyaiva mahātmanaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

*daśādhikaṃ pāvakācca samantātparivāritam \|*  
*caturthāvaraṇaṃ jñeyaṃ nabhasopi mahāprabho \|\| 9 \|\|*

8-9. It is enclosed all round by waters. The third enclosure is called Нага. It is ten times more in dimension than the dimension of fire. The fourth enclosure consists of ether.

## \|\| 10.10 \|\|

*harāddaśaguṇairevaṃ samantātparivāritam \|*  
*pañcamāvaraṇaṃ jñeyamahaṅkārākhyamevaca \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. It is enclosed all round ten times more than ether. The fifth enclosure consists of ahankara (ego) ten times more than ether.

## \|\| 10.11 \|\|

*vyomno daśaguṇairevaṃ samantātparivāri tam \|*  
*ṣaṣṭhamāvaraṇaṃ proktaṃ mahattattvaṃ khageśvara \|\| 11 \|\|*
