11\. The sixth enclosure consists of what. It is enclosed all round ten times more than ether.

## \|\| 10.12 \|\|

*ahaṅkārāddaśaguṇaṃ samantātparivāritam \|*  
*saptamāvaraṇaṃ proktaṃ triguṇāvaraṇaṃ prabho \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. The seventh enclosure consists of three gunas — sattva, rajas and tamas.

## \|\| 10.13-16 \|\|

*mahattattvāddaśaguṇairadhikaṃ parikīrtitam \|*  
*mahattattvānantaraṃ ca tamo hyāvaraṇaṃ smṛtam \|\| 13 \|\|*

*mahattattvātpañcaguṇairadhikaṃ parikīrtitam \|*  
*tasmācca dviguṇaṃ jñeyaṃrajo hyāvaraṇaṃ smṛtam \|\| 14 \|\|*

*tataśca dviguṇaṃ jñeyaṃ sattvāvaraṇamuttamam \|*  
*trayaścaivaṃ militvā tu ekāvaraṇamīritam \|\| 15 \|\|*

*avyākṛtākhyamākāśaṃ tadanantaramīritam \|*  
*maryādārahitaścaivaṃ tatrāste hariravyayaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

13-16. It is enclosed all round ten times more than mahat. After mahat comes the enclosure tamas. It is enclosed all round, ten times more than mahat.

After mahat comes the enclosure tamas. It is five times more than mahat. Thereafter comes rajas which is two times more than tamas. Thereafter comes the enclosure sattva which is two times more than rajas. The three combined are called Ekavarana 'a single enclosing'.

The prime germ of nature 'ether' comes thereafter. It is limitless. The eternal Visnu abides therein.

## \|\| 10.17 \|\|

*aṣṭamāvaraṇaṃ vyomnoraṃ tarā virajā nadī \|*  
*pañcayojanavistīrṇā samantātparīdhīkṛtā \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. The river Viraja, forms the eighth enclosure. It flows in between the spaces of ether. It spreads over five yojanas all round

## \|\| 10.18 \|\|

*asti puṇyatamā jñeyā lokasaṃsāranāśinī \|*  
*evaṃ caturmukhenaiva tadā hṛṣyaṃ ti cāṇḍaja \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. It is the most sacred river that wards of rebirth. О bird, those who bathe herein go to Brahmaloka where they rejoice with the fourfaced Brahma.

## \|\| 10.19 \|\|

*te sarve virajānadyāṃ samyak snātvā visarjya ca \|*  
*liṅgadehaṃ tataḥ paścānmokṣaṃ vindanti te hareḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Those who take bath in the Viraja river abandon their subtle bodies and attain liberation.

## \|\| 10.20 \|\|

*aparokṣadṛśāmevaṃ brahmaṇā saha gāminām \|*  
*virajātaraṇaṃ viddhi nānyeṣāṃ vinatāsuta \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Those alone who have realized the Self and abide in Brahmana can cross the Viraja river.

## \|\| 10.21-23 \|\|

*aparokṣadṛśāṃ brahmanvyāsādīnāṃ khageśvara \|*  
*virajātaraṇaṃ nāsti bhoktavyatvācca karmaṇaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

*viriñcenaiva sākaṃ tu kalpesminnadhikāriṇām \|*  
*teṣāṃ tu niyamenaiva sarvaprārabdhasaṃkṣayaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

*bhavatyevaṃ na saṃdeho nānyeṣāṃ sarvasaṃkṣayaḥ \|*  
*atastu virajātaraṇaṃ teṣāmeva bhavetpaṭo \|\| 23 \|\|*

21-23. О lord of birds! The sage Vyasa and others, though they have realized the Self cannot cross the Viraja river, since they have still to reap the fruits of their karman.

They live on this earth as far as Brahma lives till the seeds of their karman are destroyed root and branch. They can then cross the river Viraja, О lord.

## \|\| 10.24 \|\|

*virajātaraṇaṃ nāsti teṣāṃ ta (tayosta)tsaṃgināṃ tathā \|*  
*sarvārabdhakṣayo nāsti yatasteṣāṃ khagādhipa \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Others who have the seeds of their karman still fructifying cannot cross Viraja.

## \|\| 10.25 \|\|

*ataśca sarvathā nāsti virajātaraṇaṃ prabho \|*  
*pralaye virajānadyā layo nāsti khageśvara \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. О lord of birds, the river Viraja does not disappear even at the time e of dissolution.

## \|\| 10.26-27 \|\|

*lakṣmyātmikā tu sā jñeyā liṅgadehavidāriṇī \|*  
*brahmatvayogyā ṛjavo nāma devāḥ prakīrtitāḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

*tepi pratyekaśaḥ saṃti hyanantāśca pṛthaggaṇāḥ \|*  
*pṛthakpṛthak ca taiḥ sākaṃ mokṣayogyāḥ khageśvara \|\| 27 \|\|*

26-27. The river Viraja is identical with Laksrm and it has the function of destroying the subtle bodies.

There are devas Rjus by name who are worthy of attaining Brahmana.

## \|\| 10.28 \|\|

*jīvāḥ saṃti hyaneke ca pratikalpe sṛjanti te \|*  
*dvātriṃśallakṣaṇaiḥ samyagyuktā vāyutvayogyakāḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. They are many in number being at par with the jivas who attain liberation individually or along with the Rjus.

These are characterized by thirtytwo marks and are capable of attaining the position of Vayu.

## \|\| 10.29 \|\|

*aṣṭāviṃśallakṣaṇaiśca girīśapadayoginaḥ \|*  
*caturviṃśatimārabhyāṣoḍaśācca surāḥ smṛtāḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Inferior to these are the yogins called Siva- Yogins who are characterized by twentyeight marks. In between the compass come Suras oho are characterized by sixteen to twenty-four marks.

## \|\| 10.30-31 \|\|

*aṣṭakā ṛṣayaḥ proktāstadūnāścakravartinaḥ \|*  
*śatajanma samārabhya brahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

*aparokṣamiti proktaṃ tathā hyārabdhasaṃkṣayaḥ \|*  
*ekena śatakalpena vāyutvaṃ yāti bho dvija \|\| 31 \|\|*

30-31. After Suras come Astakas. They are called sages. Inferior to these are Cakravartins. With in one hundred lives of god Brahma they can realize their Self and get 'their karman destroyed. О bird, within one hundred kalpas one becomes identical with Vayu.

## \|\| 10.32 \|\|

*śatajanmani brahmatvaṃ yāti paścāddhareḥ padam \|*  
*catvāriṃśadbrahmakalpaṃ samārabhya khageśvara \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Thereafter, having passed through himdred births, one becomes Brahma and thereafter one goes to the abode of Visnu.

## \|\| 10.33 \|\|

*rudrasyāpyāparokṣyaṃ syāttathā prārabdhasaṃkṣayaḥ \|*  
*ekacatvāriṃśakalpe śeṣatvaṃ yāti suvrata \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. О lord of birds, in the forty kalpas of Brahma after getting all karmans destroyed even Rudra can realize his Self.

## \|\| 10.34 \|\|

*brahmaṇā saha mokṣaṃ ca yāti samyaṅ na cānyathā \|*  
*kalpaviṃśatimārabhya brahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. О bird of good vows, after the expiry of forty-one kalpas one becomes Sesa and attains liberation becoming one with Brahman.

## \|\| 10.35 \|\|

*indrasyāpyāparokṣyaṃ syāttathā prārabdhasaṃkṣayaḥ \|*  
*brahmaṇaiva sahāyāti hariṃ nārāyaṇaṃ param \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Even Indra can get his karman destroyed, realize Self and attain with Brahma the highest region of Hari.

## \|\| 10.36-37 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*pañcāśītibrahmakalpaṃ samārabhya mahāprabho \|*  
*rudrasyāpyāparokṣyaṃ syāttathā prārabdhasaṃkṣayaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

*iti śrutaṃ mayā brahmanbrahmaṇoktaṃ hareḥ priyāt \|*  
*itthaṃ tvayoktaṃ śrīkṛṣṇa saṃśayobādhate mama \|\| 37 \|\|*

36-37. О lord, I have heard from Brahma that even Rudra can realize Brahmans when his karman is destroyed after completing the eightyfive kalpas of Brahma, then how can you adjust your statement that even Rudra can attain Brahma after completing the forty kalpas of Brahma'. О lord, how can the contrary statements be reconciled?

## \|\| 10.38 \|\|

*ato me saṃśayaṃ chindhi yathā na syāttathā punaḥ \|*  
*iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā kṛṣṇo vacanamabravīt \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. On hearing the words of Garuda the lord spoke again.

## \|\| 10.39 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*brahmoktasya mayoktasya vivādo nāsti sarvathā \|*  
*saṃdehastvajñadṛṣṭīnāṃ jñānināṃ nāsti sarvathā \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Shri Krsna said: — There is no contrarily between two statements. The stupid alone will cherish doubt, the wise have no doubts at all.

## \|\| 10.40 \|\|

*aśītihyaṣṭakā proktā aṣṭapañca khageśvara \|*  
*catvāriṃśadbrahmakalpa evaṃ prāha caturmukhaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. О lord of birds, astakas are eighty, with the additional thirteen they come to mnetythree in the fortieth Brahma kalpa the four-faced Brahma has so declared.

## \|\| 10.41 \|\|

*tattvānāṃ bahugopyatvāttathoktaṃ brahmaṇā purā \|*  
*abhiprāyastvevameva jñātavyo nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Since Reality is to be kept strictly secret, Brahma said so in olden days. О bird, you should understand this problem in the fight way.

## \|\| 10.42 \|\|

*pañcā śītibrahmakalpaṃ ye vijānanti bho dvija \|*  
*tendhaṃ tamaḥ praviśanti satyaṃsatyaṃ mayoditam \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. О bird, those who misinterpret the term Pancaslti Brahma-katpa as the eightyfive kalpas of Brahma are totally ignorant. I have told you the truth.

## \|\| 10.43-44 \|\|

*virajānantaraṃ vipraṃ tathā vyākṛtamaṃbaram \|*  
*anantapāraṃ tadapi lakṣmīstasyābhimāninī \|\| 43 \|\|*

*saṃkhyānugaṇanaṃ nāma yasya nāsti mahāprabho \|*  
*na dānaṃ jātiproktaṃ sarvadā nāsti saṃśayaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

43-44. After the Viraja river, О bird, comes the boundless ether presided over by LaksmI. The dimension of the ether cannot be measured by any count, О lord of birds.

## \|\| 10.45-46 \|\|

*aṇḍābhimānī brahmā tu virāḍākhyo hyabhūttadā \|*  
*evaṃ mataṃ sa nirmāya bhagavānharikhyayaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

*viśeṣāttatra garuḍa devaistattvābhimānibhiḥ \|*  
*adhaścordhvaṃ tadākramya haristiṣṭhati sarvadā \|\| 46 \|\|*

45-46. The Virat Brahma, was made the presiding deity of the universal egg.

Thus the everlasting, eternal Hari made special arrangements by appointing the presiding deities over the different principles. The lord Visnu stood encompassing all regions above and below.

## \|\| 10.47 \|\|

*evaṃ prākṛtasargoktirvaikṛtaṃ śṛṇu pakṣirāṭ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. О lord of birds, the above account refers to the primary creation. Now, I shall tell you abbutthe secondary creation.

## \|\| 10.48 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*sṛṣṭiruktā tvayā pūrvaṃ śrutā samyaṅ mayā hare \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Garuda said: — Olord, you have already spoken on creation and I have heard of the same very attentively.

## \|\| 10.49 \|\|

*kiṃ nāma prākṛtaṃ jñeyaṃ tathā kiṃ vaikṛtaṃ prabho \|*  
*etadvistīrya me brūhi śrotuṃ kautūhalaṃ hi me \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. О lord, now tell me in detail, what is the primary and what is the secondary creation. I am curious to hear about the same from you.

## \|\| 10.50 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*avyaktādyāḥ pṛthivyantā aṇḍācca bahirudbhavāḥ \|*  
*te sarve prākṛtāḥ proktāsteṣāṃ jñānādvimacyate \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Sri Krsna said: — From the unmanifest Prakrti to the gross elements the evolutes of Prakrti are primary. The knowledge of the same leads one to liberation.

## \|\| 10.51 \|\|

*brahmāḍaṃ vikṛtaṃ jñeyaṃ brahmāṇḍāntaḥ khageśvara \|*  
*yā sṛṣṭirucyate sadbhiḥ saivoktā vikṛteti ca \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. О lord of birds, the universe evolved out of the Cosmic Egg is secondary creation.

## \|\| 10.52-53 \|\|

*sṛṣṭiśca pralayaścaiva saṃsāro bhaktireva ca \|*  
*devatā ṛṣimukhyāśca lokā bhūrādayastathā \|\| 52 \|\|*

*anādyanantakālīnāḥ sarvadaikaprakārakāḥ \|*  
*jagatpravāhaḥ satyo 'yaṃ naiva mithyā kathañcana \|\| 53 \|\|*

52-53. Creation, dissolution, recreation and release, deities, major sages and regions Bhu, Bhuvah, Seat are eternal and unchangeable. The existence of the universe is actual and not a fiction.

## \|\| 10.54 \|\|

*yattvetadanyathā brūyuḥ sarvahantāra eva te \|*  
*jagatpravāhaḥ satyo 'yaṃ harisevetisāthā \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. Those who speak otherwise are the slayers of truth. The course of the universe is true, О lord, the service of the lord is also true.

## \|\| 10.55 \|\|

*satyaṃ satyaṃ punaḥ satyamuddhatya bhujamutyate \|*  
*vedācchāstraṃ paraṃ nāsti na devaḥ keśavātparaḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Raising up my arm I declare that no scripture is superior to the Vedas and no deity is superior to Kesava.

## \|\| 10.56 \|\|

*sarvotkṛṣṭaṃ keśavaṃ ca vihāyānyamupāsate \|*  
*teṣāmandhaṃ tamo jñeyaṃ pitṝṇāṃ garuṇāmapi \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. Those who worship other deities, may they be Pitrs or preceptors, at the cost of lord Visnu go to the darkest regions.

## \|\| 10.57 \|\|

*idānīṃ śṛṇu pakṣīndra vaikṛtaṃ sargamuttamam \|*  
*samyagjānāti yo loke sa yāti paramaṃ padam \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. О lord of birds, now hear about the secondary creation. He who understands the same, attains the highest region.

## Chapter 11

## \|\| 11.1-2 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*puruṣākhyo hariḥ sākṣādbhagavānpuruṣottamaḥ \|*  
*śiśyetvaṇḍodaka viṣṇurnṛṇāṃ sāhasravatsaram \|*  
*lakṣmīścodakarūpeṇa śayyārūpeṇa bhoṇḍajā \|\| 1 \|\|*

*vidyā taraṅgarūpeṇa vāyurūpeṇa bhoṇḍaja \|\| 2 \|\|*

1-2. Shri Krsna said: — Lord Visnu, the primeval Being, slept in the Cosmic waters for full one thousand years. Laksmi served as a couch of water, О bird, and Vidya as the air propelling water into waves.

## \|\| 11.3 \|\|

*tamorūpeṇa saivāsī nnānyadāsītkathañcana \|*  
*asīdgarbhodake caiva nānyadāsītkathañcana \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. The same Goddess pervaded the embryonic waters in the form of darkness (tamas). In the embryonic waters there was nothing else

## \|\| 11.4-5 \|\|

*lakṣmīstuṣṭāva ca hariṃ garbhode pakṣisattama \|*  
*lakṣmīdharābhyāṃ rūpābhyāṃ prakṛtirhariṇā tathā \|\| 4 \|\|*

*śeta śrutisvarūpeṇa stauti garbhodake harim \|*  
*nārāyaṇa namaste 'stu śṛṇu vijñāpanaṃ mama \|\| 5 \|\|*

4-5. О best of birds, Laksmi praised Hari in the embryonic waters. Prakrti assumed the forms of Laksmi and Prthivi. She slept with Hari and praised the lord in the Cosmic waters with the mantras: О god Narayana, I offer my homage to you. Please attend to my entreaties

## \|\| 11.6 \|\|

*ajāṃ jahi mahābhāga yogyānāṃ muktimāvaha \|*  
*ajā tu prakṛtiḥ proktā cāparā prakṛtiḥ parā \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. О blessed one, please remove my illusion. Confer liberation to the worthy. The primordial Prakrti is twofold: Para and Apara.

## \|\| 11.7 \|\|

*śatatovarā tu brahmāṇī brahmapatnī varānanā \|*  
*umā śacyavarā tasyā avarāḥ saṃprakīrtitāḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Inferior to Prakrti is Brahmans. The fairfaced Uma is inferior to Brahmans. &acl, the consort of Indra, is inferior to her. The three Prakrti, the primordial nature.

## \|\| 11.8 \|\|

*etāsāṃ hananaṃ naiva prārthayāmaḥ sadā hare \|*  
*asti pratinṛṣu brahma prakṛtī dve vyavasthite \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. О Hari, we do not beg of you for the removal of the three. In every man there exist two Prakrtis.

## \|\| 11.9-10 \|\|

*ekā tu nityasaṃsārā tvajaśabdābhidhāyikā \|*  
*dvitīyā tu tamo 'pohyā ajaśabdābhidhāyikā \|\| 9 \|\|*

*ata eva tvaje jyeṣṭhe iti loke prakīrtite \|*  
*sukhaduḥ khapradā caiva aparā duḥ khadaiva tu \|\| 10 \|\|*

9-10. One is eternal and unborn, the other is wrapped up by Tamas. The two are popularly known as Ajas, the elder and Aja the younger. One awards pleasure and pain, the other awards only pain.

## \|\| 11.11 \|\|

*mokṣādhikāriṇāmeva jñānaiśvaryādayo gaṇāḥ \|*  
*teṣāmācchādikā hokā tamoṅgā sā prakīrtitā \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Those worthy of liberation are blessed to share knowledge and supremacy. Prakrti that wards off knowledge and supremacy is named dark-complexioned.

## \|\| 11.12 \|\|

*jīvaṃ prati mahāviṣṇuṃ pāhyācchādayati prabho \|*  
*sā parā prakṛtirjñeyā paramācchādikā smṛtā \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. The Apara Prakrti is Paramacchadika. since it covers "the Self from the vision of the Supreme Self.

## \|\| 11.13 \|\|

*evaṃ sā paramā duṣṭā hokā tamoṅgā tu prakīrtitā \|*  
*jīvavargeṣveva khaga brahmādernāsti sā kvacit \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Such a wretched tamasic Prakrti abides in human beings, О bird, she does not abide in Brahmana.

## \|\| 11.14 \|\|

*piśācavatsamuddiṣṭā jīvasyetyadhikāriṇaḥ \|*  
*prerikā tu tayordevyo stvahamādyā sukhātmikā \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. She assumes the role of a Pisacika for the individual struggling for release. I am the propeller of the two (Para and Apara).

## \|\| 11.15 \|\|

*tatra viṣṇo mahābhāga saguṇācchādako hitaḥ \|*  
*paramācchādikāṃ duṣṭāṃ vyāmucyaiva mahāprabho \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. О lord Visnu О blissful one, that which envelops the gunas is the most beneficial.

## \|\| 11.16 \|\|

*mokṣaṃ dehi mahādeva tvadbhaktānāṃ mahāprabho \|*  
*paramācchādikā hyasmānnitya saṃsāriṇo yataḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. О sovereign lord, О supreme god, you confer liberation to your devotees after deserting the wicked Prakrti that envelops the universe all round. She envelops all of us who are ever transmigrating.

## \|\| 11.17 \|\|

*ata eva ca nityatvāttasmāttadapasāraṇam \|*  
*kuru deva mahābhāga iti vijñāyatāṃ mama \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Since she is eternal, her removal becomes a necessity. О auspicious lord, accept my entreaties, drive her away from me.

## \|\| 11.18 \|\|

*evaṃ stuto hariḥ kṛṣṇo suprabuddho 'pi sarbadā \|*  
*udvuddhavanmahā viṣṇurabhūdajñaparīkṣayā \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Lord Hari, though always wakeful, plays the role of one who has just wakened from sleep. It is just to test the ignorant.

## \|\| 11.19 \|\|

*tasya nābherabhūtpadmaṃ sauvarṇaṃ bhuvanāśrayam \|*  
*tatprākṛtaṃ ca vijñeyaṃ bhūdevī tvabhimāninī \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Out of his navel there grew up a golden lotus which became the substratum of the world. The lotus was constituted of matter, of which the earth was the presiding deity.

## \|\| 11.20 \|\|

*anantasūryavaccaiva prakāśakaramīritam \|*  
*cidānandamayo viṣṇustasmādbhinno na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. It was as illuminating as the eternal solar deity. Visnu whose nature is consciousness and bliss was surely distinct from it.

## \|\| 11.21 \|\|

*viṣṇoḥ svarūpabhūtaṃ ca ye vijānanti te narāḥ \|*  
*te yānti hyadharaṃ lokaṃ tathā tatsaṃgisaṃginaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Those who think that Visnu is identical with Prakrti go to inferior regions, as also those who are associated with these.

## \|\| 11.22-23 \|\|

*kirīṭādikavaccaiva jñātavyaṃ ca khageśvara \|*  
*kirīṭādyā api harerdvidhā saṃti na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

*svarūpā hyasvarūpāśca svasvarūpani darśane \|*  
*gṛhītā iti vijñeyā na svarūpāḥ khageśvara \|\| 23 \|\|*

22-23. О lord of birds, the phenomenon can be explained by the analogy of a diadem.

Diadems are twofold: identical and nonidentical. As a matter of illustration they are assumed to be identical. Intact, they are not identical, О lord of birds.

## \|\| 11.24 \|\|

*bhmāṇḍaṃ hyasṛjattatra sarvalokavidhāyakam \|*  
*pralaye muktihīnaśca supta ityucyate budhaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. He created the universe, divided into several regions and inhabited the people therein. During the period of dissolution the lord alone exists taking rest which the learned declare to be a sort of sleep.

## \|\| 11.25 \|\|

*tasya samivastrivaṃ ca na jñātavyā khageśvara \|*  
*pralayopi mahābhāga brahmavāyvorna cāsti hi \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. О lord of birds, with the dissolution of the Universe the lord is not effected. Brahma and Vayu do not perish ever.

## \|\| 11.26 \|\|

*vṛttirūpaṃ paraṃ jñānaṃ pādyārghyaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*indriyāṇāmuparatiḥ suptirityucyate budhaiḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Knowledge of the Supreme lord is circumscribed by condition as the water for washing the feet. When the organs of senses stop to work it is called 'sleep'.

## \|\| 11.27 \|\|

*brahmavāyvośca pāsagni vāstavaṃ syātkhageśvara \|*  
*kathaṃ tarhi tayorvarte hyavilatyatvamucyate \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. О lord of birds, Brahma and Vayu are elernal. How can it be said that they are not dissolved at dissolution?

## \|\| 11.28 \|\|

*tasmāttadvāstavaṃ nāsti brahmavāyvoḥ khageśvara \|*  
*svapnāvasthāyāḥ sadṛśī hyavasthā suptisaṃjñikā \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. О lord of birds, Brahma and Vayu are not the real entities. The sub-conscious state Supti is similar to another subconscious state named dream (svapna).

## \|\| 11.29-30 \|\|

*brahmaṇyamukhyayā vṛttyā hyastītyevaṃ nibodha me \|*  
*ato na vāstavamidamaṅgīkāryaṃ khageśvara \|\| 29 \|\|*

*vāstavaṃ ye vijānanti teṣāṃ nityaṃ dhanaṃ tapaḥ \|*  
*śrīgaruḍa uvāca \|*  
*suptistvajñānakāryatvātsuptirnāstītyudīritā \|\| 30 \|\|*

29-30. This state is metaphorically applied to Brahma. Hence, О lord of birds, the existence of Brahma, and Vayu should not be taken for granted. Those who realize this fact are for ever blessed with wealth as the merit of penance.

## \|\| 11.31-32 \|\|

*yadā hi kāraṇaṃ cāsti tadā kāryamiti prabho \|*  
*ityabhiprāyagarbheṇa tvaṃ samādhāsya te yadi \|\| 31 \|\|*

*tarhi tasya mahābhāga kathaṃ brūhi bhayādikam \|*  
*bhayādikaṃ hyastu nāma kā vāsmākaṃ kṣatirbhavet \|\| 32 \|\|*

Garuda said: — The notion of Supti is caused by ignorance. Supti is not actual.

31-32. О lord, if there is a cause, there can be effect.

There can be no effect without a cause. It you base your arguments on this statement, then О blessed one, there being no cause how could Brahma suffer from fear.

Or assuming that Brahma suffers from fear, can we lose anything from this assumption.

## \|\| 11.33 \|\|

*evamuktastu govindobravīttatrāpi kāraṇam \|*  
*bhayaṃ tvajñānakāryaṃ syātkāryākāraṇamatra hi \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Thus addressed, the lord said in reply. Fear is caused by ignorance. Thus the causal theory becomes applicable in regard to Brahma.

## \|\| 11.34-35 \|\|

*prīyate matvā brahma tasmātsuptiśca tatra hi \|*  
*ajñādikaṃ yadi brahma tasya na syātkathañcana \|\| 34 \|\|*

*kathaṃ sukhī pradṛśyeta na kathañcitkariṣyati \|*  
*kathaṃ vā muktiparyantaṃ jñānavyaktirvadasva me \|\| 35 \|\|*

34-35. If we do not admit ignorance to be the cause of fear or pleasure then how could there ever be a feeling of fear or pleasure.

Hence, whatever feeling of fear or pleasure inspires the devotee is caused by ignorance.

## \|\| 11.36 \|\|

*yadyajñānaṃ tasya satyaṃ na syāttarhi mahāprabho \|*  
*atyādarātkathaṃ brahmañchravaṇaṃ kurute vada \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. If fear or pleasure are caused by ignorance, then there should be no actual fear or pleasure, both being the results of ignorance О lord, then how is it that the seeker of the truth is extremely devoted to the lord

## \|\| 11.37 \|\|

*iti tasya vacaḥ śrutvā kṛṣṇo vacanamabravīt \|*  
*bhayaṃ ca vāstavaṃ tasya na jānīhi mahāmate \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. On hearing the words of Garuda, lord Krsna said in reply. О wise one, the fear is assumed, it is not actual.

## \|\| 11.38 \|\|

*dṛśyate madbhayaṃ tasya hariprītyarthameva ca \|*  
*bhayākāmavatīvānamuvāstavamīritam \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. It is assumed just for the pleasure of the lord. Fear etc. are, in fact, not real.

## \|\| 11.39 \|\|

*prāptaprābdhaleśasta tasya nāsti khageśvara \|*  
*duḥ khājñānādikaṃ kiñcitkathaṃ tasmin bhaviṣyati \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. For him who has attained the fruits of his activities no sorrow is caused by ignorance. How can there be a sorrow in the lord?

## \|\| 11.40 \|\|

*viṣṇorājñānusāreṇa bhayāyānukarotyasau \|*  
*tena prīṇāti ca haristasya nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. By the command of the lord the devotee assumes fear. The lord is pleased thereby. Intact, the fear never enters in him.

## \|\| 11.41 \|\|

*śṛṇoti satataṃ brahmā na cintyāttāvatājñatā \|*  
*kadāciddṛśyate brahmā duḥ khī na ca khageśvara \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Brahma observes all this. But this does not prove that ignorance (as a cause) is existent in Brahma. О lord of birds, Brahma is never found to be sorrowful.

## \|\| 11.42 \|\|

*yadbrahma ca na jānīyāddhariprītyarthameva ca \|*  
*duḥ khivaddṛśyate brahmā ājñānāṃ mohanāya ca \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. If he makes no attempt to realize Brahma and obtain pleasure thereby, Brahma appears to be distressed just for deluding the ignorant.

## \|\| 11.43 \|\|

*yogyatāmanatikramya yāvajjñānaṃ ca tiṣṭhati \|*  
*brahmaṇastāvadevāsti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. The knowledge of Brahma comes to the seeker according to his power of receptivity.

## \|\| 11.44 \|\|

*jñānasya vyaktatā nāma vidyamānasya cādarāt \|*  
*jñānasyāsādanaṃ caiva jñānavyaktiriti smṛtā \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. The manifestation of the unmanifest knowledge and the acquirement thereof is called the visibility of knowledge (Jnana).

## \|\| 11.45 \|\|

*ato jñānādikaṃ nāsti brahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ \|*  
*padmāddhiraṇma yājjāto brahmā tu caturānanaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Hence, the supreme lord being knowledge itself there is no scope of ignorance. The fourfaced Brahma was evolved out of a golden lotus from the navel of Visnu.

## \|\| 11.46-47 \|\|

*sarvadā'locanāyuktastena svālocanaṃ kṛtam \|*  
*ajñānāṃ mohanārthāya hariprītyarthameva ca \|\| 46 \|\|*

*saṃkalpopi tathaivāsti na hyajñānātkṛtastathā \|*  
*ko vā māṃ sṛṣṭavānatra iti hyālocya sa prabhuḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

46-47. He was endowed with the power of reflection. He reflected to himself: "Will or desire has been created, for deluding the ignorance and propitiating the lord, out of knowledge itself, not from ignorance. Lord

Brahma queried himself: "Who has created me."

## \|\| 11.48 \|\|

*taṃ vicārayituṃ brahmā padmanāḍīṃ viveśa ha \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. For an answer to this query Brahma entered the lotus-stalk.

## Chapter 12

## \|\| 12.1 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*nāḍīṃ samāviśya mahānubhāvaḥ śrīviṣṇubhakto tvatha puṣkarasthaḥ \|*  
*vicārayāmāsa guruṃ svamūlaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ nirguṇamadvitīyam \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Shri Krsna said: — The noble Brahma, the devotee of Visnu who was seated on the lotus entered the lotus-stalk.

He meditated upon lord Visnu who though non-dual and qualityless is the cause of his origin.

## \|\| 12.2 \|\|

*etāvatā haribhaktasya tasyāpyacchinnabhaktasya caturmukhasya \|*  
*vicārakāle ca vicintanīyo hyajñānaleśastu khageśvareśvara \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Brahma, the perpetual devotee of Hari, О lord of birds, was swayed by ignorance when he meditated upon the source of his birth.

## \|\| 12.3 \|\|

*yathāsti viṣṇormanaḥ saṅkalpa eva tathaiva sopi prakaroti nityam \|*  
*ālocane tasya sadāsti bhūmansvayogyatāmanatikramya caiva \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. As the mind of Visnu is constituted of nothing but desire, to his utmost he indulges in self-reflection.

## \|\| 12.4 \|\|

*hareḥ svarūpe ca tathā prapañcaḥ svasminsvarūpe ca khagasti jñānam \|*  
*yathāpi nityaṃ paricāravāri ca ajñātavaddṛśyate viṣṇunā ca \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. In the form of Hari, О lord, there exit both, simultaneously, illusion and knowledge. But the two remain invisible like moving water in the clouds.

## \|\| 12.5-6 \|\|

*hareḥ prītyarthaṃ kurute sau kadāccittatrāpi kaścidviśeṣo 'sti vīndra \|*  
*śṛṇuṣva samyaṅnigṛhītacitto yathā provāca sa vijānāti devaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

*sadā tvadoṣaṃ praviśeṣeśca muktavedāstathā vā pavijānanti nityam \|*  
*tasya svarūpaṃ na tathā hariṃ ca svayogyatāmanatikramya vedhāḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

5-6. Sometimes, he manifests both, there being a distinct purpose for this manifestation.

Now, hear attentively how the lord himself declared his holy form, devoid of differences as it is known to the Vedas, but which Brahma with his limited capacity is not capable of knowing, as he (Brahma) does not realize his form to be identical with Visnu.

О lord of birds, there is no ignorance in this conception.

## \|\| 12.7-8 \|\|

*hareḥ svarūpaṃ na vijānāti sarvaṃ svayo gyarūpaṃ sarvadā vetti viṣṇoḥ \|*  
*tatrājñānaṃ nāsti kiñciddvijendra yāvatsvarūpaṃ ca tathaiva lakṣmyāḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

*vedhā na jānāti kutastadanye tayoḥ svarūpaṃna vijānāti sarvam \|*  
*tathāpi vedānekadeśena veda jānāti lakṣmīrharirūpaṃ ca yāvat \|\| 8 \|\|*

7-8. Similarly, Brahma is incapable of knowing the form of LaksmL Not to speak of others, О lord of birds, Brahma does not know the form of both Visnu and LaksmL The Vedas know the form of Visnu in entirety. So does LaksmL

## \|\| 12.9 \|\|

*tāvanna jānāti vidhiḥ khagendra jñāne vidhātuśca svayogyabhūte \|*  
*ato viriñcasya na cintanīyo hyajñānaleśaḥ kvāpi deśe ca kāle \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. О lord of birds, since Brahma with his circumscribed knowledge does not know the form of Visnu he is charged with the fault of eternal ignorance not conditioned by time or place.

## \|\| 12.10 \|\|

*nāḍīṃ samāviśya tadā viriñco na veda nārāyaṇamekavacca \|*  
*tadā śṛṇottaṃ kamalāsanaṃ prabhustapastapa dvyakṣaraṃ sādareṇa \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Brahma entered the lotus-stalk but he could not visualize Visnu, the source of his being. Thereafter, Brahma heard from the lotus-stalk a voice containing two syllables О Brahman, perform tapa — penance.

## \|\| 12.11 \|\|

*abhiprāyaṃ tasya samyagvidittvā tapaḥ kuru tvaṃ harituṣṭyarthameva \|*  
*tapo 'karoddharipādaika niṣṭho hareḥ prītyarthaṃ divyasahasravarṣam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Recognizing clearly that it was the voice of the lord enjoining penance, he practiced penance at the feet of the lord, just for his pleasure.

## \|\| 12.12 \|\|

*tato hariḥ prādurāsītkhagendra varaṃ dātuṃ bhaktavarasya divyam \|*  
*sadā viṣṇuṃ devadevo dadarśa caturbhujaṃ taṃ jalajāyatākṣam \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. О lord of birds, then lord Visnu manifested himself to confer favour to his best devotees.

Then the great god Brahma looked at the four-armed Lord Visnu with eyes resembling full blown lotus.

## \|\| 12.13 \|\|

*śrīvatsalakṣmaṃ galaśobhikaustubhaṃ saṃpaśyantaṃ suprasannārdradṛṣṭyā \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā hariṃ brahma nārāyaṇaṃ ca puraḥ sthitaṃ bhaktavaśyaṃ dayālum \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. The lord wore Shrivatsa gem on his chest and the Kaustubha round his neck. He looked with the most agreeable and affectionate glance.

## \|\| 12.14 \|\|

*samarcayāmāsa mahāvibhūtyā bhaktyā hareḥ pādatīrthaṃ dadhāra \|*  
*astaunmahābhāgavatapradhāno hariṃ guruṃ bhaktivivardhitātmā \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. At the sight of compassionate Visnu who is fascinated by his devotees, Brahma knelt and worshipped him with devotion. With his soul engrossed in meditation he, the eminent devotee, lauded Hari thus.

## \|\| 12.15 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*rameśa lokeśa jagannivāsa tava svarūpaṃ na vijānāti devī \|*  
*tava prasādātsuvijānāti devī guṇānvedoktānsarvadā vīndra sarvān \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. О lord of LaksmI, О lord of the world, abode of the people, goddess LaksmI does not know your real form. By your grace, she understands your qualities which are known to the Vedas.

## \|\| 12.16 \|\|

*tathāpi sā na vijānāti devī sākalyenāśeṣitaḥ sadguṇāṃśca \|*  
*yadyapyanuktaṃ pañcabhirnāsti vedaistathāpi deve 'tra viśeṣa āste \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Still Laksrtti does not know your traits, in entirety. Nothing remains which the Vedas have not covered up. There are yet some distinct traits which are not expressed by the Vedas.

## \|\| 12.17 \|\|

*tattvecchavaḥ pravijānanti nityaṃ vede sūktānkvāpyanuktāṃśca sarvān \|*  
*ādau jānantyatra vedā murāre ṛgādayaḥ suṣṭhu catvāra eva \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Some special traits are declared in the Vedas which are known only to the aspirants. О lord, at first, the four Vedas — Rg, Yajus, Saman and Atharvan — know them rightly.

## \|\| 12.18 \|\|

*vedā hyete vedayantīti deva tathā purāṇaṃ bhārataṃ pañcarātram \|*  
*kramādito vicintya sā viṣṇuguṇānsvayogyānsadā vijānāti ramāpi devī \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. The Vedas are so called because they know the lord. So do the Puranas, Mahabharata and Pancaratra texts. Goddess LaksmI too knows of the traits of Visnu as large in number as they come within her knowledge.

## \|\| 12.19 \|\|

*viśeṣato hyuktaguṇā nṛgādiṣu svayogyabhūtānsaṃvijānāti devī \|*  
*sāmānyataḥ pravijānāti devī harerguṇānna viśeṣācca nityam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. She knows the distinct qualities of the lord which are mentioned in the Vedas, as far as her capacity permits. She knows the traits in general but not in particular.

## \|\| 12.20 \|\|

*ahaṃ vijānāmi ramāpra sādāttava prasādācca guṇānsadaiva \|*  
*svayogyabhūtāñchrutipūktān guṇāṃśca kāṃścidvijānāti harerna kaścit \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. I too know certain traits declared in the Vedas as far as my capacity, by the favour of LaksmI as well as yours.

## \|\| 12.21 \|\|

*tava prasādācca mama prasādātkālāntare tāṃśca jānāti śeṣaḥ \|*  
*duṣkarmaleśānna tirohitān guṇānyāneva pūrvaṃ viditān svayogyān \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. There are still certain traits which remain unknown to many. But Shesa, the Serpent-chief knows them too. These remain hidden to others due to their bad Karman.

## \|\| 12.22 \|\|

*tāneva jñātvā punareva śeṣastiro hitāṃllabdhaguṇastataḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*sadā svayogyāṃśca harerguṇāṃśca umāpatiścāpi tava prasādāt \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Sesa knows those traits which are known to many as far as he is able to know. Thereafter, he knows those too, which are hidden to others. He is, therefore, called the one who has the knowledge of all traits of Visnu.

## \|\| 12.23 \|\|

*yadā vijānāti hare murāre aprāptalabdheti tadocyate haraḥ \|*  
*mamāpi lokaṃ ca yadā murāre tadā vijānāti tava svarūpam \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. The lord of Uma, Нага, knows, by your favour, the traits of the lord as far as his capacity. О lord, therefore, he is called the one who has obtained what is not accessible to others. О lord, when he knows my regions he knows your form as well.

## \|\| 12.24 \|\|

*govinda nityāvyaya citsupūrṇa tava prasādānnāsti śateṣu tanmamayeye hi devāśca śarīradhāriṇaste jñānahīnā viṣayeṣu niṣṭhāḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. О lord, you are eternal, indestructible super consciousness. I cannot make a better choice among many.

The gods who incarnate on earth are devoid of knowledge, absorbed in the objects of senses.

## \|\| 12.25 \|\|

*yeye devā viṣayeṣu niṣṭhāstete devā bahirarthabhāvāḥ \|*  
*yeye devā bahirarthabhāvā mokṣā danye pralapantaḥ sadaiva \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. The gods who are absorbed in the objects of senses are attached to outward pleasures. The gods who are attached to sexual pleasures talk in vain of liberation.

## \|\| 12.26 \|\|

*tava svarūpe ca jagatsvarūpe tavāsamānaṃ nāsti viṣṇo sadaiva \|*  
*yatastava prākṛto nāsti deho yato jñānaṃ nāsti nāstye va nityam \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. О lord, there is no distinction between your nature and that of the lord. Since you possess no material body there cannot be eternal ignorance associated with you.

## \|\| 12.27 \|\|

*pūrṇānandajñānadeho 'pi nityaṃ sadā śarīrī bhāṣyate bhaktimadbhiḥ \|*  
*yatastava prākṛto nāsti deho hyatopi nityamaśarīrīti ca smṛtaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. You possess a body eternally constituted of bliss and knowledge. Still the devotees regard, you have a physical body. Since you have no physical body, their calling you a bodiless one should only be correct.

## \|\| 12.28 \|\|

*nato 'haṃ sarvadāsmiñśarīre 'haṃmametyabhimānena śūnyaḥ \|*  
*atto 'pyahaṃ tvaśarīrī sadaiva tathaiva nityaṃ bahirarthaiśca śūnyaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Му homage to you, О bodiless one who are devoid of Ego or attachment. I too am devoid of physical body and unattached to outward objects.

## \|\| 12.29-39 \|\|

*svabhogabhāryāsatyalokādibhogaḥ svayogyabhogo vastramālyādibhogaḥ \|*  
*ete hi sarve bahirarthasaṃjñakāḥ naisargakāmāḥ sarvadā me hi viṣṇo \|\| 29 \|\|*

*tathāpyahaṃ kāmahīno hi nityaṃ rudrādayaḥ kāmavanto yatotaḥ \|*  
*śarīriṇaste bahirarthabhāvā ajñānavanto 'pi ca saṃsmṛtāḥ khaga \|\| 30 \|\|*

*svadārabhoge kevalāṃ prītimevaṃ harerevaṃ sarvadāhaṃ karoti \|*  
*stambāstrādīndhāriyiṣye sadaiva viṣṇoḥ prītyarthaṃ naiva gātrārthameva \|\| 31 \|\|*

*nityānandādanyakāmo na mesti ataḥ sadā bahirarthaiśca śūnyaḥ \|*  
*mamāpi bhāryā bahirarthaśūnyā amūḍhabhāvā mūḍhavatīva dṛśyate \|\| 32 \|\|*

*amūḍhabhūtā jñānināṃ sarvadaiva tathājñānāṃ jñānahīneti bhāti \|*  
*yāvajjñānaṃ cāsti me vāstudeva tāvajjñānaṃ vāsudevasya cāsti \|\| 33 \|\|*

*yāvajjñānaṃ vāsudevasya cāsti tāvajjñānaṃ jñānavatāmṛjūnām \|*  
*krameṇaivājñānināṃ vānṛjūnāmaspaṣṭarūpo jñānagato viśeṣaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

*sauriprakāśe ca yathaiva darśanaṃ tathā mama jñānagato viśeṣaḥ \|*  
*dīpaprakāśe ca yathaiva darśanaṃ tathā jñānaṃ vāsudevasya cāsti \|\| 35 \|\|*

*aspaṣṭaparūpā nyūnatā hyasti vāyau tathā jñānaṃ naiva saṃcintanīyam \|*  
*etādṛśī jñānaśaktirmurārervāyvādīnāṃ mokṣaparyantamasti \|\| 36 \|\|*

*jñānaṃ tvṛjūnāṃ mokṣakāle pipañcavāyvādīnāṃ pralayenādrādīrna \|*  
*vāyormama pralaye sṛṣṭikāle tathā gāyatryā nāstināstyeva mohaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

*gāyatrīvadbhāratyā devadeva jñātavyamevaṃ haritattvavedibhiḥ \|*  
*mamājñānaṃ dṛśyate yatra kutra daityādīnāṃ mohanārtha sadaiva \|\| 38 \|\|*

*tena prītirdevadevasya viṣṇarbhaviṣyatītyeva viniścitātmā \|*  
*praśrādikaṃ tvajñavatsarvadaivaṃ kariṣyehaṃ mohanāyādhamānām \|\| 39 \|\|*

29-39. О lord, the objects of enjoymentwoman, heaven, silken clothes, garlandscome within the range of my material desires, still I am devoid of any material desire.

Rudra, etc., invested with physical bodies and attached to outward objects are possessed of ignorance, О bird.

## \|\| 12.31 \|\|

*svadārabhoge kevalāṃ prītimevaṃ harerevaṃ sarvadāhaṃ karoti \|*  
*stambāstrādīndhāriyiṣye sadaiva viṣṇoḥ prītyarthaṃ naiva gātrārthameva \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. О lord, I become attached to women. I wear silken clothes and put on garlands, just for the propitiation of lord Visnu, not for my physical enjoyment.

## \|\| 12.32-36 \|\|

*nityānandādanyakāmo na mesti ataḥ sadā bahirarthaiśca śūnyaḥ \|*  
*mamāpi bhāryā bahirarthaśūnyā amūḍhabhāvā mūḍhavatīva dṛśyate \|\| 32 \|\|*

*amūḍhabhūtā jñānināṃ sarvadaiva tathājñānāṃ jñānahīneti bhāti \|*  
*yāvajjñānaṃ cāsti me vāstudeva tāvajjñānaṃ vāsudevasya cāsti \|\| 33 \|\|*

*yāvajjñānaṃ vāsudevasya cāsti tāvajjñānaṃ jñānavatāmṛjūnām \|*  
*krameṇaivājñānināṃ vānṛjūnāmaspaṣṭarūpo jñānagato viśeṣaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

*sauriprakāśe ca yathaiva darśanaṃ tathā mama jñānagato viśeṣaḥ \|*  
*dīpaprakāśe ca yathaiva darśanaṃ tathā jñānaṃ vāsudevasya cāsti \|\| 35 \|\|*

*aspaṣṭaparūpā nyūnatā hyasti vāyau tathā jñānaṃ naiva saṃcintanīyam \|*  
*etādṛśī jñānaśaktirmurārervāyvādīnāṃ mokṣaparyantamasti \|\| 36 \|\|*

32-36. I have no other desire, except the attainment of eternal bliss. I am not, therefore, attached to outward pleasures nor my wife too is attached to them. Though not actuallystupid she appears to be stupid. To the wise she appears to be wise. To the stupid she appears to be stupid.

O lord, I possess as much of knowledge as is possessed by Vasudeva. As much knowledge is possessed by Vasudeva, so much knowledge is possessed by the wise men of straight nature.

The stupid men of complex nature are characterized by the intricacy of ignorance.

I have a perfect vision of knowledge as distinct and clear as the perception of an object m the light of the sun. Vasudeva possesses as much of knowledge as the perception of an object in the light of a lamp.

Vayu is inferior to us both, though his inferiority is not too visible. Hence, one. should not think of complete knowledge to be present in Vayu. Thus, I have explained to you the extent of knowledge in the lord as well as in Vayu and others till they obtain release.

## \|\| 12.37 \|\|

*jñānaṃ tvṛjūnāṃ mokṣakāle pipañcavāyvādīnāṃ pralayenādrādīrna \|*  
*vāyormama pralaye sṛṣṭikāle tathā gāyatryā nāstināstyeva mohaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. The five Rjus attain knowledge at release; Vayu and others at dissolution; Gayatri suffers stupefaction either at dissolution or at recreation.

## \|\| 12.38 \|\|

*gāyatrīvadbhāratyā devadeva jñātavyamevaṃ haritattvavedibhiḥ \|*  
*mamājñānaṃ dṛśyate yatra kutra daityādīnāṃ mohanārtha sadaiva \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. О lord of lords, Bharat! is at par with ^Gayatri. Those who are versed in the principles of knowledge related to Visnu should hnow all about this.

## \|\| 12.39 \|\|

*tena prītirdevadevasya viṣṇarbhaviṣyatītyeva viniścitātmā \|*  
*praśrādikaṃ tvajñavatsarvadaivaṃ kariṣyehaṃ mohanāyādhamānām \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. I too suffer from illusion, sometimes but this illusion is assumed for stupefying Daityas, so that the supreme god Visnu may be gratified thereby.

Like an ignorant person I put queries for stupefying people.

## \|\| 12.40 \|\|

*sūryodaye nāsti yathā tamaśca tathājñānaṃ nāstināstyeva deva \|*  
*karomyahaṃ śravaṇaṃ sarvadaiva hariprītyarthaṃ niścatārthaṃ satāṃ hi \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. As darkness does not exist with the rise of the sun so ignorance does not exist with the rise of knowledge.

I know reality for certain, still I assume listening to, as if I know it not.

## \|\| 12.41-42 \|\|

*śatajanmagatānāṃ tvanṛjūnāṃ pūrvameva tu \|*  
*aparokṣābhāva eva hyajñānaṃ samudīritam \|\| 41 \|\|*

*aparokṣānantaraṃ tu nāstyajñānaṃ na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*śatajanmasu deveśa aparokṣeṇa sarvadā \|\| 42 \|\|*

41-42. Men of complex nature, though they have passed through hundred births, remain in ignorance so long as they do not realize Self. After they have realized Self there remains no ignorance.

## \|\| 12.43 \|\|

*pūrṇajñānaṃ mamāstyeva nātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|*  
*śatajanmasu pūrvaṃ tu parokṣeṇa mama prabho \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. О lord of gods, after passing through series of births and realizing Self I obtained complete knowledge.

## \|\| 12.44 \|\|

*pūrṇaṃ jñānaṃ sadāpyastītyevamāhurmaharṣayaḥ \|*  
*saṃjñājanmagatāyāśca sarasvatyā mahāprabho \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. But the great sages have declared that even when passing through the series of births and before attaining to complete integration I possessed a complete knowledge of the Self. О great lord, Sarasvatl, who was born as Samjha, has no ignorance.

## \|\| 12.45 \|\|

*nājñānaṃ cintanīyaṃ hi brahmayvośca deva hi \|*  
*atra kaścidviśeṣosti jñātavyastatvamicchubhiḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. No notion of ignorance should be ascribed to Brahma and Vayu. Now I relate to you a secret which every aspirant shall desire to know.

## \|\| 12.46 \|\|

*avatāreṣu bhāratyāḥ kadācijjñānapūrvakam \|*  
*sarvadā jñānarūpā sā sarvaduḥ khavivarjitā \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. Among incarnations, Bharatl is knowledge incarnate, devoid of all sorrows.

## \|\| 12.47 \|\|

*daityānāṃ mohanārthāya aṃśe duḥ khīva dṛśyate \|*  
*tasyā duḥ khādikaṃ kiñcinnāstināstyeva sarvathā \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. For stupefying Daityas she professes to be sorrowful, partially. In fact, she imbibes no sorrow whatsoever.

## \|\| 12.48-49 \|\|

*aparokṣatirobhāva īṣatkāle pradṛśyate \|*  
*tāvanmātreṇa vājñānaṃ tasyāṃ naivāhitaṃ ca yat \|\| 48 \|\|*

*mūlarūpe tu nāstyeva bhāratyā jñānavismṛtiḥ \|*  
*bhāratyāstu yathā nāsti sarasvatyāstu kiṃ punaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

48-49. For a short while, when the realized object deludes the grasp, there is ignorance. But in the basic form, there is no ignorance in Bharatl. How can there be one in Sarasvatl?

## \|\| 12.50 \|\|

*aṃśāvataraṇaṃ nāsti sarasvatyāḥ kadācana \|*  
*aṃśāvataraṇaṃ nāsti mamāpi madhusūdana \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. So Sarasvatl never incarnates partially. О slayer of Madhu, I too do not incarnate in part.

## \|\| 12.51-54 \|\|

*tathaiva jñānamastyeva harernārāyaṇasya ca \|*  
*vāyoraṃśāvatārosti yathā mūle tathaiva ca \|\| 51 \|\|*

*balajñānā dikaṃ sarvaṃ cintanīyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*tathāpi vāyau dṛśyante balajñānādivyaktayaḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

*avatāreṣu vāyostu samyak śaktyātmanāsti hi \|*  
*aparokṣatirobhāvau nāṃśāvataraṇeṣvapi \|\| 53 \|\|*

*balajñānādikaṃ yāvanmūlarūpe pradṛśyate \|*  
*tretāyugasvarūpe ca na darśayati tādṛśam \|\| 54 \|\|*

51-54. She and myself are possessed of knowledge. Vayu who incarnates in part possesses strength and knowledge in the original form but less when he incarnates.

Strength and knowledge are manifest in Vayu in all incarnations, though the proportion is less in Treta than in any other age.

## \|\| 12.55 \|\|

*tretāyugasvarūpe ca yādṛk cādarśayatprabho \|*  
*dvāparasthe svarūpe tattaddarśayati tādṛśam \|*  
*tretāyugasvarūpe ca yādṛk cādarśayatprabho \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. As much of strength and knowledge as he reveals in Treta, the same he manifests when lie incarnates in Dvapara.

## \|\| 12.56 \|\|

*dvāparasthe vāyurūpe yādṛgvā darśayatprabhuḥ \|*  
*vāyuḥ kaliyuge rūpe taddarśayati tādṛśam \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. As much of strength and knowledge as he manifests in Dvaparam, the same he manifests when he is born in Kali.

## \|\| 12.57-58 \|\|

*tathā darśayate vāyurdaityānāṃ mohanāya ca \|*  
*avatāreṣu vāyośca antaraṃ ye viduḥ prabho \|\| 57 \|\|*

*te 'dhaṃ tamaḥ praviśante te daityā na ca te surāḥ \|*  
*vāyāvapyantaraṃ nāsti haritattvavinirṇaye \|\| 58 \|\|*

57-58. Vayu shows his strength and knowledge for stupefying Daityas.

О lord, those who recognize difference in each incarnation of Vayu go to dark regions. They are daityas, not devas.

We find no difference between Vayu and Hari as we investigate into the nature of Hari.

## \|\| 12.59 \|\|

*nindāṃ kurvanti ye viṣṇorjihvāchedaṃ karomyaham \|*  
*tadarthameva vāyośca avatāraḥ sadā bhuvi \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. I sever the tongues of those who reproach Hari. Vayu incarnates on earth with that object in view.

## \|\| 12.60 \|\|

*guṇapūrṇasya viṣṇostu nirguṇatvavicintanam \|*  
*jātānandādipūrṇāṅkhyaṃ sohamityādicintanam \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. \[The following should bs avoided as they amount to the censure of Visnu.\]

To think that Lord Visnu is devoid of qualities while he is possessed of qualities.

To think that he is identical with me, while full of bliss he is not identical with me.

## \|\| 12.61 \|\|

*cidānandātmake dehe utpattyādivicintanam \|*  
*acchedyābhedyagātreṣu cchedabhedādicintanam \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. To think that he takes birth while possessed of cternality, consciousness and bliss he does not take birth.

## \|\| 12.62 \|\|

*devyā nityāviyoginyā viyogādivicintanam \|*  
*kleśaśokādiśūnyasya hareḥ kleśādicintanam \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. To think that he can be cut and severed in parts while he cannot be cut and severed in parts.

To think that he can get separated from Laksmi while he is inseparable from Laksmi.

To think that he suiters from sorrows while in fact he is devoid of sorrows.

## \|\| 12.63 \|\|

*vyāsarāmādirūpeṣvanṛṣivipratvacintanam \|*  
*kṛṣṇarāmādirūpeṣu antarasya vicintanam \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. To think that Vyasa and Rama (Parasurama) are not the sage and the Brahmana.

To differentiate among Krsna, Rama and other incarnations.

## \|\| 12.64 \|\|

*rāmakṛṣṇādirūpeṣu antarasya vicintanam \|*  
*rāmakṛṣṇādirūpeṣu parājayavicintanam \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. To think that Rama (son of Dasaratha), Krsna and other incarnations can be vanquished in the battle.

## \|\| 12.65 \|\|

*santānārthaṃ tu kṛṣṇe na śivapūjādicintanam \|*  
*rāmeṇa duḥ khayuktena liṅgasya sthāpanaṃ kṛtam \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. То think that Lord Krsna worshipped Siva for the continuation of his line by getting offspring.

To think that Rama, aggrieved at the separation of Sita, set up the image of Lord Shiva (at Ramesvaram).

## \|\| 12.66 \|\|

*pañcadhātumaye kṛṣṇe harirūpavicintanam \|*  
*svayaṃ vyaktasthale cāpi cidā nandatvakalpanam \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. To think that Lord Krsna is Visnu while in his physical form he is composed of five bhutas.

To think that man is constituted of supreme consciousness and bliss while in fact man is not constituted of supreme consciousness and bliss.

## \|\| 12.67 \|\|

*pitṛmātṛdvijātīnāṃ harirūpatvacintanam \|*  
*asvatantreṇa rudreṇa hareraikyadicintanam \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. To assume that father, mother and Brahmins are made in the form of Visnu, while in fact they are not made in the form of Visnu.

To think that lord Visnu and Rudra are identical in status while in fact Rudra is subordinate to Visnu.

## \|\| 12.68 \|\|

*viṣṇoḥ sūryeṇa sākaṃ ca abhedā divicintanam \|*  
*sarvottamaḥ sūrya eva viṣṇvādyāstasya kiṅkarāḥ \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. To think that lord Visnu and Surya are identical.

To think that Surya is the greatest of deities and Visnu is his subordinate.

## \|\| 12.69-70 \|\|

*ityādicintanaṃ doṣo harinindeti cocyate \|*  
*asvayaṃ vyaktiliṅgeṣu aśvatthatulasīṣu ca \|\| 69 \|\|*

*śālagrāmaṃ vihāyaiva namanaṃ ye prakurvate \|*  
*te sarve harinindāyāmavikāriṇa eva hi \|\| 70 \|\|*

69-70. Those who offer homage to the holy fig tree or the sacred basil plant, unless these grow up naturally, and those who overpass Salagrama incur sin in Common with those who censure the lord.

## \|\| 12.71 \|\|

*mokṣādhikāriṇo ye tu ajñānātparameśvaram \|*  
*pārthakyanayanaṃ yeṣu kurvanti yarhi vā prabho \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. Those who, being on the verge of liberation, regard the lord as a separate entity come to grief in course of time.

## \|\| 12.72-73 \|\|

*tarhi teṣāṃ hi kāleṣu duḥ khaṃ yāti na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*ataḥ prārthakyanayanaṃ ye kurvantyeṣu sarvadā \|\| 72 \|\|*

*te sarve tvabudhā jñeyā nātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|*  
*asvayaṃvyaktaliṅgeṣu namanaṃ ye prakurvate \|\| 73 \|\|*

72-73. Those who regard the lord as a separate entity are stupid. Those who pay homage to the plants, unless these grow naturally, should be regarded as asuras.

## \|\| 12.74-75 \|\|

*te sarve hyasurā jñeyā nānyathā tu kathañcana \|*  
*vihāya śūnyamaśvatthaṃ namanaṃ ye prakurvate \|\| 74 \|\|*

*dvimāsahīnāṃ tulasīmaprasūtāṃ ca gāṃ navām \|*  
*te sarve hyasurā jñeyā nātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|\| 75 \|\|*

74-75. Neglecting the holy fig-tree, devoid of boughs, those who pay respects to the holy basil plant less than two months old or a young cow that has not delivered-should be declared asuras.

## \|\| 12.76 \|\|

*gulmādyāśca manuṣyāntāste jñeyā brahmabāhavaḥ \|*  
*asmacchatāyuḥ paryantameka eva kaliḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. Life beginning with the plants and ending with humanity constitutes my arms. Full one hundred years of my life constitute an age of Kali.

## \|\| 12.77 \|\|

*kalau saṃti kalpamānaṃ kalerante saṃti ca \|*  
*tasmindine brahmarūpe gacchanti ca tamontikam \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. The period of Kali is counted by Kalpas. Life exists though Kali expires.

During the day of Brahma the sinners who have strayed from the path of virtue go to the dark regions.

## \|\| 12.78 \|\|

*tatra sthitvā lokamārgaṃ pratīkṣante na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*sādhakairviṣṇukāryāṇāṃ vāyudāsaiḥ prapīḍitāḥ \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. While staying there they wait for the turn of their destiny while the attendants of Vayu carry out the orders of the lord and torture them severely.

## \|\| 12.79 \|\|

*śatavarṣānantaraṃ ca sarveṣāṃ kalinā saha \|*  
*vāyorgadāprahāreṇa liṅgabhaṅgo bhaviṣyati \|\| 79 \|\|*

79\. After the lapse of one hundred years the living beings together with Kali will have their subtle bodies smashed with the thrust of a club by Vayu.

## \|\| 12.80 \|\|

*tamondhaṃ praviśantyete tāratamyena sarvaśaḥ \|*  
*tamasyandhepi saṃsāre nātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|\| 80 \|\|*

80\. Gradually, they will enter the dark regions all round.

## \|\| 12.81 \|\|

*sarveṣāmuttamonte yaḥ kalireva na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*dūṣako viṣṇubhaktānāṃ tatsamo nāsti sarvadā \|\| 81 \|\|*

81\. Of all the ages, Kali comes at the end. Kali is the foremost of those who slander the devotees of Visnu.

## \|\| 12.82-83 \|\|

*saṃsāre vāndhatamasi sarvatra haridūṣakaḥ \|*  
*mithyādāne jñānabuddhirduḥ khe ca sukhabuddhimān \|\| 82 \|\|*

*tasmātkalisamo loke śivabhakto na kutracit \|*  
*duryodhanaḥ sa evokto duḥ khānantyasvarūpavān \|\| 83 \|\|*

82-83. In this world of mortals or in the world of extreme darkness, there is none equal to Kali who slanders the lord among the devotees of Shiva who find pleasure in ignorance and aversion in knowledge.

Kali is known as Duryodhana, the endless pain incarnate.

## \|\| 12.84 \|\|

*tasmācchataguṇāṃśena kalibhāryā tu sarvadā \|*  
*alakṣmīriti vikhyātā sā loke mantharā smṛtā \|\| 84 \|\|*

84\. The wife of Kali hundred percent less in qualities, is known as Alaksml, popularly known as Manthara.

## \|\| 12.85-87 \|\|

*tasmāddaśaguṇāṃśono vipracittistu sarvadā \|*  
*jarāsaṃdhaḥ sa evoktaḥ kālanemistataḥ param \|\| 85 \|\|*

*tasmācchataguṇāṃśonaḥ sa tu kaṃseti viśrutaḥ \|*  
*tasmātpañcaguṇairhīnau madhukraiṭabhasaṃjñakau \|\| 86 \|\|*

*tāveva haṃsahiḍaṃbakau jñeyau tau ca janārdana \|*  
*vipracittisamo jñeyo bhaumo vai bhūtale smṛtaḥ \|\| 87 \|\|*

85-87. Hundred per cent less in qualities was Vipracitti. Then came Jarasandha, Kalanemi and Kamsa. The latter was hundred per cent less in qualities. Less by five per cent in. qualities were Madhu and Kaitabha, also known as Hamsa and Hidambaka. Bhauma was equal to Vipracitti.

## \|\| 12.88 \|\|

*tasmādaṣṭa guṇairucyo hariṇyakaśiṣuḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*tasmācca triguṇairhīno hiraṇyākṣo mahāsuraḥ \|\| 88 \|\|*

88\. Less by eight per cent in qualities was Hiranyakasipu. Less by three per cent in qualities was the great Asura Hiranyaksa.

## \|\| 12.89 \|\|

*maṇimāṃstatsamo jñeyaḥ kiñcidūno bakaḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*tasmādviṃśadguṇairhīnastārakākhyo mahāsuraḥ \|\| 89 \|\|*

89\. Maniman was equal to him in qualities. Baka was a little inferior to Maniman. The great Asura Taraka was less by twenty per cent in qualities.

## \|\| 12.90 \|\|

*tasmātṣaḍguṇato hīnaḥ śaṃbaro lokakaṇṭakaḥ \|*  
*śaṃbarasya samo jñeyaḥ śālvo daityeṣu cādhamaḥ \|\| 90 \|\|*

90\. Sambara, the tormentor of people was less by six percent in qualities. Salva the meanest of the Daityas, was equal to Sambara.

## \|\| 12.91 \|\|

*śaṃbarāttu dviguṇato hiḍiṃbo nyūna ucyate \|*  
*bāṇastato 'dhamo jñeyaḥ sa tu kīcakanāmataḥ \|\| 91 \|\|*

91\. Hidimba was two per cent less in quality than Sambara. Bana was inferior to Hidimba. Then came Kicaka.

## \|\| 12.92 \|\|

*dvāpārakhyo mahāhāsobāṇāsurasamaḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*tasmāddaśaguṇairhīno namucidaityasattamaḥ \|\| 92 \|\|*

92\. Dvapara Mahahasa was equal to Asura Bana. Inferior to him by ten per cent in quality was Namuci, the strongest of Daityas.

## \|\| 12.93 \|\|

*namucestusamau jñeyau pāka ilvala ityubhau \|*  
*tasmāccaturguṇairhīno vātāpirdānavādhamaḥ \|\| 93 \|\|*

93Рака and Ilvala were equal to Namuci. The vilest of daityas was Vatapi less by four per cent in qualities than Ilvala.

## \|\| 12.94 \|\|

*tasmātsārdhaguṇairhīno dhenuko nāma daityarāṭ \|*  
*dhenukādardhaguṇataḥ keśī daityastu cāvaraḥ \|\| 94 \|\|*

94\. Dhenuka, the king of daityas, was less, than one and a half per cent in qualities. The meanest of the daityas was Kesin less than hail per cent in qualities than Dhenuka.

## \|\| 12.95 \|\|

*keśīdaityasamo jñeyastṛṇāvarto mahāsuraḥ \|*  
*tasmāddaśaguṇairhīno haṃso nāmaramāpate \|\| 95 \|\|*

95\. The great asura Trnavana was equal to esi. О lord, Hamsa was less by ten per cent qualities than Kesin.

## \|\| 12.96 \|\|

*tririkastu samo jñeyastatsamaḥ paurukasmṛtaḥ \|*  
*vetaḥ sa eva vijñeyaḥ pūrvajanmani sattama \|\| 96 \|\|*

96\. Tririk was equal to f Hamsa and Paurika was equal to him likewise. In the previous birth he was known as Vena.

## \|\| 12.97 \|\|

*tasmādekaguṇairhīnau kuṃbhāṇḍakakuparṇakau \|*  
*duḥ śāsanastu vijñeyo jarāsaṃdhasamaḥ prabho \|\| 97 \|\|*

97\. Kumbhandaka and Kuparnaka were less by one per cent qualities than him. О lord, Duhsasana was equal to Jarasandha.

## \|\| 12.98 \|\|

*kaṃsena tulyo vijñeyo vikarṇo daityasattamaḥ \|*  
*kuṃbhakarṇācchataguṇairhīnau kradhyeti viśrutaḥ \|\| 98 \|\|*

98\. The best of Daityas, Vikarna was equal to Kamsa.

Lessby hundredpercent in qualities than Kumbhakarna was Kradhya.

## \|\| 12.99 \|\|

*tasmācchataguṇairhīnaḥ śatadhanvā mahāsuraḥ \|*  
*samānastasya vijñeyaḥ karmārirdaityasattamaḥ \|\| 99 \|\|*

99\. Less than hundred per cent in qualities was the great asura Satadhanvan. The best of Daityas, Karmari was equal to him.

## \|\| 12.100 \|\|

*kālakeyastu vijñeyaḥ sadā venasamo mataḥ \|*  
*adhamānāṃ tu daityānāmuttamaiḥ sāmyamucyate \|\| 100 \|\|*

100\. Kalakeya was equal to Vena. The meanest of daityas is equal to his superior.

## \|\| 12.101 \|\|

*tatrāveśācca vijñeyaṃ devānāṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*tasmācchatagurṇaihīnaścittamānasuro mahān \|\| 101 \|\|*

101\. This is due to the influence the gods exercise over them. Less by hundred per cent in qualities than Kalakeya was asura Cittaman. The presiding deity of Cittaman was superior to him by hundred per cent in qualities.

## \|\| 12.102 \|\|

*taccharīrābhimānī tu tasmācchataguṇairvaraḥ \|*  
*tasmācchataguṇaihīṃno hastamānasuro mahān \|\| 102 \|\|*

102\. Less by hundred per cent in qualities was the asura Padaman. The presiding deity of the eyes is superior to him by hundred per cent in quality.

## \|\| 12.103 \|\|

*tasmācchataguṇairhīnaḥ pādamānasuro mahān \|*  
*netrendriyābhimānī tu tasmācchataguṇo varaḥ \|\| 103 \|\|*

103\. Less by hundred percent in qualities was the asura Saktiman.

## \|\| 12.104 \|\|

*cakṣurindriyamānī tu tasmācchataguṇo varaḥ \|*  
*tasmācchaguṇairhīnaḥ sparśamānasuro mahān \|\| 104 \|\|*

104\. Less by hundred per cent in qualities than Padaman was the great asura Sparsaman.

## \|\| 12.105 \|\|

*tasmācchatagurṇaihīnaścaṇḍamānasuro mahān \|*  
*tasmācchatagurṇairhīnaḥ śiśramānasuro mahān \|\| 105 \|\|*

105\. Less by hundred per cent in qualities was Candaman. Less by hundred percent in qualities was Shisnaman.

## \|\| 12.106 \|\|

*tasmācchataguṇairhīnaḥ karmamānasuraḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*kalpādyaiḥ preritāḥ sarve rudrādyā adhikāriṇaḥ \|\| 106 \|\|*

106\. Less by hundred per cent in qualities was Karma-man. The presiding deities Rudra and others are urged by the deities who lived at the beginning of Kalpas.

## \|\| 12.107 \|\|

*kadācitsuviruddhaṃ ca kurvanti tava sattama \|*  
*kadāpyahaṃ ca vāyuśca viruddhaṃ nācareva bhoḥ \|\| 107 \|\|*

107\. О lord, sometime they go contrary to your wishes, while I and Vayu, О lord, never go contrary to your wishes.

## \|\| 12.108 \|\|

*mūleṣvaṃśāvatāreṣu rudrādīnā mahāprabho \|*  
*buddhirvinaśyate yasmāttasmācchinnā hi te 'khilāḥ \|\| 108 \|\|*

108\. О lord, Rudra and other gods lose their link with their root when they incarnate. Hence, they become powerless.

## \|\| 12.109 \|\|

*mahīpate ca madbuddhistasmādacchinnasaṃjñikaḥ \|*  
*etādṛśopyahaṃ deva na ca śaktistu nastave \|\| 109 \|\|*

109\. О lord of earth, my intellect alone remains unimpaired. Even then, О lord, I need sufficient strength to fully offer my praise.

## \|\| 12.110 \|\|

*mahyamacchinnabhaktāya dayāṃ kuru mahāprabho \|*  
*iti stutvā hariṃ brahmā sthitaḥ prāñja liragrataḥ \|\| 110 \|\|*

110\. О Sovereign lord, be compassionate to me who am your constant devotee. Thus having offered praise Brahma stood before the lord with his palms joined in reverence.

## Chapter 13

## \|\| 13.1 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*iti stutaḥ sa bhagavān svapūtreṇa dayānidhiḥ \|*  
*medhagaṃbhīrayā vācā provāca madhusūdanaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Shri Krsna said: — Lord Visnu, the slayer of Asura Madhu, the receptacle of compassion, was thus praised by his son Brahma, spoke in a voice as loud as the thunder of reigning clouds.

## \|\| 13.2 \|\|

*sṛja brahmannimāndevānmatprasādātkrameṇa ca \|*  
*yathā vai prākkṣaṇetadvatsṛja sarvaṃ mahāprabho \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. О Brahman, please start re-creaung the universe in the fashion you did in the previous Kalpas.

## \|\| 13.3 \|\|

*nāsti prayojanaṃ tena tava matprītaye sṛja \|*  
*evamuktastu hāriṇā brahmā stutvā hariṃ param \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. You have no axe to grind thereby. You create the universe to carry out my instructions." Thus spoken to by Visnu, Brahma praised die lord.

He thought of creating the Universe, just for pleasing Him.

## \|\| 13.4-5 \|\|

*sṛṣṭiṃ kartuṃ mano dadhre prīṇayanneva mādhavam \|*  
*mahattatvātmako brahmā vāyuṃ jīvabhimāninam \|\| 4 \|\|*

*ādau sasarja garuḍa puruṣātmā sa eva ca \|*  
*tato dakṣiṇahastāttu brahmāṇīṃ bhārantī tathā \|\| 5 \|\|*

4-5. О Garuda, at first, Brahma, who represented intellect created Vayu who became the presiding deity of life. He is the soul of mankind.

## \|\| 13.6 \|\|

*asṛjatte mahābhāge avyaktasya niyāmike \|*  
*vāmahastātsatyaputro mahattatvātmako 'nalaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Then, from his right hand he created Brahman!! and Bharat!. The two control the unmanifest Prakrti.

From his left hand was created Fire, the son of Truth, representing the principle of Intellect.

## \|\| 13.7 \|\|

*brahmaṇo dakṣiṇāddhastādahaṅkārātmako haraḥ \|*  
*ādau śeṣastato jajñe garuḍatadanantaram \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. From his right hand was created Нага representing Ego. At first, Shesa was born. Garuda came next.

## \|\| 13.8-9 \|\|

*tadanantarajo rudraḥ sa evaṃ sṛṣṭavānprabhuḥ \|*  
*svotpattyanantaraṃ brahmā daśavarṣānmahāprabhuḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

*vāyumuttpādrayāmāsa vatsarānantare prabhuḥ \|*  
*gāyatrīṃ janayāmāsa vāyorutpattyanantaram \|\| 9 \|\|*

8-9. Thereafter Brahma created Rudra. Thus, Brahma created the universe. Ten years after his own birth, Brahma created Vayu. A year after, he created Gayatri.

## \|\| 13.10 \|\|

*saṃvatsarānantare tu bhāratīmasṛjatprabhuḥ \|*  
*bhāratyanantaraṃ śeṃ divyasāhasravatsarāt \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. A year after, he created Bharat!. A thousand divine years after, he created &esa.

## \|\| 13.11 \|\|

*anantaraṃ saṃbabhūva garuḍastu tataḥ param \|*  
*divyasāhasravarṣāttu tathā rudrañca sṛṣṭavān \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. A thousand divine years after, he created Garuda.

## \|\| 13.12 \|\|

*śeṣasyānantaraṃ devīṃ vāruṇīṃ ca mahāprabhuḥ \|*  
*daśavarṣānantaraṃ tu hyasṛjatkamalāsanaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. After she lapse of the same period, he created Varuni.

## \|\| 13.13 \|\|

*garuḍānantaraṃ devīṃ sauparṇīmasṛjatprabhuḥ \|*  
*daśavarṣānantaraṃ ca pārvatīṃ ca tathaiva saḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. When ten years had lapsed after Garuda was created he created Saupami.

## \|\| 13.14-15 \|\|

*pārvatyanantaraṃ candraṃ manastattvaniyāmakam \|*  
*daśavarṣānantaraṃ tu vāsavaṃ hyasṛjattataḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

*abhimānī dakṣiṇasya bāhośca parameṣṭhinaḥ \|*  
*daśavarṣānantaraṃ tu śacī tāmasṛjatprabhuḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

14-15. After the lapse of ten years, he created Parvati; after the lapse of ten years, he created the moon, the presiding deity of the mind. After the lapse of ten years he created Indra, the presiding deity of the right arm. After the lapse of ten years he created Saci.

## \|\| 13.16 \|\|

*indrasyānantaraṃ kāmaṃ triṃśadvarṣādanantaram \|*  
*asṛjadvāmabāhoścamanastatvābhimāninam \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. After the lapse of thirty years since Indra was bom, he created, from his left arm, Kama, the presiding deity of the mind.

## \|\| 13.17-18 \|\|

*tadanantarajāṃ devīṃ daśavarṣādanantaram \|*  
*ratiṃ sa janayāmāsa kāmabhāryāṃ mahāprabhuḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

*kāmasyāpyabhimānī tu sa eva parikīrtitaḥ \|*  
*brahmāhaṅkārikaṃ prāṇaṃ kāryotpatteranantaram \|\| 18 \|\|*

17-18. After the lapse of ten years since the birth of Kama, he created Rati, the wife of Kama, Brahma is the presiding deity of Kama. After the creation of the universe, he created Ego.

## \|\| 13.19 \|\|

*daśavarṣānantaraṃ tu nirmame nāsika tataḥ \|*  
*tasya bhāryāṃ nāsikasyaḥ pañcavarṣādanantaram \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. After the lapse of ten years, he created the right nose. After the lapse of five years he created the left nose.

## \|\| 13.20-21 \|\|

*nirmame nāsikāṃ vāmāṃ brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ \|*  
*ahaṅkārādanu brahmā sajñānaṃ ca bṛhaspatim \|\| 20 \|\|*

*nirmame ca varṣayugmapañcavarṣādanantaram \|*  
*pañcavarṣānantaraṃ tu tārāṃ bhāryāṃ vinirmame \|\| 21 \|\|*

20-21. Seven years after the creation of ego, he created Brhaspati. Five years after, he created Tara who became the wife of Brhaspati.

## \|\| 13.22 \|\|

*guroranantaraṃ brahmā pañcaviṃśādanantaram \|*  
*svāyaṃbhuvaṃ manuṃ caiva nirmame manasā vibhuḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Twentyfive years after, he created Manu his son from his mind..

## \|\| 13.23-28 \|\|

*pañcavarṣānantaraṃ tu śatarūpāṃ vinirmame \|*  
*śatarūpānantaraṃ tu viṃśadvarṣadināntaram \|\| 23 \|\|*

*dakṣaḥ śiṣyātmako jajñe dakṣiṇāṅguṣṭhataḥ prabhoḥ \|*  
*pañcavarṣānantaraṃ tu vāmāṅguṣṭhāccaturmukhaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

*prasūtimasṛjadbrahmā sṛṣṭyarthaṃ paramādarāt \|*  
*dakṣasyānantaraṃ brahmā pañcaviṃśādanantaram \|\| 25 \|\|*

*nirmame hyaniruddhaṃ ca madhyamāṅguliparvataḥ \|*  
*pañcavarṣānantaraṃ tu sasarja bhagavānajaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

*virājasaṃjñakāṃ bhāryāṃ madhyamāṅguliparvataḥ \|*  
*aniruddhānantara tu śatavarṣādanantaram \|\| 27 \|\|*

*nirmame pravahaṃ vāyuṃ kaniṣṭhāṅguliparvataḥ \|*  
*daśavarṣānantaraṃ tu pravāhīṃ nirmame prabhuḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

23-28. Five years after, he created Satarupa. Twenty years after, he created Daksa from his right thumb. Five years after, the fourfaced Brahma created Prasuti from his left thumb.

Brahma made an all-out effort and created offspring for the increase of population. First of all, he created Daksa. Twenty-five years after Daksa, he created Aniruddha, out of the joints of his middle finger.

Twenty five years after, he created viraja out of the joints of his middle finger.

One hundred years after, Aniruddha, he created Pravaha from the joints of his little finger. Ten years after, he created Parvatl.

## \|\| 13.29 \|\|

*kaniṣṭhāṅguliparvācca vāmadevaṃ na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*pravahānantaraṃ tabrahmā śatavarṣādanantaram \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. One hundred years after, he created Vamadeva from the joints of his little finger.

## \|\| 13.30 \|\|

*yamaṃ vinirmame pṛṣṭhādaṣṭavarṣādanantaram \|*  
*tadbhāryāṃ śāmalāṃ devīṃ tasmādeva mahāprabhuḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. He created Yama from his back. Fight years after, he created Syamala who became the wife of Yama.

## \|\| 13.31 \|\|

*yamasyānantaraṃ candraṃ triṃśadvarṣādanantaram \|*  
*asṛjaddakṣiṇācchotrācchotratattvaniyāmakam \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Thirty years after Yama, he created the moon from tile right ear. The moon became the presiding deity of ears.

## \|\| 13.32 \|\|

*navavarṣānantaraṃ tu rohiṇīsamṛjatprabhuḥ \|*  
*vāmaśrotrācca garuḍaṃ vāmaśrotrābhimāninam \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. 'Nine years after, the lord created Rohini. He created Garuda from his left ear, of which Garuda became the presiding deity.

## \|\| 13.33 \|\|

*candrasyānantaraṃ sūryaṃ viṃśadvarṣādanantaram \|*  
*samyagvinirmame brahmā dakṣiṇākṣṇaśca devatām \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Twenty years after the creation of the moon, he created the sun from his right eye.

## \|\| 13.34 \|\|

*vāmākṣṇo nirmame saṃjñāṃ ṣaḍvarṣānantaraṃ prabhuḥ \|*  
*sūryasyānantaraṃ brahmā śatavarṣādanantaram \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Six years after the creation of the sun, he created Samjna from his left eye.

## \|\| 13.35-36 \|\|

*rasanendriyācca varuṇaṃ nirmame tasya māninam \|*  
*viṃśadvarṣānantaraṃ tu tasmādevedriyātprabhuḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

*gaṅgāṃ vinirmame brahmā rasanendriyadevatām \|*  
*varuṇasyānantaraṃ tu daśavarṣādanantaram \|\| 36 \|\|*

35-36. A hundred years after, he created Varuna from, his organ of taste and made him the presiding deity of that organ.

Twenty years after, he created Ganga from the very organ of taste and made her the presiding deity of that organ.

## \|\| 13.37 \|\|

*utsaṃgānnirmame brahmā nāradaṃ bhagavatpriyam \|*  
*nāradasyānantaraṃ tu ṣaṣṭivarṣādanantaram \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Ten years after, he created Narada from, his lap. Narada became very dear to him.

## \|\| 13.38-40 \|\|

*agniṃ vinirmame brahmātvagindriyataḥ prabhuḥ \|*  
*ato vāgabhimānī saṃ pañcavarṣādanantaram \|\| 38 \|\|*

*svāhāṃ vinirmame brahmā tāmāhurmantradevatām \|*  
*agneranantaraṃ vīndra bhṛguṃ brahmarṣisattamam \|\| 39 \|\|*

*daśavarṣānantaraṃ tu bhruvormadhyādvinirmame \|*  
*saṃvatsarānantaraṃ tu bhṛgubhāryāṃ vinirmame \|\| 40 \|\|*

38-40. He created Agni from his organ of touch, sixty years after.

He, the presiding deity of speech, created Svaha after five years. Svaha became the presiding deity of the mantras.

О lord of birds, ten years after, from his eye-brows, he-created Bhrgu, the best of the Brahmanic sages.

After a year, he created a woman who became the wife of Bhrgu.

## \|\| 13.41 \|\|

*bhṛgoranantaraṃ brahmā śatavarṣādanantaram \|*  
*kaśyapañjanayāmāsa manasā ca svayaṃ prabhuḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. A hundred years after Bhrgu, he created Kasyapa out of his mind.

## \|\| 13.42 \|\|

*saṃvatsarānantaraṃ tu aditiṃ nirmame prabhuḥ \|*  
*kaśyapānantaraṃ cātriṃ daśavarṣādanantaram \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. A year after, he created Aditi. Ten years after Kasyapa. he created Atri.

## \|\| 13.43 \|\|

*atreranantaraṃ brahmā daśavarṣādanantaram \|*  
*ajījanadbharadvājaṃ vasiṣṭha tadanataram \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Ten years after, he created Bharadvaja. Ten years after, he created Vasistha

## \|\| 13.44 \|\|

*daśavarṣānantaraṃ tu teṣāṃ bhāryāḥ krameṇa tu \|*  
*saṃvatsarānantareṇa asṛjatkamalāsanaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Ten years after, he created women, to become their consorts, at the interval of one year each.

## \|\| 13.45 \|\|

*vasiṣṭhasyānantaraṃ tu gautamaṃ hyasṛjatprabhuḥ \|*  
*daśavarṣānantareṇa jamadagniṃ tato 'sṛjat \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. After Vasistha, he created Gautama. Ten years after, he created Jamadagni.

## \|\| 13.46-47 \|\|

*daśavarṣānantareṇa manurvaivasvato 'bhavat \|*  
*manoranantaraṃ jajñe śatavarṣādanantaram \|\| 46 \|\|*

*viṣvakseno mahābhāgo vāyuputro mahābalaḥ \|*  
*tasmāccaturdaśe varṣe gaṇapo hyabhavadvibhoḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

46-47. Ten years after, he created Vaivasvata Manu. A hundred years after Manu, was born Visvaksena, the most powerful son of Vayu. Fourteen years after, the lord of ganas was born.

## \|\| 13.48 \|\|

*tadanantarajo vīndra aṣṭavarṣādanantaram \|*  
*dhanapo hyabhavattatra tadbhāryā vatsare pare \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. О lord of birds, eight years after, he created Kubera; a year after, he created his consorts.

## \|\| 13.49 \|\|

*viṣvaksenānantaraṃ tu daśavarṣādanantaram \|*  
*jayādīnbha gavadbhaktānsṛṣṭavān kamalāsanaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Ten years after Visvaksena, he created Jaya and others the devotees of the lord.

## \|\| 13.50 \|\|

*jayādyānantaraṃ brahmā vallādyāḥ karmadevatāḥ \|*  
*śatavarṣānantaraṃ tu sṛṣṭavāñchivavāhanam \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. After Jaya, he created the deities of activities Balia and others. A hundred years after, he created the bull Nandi, the Vehicle of Shiva.

## \|\| 13.51 \|\|

*karmadevānantaraṃ tu triṃśadvarṣādanantaram \|*  
*parjanyamasṛjbrahmā mantrayantrābhimāninam \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Thirty years after, he created Parjanya, the presiding deity of Mantras and Yantras.

## \|\| 13.52 \|\|

*parjanyānantaraṃ brahmā daśavarṣādanantaram \|*  
*puṣkaraṃ janayāmāsa karmatattvābhimāninam \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Ten years after, he created Puskara, the presiding deity of the principle of activity.

## \|\| 13.53 \|\|

*evaṃ vinirmame brahmā matprasādātkhageśvara \|*  
*evaṃ jñātvā mokṣameti nānyathā tu kathañcana \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. О lord of birds, thus Brahma started creation at my bidding, in order to oblige' me.

He who understands this obtains liberation.

## Chapter 14

## \|\| 14.1 \|\|

*śrīgaruḍa uvāca \|*  
*avatārānhare brūhi tathā lakṣmyā divaukasām \|*  
*guṇānāmantara brūhi śiṣyasya mama savrata \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Garuda said: — О lord, let me know the incarnations of lord Visnu, LaksmI and gods, О you of good vow, as also the difference amongst them in regard to their qualities.

## \|\| 14.2 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*yo mūlarūpī bhagavānananto brahmādibhiḥ purṇaguṇaḥ svatantraḥ \|*  
*purātanaḥ pūrṇatanurmadātmā na tādṛśāḥ saṃti kadāpi vīdra \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Sri Krsna said: — The eternal, primeval lord has all the qualities in common with Brahma and others.

He is independent, ancient and complete in himself. Such a being is second to none, О lord of birds.

## \|\| 14.3 \|\|

*pādaśca pūrṇaḥ pādatalaṃ ca pūrṇaṃ nakhāśca pūrṇāḥ kaṭikaṇṭhau ca pūrṇau \|*  
*ūrū ca pūrṇe udaraṃ ca pūrṇaṃ labdhvāpi pūrṇāñjagṛhe tathāpyuraḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. His feet and their soles, his nails, hips and throat are all full.

## \|\| 14.4 \|\|

*skandhaḥ supūrṇāḥ sakalāśca bāhavaḥ pūrṇāḥ keśāḥ śmaśrudantāśca pūrṇāḥ \|*  
*lomāni pūrṇāni tathaiva romakūpāśca pūrṇāstu tathaiva śiśraḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Other parts of his body, viz, thighs, belly, breast, shoulders, arms, hair of the head and the body pores of the hair, beards, teeth, penis, scrotum, hair on the scrotum, armpit, eye, ear are also complete.

## \|\| 14.5-6 \|\|

*aṇḍaśca pūrṇo hyaṇḍaromāṇi kakṣāścakṣuśca śrotre sarva ete ca pūrṇāḥ \|*  
*kiṃ varṇaye mūlarūpaṃ hareśca yāvadvalaṃ pūrṇaṃ samagradehe \|\| 5 \|\|*

*tāvadbralaṃ hyekaromādikeṣu saṃtitvime hi yataḥ sa eva pūrṇaḥ \|*  
*sa eva tu sarvasya kartā sa eva harthā sa tu sārāṃśabhoktā \|\| 6 \|\|*

5-6. How shall I describe the original form of lord Hari? So long as there is strength in his composite body there is strength in his each and every hair, he can be called complete. He is the creator of all, the destroyer of all and, the enjoyer of quintessence of all substances.

## \|\| 14.7-8 \|\|

*asārāṃśaṃ naiva bhoktā haristu sārānvakṣye śṛṇu pakṣīndra samyak \|*  
*drākṣekṣusāraṃ nārikelasya sāraṃ cūtasya sāraṃ panasasyāpi sāram \|\| 7 \|\|*

*nāraṅgasāraṃ kramukasyāpi sāraṃ kharjūrasāraṃ kadalīphalasya \|*  
*nārāyaṇo bījarūpasya sāraṃ gṛhṇāti nityaṃ bhaktavaryo dayāluḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

7-8. He is not the enjoyer of non-essential part of substances. О lord of birds, now hear, I shall tell you the essence of substances.

The juices of the grape, sugar-cane, cocoanut, mango, bread-fruit, orange, betelnut, date, banana fruit-the compassionate lord drinks these juices when these are in their form of seed.

## \|\| 14.9-10 \|\|

*tāmbūlasāraṃ khadirasya sāraṃ puṣpasya sāraṃ candanasyāpi sāram \|*  
*godhūmasāraṃ yavānāṃ ca sāraṃ māṣasya sāraṃ hareṇośca sāram \|\| 9 \|\|*

*śuddhaṃ tathā vrīhinīvārasāraṃ śyāmākasāraṃ śuddhadhānyasya sāram \|\| 10 \|\|*

9-10. He drinks thejuices of the betel, Khadira tree, flower. Sandal, wheat, barley, bean, harenu (?), cultivated and wild rice, corn and unhusked rice.

## \|\| 14.11-16 \|\|

*niṣiddhānsarvaśākasya sārāṃstathā nipiddhāllāṃvaṇasvāpi sārān \|*  
*gṛhṇāti viṣṇuḥ paramādareṇa annasya sāraṃ bhakṣyabhojyasya sāram \|\| 11 \|\|*

*sūpasya sāraṃ paramānnasya sāraṃ dugdhasya sāraṃ dadhitakrasya sāram \|*  
*ghṛtasya sāraṃ rāmaṭhasyāpi sāraṃ gṛhṇāti viṣṇuḥ sarṣapasyāpi sāram \|\| 12 \|\|*

*marīcasāraṃ jīrakasyāpi sāraṃ tathā havirghṛtapakvasya sāram \|*  
*taileṣu pakvasya ca bharjitasya guḍasya sāraṃ navanītasya sāram \|\| 13 \|\|*

*lavaṅgasāraṃ śarkarāyāśca sāramityādisārān vāsudevastu bhukte \|*  
*lakṣmīpatiḥ sarvajagannivā sastasyājñayā vāsudevopi nityam \|\| 14 \|\|*

*taccheṣasārānapi cāvanīśo mahātmanoṃśāñchṛṇu śiṣyavarya \|*  
*evaṃ vimūḍhā vāsudevasya bhaktāḥ kiṃ vaktavyaṃ viṣṇubhaktā hi loke \|\| 15 \|\|*

*kalyāṇāste sārabhoktāra eva naiṣāṃ bhavettena duḥ khābhivṛddhiḥ \|*  
*bhuñjanti ye vaiśvadevaṃ vihāya duṣṭāṃstānvai bhukticinttāṃśca viddhi \|\| 16 \|\|*

11-16. Lord Visnu receives the juices of forbidden vegetables, salts, foodstuff, prescribed eatables, soup, substantial foodstuff milk, curd, buttermilk, ghee, foetid, mustard, pepper, cumin seed food cooked in the sacrificial ghee, treacle roasted or fried in the oil, butter, clove, sugar, lord Vasudeva partakes of these essences with pleasure.

By order, of the lord of LaksmI, the abode of all living beings, Vasudeva enjoys the essences of all these things. О best of disciples, the rest of the juices are enjoyed by the ruling monarch, the lord of the earch.

The devotees of lord Vasudeva, usually, have no knowledge of essences. Leaving aside these devotees, we talk of the devotees of Visnu who enjoy the essences of substances and thereby avoid coming to grief.

Those who partake of meals without performing Vaisvadeva are the low class of people who eat the stale food.

## \|\| 14.17-18 \|\|

*vakṣye viśeṣaṃ vaiśvadeve khagendra gopyaṃ no vadānyatra vidvān \|*  
*sūryādīnāṃ ye ca dāne ca dadyurvinā vāyorantarasthaṃ hariṃ ca \|\| 17 \|\|*

*te vai sadā sārabhoktāra eva jñeyāstvato viṣṇureko mahātmā \|*  
*sārāṃśabhoktā na tu sarvasya bhoktā bhunakti sarvaṃ tvaviruddhaśaktiḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

17-18. О lord of birds, I shall tell you the additional benefits accruing from Vaisvadeva — benefits which are most guarded secrets, not to be disclosed to any.

Those who offer gifts in respect of the Sun, leaving Vayu and the Supreme lord Нага stationed therein can be said to enjoy the essence. Lord Visnu is said to enjoy the part of essence, not of the total quintessence, although he can enjoy the entire essence by his might unchecked.

## \|\| 14.19 \|\|

*vakṣye ha sārānpunaranyānkhagendra śṛṇuṣva guhyaṃ paramādareṇa \|*  
*drākṣādayaḥ sarva eva tvasārāḥ kālādiduṣṭā bhāvaduṣṭāḥ padārthāḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. О lord of birds, I quote another list of essences of substances. Juicy substances, grapes and the like lose their quintessence with the lapse of time.

## \|\| 14.20-28 \|\|

*atipakvānantaraṃ tu tathā dinacatuṣṭaye \|*  
*asārāḥ kaluṣā jñeyāstathā jaṃbūphalaṃ smṛtam \|\| 20 \|\|*

*māsasyānantaraṃ vīndra tvasāraṃ panasaṃ smṛtam \|*  
*ṣaṇmāsānantaraṃ vīndra kharjūraṃ tiktavatsmṛtam \|\| 21 \|\|*

*ārdraṃ pūtaṃ nārikelaṃ sphoṭanānantaraṃ prabho \|*  
*ahorātrānantaraṃ tu asāraṃ parikīrtitam \|\| 22 \|\|*

*śuṣkabhūtaṃ nārikelaṃ khajūraṃ tu yathā tathā \|*  
*pakṣasyānantaraṃ cūtamasāraṃ parikīrtitam \|\| 23 \|\|*

*varṣasyānantaraṃ vīndra pūgīphalamudāhṛtam \|*  
*ghaṭikānantaraṃ vīndra tāṃbūlaṃ parikīrtitam \|\| 24 \|\|*

*yāmasyānantaraṃ cānnaṃ sūpānnaṃ pāyasaṃ tathā \|*  
*bhakṣyaṃ ca kvathitaṃ vīndra asāraṃ parikīrtitam \|\| 25 \|\|*

*tripakṣānantaraṃ vīndra tailapakvaṃ tathā smṛtam \|*  
*caturyāmānantaraṃ ca tvasāraṃ ghṛtapakvakam \|\| 26 \|\|*

*triyāmānantaraṃ śākā niḥ sārāḥ parikīrtitāḥ \|*  
*jaṃbīraṃ śṛṅgabere dhātrī karpūraṃ ca cūtakam \|\| 27 \|\|*

*vatsarānantaraṃ vīndra niḥ sāraṃ parikīrtitam \|*  
*parpaṭaḥ pakṣamātreṇa niḥ sāraḥ parikīrtitaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

20-28. Ripe grapes lose their essence after four days; the rose apple after the same period; the bread fruit after a month, the date after six months, the cocoanut after twentyfour hours since its breaking forth; the cocoanut and the date after they go dry, the mango after a fortnight, the areca nut after a year, the betel after twentyfour minutes; the cooked food, soup, milk-rice after three hours, the foodstuff roasted in the oil after a month and a half, the food roasted in ghee after twelve hours; the vegetables after nine hours; the citron, ginger, amalaka, camphor and mango lose their value after a year.

Parpata loses essence after a fortnight. The holy basil is always full of essence. Dry or green it is always fresh.

## \|\| 14.29-33 \|\|

*tulasī sarvadā sārā ekādaśyāmapi dvija \|*  
*ārdrā vāpyathavā śuṣkā sārdrā sāravatī smṛtā \|\| 29 \|\|*

*ekādaśyāṃ tu tulasī sārā grāhyā manīṣibhiḥ \|*  
*tvacā nāseṃdriyeṇāpi na tu jihvendriyeṇa ca \|\| 30 \|\|*

*ekādaśyāṃ harerannaṃ niḥ sāraṃ parikīrtitam \|*  
*ekādaśyāṃ harestīrthaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ khageśvara \|\| 31 \|\|*

*ekavāre ca sāraṃ syāddvivāre ca tatodhikam \|*  
*ekādaśyāṃ mahābhāga tīrthaṃ gandhādimiśritam \|\| 32 \|\|*

*asāramiti saṃproktaṃ tathā svādūdamiśritam \|*  
*ekādaśyāṃ hareḥ sāraṃ kṣīraṃ sarpirmadhūdakam \|\| 33 \|\|*

29-33. The holy basil, as thought of containing essence even on the EkadasI (eleventh) day should be held either by the hand or the nose, but never by the organ of the tongue.

On EkadasI, the day sacred to Visnu, the cooked food loses essence.

On EkadasI О lord of birds the holy centre of Visnu is full of essence for the whole of the day, exceeds in essence on the second day (so on and so forth). О blessed one, the holy' places of bath, if polluted by the articles of worship, scent, etc., lose their essence, similarly if mixed with sweet water.

О lord of birds, on Ekadagi, sacred to Visnu, milk, ghee, honey and water are full of essence, while ether stuffs are without essence. The best of the sages have declared thus.

## \|\| 14.34 \|\|

*niḥ sāraṃ manujendrāṇāmiti vedavidāṃ matam \|*  
*āṣāḍhamāse garuḍa śāko niḥ sāra ucyate \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. In the month of Asadha, О Garuda, the vegetable loses all essences.

## \|\| 14.35 \|\|

*māsi bhādrapade vīndra hyasāraṃ dadhi cocyate \|*  
*kṣīraṃ tu hyāśvine māse niḥ sāraṃ parikīrtitam \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. In the month of Bhadrapada, О lord of birds, the curd loses all essences. In the month of Asvina the milk loses all essences.

## \|\| 14.36 \|\|

*ūrdhvapuṇḍragadāhīnā nāryasāreti gīyate \|*  
*haribhaktivihīnā ye hyasurāḥ parikīrtitāḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. If a woman does not put the traditional mark Urdhvapundra on her forehead she becomes devoid of essence. Those, who are averse to devotion of Hari are called asuras.

## \|\| 14.37 \|\|

*harināmavihīnaṃ tu mukhaṃ niḥ sāramucyate \|*  
*harinaivedyahīnastu pāko niḥ sāra ucyate \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. The mouth devoid of Hari's name is without an essence. The cooked food, a part of which is not offered to Hari is essenceless.

## \|\| 14.38-41 \|\|

*tridinaiścātasīpuṣpaṃ niḥ sāraṃ parikīrtitam \|*  
*praharaṃ mallikā sāraṃ jātī tu praharārdhakam \|\| 38 \|\|*

*triyāmaṃ śatapatraṃ syātkaravīramaharniśam \|*  
*ghaṭikāvadhi sāraṃ syātpārijātaṃ khageśvara \|\| 39 \|\|*

*trivarṣaṃ kesaraṃ phalga sāramityucyate budhaiḥ \|*  
*kastūrī daśavarṣaṃ tu karpūraṃ varṣamātrakam \|\| 40 \|\|*

*sasāramiti saṃproktaṃ candanaṃ sarvadā smṛtam \|*  
*śuddhanniḥ sārabhūtāṃśca vakṣye śṛṇu khageśvara \|\| 41 \|\|*

38-41. The hemp blossom becomes essenceless after three days.
