Mallika (Jasmine) for three hours and jati (a kind of jasmine) for half that period; a lotus after nine hours, Karavlra after twentyfour hours, Parijata after twentyfour minutes, saffron after three years, milk after ten years, camphor after a year, sandal for all times.

I shall now tell you of things that have no essence.

## \|\| 14.42-47 \|\|

*tuṣā medhyā āranālaṃ puṇyakaṃ bhiḥ saṭā tathā \|*  
*upodvrajī alābūśca bṛhatkośātakī tathā \|\| 42 \|\|*

*vṛntākaṃ cukraśākaśca bilvamauduṃvaraṃ tathā \|*  
*palāñjurlaśunaṃ vṛntaṃ kalañjaṃ ca tathā dvijā \|\| 43 \|\|*

*etatsarvatra kāle ca niḥ sāramiti kīrtitam \|*  
*ekādaśyāṃ vaiśvadevaṃ śrāddhaṃ tarpaṇameva ca \|\| 44 \|\|*

*mantreṇa pretadahanamasāraṃ parikīrtitam \|*  
*havirnārāyaṇo devo etāṃśca hyaśubhānra sān \|\| 45 \|\|*

*na gṛhṇāti na gṛhṇāti na gṛhṇāti hariḥ svayam \|*  
*tathāpi sarvaṃ jānāti jīvānāṃ pāpakarmaṇām \|\| 46 \|\|*

*āsvādanaṃ kārayati svayaṃ nāsvādate hariḥ \|*  
*asārabhojanaṃ caiva jīvānāṃ karmajaṃ phalam \|\| 47 \|\|*

42-47. The chaff of gram, Medhya (a fruitbearing plant), gruel, the holy basil, parched or fried grain Upodvaji gourd, Kosalaki (?) the egg plant, tamarind, Bilva fruit, Udumbara fruit, onion, garlic — these are essenceless in all times.

The performance of Vaisvadeva-^raddha on EkadasI, offering of water libation to the manes, cremation of the dead with the mantras are always essenceless.

Lord Нага does not accept such inauspicious gifts. He, the omniscient lord, makes sinners reap their evil deeds while he himself does not taste them. The partaking of essenceless food is the result of their evil deeds.

## \|\| 14.48 \|\|

*amukhyabhojino jīvāḥ kuntyādyā mukhyabhojinaḥ \|*  
*śubhāni ca pibedviṣṇuraśubhaṃ no pibedvibhuḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Common people do not partake of essential food. On the other hand, noble persons like Kuntl and others partake of essential food. Lord Visnu himself drinks the sacred but not the polluted water.

## \|\| 14.49 \|\|

*ko vadettasya ceṣṭāṃ tu pūrṇānando hariḥ svayam \|*  
*na tena sadṛśaḥ kopi deśe kāle ca vidyate \|*  
*tasyāvatārānvakṣyahaṃ śṛṇu pakṣīndrasattama \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Who can tell the movements of Hari? The lord is of blissful nature. There is none equal to him at any time or place. О lord of birds, now I shall tell you all about the j incarnations of Hari

## Chapter 15

## \|\| 15.1 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*athāvatā rānpuruṣākhyo hariśca gato dhyānaṃ kartumīśo mahātmā \|*  
*prādurbabhūvākhilasadguṇārṇavaḥ sa eva viṣṇuḥ sa ca bījabhūtaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Shri Krsna said: — Lord Hari, the primeval being, thought of incarnating himself. He appeared as Visnu, the repository of all virtuous deeds.

## \|\| 15.2 \|\|

*yo bījabhūtaḥ puruṣākhya viṣṇuḥ sa evābhūdvāsudevo mahātmā \|*  
*sṛṣṭiṃ kartuṃ puruṣākhyasya vāyormāyākhyāyāṃ mūlarūpo yathā'sa \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Lord Visnu, the germ of all creation, incarnated as Vasudeva. He entered the womb of Maya and created Vayu

## \|\| 15.3 \|\|

*yo vāsudevastu sa eva jātaḥ saṃkarṣaṇākhyo khilasadguṇātmā \|*  
*sṛṣṭiṃ kartuṃ sūtrabhūtasya vāyorjayākhyāyaṃ pūrṇasaṃvitparātmā \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. He entered the womb of Jay a and was born as Sankarsana (Balarama).

## \|\| 15.4 \|\|

*sa evaṃ saṃkarṣaṇanāmadheyaḥ pradyumnanāmā ca sa eva viśrutaḥ \|*  
*sarasvatībhāratīsarjanārthaṃ sa eva devyā mūlarūpo babhūva \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. The lord was born as Pradyumna. He created Sarasvatl, Bharat! and the Goddess (Durga).

## \|\| 15.5 \|\|

*sṛṣṭvā yuktaṃ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ kalābhirmahattattvaṃ sūkṣmarūpaṃ sa eva \|*  
*sāhaṅkāraṃ krīḍayāmāsa devaḥ śṛṇu tvaṃ vai ṣoḍaśākhyāḥ kalāśca \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. He created Mahat (in subtle form) with, sixteen digits accompanied by ego. I shall now tell you the sixteen digits.

## \|\| 15.6-7 \|\|

*bhūtāni karmendriyapañcakāni jñānendriyāṇīha tathā manaśca \|*  
*tato babhūva hyaniruddhasaṃjñako jīvāṃśca saṃgṛhya supūrṇaśaktiḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

*soyaṃ viriñcyādisamastadevān sthūlena dehena sasarja nāthaḥ \|*  
*tathā sa viṣṇuḥ puruṣābhidhastu sanatkumāratvamavāpa vīndra \|\| 7 \|\|*

6-7. They are: Five primary elements (mahabhutas) five organs of actions (karmen- driyas) and five organs of knowledge (jnanendriyas) and one mind.

Thereafter was born Aniruddha. The omnipotent lord gathered Souls and created Gods Brahma adn others and invested them with the gross body. О lord of birds, then the primeval Purusa Visnu was born as Sanat Kumara.

## \|\| 15.8 \|\|

*ananyasādhyaṃ brahmacaryaṃ ca kartuṃ daśendriyāṇāṃ śoṣaṇārthaṃ sadaiva \|*  
*sanandanādau paṭhitaḥ kumārastasmānnānyo nātra vicāryamasti \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. The lord then became Sanandana and in that form he practised celibacy and subdued the ten organs of senses, a feat which cannot be accomplished by everybody. The prince Sanandana was no other than the lord himself.

## \|\| 15.9 \|\|

*sa eva viṣṇuḥ sūkaratvaṃ hyavāpa kṣopaṇīmuddhartuṃ daityavapurnihantum \|*  
*hiraṇyākṣaṃ sajjanānāṃ khagendra tathā bhūmeḥ sthāpanārthaṃ ca devaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Lord Visnu became a hog and took the earth out of waters. He slew Hiranyaksa, protected the pious and established rule of law over the earth.

## \|\| 15.10 \|\|

*tato harirmadvidāsatvamāpānṛṣerbhāryāyāṃ yāminyāṃ yo mahātmā \|*  
*tatrāvatāre pañcarātraṃ samagramupādeṣṭuṃ nāpa dānaṃ svatantraḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. The lord incarnated himself as Mahldasa from his mother Yaminl and father who was not a sage. In his incarnation as Mahidasa he preached philosophy of Pancaratra.

## \|\| 15.11 \|\|

*sa eva viṣṇuḥ samabhūdbradaryāṃ nārāyaṇākhyaḥ śamalāpahaśca \|*  
*tapastaptuṃ śikṣayituṃ tvṛṣīṇāṃ tiraskartuṃ hyapsarasāṃ sahasram \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Visnu, known as Narayana, was born in Badarl for effacing sin and destroying impurities. He practiced penance, taught sages and humbled celestial women.

## \|\| 15.12 \|\|

*tato hariḥ kapilatvaṃ hyavāpya tirohitānkālabalena tattvān \|*  
*caturviśatiṃ saṃśayaṃ coddhariṣyannupādiśaccāsuraye mahātmā \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. The lord became Kapila, explained tattvas, removed doubts and instructed Asuri.

## \|\| 15.13 \|\|

*sa eva dataḥ samabhūdrameśonasūyāyāmatrirūpaḥ parātmā \|*  
*ānvīkṣikiṃ nāma sutarkavidyāmalarkanāmne pradadāttāṃ mahātmā \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. The lord was born of Aid and Anasuya. He was known as Datta. He imparted instructions in logic to his disciple Alarka.

## \|\| 15.14 \|\|

*sa eva vaṃśepyabhavadraveśca ākūtyāṃ yaḥ saccidānandarūpaḥ \|*  
*svāyaṃbhuvaṃ yattu manvantaraṃ ca devaiḥ sākaṃ pālayāmāsa vīndra \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Born in the Solar race from Akuti and constituted of permanency (consciousness and bliss) he ruled in the Svayambhuva Manvantara together with the Devas.

## \|\| 15.15 \|\|

*sa eva viṣṇuḥ sa urukramobhūdāgnīdhraputryāṃ merudevyāṃ ca nābheḥ \|*  
*vidyāratānāṃ mānināṃ sarvadaivamatyāścaryaṃ darśayituṃ ca vīndra \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. О lord of birds, the widestrider Visnu was born of Nabhi and MarudevI, the daughter of Agnidhra and showed marvels to the honourable learned men of Bharata.

## \|\| 15.16 \|\|

*tato harirjagṛhe kūrmarūpaṃ surāsurāṇāmudadhiṃ vimathnatām \|*  
*pṛṣṭhe dhartuṃ mandaraṃ parvataṃ ca brahmāṇḍaṃ vā dhartumīśo mahātmā \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Lord Hari incarnated himself as tortoise supporting the Mandam moimtain when it was being used by gods and asuras for churning the ocean.

## \|\| 15.17-18 \|\|

*tato hariḥ prādurabhūnmahātmā dhanvantarirnāma harinmaṇidyutiḥ \|*  
*apathyadoṣānparihartumeva haste gṛhītvā pūrṇakubhaṃ sudhābhiḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

*tato harirjagṛhe śrīvapuśca yanmohinīti pravadanti loke \|*  
*udvṛttānāṃ ditijānāṃ mahātmā samyakteṣāṃ vañcayituṃ harirbalam \|\| 18 \|\|*

17-18. Lord Hari was born as Dhanvantari who was as bright as an emerald.

Lest the undesirable things should occur, the lord seized the jar of nectar and assumed the form of a pretty damsel Mohinl and deluded the puffed Asuras.

## \|\| 15.19 \|\|

*tato hariḥ prādurabhūnmahātmā nṛsiṃhanāmā bhagavānanantaḥ \|*  
*daityo hiraṇyakaśipuśca tathorudeśe saṃsthāpitaḥ karajairdāritaśca \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. The noble lord incarnated himself as Nrsimha and killed Hiranyakasipu by tearing up his thighs with his nails.

## \|\| 15.20 \|\|

*tato harirbhagavānvāmanobhūdadityāṃ vai kaśyapāddavadevaḥ \|*  
*indrāyedaṃ dātukāmaḥ khagendra tadarthaṃ vai pāvituṃ sovituṃ ca \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. The lord was born as Vamana to Aditi and Kasyapa for restoring to Indra the land which had been usurped by Bali.

## \|\| 15.21 \|\|

*tato harirjamadagneḥ sutobhūlloke sarve parśurāmaṃ vadanti \|*  
*brahmadviṣāṃ kṣatriyāṇāṃ ca vīndra bhūmiṃ niḥ kṣatrāṃ kartukāmo maheśaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. The lord was born to sage Jamadagni and was known as Parasurama. He wanted to liberate the earth from the yoke of Ksatriyas who hated the Brahmanas.

## \|\| 15.22 \|\|

*tatobhavadvyāsarūpī sa viṣṇuścaturvāraṃ rāghavāsyāpi pūrṇaḥ \|*  
*parāśarātsatyavatyāṃ babhūva pailādibhirvedabhāgāṃśca kartum \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. The lord was born as Vyasa from Parasara-Satyavati. He divided the Vedas into four Samhitas for his pupils Paila and others.

## \|\| 15.23 \|\|

*tato harī raghuvaṃśevatīrṇaḥ kausalyāyāṃ rāghavaḥ sūryavaṃśe \|*  
*samudrādorvigrahaṃ kartumīśo haṃ tuṃ bhūmyāṃ rāvaṇādīṃśca vīndra \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. The lord was born in the house of Raghu in the Solar dynasty, to Kausalya and Dasaratha. He challenged the ocean to fight and subjugated him. He fought against Ravana and killed him.

## \|\| 15.24 \|\|

*tato harirvyāsarūpī babhūva aṣṭāviṃśe dvapare jñānarūpī \|*  
*parāśarātsatyavatyāṃ mahātmā svayaṃ vedān saṃvibhaktuṃ ca devaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Then, in the twenty-eighth Dvapara age, lord Visnu incarnated as Vyasa.

Born as the son of Parasara and Satyavatl, he classified the Vedas into four divisions.

## \|\| 15.25 \|\|

*tato hariḥ kṛṣṇarūpī babhūva devakyāṃ vai vasudevātsa viṣṇuḥ \|*  
*kaṃsādīnvai nitarāṃ hantukāmaḥ samyakpātuṃ pāṇḍavāṃścāpi vīndra \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. The lord was born as Krsna to Devaki and Vasudeva. He killed Kansa and his associates, and protected the Pandavas.

## \|\| 15.26 \|\|

*tataḥ kalau saṃpravṛtte haristu saṃmohanārthaṃ cāsurāṇāṃ khagendra \|*  
*nāmnā buddho kīkaṭeṣu prajāto vedapramāṇaṃ nirākartumeva \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Then in the Kali age, the lord was born in the Kikatas as Buddha. He deluded the asuras and flouted the Vedas.

## \|\| 15.27 \|\|

*tato hariḥ kalkisaṃjñaśca vīndra utpatsyate yugayormadhyasaṃdhau \|*  
*dasyuprāyānbhūmipānvai nihantuṃ nāmnā harirviṣṇuguptasya gehe \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Then, at the interval between Kalki and Sweat age, the lord will be born in the house of Visnugupta and slay the rulers who have turned thieves.

## \|\| 15.28-29 \|\|

*keśavādyāścaturviṃśatirvai saṃkarṣaṇādayaḥ \|*  
*viśvādaya sahasraṃ ca parādyā amitāḥ smṛtāḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

*avatārā hyasaṃkhyātā viṣṇornārāyaṇasya ca \|*  
*svayaṃ nārāyaṇāste te nāṇumātraṃ vibhidyate \|\| 29 \|\|*

28-29. The incarnations of Visnu Narayana are twentyfour: beginning with Kesava and Sankarsana. Those beginning with Visva are one thousand. Those beginning with Para are, enumerable.

## \|\| 15.30 \|\|

*balatorūpataścāpi guṇataśca kathañcana \|*  
*anantonantaguṇataḥ pūrṇo viṣṇurna cānyathā \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. These incarnations are of lord Narayana himself. There is not the slightest variation is eternal by virtue of his eternal qualities, not otherwise.

## Chapter 16

## \|\| 16.1 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*mahālakṣmyāḥ svarūpaṃ ca avatārānkhageśvara \|*  
*śṛṇu samyaṅ mahābhāga tajjñānasya vinirṇayam \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Sri Krsna said: — О lord of birds, now, listen to the nature of MahalaksmI, her incarnations and the extent of knowledge peculiar to her.

## \|\| 16.2 \|\|

*īśādanyasya jagato hyātmo locana eva tu \|*  
*viṣayīkurute tatsyājjñānaṃ lakṣmyāḥ prakīrtitam \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. О lord of birds, the universe is distinct from the lord. The personal soul is the eye that perceives the universe. The knowledge of the universe is the knowledge belonging to Laksmi.

## \|\| 16.3 \|\|

*nityāviyoginī devī haripādaikasaṃśrayā \|*  
*nityamuktā nityabuddhā mahālakṣmīḥ prakīrtitā \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. The eternal Goddess Laksmi is inseparable from the lord. The feet of the lord are her only shelter. She is a released soul. She is always awakened.

## \|\| 16.4-5 \|\|

*mūlasya ca harerbhāryā lakṣmīḥ saṃprakīrtitā \|*  
*puṃso hibhāryā prakṛtiḥ prakṛteścā bhimāninī \|\| 4 \|\|*

*sṛṣṭiṃ kartuṃ guṇānvīndra puruṣeṇa saha prabho \|*  
*tamaḥ pānaṃ tathā kartuṃ prakṛtyākhyā tadābhavat \|\| 5 \|\|*

4-5. О lord of birds, she is called Prakrti, for, she creates the Universe by her qualities — sattva, rajas and tamas and consumes the world by tamas.

## \|\| 16.6-8 \|\|

*vāsudevasya bhāryā tu māyā nāmnī prakīrtitā \|*  
*saṃkarṣaṇasya bhāryā tu jayeti parikīrtitā \|\| 6 \|\|*

*aniruddhasya bhāryā tu śāntā nāmnīti kīrtitā \|*  
*kṛtiḥ pradyumnabhāryāpiṃ sṛṣṭiṃ kartuṃ babhūvaha \|\| 7 \|\|*

*viṣṇupatnī kīrtitā ca śrīdevī sattvamāninī \|*  
*tamobhimāninī durgā kanyaketi prakīrtitā \|\| 8 \|\|*

6-8. As the consort of Vasudeva, she is caged Maya; as the consort of Sankarsana, Jaya; as the consort of Aniruddha, Santa; as the consort of Pradyumna, Krti; as the consort of Visnu, Laksmi — the presiding deity of Sattva guna. As the presiding deity of tomoguna, she is Durga or Kanyaka. As the sister of Krsna the son of Nanda, she is caved Kanyaka.

## \|\| 16.9-10 \|\|

*kṛṣṇāvatāre kanyeva nandaputrānujā hi sā \|*  
*rajobhimānibhūdevī bhāryā sā sūkarasya ca \|\| 9 \|\|*

*vedābhimāninī vīndra annapūrṇā prakīrtitā \|*  
*nārāyaṇasya bhāryā tu lakṣmīrūpā tvajā smṛtā \|\| 10 \|\|*

9-10. As the goddess of earth, the presiding deity of rajas, she is the consort of Boar. As the presiding deity of the Vedas, she is Annapurna. As the consort of Narayana, she is Laksmi, the unborn.

## \|\| 16.11 \|\|

*yajñākhyasya harerbhāryā dakṣiṇā saṃprakīrtitā \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. As the consort of Yajna Varaha, she is Daksina.

## \|\| 16.12 \|\|

*jayantī vṛṣabhasyaiva patnī saṃparikīrtitā \|*  
*videhaputrī sītā tu rāmabhāryā prakīrtitā \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. As the consort of Vrsabha, she is Jayanti. As daughter of janaka and the wife of Rama, she is SIta.

## \|\| 16.13-14 \|\|

*rukmiṇīsatyabhāmā ca bhārye kṛṣṇasya kīrtite \|*  
*ityādikā hyanantāścāpyāvatārāḥ pṛthagvidhāḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

*ramāyāḥ saṃti viprendra bhedahīnāḥ parasparam \|*  
*anantānantaguṇakādviṣṇornyūnāḥ prakīrtitāḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

13-14. RukminI and Satyabhama were the wives of lord Krsna. The incarnations of Laksmi are many, О lord of birds and there is no difference among them. The forms of Laksmi, О bird, are inferior to Visnu who possesses enumerable distinctions.

## \|\| 16.15 \|\|

*atha brahmā ca vāyuśca śriyaḥ koṭiguṇādhamau \|*  
*vakṣye ca brahmaṇo rūpaṃ śṛṇu pakṣīndrasattama \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Brahma and Vayu are inferior to Visnu by millions of qualities. Now, I shall tell you about the nature of Brahman, О lord of birds.

## \|\| 16.16 \|\|

*vāsudevātsamutpanno māyāyāṃ ca khageśvara \|*  
*sa eva puruṣonāma viriñca iti kīrtitaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Born of Lord Visnu and his consort Maya, Brahma is called Virinca.

## \|\| 16.17 \|\|

*aniruddhāttu śāntāyāṃ mahattattvatanustvabhūt \|*  
*tadā mahānviriñceti saṃjñāmāpa khageśvara \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Born of Animddha and his consort Santa, and constituted of intellect (Mahat), he obtained the appellation — Mahavirinca.

## \|\| 16.18 \|\|

*rajasātra samutpanno māyāyāṃ vāsudevataḥ \|*  
*vidhisaṃjño viriñcaḥ sa jñātavyaḥ pakṣisattama \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Born of Vasudeva from Maya by rajas, he (Virinci) is called Vidhi.

## \|\| 16.19 \|\|

*brahmāṇḍāntaḥ padmanābho yo jātaḥ kamalāsanaḥ \|*  
*sa cartumukhasaṃjñāṃ cāpyavāpa khagasattama \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Born of Universal egg from the navel lotus of Visnu and seated in the lotusitself, he is called Caturmukha — the four-faced god, О best of birds.

## \|\| 16.20 \|\|

*evaṃ catvārirūpāṇi brahmaṇaḥ kīrtitāni ca \|*  
*vāyornāmāni vakṣyehaṃ śṛṇu pakṣīndrasattama \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Thus they declare the four names of Brahma. I shall now tell you the names of Vayu, О lord of birds.

## \|\| 16.21 \|\|

*saṃkarṣaṇācca garuḍa jayāyāṃ yo vabhūva ha \|*  
*sa vāyuḥ prathamo jñeyo pradhāna iti kīrtitaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Born of Sankarsana and Jaya, he is called Vayu known by the appellation Pradhana.

## \|\| 16.22-23 \|\|

*lokaceṣṭāpradatvātsa sūtranāmnāpi kīrtitaḥ \|*  
*badarīsthasya viṣṇośca dhairyeṇa stavanāya saḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

*dhṛtirūpaṃ yayau vāyustasmāddhṛtiriti smṛtaḥ \|*  
*yogyānāṃ haribhaktānāṃ dhṛtirūpeṇa saṃsthitaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

22-23. As he activates men to work he is called Sutra-Vayu who assumed the form of dhrti to puaise Narayana at Badari with steadfast devotion. He stays in the heart of his loyal devotees and is called Hari. Present in the heart of all, V ayu. ever remembers lord Hari.

## \|\| 16.24-25 \|\|

*yato hṛdi sthito vāyustato vai dhṛtisaṃjñakaḥ \|*  
*sarveṣāṃ ca dṛdi sthitvā smarate sarvadā harim \|\| 24 \|\|*

*ato vāyuḥsthitirnāma babhūva khagasattama \|*  
*athavā vāyurevaikaḥ śvetadvīpagataṃ harim \|\| 25 \|\|*

24-25. Vayu is, therefore, called Sthiti, О lord of birds. Or Vayu alone remembers Hari. Staying in the White Island (svetadvvpa), О lord of birds, he is caged Smrti.

## \|\| 16.26 \|\|

*sadā smarati vai vīndra atosau smṛtisaṃjñakaḥ \|*  
*sarveṣāṃ ca hṛdisthitvā jñāto viṣṇorudīraṇāt \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Vayu abides in the hearts of all peoplethis is what Lord Visnu has declared Hence, he is caged Mukti.

## \|\| 16.27 \|\|

*ato me muktināmābhūdvāyureva na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*jñānadvāreṇa bhaktānāṃ muktido madanujñayā \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. With my consent, he confers liberation to his devotees by imparting knowledge to them. Vayu is called mukti.

## \|\| 16.28 \|\|

*yato sau vāyurevaiko muktināmā bhūvaha \|*  
*viṣṇau bhaktiṃ vardhyati bhaktānāṃ hṛdi saṃsthitaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Abiding in the hearts of his devotees he multiplies their devotion for Visnu. Therefore, he is called the devotee of Visnu.

## \|\| 16.29 \|\|

*atosau viṣṇubhaktaśca kīrtito nātra saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*eṣosau sarvajīvānāṃ cittasaṃjñānameva ca \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. He constitutes the consciousness of living beings. He is called consciousness.

## \|\| 16.30 \|\|

*cittarūpo yato vāyurataścittamiti smṛtaḥ \|*  
*prabhuḥ prabhūṇāṃ garuḍa sodarāṇāṃ ca sarvaśaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. О bird, he is the lord of gods and of his associates. Hence, he is called an Overlord.

## \|\| 16.31 \|\|

*atastu vāyurevaiko mahāprabhuriti smṛtaḥ \|*  
*sarveṣāṃ ca hṛhi sthitvā balaṃ paśyati sattama \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Staying in the heart of the people, О lord, he observes their strength. О lord of Vinata, he is, therefore, called Bala.

## \|\| 16.32 \|\|

*ato balamiti hyākhyāmavāpa vinatāsuta \|*  
*sarveṣāṃ ca hṛdi sthitvā putrapautrādikairjanaiḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. A biding in the hearts of people he conducts sacrifices through their sons and grandsons. He is, therefore, called a sacrificer.

## \|\| 16.33 \|\|

*yājanaṃ kurute nityamatosau yaṣṭṛsaṃjñakaḥ \|*  
*anantakalpamārabhya vāyuparyantameva ca \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. From Anantakalpa to Vayukalpa, there is no complexity in Yoga. Therefore, Rju is called Yogya.

## \|\| 16.34-35 \|\|

*vakratvaṃ nāsti yogasya ṛjuryogya iti smṛtaḥ \|*  
*yogasya vakratā nāma kāmyatā haripūjane \|*  
*īśarudrādikānāṃ ca kāmyena haripūjanam \|\| 34 \|\|*

*kasyacittvatha pakṣīndra hyatastvanṛjavaḥ smṛtāḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

34-35. Complexity of Yoga arises from Hari's worship or from the worship of Isa, Rudra, and others with an object in view. Those who perform such worship are called anrjavas.

## \|\| 16.36 \|\|

*ṛṣyādīnāṃ ca madhyepi kāmyena haripūjanam \|*  
*ato na ṛjavo jñeyā manuṣyāṇāṃ ca kā kathā \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Among the sages too, those who worship Hari for attaining the object of their desire are called anrjus. The same practice is current among the people.

## \|\| 16.37 \|\|

*yāvatkāmyasaparyāṃ vai na jahāti narottamaḥ \|*  
*tathā ṛṣyādayaścaiva mokṣasya paripanthinīm \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\[He cannot obtain release\] until he gives up Hari's worship with an object in view, for desire is an obstacle in the path of realization, even for the sages.

## \|\| 16.38 \|\|

*anādikālamārabhya karmajanyā ca vāsanā \|*  
*mokṣādhikāriṇaḥ sarve kurvate kasya pūjanam \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Impressions of countless activities performed in previous ages are obstacles to the person seeking for liberation. He should, therefore, give up worship which aims at the fulfilment of desire.

## \|\| 16.39 \|\|

*naṣṭaprāyaṃ ca tatsarvaṃ guroḥ saṃjñānabodhakāt \|*  
*prāpyayogaṃ samācarya ante mokṣamavāpnuyāt \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. By enlightenment received from Guru's instructions all activities practically cease to be. Being initiated in Yoga and practising the same, the aspirant achieves liberation after all.

## \|\| 16.40 \|\|

*kāmyena pūjanaṃ viṣṇoraiśvaryaṃ pradadāti ca \|*  
*jñānaṃ ca viparītaṃ syāttena yātyadharaṃ tamaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. The worship of the lord with an object in view imparts riches which controvert knowledge and lead to darkness.

## \|\| 16.41-44 \|\|

*tadeva viparītaṃ cejjñānāya parikīrtitam \|*  
*śilāyāṃ viṣṇubuddhistu viṣṇubuddhirdvije tathā \|\| 41 \|\|*

*salile tīrthabuddhistu roṇukāyāṃ tathaiva ca \|*  
*śive sūrye paṇmukhe ca viṣṇubuddhiḥ khageśvara \|\| 42 \|\|*

*ityādyamakhilaṃ jñānaṃ viparītamiti smṛtam \|*  
*śilādyeṣu ca sarveṣu aikyenava vicintanam \|\| 43 \|\|*

*viṣṇubuddhiriti proktaṃ na tu tatrasthavedanam \|*  
*anādyanantakālepi kāmyena haripūjanam \|\| 44 \|\|*

41-44. If the lord is worshipped without any motive that worship leads him to perfect knowledge.

To take a stone, say Salagrama or a Brahmana for lord Visnu or water for a holy place or Shiva, Surya or Karttikeya for Visnu, О lord of birds, is a false knowledge. To take stone, etc., as nothing but an entity inseparable from Visnu is called Visnubuddhi. One should not differentiate one form of the lord from the other.

## \|\| 16.45 \|\|

*yato nāsti tato vāyurṛjuryogyaḥ prakīrtitaḥ \|*  
*anyeṣāṃ sarvadā nāsti ato na ṛjavaḥ smṛtāḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Vayu is called Rju the worthiest since he worshipped the lord without a motive. Those who worship the lord with a motive are called anrjus.

## \|\| 16.46 \|\|

*hariṃ darśayate vāpi aparokṣeṇa sarvadā \|*  
*mokṣādhikāriṇāṃ kāle ataḥ prajñeti kathyate \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. Vayu is called wisdom (prajna) since he shows the lord ever in sight to those who are on the verge of liberation.

## \|\| 16.47 \|\|

*parokṣeṇāpi sarveṣāṃ hariṃ darśayate sadā \|*  
*ato vāyuḥ sadā vīndra jñānamityeva kīrtitaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. He is called jnana (knowledge) as it shows the lord even beyond the range of sight.

## \|\| 16.48 \|\|

*hitāhitopadeṣṭṛtvādbhaktānāṃ hṛdaye sthitaḥ \|*  
*tataśca gurusaṃjñāṃ cāpyavāpa sa ca mārutaḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Stationed in the heart of devotees and showing them, what is wholesome and what is not, he is called Guru.

## \|\| 16.49 \|\|

*yogināṃ hṛdaye sthitvā sadhyāyati hariṃ param \|*  
*pārthakyenāpi taṃ dhyāyanmahādhyāteti sa smṛtaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Stationed in the heart of Yogins and meditating on the lord as a distinct entity he is called a meditator.

## \|\| 16.50 \|\|

*yadyogyatānusāreṇa vijānāti paraṃ harim \|*  
*rudrādau vidyamānāṃśca guṇāñjānāti sarvadā \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. As he comprehends the lord according to his ability and as he knows the qualities abiding in Rudra and other gods he is called Vijna, О best of birds.

## \|\| 16.51 \|\|

*ato vai vijñanāmāsau prokto hi khagasattama \|*  
*kāmyānāṃ karmaṇāṃ tyāgādvirāga iti sa smṛtaḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. He is non-attached Viraja since he has abandoned all activities associated with the fulfilment of desire.

## \|\| 16.52 \|\|

*athavāyogināṃ nityaṃ hṛdi sthitvā sa mārutaḥ \|*  
*vairāgyaṃ saṃjanayati virāga iti sa smṛtaḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Or he is called Viraja because abiding in the hearts of Yogins he creates, aversion in them for worldly pleasures.

## \|\| 16.53 \|\|

*devānāṃ puṇyapāpābhyāṃ sukhamevottarottaram \|*  
*tatsukhaṃ tūttareṣāṃ ca vāyuparyantameva ca \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. When the results of virtue and sin are exhausted, the gods, as far as Vayu, deserve perfect bliss.

## \|\| 16.54 \|\|

*devānāṃ ca ṛṣīṇāṃ ca uttamānāṃ nṛṇāṃ tathā \|*  
*sukhāṃśaṃ janayedvāyuryatotaḥ sukhasaṃjñakaḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. As Vayu creates objects of enjoyment for the pleasure of gods, sages and nobles, he is called joyful.

## \|\| 16.55 \|\|

*bhunakti sarvadā vīdraṃ tatra mukhyastu mārutaḥ \|*  
*duḥ khaśokādikaṃ kiñciddevānāṃ bhavati prabho \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. О lord of birds, Vayu is the principal deity who suffers both pleasure and pain.

## \|\| 16.56 \|\|

*taccāsurāveśavaśādityavehi na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*tajjīvasya bhavetkiñciddaityānāṃ kramaśo bhavet \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. Whatever calamity befalls the deities is due to the influence of Asuras.

## \|\| 16.57 \|\|

*yataḥ kaliścādhikaḥ syādato duḥ khīti sa smṛtaḥ \|*  
*daityānāṃ puṇyapāpābhyāṃ duḥ kha mevottarottaram \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Human soul suffers from affliction caused by the Daityas. People suffer often and anon specially in the Kali age.

## \|\| 16.58 \|\|

*tadduḥ khamuttareṣāṃ ca kaliparyantameva ca \|*  
*bhunakti sarvadā vīndra tataḥ kaliriti smṛtaḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. Vayu consumes all sorrows accruing both from virtue and vice in the Kali age. Hence, Vayu is called Kali.

## \|\| 16.59 \|\|

*sukhaharṣādikaṃ kiṃ ciddaityānāṃ bhavati prabho \|*  
*devāveśo bhavettasya nātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. О lord, whatever joys or pleasures accrue to the people are due to divine influences. There is not in the least any doubt in this.

## \|\| 16.60 \|\|

*devānāṃ nirayo nāsti daityānāṃ vinatāsuta \|*  
*sukhasvarūpaṃ tannāsti viṣayotthamapi dvija \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. О son of Vinata, Devas do not go to hell, and Daityas do not seek pleasure from the objects of pleasure.

## \|\| 16.61-62 \|\|

*viṣayotthaṃ kiñcidapi devāveśādudīritam \|*  
*tamo nāstyeva devānāṃ duḥ khaṃ nāsti svarūpataḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

*viṣayotthaṃ mahāduḥ khaṃ devānāṃ nāsti sarvadā \|*  
*duḥ khaśokādikaṃ kiṃ cidasurāveśato bhavet \|\| 62 \|\|*

61-62. Whatever pleasure they enjoy accrues to them from Devas. Devas have no tamas; they suffer from no pain by coming in contact with the objects of pleasure. Whatever pain they suffer is due to the influence of asuras.

## \|\| 16.63 \|\|

*ataḥ kaliḥ sadā duḥ khī sukhī vāyustu sarvadā \|*  
*manuṣyāṇā mṛṣīṇāṃ ca sukhaṃ duḥ khaṃ khageśvara \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. Hence, Kali is a sufferer, Vayu is joyous. Ordinary people and sages have both pleasure and pain.

## \|\| 16.64 \|\|

*bhavettatpuṇyipāpābhyāṃ puṇyabhogī ca mārutaḥ \|*  
*kaṣṭabhaṅgaḥ kalilayo nātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. Vayu enjoys the fruits of his virtuous deeds. He suffers from his sinful actions. It is hard to escape the results of good or bad activities.

## \|\| 16.65 \|\|

*prāṇādisukhaparyantā aṃśā ekonaviṃśatiḥ \|*  
*praviṣṭāḥ saṃti lokeṣu pṛthaksaṃti khageśvara \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. Partial incarnations of Lord Vayu from Prana to Sukha are nineteen in number. О lord of birds, the lord has descended on earth several times, each time with a separate identity:

## \|\| 16.66-67 \|\|

*mārutarevatārāṃśca śṛṇu pakṣīndrasattama \|*  
*caturdaśasu candreṣu dvitīyauyo virocanaḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

*sa vāyuriti saṃprokta indrādīnāṃ khageśvara \|*  
*haritattveṣu sarveṣu sa viṣvagyāvyatekṣaṇaḥ \|\| 67 \|\|*

66-67. О lord of birds, I shall explain to you the total incarnations of Vayu. Listen.

Of the fourteen Indras, the second is called Virocana who is identical with Vayu. With his eyes expanded all aroimd, he, the partial incarnation of Marut, is also called Rocana.

## \|\| 16.68 \|\|

*ato rocananāmāsau marudaṃśaḥ prakīrtitaḥ rāmāvatāre hanumānrāmakāryārthasādhakaḥ \|*  
*sa eva bhīmasenastu jāto bhūmyāṃ mahābalaḥ \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. When lord Rama incarnated on earth, Vayu was born as Hanuman for rendering assistance to Rama.

## \|\| 16.69 \|\|

*kṛṣṇāvatāre vijñeyo marudaṃśaḥ prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. When lord Krsna descended on earth, he was born as Bhima, the off-spring of Vayu.

## \|\| 16.70-72 \|\|

*maṇimānnāma daityastu saṃrākhyo bhaviṣyati \|*  
*sarveṣāṃ saṃkaraṃ yastu kariṣyati na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 70 \|\|*

*tena saṃkaranāmāsau bhaviṣyati khageśvara \|*  
*dharmānbhāgavatānsarvānvināśayati sarvathā \|\| 71 \|\|*

*tadā bhūmau vāsudevo bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*yajñārthaiḥ sadṛśo yasya nāsti loke caturdaśe \|\| 72 \|\|*

70-72. A demon named Maniman will be born as one called Sankara, who will, without doubt, pollute everything. This is why, О king of birds, his name will be Sankara; he will pollute and destroy all Bhagavata Dharmas. Then (Vayu) will, without doubt, come into being as Vasudeva; there will be none in the fourteen worlds to match him. lie will truly be in perfect wisdom

## \|\| 16.73 \|\|

*ataḥ sa prajñayā pūrṇo bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*avatārāstrayo vāyormataṃ bhāgavatābhidham \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. The full incarnations of Vayu are three. Their creed is one and the same which is propounded in the Bhagavata.

## \|\| 16.74 \|\|

*sthāpanaṃ duṣṭadamanaṃ dvayameva prayojanam \|*  
*nānyatprayojanaṃ vāyostathā vairocanātmake \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. The purpose of each and every incarnation is twofold: establishment of order and suppression of the wicked. There is no other purpose for the lord to incarnate.

## \|\| 16.75-76 \|\|

*avatāratraye vīndra duḥ khaṃ garbhādisaṃbhavam \|*  
*nāsti nāstyeva vāyostu tathā vairocanādike \|\| 75 \|\|*

*śukraśoṇitasaṃbandho hyavatāracatuṣṭaye \|*  
*nāsti nāstyeva pakṣīndra yato nāstyaśubhaṃ tataḥ \|\| 76 \|\|*

75-76. In the three incarnations of Vayu, viz. Vairocana etc., О lord of birds, there are no pangs of birth. In the four incarnations there is no fusion of semen and blood. Hence, in these four incarnations there is nothing inauspicious.

## \|\| 16.77 \|\|

*pūrvaṃ garbhaṃ samāśoṣya samaye prabhavasya ca \|*  
*prādurbhavati deveśī hyavatāracatuṣṭaye \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. In the four incarnations, at the hour of birth, the lord, at first, dries up the embryo before he is born out of the womb.

## \|\| 16.78-80 \|\|

*trayoviṃśatirūpāṇāṃ vāyoścaiva khageśvara \|*  
*rūpairṛjusvarūpaiśca brahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ \|\| 78 \|\|*

*satyameva na saṃdeho nityānandasukhādiṣu \|*  
*evameva vijānīyānnānyathā tu kathañcana \|\| 79 \|\|*

*etasya śravaṇādeva mokṣaṃ yānti na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*tadanantarajānvakṣye śṛṇu pakṣīndrasattama \|\| 80 \|\|*

78-80. Of the twenty-three forms of the Supreme lord Brahma, Vayu is one- In the enjoyment of eternal bliss, pleasure, etc., Vayu is at par with Brahma. This truth knows no variation; listening to this leads to release. О lord of birds, I shall now tell you about the descent of Vayu.

## \|\| 16.81 \|\|

*kṛtau pradyumnataścaiva samutpanne khageśvara \|*  
*striyau dve yamale caiva tayormadhye tu yadyikā \|\| 81 \|\|*

81\. In the Krta age, there were born from Pradyumna twins Savitrl and Sarasvatl.

## \|\| 16.82 \|\|

*vāṇītisaṃjñakāṃ vīndra brahmāṇīsaṃjñakāṃ viduḥ \|*  
*puruṣākhyaviriñcasya bhāryā sāvitrikā matā \|*  
*caturmukhasya bhāryā tu kīrtitā sā sarasvatī \|\| 82 \|\|*

82\. In between the two, was born Van!. Savitrl was married to Virinca, Sarasvatl to four-faced Brahma.

## \|\| 16.83 \|\|

*evaṃ trirūpaṃ vijñeyaṃ vāṇyāśca khagasattama \|*  
*vakṣye 'vatārān bhāratyāḥ samāhitamanāḥ śṛṇu \|\| 83 \|\|*

83\. These are the three forms of Vani. I shall now tell you about the incarnations of Bharatl. Please hear attentively.

## \|\| 16.84 \|\|

*sarvavedābhimānitvātsarvavedātmikā smṛtā \|*  
*mahādhyātuśca vāyostu bhāryāsā parikīrtitā \|\| 84 \|\|*

84\. Being the presiding deity of the Vedas she is die Veda itself. She is the mistress of Vayu, the great meditator.

## \|\| 16.85 \|\|

*jñānarūpasya vāyostu bhāryā sā parikīrtitā \|*  
*sadā sukhasvarūpatvādbhāratī tu sukhātmikā \|\| 85 \|\|*

85\. She is also the mistress of Vayu — incarnate. Being of the nature of joy she is identical with joy.

## \|\| 16.86 \|\|

*sukhasvarūpa vāyostu bhāryā sā parikīrtitā \|*  
*gurustu vāyurevoktastasmin bhaktiyutā satī \|\| 86 \|\|*

86\. She is the mistress of Vayu who is identical with joy. Vayu is the preceptor of Bharati. Since she is devoted to Vayu, the preceptor, she is called Gurubhakti. She is the mistress of Vayu, the great preceptor.

## \|\| 16.87-88 \|\|

*tatastu bhāratī nityā gurubhaktiriti smṛtā \|*  
*mahāgurorhi vāyośca bhāryā vai parikīrtitā \|\| 87 \|\|*

*harau snehayutatvācca hariprītiriti smṛtā \|*  
*dhṛtirūpasya vāyośca bhāryā sā parikīrtitā \|\| 88 \|\|*

87-88. She has devotion for Hari; hence, she is called Hari-Prlti. She is the mistress of Vayu of the form of dhrti.

## \|\| 16.89 \|\|

*sarvamantrābhimānitvātsarvamantrātmikā smṛtā \|*  
*mahāprabhośca vāyośca bhāryā vai sā prakīrtitā \|\| 89 \|\|*

89\. Since she is the presiding deity of all sacred texts, she is identical with them. She is the wife of lord Vayu.

## \|\| 16.90 \|\|

*bhujyante sarvabhogāstu viṣṇuprītyarthamevaca \|*  
*atastu bhāratī jñeyā bhujināmnā prakīrtitā \|\| 90 \|\|*

90\. She presents all objects of senses to lord Visnu for propitiating him or for his enjoyment. Hence, she is called Bhuji.

## \|\| 16.91 \|\|

*citrarūpasya vāyostu bhāryā sā parikīrtitā \|*  
*rocanendrasya bhāryā ca śraddhākhyā parikīrtitā \|\| 91 \|\|*

91\. She became the mistress of Citra. In the name of Shraddha she became the mistress of lord Rocana.

## \|\| 16.92 \|\|

*hanumāṃśca tadā jajñe tretāyāṃ pakṣisattama \|*  
*tadā śivākhyaviprācca jajñe sā bhāratī smṛtā \|\| 92 \|\|*

92\. О lord of birds, Hanuman, the son of Vayu, lived in the Treta age. Then, Bharati was born as the daughter of a Brahmin, Siva, by name.

## \|\| 16.93 \|\|

*na kevalaṃ bhāratī sāśacyādyaiścaiva saṃyutā \|*  
*tasminsaṃjanitāḥ sarvāḥ prāpuryogaṃ svabhartṛbhiḥ \|\| 93 \|\|*

93\. Not only Bharati and her companions such as SacI, etc., but also all the rest who were born of him were married to their partners.

## \|\| 16.94-95 \|\|

*anyageti ca vijñeyā kanyā tanmatisaṃjñikā \|*  
*tretānte saiva pakṣīndra śacyādyaiścaiva saṃyutā \|\| 94 \|\|*

*damayantyanalājjātā indraseneti cocyate \|*  
*nalaṃ nandayate yasmāttasmācca nalanandinī \|\| 95 \|\|*

94-95. His daughter named Mati was also called Anyaga (one who has relations with another than her partner). She was born together with SacI, etc., at the end of Treta, О lord of birds. born of Anala, she is known as Indrasena. As she delighted Nala, she is called the delighter of Nala.

## \|\| 16.96-97 \|\|

*tatra svabhartṛsaṃyogaṃ naiva cāpa khageśvara \|*  
*tatrānyagātvaṃ vijñeyaṃ puruṣasthena vāyunā \|\| 96 \|\|*

*kiñcitkālaṃ tathā sthitvā kanyaiva mṛti māpa sā \|*  
*śacyādisaṃyutā saiva drupadasya mahātmanaḥ \|\| 97 \|\|*

96-97. She was separated from her lord, since in her previous birth she had illicit relations with Vayu in human form.

In her previous birth Mati had died as a maiden together with Sad and others.

## \|\| 16.98 \|\|

*vedimadhyātsamudbhūtā bhīmasenārthameva ca \|*  
*tatrānyagātvaṃ nāstyeva yogaśca saha bhartṛbhiḥ \|\| 98 \|\|*

98\. She was born out of the sacrifice conducted by Drupada and was named Draupadl. She married Bhlmasena. The sin of illicit copulation with a person in her previous life did notvisit her in the next birth. Hence, she had no separation from her husband.

## \|\| 16.99 \|\|

*kevalā bhāratī jñeyā kāśirājasya kanyakā \|*  
*kālī nāmnā tu sā jñeyā bhīmasenapriyā sadā \|\| 99 \|\|*

99\. Bharati, the daughter of Kaslraja known by her popular name Kali became the mistress of Bhlmasena.

## \|\| 16.100 \|\|

*vācyādibhiḥ saṃyutaivadraupadī drupadātmajā \|*  
*dehaṃ tyaktvāviśiṣṭaiva kāraṭīgrāmasaṃjñakai \|\| 100 \|\|*

100\. Draupadl, daughter of Drupada, gave up her body together with her maidens. О lord of birds, she will be born in the Kali age in the house of Sankara in the village of Karati.

## \|\| 16.101 \|\|

*saṃkarasya gṛhe vīndra bhaviṣyati kalau yuge \|*  
*vāyostṛtīyarūpārthaṃ sā kanyaiva mṛtiṃ gatā \|\| 101 \|\|*

101\. She died as a maiden to become the mistress of Vayu in her third birth.

## \|\| 16.102 \|\|

*ityādyā vāyubhāryāśca brahmabhāryāśca satama \|*  
*svabhartṛbhyāṃ ca pakṣīndra guṇaiścaiva śatādhamāḥ \|\| 102 \|\|*

102\. O good one, such are the mistresses of Vayu and Brahma. О lord of birds, they are inferior to their husbands in hundreds of merits.

## Chapter 17

## \|\| 17.1-2 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*caturjanmasu vai kṛṣṇa śacyādyaiḥ saha bhāratī \|*  
*ekadeha viśiṣṭaiva bhuvi jāteti coktavān \|\| 1 \|\|*

*kāraṇaṃ brūhi me brahman śiṣyāya tava suvrata \|*  
*garuḍenaivamuktastamuvāca madhusūdanaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

1-2. Garuda said: — You have said, О lord, that Bharatl, JsacI, etc., had the self-same bodies in all their four births. О cherisher of good vows, please tell me the reason thereof. Asked by Garuda thus, lord Krsna, the destroyer of asura Madhu, answered him thus.

## \|\| 17.3 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*viśiṣṭadehasaṃ prāptau bhāratyāḥ pakṣisattama \|*  
*vakṣyāmi kāraṇaṃ vīndra sāvadhānamanāḥ śṛṇu \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Shri Krsna said: — О lord of birds, О noble one, 1 shall tell you the reason thereof. Please listen.

## \|\| 17.4-5 \|\|

*purā kṛtayuge vīndra rudrabhāryā ca pārvatī \|*  
*indrabhāryā śacī devī yama bhāryāca śāmalā \|\| 4 \|\|*

*aśvibhāryā uṣā devī bhartṛbhiḥ sahitā khaga \|*  
*brahmalokaṃ yayustatra brahmāṇaṃ dadṛśustadā \|\| 5 \|\|*

4-5. О lord of birds, in the Krta age, formerly, Parvaii the mistress of Rudra, Sacl, the mistress of Indra, Syamala, the mistress of Yama, Usa, the mistress of Asvins, went to the region of Brahma.

## \|\| 17.6 \|\|

*hāvaṃ bhāvaṃ vilāsaṃ ca darśayāmāsurañjasā \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā tā uddhatā brahmā śaśāpa khagasattama \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. In the presence of Brahma, they displayed their amorous feelings. О best of birds, on seeing that they were excited by love, Brahma cursed all the four.

## \|\| 17.7 \|\|

*uddhatāśca yato yūyaṃ mānuṣīṃ yonimāpsyatha \|*  
*tatra svabhartṛsaṃyogamavāpsyatha khageśvara \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. "Since you are excited by love, you will be born as. mortals and will marry the mortals."

## \|\| 17.8-9 \|\|

*evaṃ śaptāstu tāḥ sarvā ājagmurmeruparvatam \|*  
*tatropaviṣṭaṃ brahmāṇaṃ vañcayāmāsurañjasā \|\| 8 \|\|*

*tūṣṇīmeva sthite vīndra vañcayantyaḥ sthitāḥ punaḥ \|*  
*tatastūṣṇīṃ sthitaṃ vīndra vañcayāmāsurañjasā \|\| 9 \|\|*

8-9. After they were cursed by Brahma they came to the mountain Meru where they saw Brahma again. They practised deception on Brahman while the latter stood silent. They deceived him thrice.

## \|\| 17.10-11 \|\|

*trivārānantaraṃ brahmā śaptavāṃstā mahāprabhuḥ \|*  
*trivāraṃ vañcanaṃ yasmādekavāraṃ ca darśanam \|\| 10 \|\|*

*kiṃ cāśrutvātaḥ paścāccaturjanmasu bhūtale \|*  
*ekadehānmānuṣatvaṃ bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

10-11. When lord Brahma had been deceived again, he cursed the ladies in the following way. Since you have practised deception on me four times, you will have four births in human form.

In the first birth, you will be born as normal human beings.

## \|\| 17.12 \|\|

*dvitīye janmani tathā anyagātvamavāpsyatha \|*  
*tṛtīye janmani tathā bhartṛsaṃyoga māpsyatha \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. In the second birth, you will be born as human beings but turn unchaste. In the third birth you will be born as human beings and married to men with whom you will remain for life.

## \|\| 17.13 \|\|

*janmanyādye caturthe ca nānyagātvamavāpsyatha \|*  
*tathā svabhartṛsaṃyogaṃ nāvāpsyatha ca sarvaśaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. In the fourth birth, as in the first, you will not turn unchaste and you will not be separated from your husbands.

## \|\| 17.14 \|\|

*evaṃ śaptāstu tāḥ sarvā brahmaṇā pakṣisattama \|*  
*tadā vicārayāmāsurmilitvā merumūrdhani \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Thus cursed by Brahma, the four ladies discussed the matter among themselves on the peak of Meru. They said —

## \|\| 17.15 \|\|

*brahmaśāpastvanirvāya upāyaiḥ śataśopi ca \|*  
*nīcaiḥ samāgamo nindyastathaiva ca vipattidaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. "The curse of Brahma cannot be nullified, though we make hundreds of attempts. Association of the divine with the human is not commendable. It brings misfortune.

## \|\| 17.16-17 \|\|

*uttamena ca saṃgena daivenāpyarthado bhavet \|*  
*devānāmuttamo vāyustadarthaṃ saṃgamācaret \|\| 16 \|\|*

*vicāryaivamumādyā bhāratyāḥ sevāṃ tu cakrire \|*  
*sahasravatsarānte sā bhāratī toṣitābravīt \|\| 17 \|\|*

16-17. Association with the noble brings wealth and prosperity. Of all the gods, Vayu is the best. We can associate with him in human form without degrading ourselves.

With this unanimous decision they began to worship Bharatl.

Pleased with their worship, at the end of one thousand years, Bharatl said.

## \|\| 17.18 \|\|

*matsevāṃ ca kimarthaṃ vai hyācariṣyanti suvratāḥ \|*  
*tasyāṃ raktāśca tā devyastvabruvansvacikīrṣitam \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. "O ladies of good vow, wherefore do you worship me?" Being devoted to her, they expressed their woes thus.

## \|\| 17.19-20 \|\|

*purā vayaṃ tu śaptāḥ sma brahmaṇā krodharūpiṇā \|*  
*ekadehānmānuṣatvamavāpsyatha varāṅganāḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

*caturthajanmanyapyevaṃ dvitīye janmani prabho \|*  
*samāpsyathānyagātvaṃ cetyevaṃ śaptā ha bhāmini \|\| 20 \|\|*

19-20. "O madam, Brahma has, in fury, cursed us thus- "In the first birth, you will be born as- human beings, in the second too as such, but will turn unchaste. In the third, you will marry and live with your mates for life. In the fourth, as. in the first, you will not turn unchaste."

## \|\| 17.21 \|\|

*asmākaṃ vāyunā devenānyagātvaṃ na doṣabhāk \|*  
*atastvayaikadehatvamicchāmo devi janmasu \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Now, that we shall be united with human beings we shall not incur the sin of unchastity if we unite ourselves with Vayu. О Bharatl, we desire to be identical with you in these births.

## \|\| 17.22 \|\|

*hatyuktā tābhiratha ca tathaityuktvā dvijottama \|*  
*sā pārvatyādibhiryuktā bhāratītyabhavadbhuvi \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Thus urged by the ladies, О best of birds, Bharatl agreed to their request and descended to the earth together with those ladies.

## \|\| 17.23 \|\|

*śivanāmno dvijasyaiva gṛhe sā tu kumārikā \|*  
*karmaikyārthaṃ tapaścakreḥ viṣṇośca śivasaṃjñinaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. She was born to a Brahmin named diva. She practised penance for the fulfilment other object.

## \|\| 17.24-25 \|\|

*tapasā toṣitā viṣṇuḥ śiva saṃjño mahāprabhuḥ \|*  
*varaṃ prādāttṛtīyesminkṛṣṇajanmani bho striyaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

*samyaktvabhartṛsaṃyogo bhaviṣyati vinā bhavam \|*  
*yatonayā ca pārvatyā preritā eva sarvaśaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

24-25. The great lord Visnu named Siva was' pleased with her penance and conferred a boon to all those women. О ladies, you will enjoy in the company of a suitable person in. the form of Krsna even without having an intercourse.

## \|\| 17.26 \|\|

*vilāsaṃ darśayāmāsa brahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ \|*  
*ataḥ sā pārvātī śreṣṭhā brahmadehe na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Since urged by Parvati you have displayed amours to lord Brahma, Parvati will be considered to be the best amongst

## \|\| 17.27 \|\|

*kṛṣṇadehepi tasyāstu na bhaviṣyati saṃgamaḥ \|*  
*anyagātvaṃ dvitīyesminbhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Even though she remains in the company of lord Krsna, she will not be sexually united with him.

In the second birth, you will be stained with a scandal for having illicit intercourse with a person other than your husband.

## \|\| 17.28 \|\|

*rudrāntaḥ stho hariścaiva vahaṃ dattvā striyāṃ prabhuḥ \|*  
*antardhānaṃ yayau śrīmānsvalokaṃ gatavānabhūt \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Thus, lord Hari, stationed in the heart of Rudra, conferred boon to the ladies and disappeared.

## \|\| 17.29 \|\|

*visṛjya tāśca taṃ dehaṃ babhūvurnalakanyakāḥ \|*  
*indraseneti saṃjñāṃ ca labdhvā tāśca tapovanam \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. When the lord had left for his celestial abode, the ladies gave up their physical bodies and were born as daughters to king Nala and were named Indrasena and others.

## \|\| 17.30 \|\|

*yayustatra carantyastā dadṛśurmudgalaṃ tvṛṣim \|*  
*tasya darśanamātreṇa babhūvuḥ kāmamohitāḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. They went to forest (for living an ascetic's life) but fell in love with Sage Mudgala at first sight.

## \|\| 17.31 \|\|

*mudgalasyābhimānaṃ hi nāśayitvā ca mārutaḥ \|*  
*ramayāmāsa tatrasthā bhāratyādivarāṅganāḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. At the same time, Vayu intervened and overpowered Mudgala. Vayu copulated with Bharat! and her companions.

## \|\| 17.32 \|\|

*taddehena visṛṣṭā sā babhūva draupadīti ca \|*  
*yasmātsā drupadājjātā tasmātsā draupadī smṛtā \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. When Bharati left her present body she was born as Draupadl. She was so called as she was the daughter of Drupada.

## \|\| 17.33 \|\|

*vedimadhyātsamudbhūtā tasmātsāyonijā smṛtā \|*  
*kṛṣṇavarṇā yatastasmātsā kṛṣṇā bhūtale smṛtā \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Since she was born of sacrifice she was known as one not born of a womb. As she was dark-complexioned she was known as Krsna.

## \|\| 17.34 \|\|

*kṛṣṇādehapi bhāratyā abhimānaḥ sadā smṛtaḥ \|*  
*śacyāderabhimānastu tasmindehe kadācana \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. While she lived as Krsna she had the recollection that she was in fact Bharati. SacI and others too recollected their original Self but only sometimes.

## \|\| 17.35 \|\|

*yasyāḥ svabhartṛsaṃyogakāle ca khagasattama \|*  
*abhimānastadaiva syāttasyā eva na cānyathā \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Bharati alone had the memory of her previous life during the intercourse, not others.

## \|\| 17.36 \|\|

*etāsāṃ ramaṇe kāle umāyāḥ pakṣisattama \|*  
*abhimānaśca nāstyeva svāpa eva ratāḥ sadā \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. When Parvati indulged in intercourse with her husband she had no such idea. She thought she was indulging in love-sport in a dream.

## \|\| 17.37-38 \|\|

*pārthasya ramaṇe kāle draupadyāśca kalevare \|*  
*bhāratyāśca tathā śacyā abhimānadvayaṃ smṛtam \|\| 37 \|\|*

*umādeḥ śyāma lādeśca abhimānakṣatistadā \|*  
*sarvāsāṃ svāpa eva syānnātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|\| 38 \|\|*

37-38. When Draupadi copulated with Arjuna, she felt she was Bharati. Saci, Uma and Syamala thought they were sporting in dream.

## \|\| 17.39-40 \|\|

*arjunaṃ vīrarūpeṇa praviṣṭo vāyureva ca \|*  
*bhāratīṃ ramate nityaṃ śāmalāṃ ca yudhiṣṭhiraḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

*suṃdareṇa ca rūpeṇa praviṣṭo nakule marut \|*  
*ramate bhāratīṃ nityaṃ nakulaścāpyuṣāṃ khaga \|\| 40 \|\|*

39-40. In fact, Vayu had entered into the bodies of Arjuna, Yudhisthira, Nakula and Sahadeva. When the latter were copulating with Bharati, Syamala, Usa and Draupadi, it was Vayu who in fact had the intercourse.

## \|\| 17.41-42 \|\|

*nītirūpeṇa cāviṣṭo sahadeve ca mārutaḥ \|*  
*draupadīṃ ramate nityaṃ sahadevotpayuṣāṃ khaga \|\| 41 \|\|*

*śacyādyā draupadīdehe nāpuḥ saṃgaṃ ca mārutaḥ \|*  
*tāsāmatonyagāmitvaṃ kṛṣṇādehe na cintayet \|\| 42 \|\|*

41-42. In the form of Draupadi, Shacl and the rest had their intercourse with Vayu in the body of Arjuna and others, hence, their union with Arjuna and others was not illegal.

## \|\| 17.43 \|\|

*dharmādidehasaṃgaṃ ca bhāratyā naiva cintayet \|*  
*manujasya ca dehasya tāsāṃ saṃgaṃ cintayet \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Similarly, Bharati had no intercourse with Yudhisthira, Arjuna and others, since they could not copulate with a mortal.

## \|\| 17.44-46 \|\|

*aparokṣavatīnāṃ tu tāsāṃ lepo na sarvathā \|*  
*athavā mudgalasyeva ratikāle khageśvara \|\| 44 \|\|*

*ramaṇaṃ cakrurevaṃ tā ato doṣo na vidyate \|*  
*ekasmindivase vīndra dharmo vāyuśca tāvubhau \|\| 45 \|\|*

*ramaṇaṃ cakratuḥ samyakkṛṣṇādehe 'pi mānada \|*  
*tathāpyananyāgāmitvaṃ cintanīyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

44-46. As they have realized Self, even if they transgress norms they cannot invite scandal.

О lord of birds, once upon a time, Dharma and Vayu copulated with Krsna jointly. Even then Krsna cannot be accused of unchastity.

## \|\| 17.47 \|\|

*surāṇāṃ surabhogyāśca bhogaṃ jānanti devatāḥ \|*  
*na jānantyeva martyāṃstu teṣu deheṣu te punaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. The gods know some peculiar ways of copulation, of which the mortals are quite ignorant.

## \|\| 17.48 \|\|

*nīrakṣīravivikaṃ ca haṃso vetti na cāparaḥ \|*  
*ataḥ svabhartṛsaṃyogaṃ kṛṣṇādehena cintayet kṛṣṇādehenyagāmitvaṃ naiva cintyaṃ khageśvara \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. It is the swan alone who can distinguish water from, the milk.

When Bharatl, etc. in the form of DraupadI are copulating, their male partners are divine beings, not human ones. Hence, their copulation with human beings such as Arjuna is not untenable.

## Chapter 18

## \|\| 18.1 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*athānantarajānvakṣye śṛṇu pakṣīndrasattama \|*  
*śṛṇu tānsāvadhānena śrutvā tānavadhāraya \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Shri Krsna said: — I shall now tell you about the subsidiary creation for your knowledge. Please, hear attentively and retain the same in your memory, О lord of birds.

## \|\| 18.2 \|\|

*puruṣākhyaviriñcānujātaḥ śeṣo mahābalaḥ \|*  
*hare ramāyāśca yasya svasminnidrāṃ prakurvataḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. After Virinca, was born the powerful Sesa. When Lord Visnu and LaksmI rested in the ocean.

## \|\| 18.3 \|\|

*śayanārthamabhūdeṣa tena kṛtyaṃ harerna tu \|*  
*sarvadā haridāsohaṃ sarvadā haripūjakaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Sesa served as a couch, though lord Hari did not need it.

(When the lord moved) Sesa was replaced by Garuda, (Here is the praise of Hari by Garuda).

"I always remain as your slave, О Hari. I always worship you.

## \|\| 18.4 \|\|

*hare sadā namāmi tvāṃ bahu janmani janmani \|*  
*evaṃ buddhā tu garuḍo hyabhūcca śayanaṃ hareḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. I am always your devotee. I shall remain so in each and every birth of mine".

## \|\| 18.5 \|\|

*sūtranāmnastathā vāyoḥ sadāyaṃ vinatāsuta \|*  
*kālanāmā ca garuḍo vāhanārthaṃ harerabhūt \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Thus, Garuda served as the Vehicle of lord Hari. Garuda was the son of Vayu called Sutra. Born of Vinata he was popularly known as Vainateya.

## \|\| 18.6 \|\|

*tato mahattattvatanorviriñcāttu khageśvara \|*  
*ahaṃ kārātmako rudraḥ samabhūtsovituṃ harim \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. О lord of birds, identical with ego, you were born of Virinca identical with intellect. The propitiation of Hari has been the mission of your life.

## \|\| 18.7 \|\|

*traya ete mahābhāga parasparasamāḥ smṛtāḥ \|*  
*gāyatrībhāratībhyāṃ te trayaḥ śataguṇā varāḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. О lord, the three are at par with one another but are superior to Gayatri and Bharatl in respect of virtues.

## \|\| 18.8 \|\|

*śeṣaḥ sa eva vijñeyo bhakto nārāyaṇasya ca \|*  
*viṣṇorvāyoranantasya tribhiraṃśairyutaḥ sadā \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Sesa, the devotee of Narayana, was possessed of three amsas of Visnu, Vayu and Ananta.

## \|\| 18.9 \|\|

*sumitrāṃśo daśarathājjāto yo lakṣmaṇaḥ khaga \|*  
*sopi śeṣastu vijñeyo vāyvanantāṃśasaṃyutaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. О lord, born of Sumitra and Dasaratha, Laksmana was the incarnation of Shesa having certain amsas of Vayu and Ananta.

## \|\| 18.10 \|\|

*rāmasya sevāṃ kartuṃ sā sītā bhūmyāṃ khagādhipa \|*  
*balabhadrastu rohiṇyāṃ vasudevādabhūtkhaga \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. O lord of birds, SIta was born of Earth in order to propitiate Rama. Balabhadra was born of Vasudeva and RohinI having the amsas of both.

## \|\| 18.11-12 \|\|

*soyamepa tu vijñeyastvaṃśadvayasamanvitaḥ \|*  
*āviṣṭaḥ śuklakṛṣṇe hariṇā rohiṇīsutaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

*traya ete māhābhāgāvatārāḥ phaṇinaḥ smṛtāḥ \|*  
*na vīndrāsyāvatārosti bhūmyāṃ cājñā tathā hareḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

11-12. He, the son of Rohini, was pervaded by Hari of white and dark complexion.

The three — Shesa, Laksmana and Baladeva are the incarnations of lord Visnu.

Lord Visnu has ordained that the three should not be counted among his incarnations.

## \|\| 18.13 \|\|

*rudrāvatārānvakṣyehaṃ tāñchṛṇu tvaṃ samāhitaḥ \|*  
*yohaṅkārātmako rudraḥ sa evābhūtkhageśvara \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. I shall now tell you the incarnations of Rudra. Hear attentively, О lord of birds.

## \|\| 18.14 \|\|

*sadāśiva iti tvākhyāmavāpa sa vināśakaḥ \|*  
*tamobhimānī sa jñeyastvaśivatvātsadāśivaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Rudra identical with ego obtained the title'ever-inauspicious' and was assigned the task of destruction.

He became the presiding deity of tamas. Being not auspicioushe was called ever- inauspicious (Sada-asivah).

## \|\| 18.15 \|\|

*kapālamālāmaśivāṃ sadā dhārayate yataḥ \|*  
*ataḥ sadāśaivo jñeyo na ca bhāgavataḥ śivaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. As he ever wore the inauspicious garland of skulls, he was called everinauspicious. He should be ever distinguished from Shiva, the devotee of Visnu.

## \|\| 18.16-17 \|\|

*gajājinaṃ cāpavitraṃ yato dhārayate haraḥ \|*  
*lokānamaṅgalānsarvānharate ca sadā haraḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

*haryājñayā sadā lokānvipayāsaktacetasaḥ \|*  
*vimukhānkurute yasmādviṣṇostasmātsadāśivaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

16-17. As he wore the unholy hide of an elephant, destroyed the sinful wicked people and with the consent of lord Visnu made the worldly people averse to the lord, he was called everinauspicious.

## \|\| 18.18 \|\|

*kadācidasurāveśādviruddhaṃ kurute haraḥ \|*  
*ataḥ sadāśivo jñeyo na ca bhāgavataḥ śivaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Sometimes, influenced by the Asuras, he acted contrary to the wishes of the lord, he was called ever-inauspicious. As such, he should be distinguished from Shiva, the devotee of lord Visnu.

## \|\| 18.19 \|\|

*soyaṃ śmaśānavasatiṃ kartumaicchadyato haraḥ \|*  
*ataḥ sadāśivo jñeyo na ca bhāgavataḥ śivaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. As he liked to abide in the cemetery he was caged inauspicious. As such, he should be distinguished from Shiva, the devotee of lord Visnu.

## \|\| 18.20 \|\|

*daśavarṣaṃ tapaḥ kartuṃ viveśa lavaṇāṃbhasi \|*  
*ato rudrastapaḥ saṃjñāmavāpa ca khagottama \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. To practise penance, he remained in the salt- ocean for ten divine years. Hence, he obtained the title tapas, О lord of birds.

## \|\| 18.21 \|\|

*vyāsaputraḥ śukaḥ prokto vāyorāveśasaṃyutaḥ \|*  
*rudrāvatāro vijñeyo jñānārthamabhavadbhuvi \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Suka, the son of Vyasa, who had been influenced by Vayu was the incarnation of Rudra. He was born for the spread of knowledge in the world.

## \|\| 18.22 \|\|

*atripatnyanusūyāyāṃ jajñe rudro mahātapāḥ \|*  
*durvāsāstu sa vijñeyo mānabhaṅgāya bhūbhṛtām \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Rudra was born as the great ascetic Durvasas of Sage Atri and Anasuya, humiliation of Ksatriyas being the mission of his life.

## \|\| 18.23 \|\|

*droṇājjāto drauṇisaṃjño rudra eva prakīrtitaḥ \|*  
*prārabdhaṃ bhoktukāmosau parapakṣaprakāśakaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Born of Drona, Asvatthaman was Rudra himself. He was born to reap the fruits of the seeds of his actions sown in the former birth and to illumine (by contrast) the virtues of his enemy.

## \|\| 18.24 \|\|

*īśānakoṇe saṃsthito yastu rudro hyavāpa vai vāmadeveti saṃjñām \|*  
*svavāmabhāge saṃsthitaṃ caiva vāyustaṃ yogyabhaktaṃ sevate sarvadaiva \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Stationed in the north-eastern direction, Rudra obtained the title of Vamadeva. Stationed to the left of Vayu, he is propitiated by him, his worthy disciple.

## \|\| 18.25 \|\|

*ato rudro vāmadeveti saṃjñā mavāpa śiṣṭatvamathottamatvam \|*  
*kālātmakatvaṃ ca balātmakatvamavāpa rudro na tu suṃdaratvataḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Hence, Rudra got the title of Vamadeva. Further, on the strength of his lordship and nobility he obtained the titles of Kala and Bala and not by the virtue of his handsome features.

## \|\| 18.26 \|\|

*sadā rudro tripurasthāṃśca daityānviṣṇuduho hantu kāmo mahātmā \|*  
*aghorarūpaṃ dhṛpavānrudra eva tatastvaghoreti sa āpa saṃjñām \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. To slay the Asuras of Tripura who were inimical to Visnu, Rudra assumed the form of Aghora. Hence, he is called Aghora.

## \|\| 18.27 \|\|

*sevāṃ kartuṃ tvicchato daityasaṃghānkiñcitkālaṃ tapasā kliśyamānān \|*  
*varāndātuṃ sadya evābhijātaḥ sadyojātetyeva saṃjñāmavāpa \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. To confer boon to the daityas who had followed the path of service and practised penance, Rudra was born in a trice and was called Sadyojata.

## \|\| 18.28 \|\|

*uroḥ putrastu aurvaśca rudra eva prakīrtitaḥ \|*  
*ityakṛṣṭavācitvādrusturodanavācakaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Aurva, the son of Urn, was called Rudra. Uru was so called because he was given to loud weeping (Uru).

## \|\| 18.29 \|\|

*urū rudro hyataḥ proktastatputraścaurvasaṃjñakaḥ \|*  
*rudramurvaritaṃ kartumaurvobhūdrudra eva saḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. The son of Uru was called Aurva. Rudra was called Aurva since he wanted to revive the cult of weeping (in devotion).

## \|\| 18.30 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*rodanaṃ kurute kasmādurusaṃjño hare haraḥ \|*  
*rudamurvaritaṃ kasmātkurute aurvākārakaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Garuda said: — О lord, please tell me why Sage Uru wept in devotion and why his son Aurva tried to revive the cult of weeping.

## \|\| 18.31 \|\|

*etadvistārya mebrūhi pautrāya tava suvrata \|*  
*ityuktastena sa hariruvāca karuṇānidhiḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. О lord of good vow, I, your grandson, desire to hear all this in detail. Being addressed by Garuda, the compassionate lord spoke in reply.

## \|\| 18.32 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā svabiṃbaṃ suguṇaistu pūrṇaṃ saṃkarṣaṇākhyaṃ natapādapadm \|*  
*śrībrahmaśeṣairjiṣṇukāmaistathānyairbhāratyā vai svasti paiścāpi nityam \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Shri Krsna said: — Rudra saw his own self reflected in Sankarsana who was possessed of good qualities, whose lotus-feet were bowed to by Brahma, Sesa and others desirous of gaining victory (over the senses), as well as by Bharat! and Svastipas.

## \|\| 18.33-34 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā hariṃ pulakāṅgastu rudraḥ sabhāṣpacakṣū ruddhakaṇṭhaśca hṛṣṭaḥ \|*  
*anādyanantabrahmakalpeṣu naiva kṛtaṃ yayā smaraṇaṃ sarvadaiva \|\| 33 \|\|*

*pādāravinde sunakhairvibhūṣite dṛṣṭe mayā kena puṇyena deva \|*  
*dṛṣṭvādṛṣṭvā pādapadmaṃ murāreḥ punaḥ punā ruddhakaṇṭho babhūva \|\| 34 \|\|*

33-34. On seeing Hari, Rudra was extremely delighted. His eyes were brimful of tears. His throat was choked with emotion. He was beside himself with joy. He said:

О lord, during the enumerable Kalpas of Brahma that have passed so far, I have never remembered your name. О Lord, then, by what virtue have I seen your lotus feet adorned with beautiful nails?

On seeing, over and over again, the lotus feet of the lord, Garuda was choked in throat.

## \|\| 18.35 \|\|

*ruroda rudro bhayakaṃpitāṅgaḥ kathaṃ punardarśanaṃ me prabhoḥ syāt \|*  
*mukunda nārāyaṇa viśvamūrte vāgindriyeṇa stavanaṃ me kathaṃ syāt \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. With his body shivering with fear Rocca wept and spoke to the lord again.

"O Mukunda, Narayana and omniformed lord, how shall I praise you with my tongue.

## \|\| 18.36 \|\|

*maddarśanaṃ sarvadā pāpuyuktaṃ tathā madvāk sarvadā pāpayuktā \|*  
*maddarśanaṃ sarvadā strīṣu saktamabhūcca te darśanaṃ me hyasaktam \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. My very sight is productive of sin. My very speech is attended by sin. My very eyes are set on women, instead of being set on you.

## \|\| 18.37 \|\|

*āsaktatā putradārādikānāṃ samyak śaktistavane nāsti viṣṇoḥ \|*  
*viṣṇustutau nāvakāśosti vāco dṛṣṭohaṃ tvaṃ kena puṇyena deva \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. I have been attached to my children, wife and other members of my family. I have no ability to offer praise. My tongue is not free to eulogize you. By what merit have I seen you О lord?

## \|\| 18.38 \|\|

*anantakarṇeśa sucandrasaṃjña śrotreṇa nityaṃ na kathā śrutā te \|*  
*śrutā mayā bahudhā lokavārtā dṛṣṭo mayā tvaṃ kena puṇyena deva \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. О lord of many ears, I did not hear your narrative with attention. Conversely, I have been indulging in gossip with the lay people. By what merit have 1 seen you, О lord?

## \|\| 18.39 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvādṛṣṭvā pādapīṭhaṃ hareśca punaḥ punā ruddhaṅkaṭho babhūva \|*  
*ruroda rudro bhayakaṃpitāṅgaḥ kathaṃ punaḥ śravaṇaṃ syātkathāyāḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Seeing the footstool whereon the lord had put his feet, Rudra had his throat choked with emotion. He wept while his body shivered with fear, thinking how could he hear the narrative of the lord.

## \|\| 18.40-42 \|\|

*tvamīśa vaikuṇṭha suvāyusaṃjñastvadarpitaṃ gandhapuṣpādikaṃ ca \|*  
*sadā na liptaṃ ca bhujairviliptaṃ tanmūtraviṣṭhādimakardamāmbubhiḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

*strīṇāṃ kucodaiśca kacodakaiścakakṣodakair gātrajalairmukunda \|*  
*anarpitairvastragandhādikaiśca dṛṣṭo mayā kena puṇyena deva \|\| 41 \|\|*

*spṛṣṭvāspṛṣṭvā harinirmālyagandhaṃ punaḥ punā ruddhakaṇṭho babhūva \|*  
*ruroda rudro bhayakaṃpitāṅgaḥ kathaṃ punaḥ sparśanaṃ syātsadā me \|\| 42 \|\|*

40-42. О lord Vaikuntha, known as Vayu, I have not offered any scent or flower to you. I have applied these to my arms. Mixed with perspiration from my breast, arm-pits, hair of the head and other parts of my body, the flowers and scents have acquired the impurity of urine and faeces. О lord, I have not offered any cloth or scent to you. By what merit have I seen you, О lord.

Breathing in the scent of flowers that had been left after they were offered to the deity, Rudra's throat was choked and he wept while his body shivered with fear.

## \|\| 18.43 \|\|

*nṛsiṃha nāsāsthita nāsikeśa mannāsayā kvāpi supadmasaurabham \|*  
*nāghrātamitthaṃ punarāghrātameva hyanarpitaṃ gandhapuṣpādikaṃ ca \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Rudra said: — О lord! how could I touch your body. О man-lion stationed at the tip of nose, О lord of nose, I have not smelt the fragrance of fresh lotuses. If I have smelt, I have not offered the same to you.

## \|\| 18.44 \|\|

*sunāsikaṃ suṣṭhudantaṃ murāre dṛṣṭaṃ mukhaṃ kena puṇyena deva \|*  
*ghrātvā ghrātvā viṣṇunirmālyagandhaṃ punaḥ punā ruddhakaṇṭho babhūva \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. By what merits, О lord, have I seen your face with lovely nose and shining teeth.

Breathing out the scent of flowers that were left after they had been offered to the deity, hehad his throat choked again andagain.

## \|\| 18.45 \|\|

*ruroda rudro bhayakaṃpitāṅgo jighrāmi nirmālyamidaṃ kathaṃ te \|*  
*jihvāsthito jihva saṃjño murāre jihvendriyeṇāpi tathārpitaṃ ca \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. With his body shivering with fear, Rudra wept and said: О lord, how do I breathe out the smell of flowers that had been left after they were offered to you?

## \|\| 18.46 \|\|

*naivedyaśeṣaṃ tulasīvimiśritaṃ viśeṣataḥ pādajalena siktam \|*  
*yo snāti nityaṃ purato murāreḥ prāpnoti yajñāyutakoṭipuṇyam \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. О lord Jihva, as you always sit on the tongue (of the faithful), if a person in the presence of your image takes bath every day in the water containing the remains of the eatables that had been mixed with Tulasi leaf offered to you, that has been sprinkled over with the water wherewiih your feet have been washed, though the offering of the eatable has been made just by word of mouth, obtains the merit of performing ten thousand crones of sacrifices.

## \|\| 18.47 \|\|

*etādṛśaṃ tava naivedyaśeṣaṃ na bhuktaṃ vai sarvadādityarūpam \|*  
*anarpitaṃ tava devasya viṣṇorbhuktaṃ mayā bahuvāraṃ mukunda \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Such remains of eatables, as. luminous as the Sun, I have neither eaten nor offered to you. I have taken meals but not offered the same to you.

## \|\| 18.48 \|\|

*pādāravinde nārpitaṃ bhakṣyabhojyaṃ dṛṣṭo mayā kena puṇyena deva \|*  
*bhuktvābhuktvā harinairavedyajātaṃ sukhaṃ tvadīyaṃ ramayā lālitaṃ ca \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. I have not offered any eatable at you lotus-feet. By what merit have I seen you, О lord?

While eating the remains of the eatables, after they had been offered to you, I saw your face tended by LaksmI.

## \|\| 18.49 \|\|

*dyubhvāśrayaṃ tava mūrdhānamāhuḥ kirīṭayuktaṃ kuṭilaiḥ kuntalaiśca \|*  
*anekajanmārjitapuṇyasaṃcayairdṛṣṭaṃ mayā sajjanasaṃgamācca \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. I saw your head adorned with diadem and curly hair being the support of heaven and earth. I have seen your face by the aggregate of merit accumulated in my several previous births, in the company of noblepeople.

## \|\| 18.50 \|\|

*anekajanmārjitapāpasaṃcayairadarśanaṃ yāsyati devadeva \|*  
*evaṃ subhaktyā ca ruroda rudro dṛṣṭvā hariṃ survaguṇaiḥ saṃpūrṇam \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Alas! О lord, it will disappear again by the aggregate of sins accumulated in my several previous births.

Thus, at the sight of Hari adorned by all merits Rudra wept out of devotion.

## \|\| 18.51 \|\|

*pādāravindaṃ tava viśvamūrte yogīśvarairhṛdaye saṃgṛhītam \|*  
*dṛṣṭaṃ mayā dayayā vāsudeva drakṣye kathaṃ punaritthaṃ ruroda \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. O omniformed lord, your lotus-feet kept in the heart by the great Yogins, have been seen by me out of compassion. О Vasudeva, how shall I see them again. Thus Rudra wept again.

## \|\| 18.52 \|\|

*dṛṣṭaṃ mayā tvarivale bhavināśiśaṅkhacakrādikaistrijagatāpi ca deva pūrṇam \|*  
*etādṛśaṃ tvadudaraṃ ca kathaṃ rameśa drakṣye punaḥ punarahaṃ tviti saṃruroda \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. I saw your belly in the field of battle containing three worlds. 1 saw your body adorned with discus, conch, etc., which could stop the recurrence of birth. О lord of LaksmI, how could I see it over and over again.

## \|\| 18.53-54 \|\|

*ānandapūrṇa nakhapūrṇa sukeśapūrṇa lomādipūrṇa guṇapūrṇa sughoṇapūrṇa \|*  
*vakṣaḥ sthalaṃ tava vibhostu viśālabhūtaṃ sadbhūṣaṇaṃ vimalakaustubhaśobhi lakṣmyā \|\| 53 \|\|*

*sukomalaṃ śrītulasyāstathaiva supuṣpitaṃ canda naiścarcitaṃ ca \|*  
*etādṛśaṃ tava vakṣaḥ sthalaṃ ca dṛṣṭaṃ mayā tava kāruṇyadṛṣṭyā \|\| 54 \|\|*

53-54. О lord, full of bliss, equipped with good nails, hair and nose and abounding in virtues, your chest wide and shining with brilliant Kaustubha gem with bright ornaments and the company of LaksmI, tender and adorned with the leaves of Tulasi and flowers of various sorts and anointed with sandal paste — such a wide and beautiful chest I have seen by your compassion alone.

## \|\| 18.55 \|\|

*punaḥ punardarśanaṃ me kathaṃ syādevaṃ rudraḥ sa ca bhaktyā ruroda \|*  
*atastūrurnāma saṃprāpya rudrastatputrobhūddaurvasaṃjñaḥ sa eva \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. How shall I see you again and again? Thus, out of 'devotion, Rudra wept and wept again.

Thus Rudra obtained the title Uru and his son obtained the title Aurva.

## \|\| 18.56 \|\|

*yasmādrudaṃ corvaritaṃ vai cakāra tasmātsa rudrastvaurvasaṃjño babhūva \|*  
*aurvastu lokānmokṣayogyāṃśca dṛṣṭvā hyatyantaṃ vai viṣayeṣveva niṣṭhān \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. Since he made Rudra fertile, the son of Rudra was named Aurva. Aurva saw people worthy of liberation as well as engrossed in the objects of senses.

## \|\| 18.57 \|\|

*stūddaiva caurvo viṣṇupādāravidaṃ smṛtvāsmṛtvā ruddhakaṇṭho babhūva \|*  
*te pāpiṣṭhāḥ pāparūpānbhajanto dinedine durviṣayānkadindriyaiḥ \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Aurva praised the lotus-feet of Visnu, remembered them again and again and felt choked in the throat. These sinners employ their senses to evil objects every day.

## \|\| 18.58-59 \|\|

*kadā caitānheyabuddhyā vimuñce na jānehaṃ ceti samyag ruroda \|*  
*ete hi mūrkhā viṣayānarthalabdhyai kurvanti yatnaṃ paramādareṇa \|\| 58 \|\|*

*kadindriyārthaṃ hi dhanādikaṃ ca tyajanti ca sarve viṣayeṣu niṣṭhāḥ \|*  
*tvanmāyayā mohitānnaṣṭabuddhīnkadā caitānmuñcase viśvamūrte \|\| 59 \|\|*

58-59. How shall I shun them and remain unattached. I do not know this, О lord. Thus he wept bitterly.

These stupid people are addicted to the objects of senses just for attaining pleasure. Addicted to the objects and for satisfying their desire they spend their wealth. They are deluded by your Maya. They lose their power of discernment. О omniformed lord when will you relieve them of delusion.

## \|\| 18.60 \|\|

*smṛtvāsmṛtvā vāsudevasya māyāṃ ruroda caurvo bhayakaṃpitāṅgaḥ \|*  
*atīva kaṣṭena ca lokavṛttyā śritā dainyaṃ svīyakāryaṃ vihāya \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. Aurva, with his body shivering with fear remembered Maya of the lord and wept.

## \|\| 18.61 \|\|

*atīva dainyena dhanādikaṃ ca saṃpādya sarve 'pi supāpaśīlāḥ \|*  
*kaṣṭārjitaṃ dravyadhanādikaṃ ca tyajanti sarve paśavo vyarthameva \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. These men of wicked nature accumulate wealth with great effort. These animals (in the garb of human beings) earn with great toil without any return.

## \|\| 18.62 \|\|

*satpātrabhūte viṣṇubuddhyā kadāpi tyajanti naite māyayā vai murāreḥ \|*  
*eṣāmāyurvyarthamāhurmahāntaḥ kathaṃ naṣṭā iti samyagruroda \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. Deluded by the Maya of Visnu, these wealthy people do not part with their wealth in favour of a worthy person who can be equated with the lord. The noble have declared their life to be a wastage. Thus Sorrowful at their loss Rudra cried aloud.

## \|\| 18.63 \|\|

*eṣāmāyurvyarthamevaṃ gataṃ ca eṣāṃ dṛṣṭvā yauvanaṃ tu dhruvaṃ ca \|*  
*skandhasthamṛtyurhasate kṛṣṇa viṣṇo taṃ vai na jānanti vimūḍhacetasaḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. Their life is lived in vain. Their youth tools spent in vain. Death that remains ever close to a man laughs at them. But these deluded people know it not.

## \|\| 18.64 \|\|

*gṛhaṃ madīyaṃ śatavarṣaṃ ca jīvetputrā madīyā śavatavarṣaṃ tathaiva \|*  
*ahaṃ ca jīve śatavarṣaṃ sukhena madīyabhāryāpi sulakṣaṇā'ste \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. Let my family live for hundred years. Let my sons live for hundred years. Let me also live in comfort for hundred years. Let my auspicious wife too live for hundred years.

## \|\| 18.65 \|\|

*gāvaśca me saṃti sadugdhapūrṇā mitrāṇi me saṃti mudā hi yuktāḥ \|*  
*dāsye sutaṃ vāraṇārthaṃ tu vadhvai putrīṃ vivāhārthamahaṃ dadāmi \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. Let my cows always yield milk. Let me give my son to my daughter-in-law and my daughter to my son-in-law.

## \|\| 18.66 \|\|

*dāsye cāhaṃ satsu putrīṃ dhanaṃ vā dāsye cāhaṃ dhanikeṣveva nityam \|*  
*adṛṣṭaśūnyān bhagavānvāsudevo dṛṣṭvādṛṣṭvā hasate sarvadaiva \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. I shall marry my daughter in a noble and rich family. I shall give wealth to them on their marriage. Taking note of these people who are devoid of discernment, lord Vamadeva laughs at their folly.

## \|\| 18.67 \|\|

*nāhaṃ kariṣye śravaṇaṃ kathāyā madbhāgyanā śaśca bhaviṣyatīti \|*  
*nāhaṃ hariṃ pūjayiṣye sadaiva putrādināśaśca bhaviṣyatīti \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. \[The stupid person speaks thus:\]

"I shall not hear the narrative of the lord lest it should destroy my good fortune. 1 shall not worship the lord, lest it should cause destruction of my sons and prosperity."

## \|\| 18.68 \|\|

*kālekāle diṣṭanāmā haristu phalaprado vāsudevo 'khilasya \|*  
*etādṛśānmūrkhajanāṃśca dṛṣṭvā ruroda caurvo vāsudevaikaniṣṭhaḥ \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. Lord Vasudeva, designated as fortune confers fruits of acts to all and sundry, at the opportune time. Solely devoted to Vasudeva, Aurva saw these stupid people and wept.

## \|\| 18.69-70 \|\|

*atastvaurvo rudrarūpī khagendra jānīhi nityaṃ kṛṣṇasuśikṣitārthaḥ \|*  
*yadā satī dakṣaputrī khagendra dakṣādhvare svaśarīraṃ visṛjya \|\| 69 \|\|*

*jajñe punarmenakāyāṃ himādrestadā rudrastvaurvasaṃjñā mavāpa \|*  
*ūrdhvaretā bhavetyuktvā ūrdhvaretā babhūva ha \|\| 70 \|\|*

69-70. О lord of birds, Aurva is Rudra who has been rightly instructed by lord Krsna himself. When Sati, the daughter of Daksa abandoned her body in Daksa's sacrifice, О lord of birds, she was reborn as the daughter of Himadri and Menaka. Rudra was designated as Aurva.

Be celibate, asked by Brahma thus Rocks, remained celibate.

## \|\| 18.71 \|\|

*pāṇigrāhaṃ rudradevo mahātmā yadā himādreḥ kanyakāyāścakāra \|*  
*tasyāṃ paraṃ laṃpaṭaḥ saṃbabhūva ato rudraḥ parasaṃjñāmavāpa \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. But when Rudra married the daughter of Himadri he became attached to her. Hence, he got the designation Para.

## \|\| 18.72 \|\|

*sadāśivādyā daśa rudrabhrātaraḥ saumitreyo hauhiṇeyastrayaśca \|*  
*samā ete mokṣakāle sṛtau ca śatairguṇairnyūnabhūtāśca tābhyām \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. Rudra had ten brothers. Among these, the three, viz. Sadasiva Saumitreya and Rauhineya — were inferior to Rudra and his consort by one hundred merits.

## \|\| 18.73 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*ānandanirṇayaṃ brūhi kṛṣṇa pūrṇadayānighe \|*  
*nirṇetuṃ jñānināṃ yadvajjñāpanārthaṃ tathā mama \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. Garuda said: — О compassionate lord, please tell me about the Eternal Bliss (ananda), explaining its nature to satisfy my curiosity and for enlightening the seekers after truth.

## \|\| 18.74 \|\|

*brūhi śiṣyāya dayayā uddhartuṃ māṃ ca sarvadā \|*  
*pūrṇakāmasya te kṛṣṇa kā spṛhā vidyate prabho \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. О lord, please tell me, since I am your disciple, just for lifting me up, and out of compassion for me. Since you are fully satiated, you have no desire to be fulfilled.

## \|\| 18.75 \|\|

*evamukto hṛṣīkeśaḥ pakṣīśena mahātmanā \|*  
*uvāca kṛpayā kṛṣṇaḥ prasannaḥ kamalekṣaṇaḥ \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. Thus addressed by the lord of birds, the lotus-eyed lord was pleased and spoke with compassion.

## \|\| 18.76-77 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*gāyatryāśca śatānanda ekānandastu vedhasaḥ etādṛśaḥ śatānando brahmaṇaḥ parikīrtitaḥ \|\| 76 \|\|*

*śeṣādeśca śatānandaḥ sarasvatyāḥ khagottama \|*  
*ekānandastu vijñeyo bhāratyā vinatāsuta \|\| 77 \|\|*

76-77. Srl Krsna said: — Satananda was born of Gayatri, Ekananda of Brahma. Satananda belonged to Brahma, Sesa and Sarasvatl, О best of birds, while Ekananda belonged to Bharati, О son of Vinata.

## \|\| 18.78 \|\|

*evaṃ tu nirṇayo jñeya ānandasya sadā khaga \|*  
*evamuktaṃ mayā sarvaṃ kimanyacchrotumicchasi \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. О bird, such is the information about Ananda. I have told you all. What more do you desire to know?

## Chapter 19

## \|\| 19.1 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*tvayoktaṃ kṛṣṇa govinda rudrācchataguṇādapi \|*  
*brahmāṇī bhāratī cobhe adhike devasattama \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Garuda said: — О lord Krsna, Govinda, you have said that Brahmani! and Bharat! are superior to Rudra by hundred merits, О best of gods.

## \|\| 19.2-3 \|\|

*mayā śrutaṃ viriñcena umāparyantameva ca \|*  
*anantāṃśairvihīnatvaṃ viriñcoktaṃ surādhipa \|\| 2 \|\|*

*sahasrāṃśairvihīnatvaṃ tvayoktaṃ kṛṣṇa mādhava \|*  
*sarveṣāṃ caiva pūrveṣāmavekṣyaiva hare vibho \|\| 3 \|\|*

2-3. О lord of gods, I have heard all what Brahma has said of the gods and goddesses ending with Uma, about the amsas of Ananda found missing to the extent of a thousand.

## \|\| 19.4-5 \|\|

*jñānānandabalādīnāṃ vāyuparyantameva ca \|*  
*sahasrāṃśairvihīnatvaṃ jñānādīnāṃ maheśvara \|\| 4 \|\|*

*nirṇayaṃ brṛhi govinda sarvajñosi na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*garuḍenaivamuktastu vāsudevobravīddhruvam \|\| 5 \|\|*

4-5. О all-pervasive, supreme lord, please tell me your ideas on Knowledge, Bliss, Strength, etc., including Vayu at the last and the proportion of ansas in them reaching a thousand. Considering the views of your predecessors on this topic, you proclaim your judgment, О Govinda, since you are omniscient, О lord.

Thus accosted by Garuda, Vasudeva said in reply.

## \|\| 19.6-7 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*ānandāṃśairvihīnatvamapekṣyaiva khagādhipa \|*  
*uttareṣāmuttareṣāṃ yogādevamiti sphuṭam \|\| 6 \|\|*

*parimāṇe śataguṇe ānande sphuṭatāvaśāt \|*  
*anantaguṇavattvaṃ ca brahmaṇā samudīritam \|\| 7 \|\|*

6-7. Shrl Krsna said: — О lord of birds, by the degrees of Ananda present in each, I have explained to you the superiority and inferiority of jnana, bhakti, etc. Brahma has declared that if hundred per cent Ananda is felt, there would be endless gunas.

## \|\| 19.8-11 \|\|

*sahasraguṇitatvaṃ ca vāyunā samudīritam \|*  
*yathānande tathā jñāne viṣṇau bhaktau balādhike \|\| 8 \|\|*

*sarve guṇaiḥ śataguṇāḥ krameṇoktā nu te 'khilāḥ \|*  
*bhāratyāśca śataṃ jñānaṃ sukhaṃ bhaktibalādhike \|\| 9 \|\|*

*evaṃ jñānaṃ suvijñeyaṃ mārutestu balādikam \|*  
*evaṃ jñānaṃ śataṃ jñeyaṃ mārute nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

*bhāratyāśca śataṃ jñānaṃ balaṃ ca samudāhṛtam \|*  
*evameva ca vāyośca jñānaṃ caivamiti sphuṭam \|\| 11 \|\|*

8-11. Accordingly, Ananda is thousandfold in jnana, hundredfold in Bhakti, of the same proportion in Bharati, Maruti and Vayu.

## \|\| 19.12-15 \|\|

*yathā dīpācchataguṇā agnijvālā na dīpavat \|*  
*sphuṭībhavedyathaivāgnirbahulopi na sūryavat \|\| 12 \|\|*

*yathaiva sūryāddviguṇaścandro naiva sphuṭībhavet \|*  
*ānandatāratamyaṃ ca yathoktaṃ tu mayā tava \|\| 13 \|\|*

*tathaiva jānīhi khaga nānyathā tu kathañcana \|*  
*ahaṃ vijānāmi mayi sthitān guṇānsarvairviśeṣaiśca khagendra saṃyutān \|\| 14 \|\|*

*susūkṣmarūpāṃśca sadā khagendra mayāpyadṛṣṭo nāsti nāstyeva kaścit \|\| 15 \|\|*

12-15. Just as the flame of fire hundredfold more powerful than the earthen lamp is not so illuminating as the lamp itself or just as the widespread fire is not so refulgent as the Sun, just as the moon, twice as large as the Sun is not so refulgent as the Sun, in the same way, О lord, know the relative gradation of Ananda in jnana, bhakti, etc., I know my qualities, О lord of birds, which are endowed with many specialities and which possess subtle forms. There is nothing which remains unknown to me.

## \|\| 19.16 \|\|

*sarvāvatāreṣvapi vidyamānaṃ hariṃ vijānāti ramāpi devī \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Goddess Laksmi too knows me in all my incarnations.

## \|\| 19.17 \|\|

*harerguṇānsarvaviśeṣasaṃyutānakhaṇḍarūpānsā vijānāti devī \|*  
*susūkṣmarūpānsā vijānāti devī brahmādibhyo matprasādādhikaṃ ca \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. She knows my indivisible subtle qualities endowed with many specialities, through Brahma or by my grace.

## \|\| 19.18 \|\|

*svātmasvarūpaṃ pravijānāti devī susūkṣmarūpaṃ suviśeṣaiśca yuktam \|*  
*svānyaṃ prapañcaṃ pravijānāti lakṣmīstathāpyaśeṣaiḥ suviśeṣaiśca yuktam \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Goddess Laksmi knows her own subtle form endowed by special traits.

## \|\| 19.19 \|\|

*brahmāpi paśyetsarvagaṃ vāsudevaṃ vāyvādibhyo hyadhikānsadguṇāṃśca \|*  
*śrotraṃ na jānāti harerguṇāṃśca susūkṣmarūpāṃśca viśeṣasaṃyutān \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Brahma too perceives the all-pervading Vasudeva endowed with noble qualities proportionately larger than those of Vayu, Ears do not know the qualities of the lord which are subtle and special.

## \|\| 19.20 \|\|

*spaṣṭasvarūpeṇa yathā viduḥ surā muktvā brahmāṇaṃ na tathā tepyamuktāḥ \|*  
*svātmānamanyacca sadā viśeṣaryuktaṃ vijānāti vidhiśca mārutaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Except lord Brahma even the gods, until and unless they are released, are ignorant. Brahma and Vayu know their Self and the Supreme Self endowed with special traits.

## \|\| 19.21 \|\|

*vāṇī vijānāti harerguṇāṃśca svayaṃbhuvo naiva tāvadviśeṣān \|*  
*traiguṇyarūpātparataḥ sadaiva paśyedviṣṇuṃ kṛṣṇarūpaṃ khagendra \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Sarasvati perceives my special qualities, who am a sell born lord but not beyond my form as Krsna constituted of three guna.

## \|\| 19.22 \|\|

*śeṣo rudro vīndra etaiśca sarve tamo mātre pravijānanti saṃstham \|*  
*vāṇīdṛṣṭānsaviśeṣān guṇāṃste jānanti no satyamevoktamaṅga \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. О Lord of birds, Shesa and Rudra perceive me stationed in the tamoguna. But they do not know my other gunas and their special traits. О dear, I have told you the truth.

## \|\| 19.23 \|\|

*umā suparṇā vāruṇī ceti tisraḥ sahaiva taḥ pravijānanti sustham \|*  
*harerviśeṣānarudra dṛṣṭānkhagendra jānanti naitāḥ kvāpi deśe ca kāle \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Uma, Suparna and Varum three know me very well as I am stationed (in their heart). But, О lord of birds, my special traits perceived by Rudra are not known to them at any place or time.

## \|\| 19.24 \|\|

*indrādayaḥ pravijānanti vīndra ahaṅkāre vyāptarūpaṃ hariṃ ca \|*  
*dakṣādyā vai buddhitattve sthitaṃ taṃ jānanti te somasūryādayaśca \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. О lord of birds, Indra and other gods know me as peivadmg the Ego; Daksa and other Prajapatis as pervading the intellect. So also Soma and Surya.

## \|\| 19.25-26 \|\|

*viṣṇuṃ hariṃ bhūtatattve sthitaṃ ca ye cānye ca pravijānanti nityam \|*  
*anye ca paśyanti yathā svayogyamaṇḍāntarasthaṃ harirūpaṃ khagendra \|\| 25 \|\|*

*kecitprapaśyanti hareśca rūpaṃ tvadīyahṛtsthaṃ hṛdi kecitsadaiva \|*  
*evaṃprakāraṃ pravijānīhi vīndra hyatho śṛṇu tvaṃmama bhāryāḥ ṣaḍetāḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*
