47-48. She put Salagrama in front and bowed to the lord with devotion. She had traversed one hundred steps before she found herself in front of the ford where sitting comfortably she heard the recital of the Bhagavata and the portion of the Purana which contains the praise of the mountain Venkata.

With full devotion she heard the glory of lord Venkatadri from the honourable preceptor Jaigisavya.

## Chapter 24

## \|\| 24.1 \|\|

*jaigīṣavya uvāca \|*  
*kanye śṛṇu tvaṃ veṅkaṭākhyācalasya smerānanāṃ puṇyamāṃrohaṇe 'sya \|*  
*śrīgītāyāḥ paṭhanaṃ caiva kurvannārohaṇaṃ kurute sarvalokaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Jaigisavya said:-0 girl, Attend to the merit one obtains in ascending the mountain Venkata.

## \|\| 24.2 \|\|

*padepade śrīnivāsaśca devastvalaṃ hyalaṃ prīyate bhaktavargaḥ \|*  
*taṃ prīṇayanmokṣamāyānti sarve harau tuṣṭe kimalabhyaṃ ca kanye \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. In each and every step of the mountain the people recite Bhagavadglta remembering lord Srinivasa. They are transported to the highest region of delight. When the lord is pleased, they attain liberation. О girl, there is nothing inaccessible to a person with whom lord Hari is so pleased.

## \|\| 24.3 \|\|

*sopānadeśe yaḥ purāṇaṃ śṛṇoti tadā kṛtā sarvatīrthādiyātrā \|*  
*tadā divā prastuvantīha mārge sadā hariṃ śrīnivāsaṃ guruṃ ca \|\| 3 \|\|*

3Не who hears this Purana while ascending the mountain attains the merit of visiting holy places of pilgrimage. Therefore while ascending the steps the devotees should praise Hari Srinivasa and their preceptor.

## \|\| 24.4 \|\|

*sopānānāṃ mahimānaṃ ca śrutvā śālagrāmaṃ sthāpayitvā ca tatra \|*  
*namaskṛtvā punarevāpi sā tu sopānāni tvārurohātha sādhvī \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. She heard the glory of ascending the steps, set up and worshipped the holy Salagrama thereon and started ascending.

## \|\| 24.5 \|\|

*sopānānāṃ vīndra cārohaṇena tvavaiṣṇavānāṃ haritoṣo na caiva \|*  
*tenaiva teṣāṃ sādhanaṃ bhūya eva tamasyandhe pātayituṃ khagendra \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. О lord of birds, while ascending the pleasure. О lord of birds, this very fact is enough to put them into deep dark regions.

## \|\| 24.6 \|\|

*sthalesthale evamevāpi kāryaṃ jaigīṣavyaṃ punarevāha devī \|*  
*kanyovāca \|*  
*jaigīṣavyaḥ śrīnivāso haristu brahmādīnāṃ dṛśyate śrīnivāsaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. At every step of journey, one should remember Hari. The. maiden said to Jaigisavya again.

## \|\| 24.7 \|\|

*jaigīṣavya kṛpayā tvaṃ vadasva jaigīṣavyo hyevamukto hariṃ tu \|*  
*uvāca kanyāṃ somaputrīṃ satīṃ ca brahmādīnāṃ dṛśyate śrīnivāsaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. The Maid said: — О Jaiglsvya, please, tell me how Srinivasa, the lord of LaksmI appears to Brahma and others.

Thus addressed by the maiden, the daughter of Soma, Jaigisavya said in reply.

## \|\| 24.8-11 \|\|

*anantarūpodhikakāntakāntimānrūdrādīnāṃ dṛśyate veṅkaṭeśaḥ \|*  
*sasūryalakṣādhikakāntakāntito rudrādīnāṃ dṛśyate śrīnivāsaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

*sahasrasūryādhikakāntikāntaḥ savidyuttvānmānuṣāṇāṃ rameśaḥ \|*  
*ṛṣyādīnāṃ dṛśyate candravacca sanmānuṣāṇāmaparokṣo haristu \|\| 9 \|\|*

*nakṣatravaddṛśyate śrīnivāsaḥ sadā ṛṣīṇāmaparokṣo haristu \|*  
*sa sūryavaddṛśyate śrīnivāsaḥ saṃsāriṇāṃ veṅkaṭeśaḥ khagendra \|\| 10 \|\|*

*saṃdohavaddṛśyate vai prakāśo mithyāvatāṃ dṛśyate śrīnivāsaḥ \|*  
*pāṣāṇavannailyarūpaprakāśaḥ śilāmātre dṛṣyate vai kalau ca \|\| 11 \|\|*

8-11. Brahma and others can see Srinivasa as of eternal form of lustrous body. This is how Venkatesa is seen by Rudra and his associates. He appears to them as lustrous as one hundred thousand suns, which to the mortals is as.lustrous as one thousand suns, as also possessed of the lustre of lightning. To the sages he appears like the sun and the moon, to holy men like constellations, to the worldly people like the mass of milk, to the liars as a blue stone, to the lay people as an ordinary stone only.

## \|\| 24.12 \|\|

*nṛṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ śrīnivāso haristu kalau svarūpaṃ śrīnivāsasya devī \|*  
*na mānuṣāḥ pravijānanti sarve yataḥ kalau tāmasā rājasāstu \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. People do not realize the true form of lord Hari. They are swayed by tamas and rajas.

## \|\| 24.13 \|\|

*tatsaṃginaḥ sāttvikāḥ kecideva hyato bhaktā durlabhā vai kalau ca \|*  
*ye dṛśyante bhaktavatte na bhaktāḥ śiśrodarayorbharaṇe caiva saktāḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Those characterized by Sattva are seldom found in the Kali age. Those who appear to be devotees of Visnu are in fact not devotees at all. Rather, they are busy in filling up their belly and meeting their sexual desire; for they undertake journey with that end in view.

## \|\| 24.14 \|\|

*kurvanti yātrāṃ ca tadarthameva bhaktijñānaṃ durlabhaṃ vai kalau ca \|*  
*bhaktā ye vai na viraktāḥ sadaiva teṣāṃ harerdarśanaṃ durlabhaṃ ca \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Rare is the diffusion of devotion in the iron age. Those who are devotees of the lord but still not detached from worldly pleasures cannot easily get the sight of lord Visnu.

## \|\| 24.15 \|\|

*bhaktasvarūpaṃ tava vakṣye khagendra yo jñānapūrṇaḥ parame snigdha eva \|*  
*na ca dveṣairbandhuro bhaktiyuktastava dveṣāñchṛṇu vakṣye ca samyak \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. О lord of birds, I shall tell you the nature of a true devotee. A true devotee is one who is devoid of hatred, is full of affection and possesses keen insight and devotion. I shall now tell you the forms of hate.

## \|\| 24.16 \|\|

*jīvābhidā hariṇāprākṛtena svatantreṇa hyasvatantrasya nityam \|*  
*jñānānandaiḥ paripūrṇe harau ca guṇairapūrṇo harirityeva cintā \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The supreme lord is distinct from Atman. He is independent as well as free. He is full of knowledge and bliss. To think otherwise is to abhor the lord.

## \|\| 24.17 \|\|

*śrībrahmarudrādidivaukasāṃ sadā tathā dvijānāṃ saṃmānāyāśca cintā \|*  
*viṣṇoḥ sakāśādbrahmarudrādikānāṃ sadādhikyālocanaṃ dveṣa eva \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. To pay more attention to Brahma, Rudra and others or among men to Brahmins, etc. and to regard Brahma, Rudra, and others as more honourable than Visnu is nothing less than dishonouring him.

## \|\| 24.18 \|\|

*viṣṇorbhadre hastapādādikānāṃ bhedajñānaṃ dveṣamāhurmahāntaḥ \|*  
*avatārāṇā chedabhedādikaṃ ca tathocyate maraṇasyāpi cintā \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. О noble one, to regard Visnu as a human being possessed of hands, feet, etc., is equal to hating the lord. To distinguish each incarnation of the lord from the other or to think of their death is also tantamount to a hate.

## \|\| 24.19 \|\|

*tadbhaktānāṃ dveṣaṇaṃ cāhurāryāstadvākyānāṃ dūṣaṇaṃ dveṣa eva \|*  
*naca dveṣaiḥ saṃyutā ye ca loke kanye dṛśyante na tu bhaktāḥ kadācit \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. To hate the devotees of Visnu is a sin; to find fault with the instructions of the lord is showing hate. О maid, those who are full of hate can never be true devotees.

## \|\| 24.20 \|\|

*kanyovāca \|*  
*jaigīṣavya brūhi me ke ca bhaktā bhaktiṃ kathaṃ darśayāmāsurete \|*  
*teṣāṃ hariḥ śrīnivāso mahātmā trātā sadā bhaktavarge dayāluḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. The Maid said: — О sage Jaigisavya, please tell me who are the true devotees. I low did they show their devotion. Lord Visnu, Srinivasa who is compassionate to devotees is indeed their protector.

## \|\| 24.21 \|\|

*jaigīṣavyastvevamukto mahātmā uvāca kanyāṃ saṃsmaran bhaktavaryaḥ \|*  
*jaigīṣavya uvāca \|*  
*prahādādyā śrīnivāsasya bhaktāḥ kṛtvā nṛsiṃhe cottamāṃ bhaktimeva \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Thus addressed by the lord, Sage Jaigisavya revived his memory and spoke to the maid.

## \|\| 24.22 \|\|

*avāpa sāmrājyamanuttamaṃ ca jñānaṃ nṛsiṃhātsamavāpa paścāt \|*  
*parāśaraḥ śrīnibāsasya bhakto bhaktiṃ kṛtvā vyāsarūpaṃ hariṃ ca \|\| 22 \|\|*

Jaigisavya said: — Prahlada, the devotee of Srinivasa Nrsirhha attained.sovereignty of the world and the precious knowledge of atman from Nrsirhha.

22\. Parasara, the devotee of Srinivasa had a great devotion for Vyasa, the incarnation of Visnu. He praised Vyasa and obtained the quintessence of knowledge from him. With his soul elevated by devotion he achieved liberation.

## \|\| 24.23 \|\|

*stutvā tena jñānatattvaṃ hyavāpya jagāma mokṣaṃ bhaktisaṃvardhitātmā \|*  
*yo nāradaḥ śrīnivāsasya bhakto bhaktiṃ kṛtvā garbhavāse harau ca \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. The sage Narada, the devotee of Srinivasa had a devotion for Had while he was in his mother's womb. By that devotion he was adopted as a son by Brahma. By the attainment of knowledge he achieved liberation.

## \|\| 24.24 \|\|

*tayā bhaktyā brahmaputratvamāpa jñānaprāptyā tena muktiṃ jagāma \|*  
*yo hyaṃbarīṣaḥ śrīnivāsasya bhaktaḥ kṛtvā bhaktiṃ paradeva harau ca \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Ambarlsa was a devotee of Srinivasa as well as Hari. He attained knowledge from Durvasas. With his soul elevated by devotion he attained liberation.

## \|\| 24.25 \|\|

*japtvā jñānaṃ prāpya durvāsakaścāpyavāpa mokṣaṃ tena saṃvardhitātmā \|*  
*mucukundo vai śrīnivāsasya bhakto vairāgyato bhaktidārḍhyaṃ ca kṛtvā \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Mucakunda was the devotee of Srinivasa. Detached from worldly affairs and firm in devotion he received supreme knowledge of lord Visnu. With his soul elevated by devotion he attained liberation.

## \|\| 24.26 \|\|

*tattvajñānaṃ prāpya viṣṇormahātmā hyavāpa mokṣaṃ tena saṃvardhitātmā \|*  
*sa puṇḍarīkaḥ śrīnivāsasya bhaktaḥ pitrādiṣṭo viṣṇubhaktiṃ ca kṛtvā \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Pundarika, the devotee of Srinivasa was devoted to Visnu at the behest of his father. He obtained supreme knowledge by the grace of God and received liberation after his soul was elevated by devotion.

## \|\| 24.27 \|\|

*hariprāsādājjñānamanuttamaṃ cāpyavāpa mokṣaṃ bhaktisaṃvardhitātmā \|*  
*brahmā ca vāyuśca sarasvatī ca jñātavyāḥ sarve ṛjuyoginaśca \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Brahma, Vayu, Sarasvatl are great Yogins. They are the constant devotees of lord Visnu. They are of pure form, not attached to worldly objects.

## \|\| 24.28-29 \|\|

*acchinnabhaktāśca sadā murārerna kāmyaraktāḥ śuddharūpā hi te ca \|*  
*girīśanāgeśakhageśasaṃjñā devāḥ śukrārau gurucandrendusūryāḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

*jaleśognirmanudharmau kuberaḥ vighneśanāsatyamarudgaṇāśca \|*  
*parjanyamitrādaya eva sarve sadā hyete śrīnivāsasya bhaktāḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

28-29. Lord of mountains, lord of Nagas, lord of birds, Venus, Mars, Jupiter, moon, sun, lord of waters (Varuna), Agni (fire-god), Manu bharma, Kubera, lord of obstacles, Asvins, groups of Maruts, Parjanya, Mitra — these are the devotees of Visnu.

## \|\| 24.30 \|\|

*viśvāmitro bhṛguraurvaśca kutso marīciratriḥ pulahaḥ kratuśca \|*  
*śaktirvasiṣṭho sautamīyo pulastyo bhāradvājaḥ śrīnivāsasya bhaktāḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Visvamitra, Bhrgu, Aurva, Kutsa, Marlci, Atri, Pulaha, Kratu, Shakti, Vasistha, Gautamlya, Pulastya, and Bharadvaja — these are the devotees of Srinivasa.

## \|\| 24.31 \|\|

*māndhātā nahuṣoṃbarīṣasagarau rājā pṛthurhaihayo ikṣvākurbharato yayātisutalau dharmo vikukṣistathā \|*  
*uttānaśca bibhīṣaṇo daśaratho hyete mahājñāninaḥ śrīmadveṅkaṭanāyakasya ca gurorbhaktāḥ sadā saṃsmṛtāḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Mandhata, Nahusa, Ambarlsa, Sagara, Prthu, Haihaya, Iksvaku, Bharata, Yuyutsu, Siitala, Dharma, Vikuksi, Utthana, Bibhlsana, Dasaratha — these wise and intelligent persons are the devotees of lord of Venkata.

## \|\| 24.32-33 \|\|

*bhāgīrathī samudraśca yamunā ca sarasvatī \|*  
*godāvarī narmadā ca kṛṣṇā bhīmarathī tathā \|\| 32 \|\|*

*sarayūphalgukāverīgaṇḍakī kapilā stathā \|*  
*ityetāśca harerbhaktāḥ saṃti cātraiva bhāmini \|\| 33 \|\|*

32-33. Bhaglrathl, Samudra, Yamuna, Sarasvatl, Godavari, Narmada, Krsna, Bhlmarathl, Sarayu/ Phalgu, Kaverl, GandakI Kapila — these are the devotees of lord Hari.

## \|\| 24.34-35 \|\|

*abhiprāyaṃ tatra vakṣye śṛṇu kanye mayā sati \|*  
*yatra pravartate mārge kathā viṣṇormahātmanaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

*vartante vaiṣṇavā yatra haritattvārthabodhakāḥ \|*  
*tatraiva bhaktāḥ sarvepi saṃti viṣṇostathaiva ca \|\| 35 \|\|*

34-35. О maid, hear, I shall tell you a significant fact. At a congregation, where they narrate the tale of Visnu, where the devotees who understand the essence flock together-all those who are present are the devotees of the lord.

## \|\| 24.36 \|\|

*ye devayātrāṃ paramātmacintayā kurvanti te haribhaktāśca nānye \|*  
*yato harau parame vaiṣṇavānāṃ sarvaṃ niṣṭhāmeti kṛtyaṃ khagendra \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Those who go on pilgrimage intent on realizing the lord are the devotees of the lord, not others, for the life's purport of the devotees of Visnu stabilizes in the supreme lord and not in other matters.

## \|\| 24.37 \|\|

*śeṣācalaṃ samāsādya hyannavastrādibhūṣaṇam \|*  
*yo na dadyādabhaktaḥ sa tataḥ ko nu paraḥ paśuḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. If reaching the mountain &esa one does not gift food, cloth and ornaments to the deserving he is not entitled to be called a devotee of Visnu. Who can be a greater animal than him.

## \|\| 24.38 \|\|

*bhaktā hareḥ śrīnivāsācale ca gaṅgādirūpeṇa ca tatratatra \|*  
*tiṣṭhanti sevārthamurukramasya teṣāṃ pūjā naiva kāryā ca devi \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. The devotees of Hari such as Ganga, etc. live on the mountain Srinivasa in the service of the wide-strider Visnu. О maid, they should not be worshipped by the faithful.

## \|\| 24.39 \|\|

*abhiprāyaṃ tatra vakṣye śṛṇu tvaṃ tatra sthale vastragandhādidhūpaiḥ \|*  
*purāṇoktā api bhedena pūjyā dṛṣṭvā ca tānvandayetprājña eva \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. I shall narrate to you the purport thereof. I shall also tell you where one should worship and offer homage to the devotees, to each individually.

## \|\| 24.40 \|\|

*sadbrāhmaṇānvandayetpādamūle hastau ca dvau saṃpuṭīkṛtya devi \|*  
*sāṣṭāṅgarupaṃ vandanaṃ caiva viṣṇoḥ kuryāttathā gurave viṣṇubuddhyā \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. He should worship the worthy Brahmanas at their feet, with hands joined in reverence.

He should worship lord Visnu with eight organs of the body. He should worship his preceptor in the same way taking him for Visnu.

## \|\| 24.41 \|\|

*gaṅgādīnāṃ vandanaṃ kāryameva sāṣṭāṅgaṃ vai tulasīnāṃ tathaiva \|*  
*aśvatthānāṃ namanaṃ kāryameva gavādīnāṃ namanaṃ mānasena \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. He should bow to Ganga, TulasI and the rest with the eight organs of the body. He should physically bow to the holy fig tree but to the cows and others mentally.

## \|\| 24.42 \|\|

*pūjā sadā devadevasya viṣṇoḥ kāryā bhaktyā vaiṣṇavaireva nānyaiḥ \|*  
*ye nāmakā jñānavantaḥ subhaktāḥ sadaiva kāryā viṣṇupūjā ca kanye \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. The devotees of Visnu alone should worship the supreme god. Those who are rightly the devotees of Visnu but possess nominal knowledge are also entitled to worship him.

## \|\| 24.43 \|\|

*ye nāmakā jñānavantaḥ subhaktāḥ sadaivaṃ kāryā viṣṇupūjā ca kanye \|*  
*yenāmakā viṣṇubhaktāḥ sadaiva pūjā viṣṇornaiva kāryātra devi \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. But those who are nominally the devotees of Visnu, though possessed of knowledge are not entitled to his worship.

## \|\| 24.44 \|\|

*mohādyo vai pūjayeddevadevaṃ mahādharmādyāti cāndhaṃ tamo vai \|*  
*brahmādināmāni harerhi devīṃ viṣṇoḥ svanāmāni dadau divaukasām \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Those who do not worship the supreme god Visnu go to the dark regions (i.e. hell) due to the great sin.

## \|\| 24.45 \|\|

*nādāddhīraḥ keśavādīni kanye svakaṃ puraṃ pravihāyaiva rājā \|*  
*evaṃ mayoktaṃ kanyake sarvametadetatparaṃ samyagārohaṇīyam \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Brahma, etc. are the names of Visnu which he himself had given to the gods. The wise lord did not transfer some of his names such as Kesava, just as a king, when he leaves the capital does not relinquish his title or pass it on to another.

## \|\| 24.46 \|\|

*govinda nārāyaṇa mādhaveti yūyaṃ mayā sarvamārādhitavyam \|*  
*sarve militvā punarevaṃ khagendra samāruhanvaiṅkaṭādriṃ gṛṇantaḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. О maiden, all this I have told you precisely. You should investigate the matter further by yourself. Worship should be made to me by my titles — Govinda, Narayana and Madhava.

## \|\| 24.47 \|\|

*harernāmānyatra pūrvaṃ gṛṇantastvāsvādayantaḥ śrīnivāsasya nāma \|*  
*draṣṭuṃ sarve śrīnivāsaṃ tathaiva kurvantaste talaśabdaṃ nadantaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Together they ascended the mountain Venkata, chanting chants of praise, uttering the name of lord Srinivasa, feeling joy and pleasure and clapping their hands in expectation of seeing the lord.

On hearing the words of lord Krsna, Garuda asked the lord how the travellers felt pleasure by uttering his name.

## \|\| 24.48 \|\|

*iti kṛṣṇavacaḥ śrutvā tārkṣyaḥ kṛṣṇamuvācaha kathamāsvādanaṃ cakruretadvistīryame vada \|*  
*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*bho śrīnivāsa tava nāmaiva caitannāma svāmī nanu nāmaiva svāmī \|*  
*yāṃ brahmādyā āśrayantītti yasmāttasmādramā śrīriti nāma cāpa \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Lord Krsna said: — "O lord Srinivasa, this very name of yours is indeed omnipotent. Brahma and others take resort to you. Rama (LaksmI) has derived her name Shri from this very title.

## \|\| 24.49 \|\|

*ramāśrayatvānnitarāṃ sarvadā cetyato hariḥ śrīnivāsābhidhānaḥ \|*  
*bho śrīnivāseti tu nartayanto romāñcamātrāstalaśabdakāriṇaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Lord Visnu has derived his name Srinivasa from the fact that he is the resort of Sri."

Thus addressing Srinivasa by name, dancing and clapping hands, with the thrill of hair on their person, they gave expression to their feeling.

## \|\| 24.50 \|\|

*adyaiva paśyāma harestavāsyaṃ kadā vayaṃ kṛta kṛtyā bhavāmaḥ \|*  
*bhoḥ keśavādyaiva padāravindaṃ saṃdarśayitvā sudayāṃ kuruṣva \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. О lord, may we see your face this very day. When shall we get our desire fulfilled. О lord Kesava, you favour us this very day by showing your lotus-feet to us.

## \|\| 24.51 \|\|

*brahmāṇamāhuśca purāṇamāhuḥ kaśabdavācyaṃ sarvalokeśamāhuḥ \|*  
*īśaṃ cārhaṃ rudramityeva cāhustatprerakaṃ sṛṣṭisaṃhārakārye \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. In the epithet Kesava, the letter К devotes the primeval being Brahma which again signifies the lord of all beings. The word Isa denotes the worthy lord Rudra who instigates dissolution of the universe.

## \|\| 24.52 \|\|

*ato hariḥ keśavanāmadheyo bhoḥ keśaveti ca nartayantaḥ \|*  
*ānandavāpīṃ saṃstravantobhi jagmurnārāyaṇeti pravadanto hi jagmuḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Hence, Hari is called Kesava. Addressing the lord by this epithet, dancing as well as trickling the spring of joy in the form of joyful tears and uttering the name lord Narayana, they continued their upward journey.

## \|\| 24.53 \|\|

*ato doṣāstadviruddhā guṇāśca nārāśca teṣāmāśrayatvānmurāriḥ \|*  
*nārāyaṇeti pravadantīha loke nārānubandhātsarvamuktāḥ khagendra \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. As he is the resort of merits and demerits and as he abides in the waters of ocean, he is called Narayana. People who take resort to him obtain eternal release, О lord of birds.

## \|\| 24.54 \|\|

*nārāḥ proktā āśrayatvācca teṣāmatopi nārāyaṇa eva vīndra \|*  
*muktāśca ye tu prapadaṃnu jagmuraṇḍodakaṃ yasya kaṭākṣamātrāt \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. Waters are called Nara; as he resorts to waters (in the ocean) he is called Narayana. Those who resort to Narayana get an eternal release.

## \|\| 24.55 \|\|

*yadutpannaṃ tena nārāḥ khagendra teṣāṃ sadāpyāśrayatvācca vīndra \|*  
*nārāyaṇeti pravadantīha loke hyanantabrahmāṇḍavisarjakatvāt \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Naras (waters) are so called because as cosmic waters they emerge from his side-glances. As he is the resort Garuda Purana of these waters he is called Narayana and also because he is the source of this eternal universe.

## \|\| 24.56 \|\|

*evaṃ nanṛtuḥ pariśaṃsayanto govinda nānyo hi na caiva darśanam \|*  
*gośabdavācyāstu samastavāco gobhiśca sarvaiḥ pratipādyate yataḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. They danced, praising the lord thus: "O Govinda, there is no such Being as your supreme self, no such personage whose sight is so thrilling and delightful.

## \|\| 24.57 \|\|

*ato hi govinda iti smṛtaḥ sadā bho vedavedyeti tathā na nanṛtuḥ \|*  
*ānandabāṣpaiśca samanvitā hi hare murāre tava darśanaṃ hi \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. In the word Govinda go means the universal speech. As you are expressed by the medium of universal speech you are called Govinda. О lord, you are known to or by the Vedas".

## \|\| 24.58-59 \|\|

*dehi prabho vai tavadāsadāsāścaturdaśe bhuvane sarvadāpi \|*  
*yatastvamevaṃ vasatīti vāsuścātraiva nityaṃ krīḍate sarvadaiva \|\| 58 \|\|*

*yato devetyevamāhurmahāntastvato hariṃ vāsudeveti cāhuḥ \|*  
*bho vāsudeveti nanṛtuḥ sarvadaiva bho mādhaveti nanṛtuścaiva sarve \|\| 59 \|\|*

58-59. Thus they danced while their eyes flowed with joyful tears. "O Hari, tShte slayer of Asura Mura, please grant us an interview. We are slaves of your slaves.

As you pervade the fourteen worlds you are called Vajsu. As you play sports constantly or as the great declare you a god, you are called Vasudeva."

Thus uttering the names of the lord-Vasudeva, Madhava, etc., they danced joyously.

## \|\| 24.60 \|\|

*lakṣmīpate ceti vadanti sarve dhanīti śabdaḥ svābhivācī yato hi \|*  
*atopyāryā mādhaveti bruvanti lakṣmīpate pāhi tathaiva bhaktān \|\| 60 \|\|*

60"People call you Laksmpati the lord of Laksmi.

The syllable dhava is the synonym of treasure. Hence, the learned call you Madhava (the treasure of Laksmi). О lord of Laksmi, protect us, your devotees."

## \|\| 24.61 \|\|

*te vai bruvanto nanṛtuśca jagmurviṣṇo sadāsmānparipāhi nityam \|*  
*sarvatra yasmādvitatosi tasmādityādināmāni gṛṇanta eva \|*  
*jagmuśca sarve dadṛśuśca tīrthaṃ bhaktyopetāḥ śrīnivāsaṃ smarantaḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. Thus speaking they danced and persued their upward journey, uttering "O lord, grant us your eternal protection. As you are omnipresent you are called Vitala." Reciting names of the lord they reached their goal. Here, full of devotion, they sought the audience of the lord.

## \|\| 24.62 \|\|

*kanyovāca \|*  
*kiṃ nāmakaṃ tīrthamidaṃ munīndra kiṃ kāryamatra pravadāsmānkṛtīśa \|*  
*kasmai prasanno bhagavāñchrīnivāsastvasminsutīrthaṃ vada vistareṇa \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. Maid said: — О best of sages, what is the name of this shrine? What rites shall we perform here? whom did lord Srinivasa favour at this holy place, please tell us in detail.

## \|\| 24.63 \|\|

*jaigīṣavya uvāca \|*  
*kanye śṛṇu tvaṃ hyabhavatsubuddhimānprahrādasaṃjño haribhaktavaryaḥ \|*  
*niṣkāmabuddhyā tu yadā jagāma śeṣācalasthaṃ śrīnivāsaṃ ca draṣṭum \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. Jaiglsavya said: — О maid, listen to the narrative of Prahlada, the best of the devotees of Visnu. Once, when he had no desire to fulfil, Prahlada went to see lord Srinivasa on the Sesa mountain.

## \|\| 24.64 \|\|

*asmiṃsthale daityakumārakānprati hareśca tattvaṃ paripṛṣṭavānprabhuḥ \|*  
*nṛsiṃharūpaṃ śrīnivāsaṃ bhajasva sudurlabhaṃ mānuṣaṃ janma kanye \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. This was the place where Prahlada had spoken to the Daitya youths on the glory of Visnu. Hence, О maid, you should worship lord Visnu (the abode of Laksmi), in the form of Nrsiriiha.

## \|\| 24.65 \|\|

*tatrāpi viṣṇornṛhare sutattvaṃ sudurlabhaṃ suṣṭhu yātrā tathaiva \|*  
*yasyāṃ yātrāyāṃ yatra kutrāpi deśe hareḥ kathā vartate daityavaryāḥ \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. Prahlada said: — "O Daitya youths, you have attained human life which is very rare. Rarer is the knowledge of the glory of Nrsimha.

That journey is fruitful whereon throughout they utter the narrative of Hari, О Daitya youths, Hari abides at the place where they sing his narrative, though he pervades the universe."

## \|\| 24.66-68 \|\|

*tatra sthale harirāste sadaiva yato vyāptaḥ sarvato vai nṛsiṃhaḥ \|*  
*etacchrutvā daityakumārakāste prahlādamūcurbhaktavaryaṃ hareśca \|\| 66 \|\|*

*vyāpto hariścetkathamatra vai sakhe na dṛśyate jalarūpī nṛsiṃhaḥ \|*  
*sa evamukto dānavānāṃ sutaiśca tuṣṭāva viṣṇuṃ paramādareṇa \|\| 67 \|\|*

*tava svarūpaṃ mama darśayasva svayogyarūpaṃ dānavānāṃ sutānām \|*  
*iti stutaḥ śrīnivāso haristu tasminnantarjalarūpaṃ samāyāt \|\| 68 \|\|*

66-68. On hearing the same, the daitya princes said to Prahlada, the devotee of Hari. 'O friend, if Hari is all-pervading why does he withhold his presence here in the midst of water."

Thus spoken to by the Daitya youths, Srinivasa appeared in the midst of water.

## \|\| 24.69 \|\|

*asminsnānaṃ ye prakurvanti tīrthe teṣāṃ jñānaṃ paramaṃ dṛḍhaṃ syāt \|*  
*atra snāne mānuṣāṇāṃ ca tāta buddhirna hi syātkalikāle viśeṣāt \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. Those who bathe in this holy pool stabilize their intellect which remains untarnished with impurity, even in this iron age (Kali).

## \|\| 24.70 \|\|

*dattvāṃ varaṃ daityavarāya viṣṇurantardadhe jalapūrṇe sukuṇḍe \|*  
*adyāpyāste jalamadhye nṛsiṃhaḥ prahlādopi daityakumārakaiḥ saha \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. Thus bestowing favour on the Daitya youths, lord Visnu disappeared in the midst of waters.

Even today, Nrsimha abides in the midst of waters. Prahlada too abides therein together with Daitya youths.

## \|\| 24.71 \|\|

*tasmin sutīrthe paritastatratatra jayeti śabdaḥ śrūyate cāparāhne \|*  
*idaṃ tīrthaṃ nārasiṃhābhidhaṃ ca kanyesnānaṃ hyatra kāryaṃ manuṣyaiḥ \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. Here in the afternoon, there is heard all around the word 'Victory'. The place is called Narsimha in recollection of the Victory of the lord over Hiranyakasipu. Pilgrims who visit this holy place take plunge here.

## \|\| 24.72 \|\|

*snānaṃ kṛtvā tatra tīrthe ca samyagdīpaṃ dattvā dvijavaryāya mukhyam \|*  
*draṣṭuṃ punaḥ śrīnivāsaṃ prajagmurgovindagovinda iti bruvantaḥ \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. They took bath, offered an earthen lamp to a pious Brahmin Srinivasa, uttering 'Govinda, Govinda.'

## \|\| 24.73 \|\|

*mukhyaprāṇādhiṣṭhitaṃ sthānamāpya apāviśattatra devī hyuvāca \|*  
*jaigīṣavyaḥ śrīnivāsasya viṣṇoḥ kathaṃ kāryaṃ darśanaṃ tadvadasva \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. The maid sat in front of the principal deity and said. "O noble Jaiglsavya, please tell me how shall I have the audience of the lord."

On hearing this, Jaiglsavya was extremely delighted at heart and said.

## \|\| 24.74 \|\|

*jaigīṣavyaḥ prāha saṃhṛṣṭacitto bravīmi tantraṃ śṛṇu kanyake tvam \|*  
*śrutvā mattaḥ kuru sarvaṃ mayoktamādyaṃ dvāraṃ śrīnivāsasya dṛṣṭvā \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. О, maid, I tell you how you shall proceed. At the main gate of Srinivasa you should recite the following.

## \|\| 24.75 \|\|

*aparādhasahasrāṇi kriyante 'harniśaṃ mayā \|*  
*tāni sarvāṇi me deva kṣamasva puruṣottama \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. "I commit thousands of faults day and night. О lord, pardon all these faults of mine, О best of primeval beings.

## \|\| 24.76 \|\|

*mānasānvācikāndoṣānkāyi kānapi sarvaśaḥ \|*  
*vaiṣṇavadveṣahetūnme bhasmasātkuru mādhava \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. О lord, efface those causes of mine which create hatred for the devotees of Visnu, whether they are mental, oral or physical."

## \|\| 24.77 \|\|

*ādyadvāraṃ śrīnivāsasya devi samyak smaredvijayaṃ vai jayaṃ ca \|*  
*dakṣādhvare śrīnivāsasya devi caṇḍaṃ pracaṇḍaṃ saṃsmaretsamyageva \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. О maid, at the main gate of the temple of Srinivasa you should remember, in the appropriate form Jaya and Vijaya. At the right gate of Srinivasa you should remember Canda and Pracanda.

## \|\| 24.78 \|\|

*pāścātyabhāge śrīnivāsasya devi nandaṃ sunandaṃ saṃsmaredeva bhaktyā \|*  
*savyadvāre śrīnivāsasya kanye smaretkumudākṣaṃ kumudantameva \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. At the left gate, you should remember Nanda and Sunanda; at the extreme left gate you should remember the lily, eyed Kumudanetra.

## \|\| 24.79-81 \|\|

*yaścaiva dehaṃ praviśedbhaktipūrvaṃ kadā drakṣye sādareṇaiva devi \|*  
*pradakṣiṇadvādaśakaṃ ca kṛtvā pade pade saṃsmarecchrīnivāsam \|\| 79 \|\|*

*śrīsvāmitīrthe samyagācamya natvā snātvā natvā bhūvarāhaṃ ca devi \|*  
*ayudvāraṃ privaśedbhaktipṛrvaṃ govindagovinda itibruvanvai \|\| 80 \|\|*

*paścāddharernamanaṃ kāryameva sāṣṭāṅgarūpaṃ praviśeddevageham \|*  
*punarviśedvārataḥ saṃsthitaḥ sa pīṭhasthadevānmānasā cintayīta \|\| 81 \|\|*

79-81. You should enter the temple with full devotion and reverence, circumambulating the lord twelve times.

You should sip waters in the holy pool and bathe. You should enter right in the interior of the temple and other chain, tiers. While there, you should meditate on the deities sitting in their respective postures on their seats.

## \|\| 24.82-83 \|\|

*madhye pīṭhaṃ śrīnivāsaṃ ca devī nārāyaṇaṃ praṇametpūrṇameva \|*  
*devasya savye pīṭhabhāgādvahiśca namaskāryaṃ gurudevāya caiva \|\| 82 \|\|*

*pīṭhasyāgrāccāpyadhastātpradeśe āgneyakoṇe praṇamedvai khagendra \|*  
*nairṛtyabhāge vyāsadevāya devi namaskāryo vaiṣṇavaḥ sarvadāpi \|\| 83 \|\|*

82-83. In the middle of the seat you should bow to Srinivasa with all the organs of your body. To the left of the lord, outside the pedestal,.you should pay homage to the preceptor, in front of the pedestal, just below at the south-east comer. In the south-western corner you should pay homage to the honourable Vyasa. The devotee of Visnu should be honoured all times everywhere.

## \|\| 24.84 \|\|

*vāyavyakoṇe bhaktipūrvaṃ sudurgāṃ namaskuryādbhaktisaṃvardhitātmā \|*  
*pīṭhasyordhvaṃ hyagnikoṇeṣu devī dharmādhibhūtāya namo yamāya \|\| 84 \|\|*

84\. In the north-western corner you should honour goddess Durga with your soul elevated by devotion.

Above the pedestal, in the south-east region you should bow to Yama, the presiding deity of Dharma.

## \|\| 24.85 \|\|

*pīṭhasyordhvaṃ nairṛtasyordhvakoṇe jñānādhipaṃ praṇamedvāyudevam \|*  
*pīṭhasyordhvaṃ vāyukoṇe ca subhrūrvairāgyānāmadhipaṃ caiva rudram \|\| 85 \|\|*

85\. Above the pedestal, in the upper southwestern region to Vayu.

## \|\| 24.86 \|\|

*pīṭhasyordhvaṃ tvīśakoṇe ca devi aiśvaryāṇāmadhipaṃ cendradevam \|*  
*pīṭhasya pūrve praṇamennairṛtiṃ ca aryāmṇānāmadhipaṃ cātra devi \|\| 86 \|\|*

86\. Above the pedestal, in the north-eastern region to India, the supreme lord. To the east of the pedestal, to Nairrti, lord of the Aryamans.

## \|\| 24.87 \|\|

*devasya pīṭhasya ca dakṣiṇe ca durgāṃ namedugrarūpābhidhāṃ ca \|*  
*pīṭhasya kanye praṇametpaścime vai ārogyāṇā madhipaṃ kāmadevam \|\| 87 \|\|*

87\. To the south of the pedestal, the goddess Durga called Ugra. To the west of the pedestal, to Kamadeva, the lord of health.

## \|\| 24.88 \|\|

*devasya pīṭhasyottare rudradevamanaiśvaryāṇāmadhipaṃ saṃsmarecca \|*  
*pīṭhasya madhye praṇamedvai varāhaṃ sadā kanye paramaṃ pūruṣākhyam \|\| 88 \|\|*

88\. To the north of the, pedestal, to Rudra the supreme lord. To the middle of the pedestal, to Varaha the primeval man.

## \|\| 24.89 \|\|

*tasyopariṣṭācchaktisaṃjñāṃ ca lakṣmīmādhārarūpāṃ praṇameccaiva nityam \|*  
*tasyopariṣṭādvāyukūrmau namecca tasyopariṣṭāccheṣakūrmau namecca \|\| 89 \|\|*

89\. Above the pedestal, to Laksmi called sakti, the support of the universe. Above the pedestal, to Vayu and Kurma. Above that to Sesa and Karma.

## \|\| 24.90-91 \|\|

*tasyopariṣṭādabhimāninīṃ bhuvo bhūdevatāṃ praṇameccaiva subhrūḥ \|*  
*tasyopariṣṭādvaruṇaṃ saṃsmarecca kṣīrodadheradhipaṃ caiva devam \|\| 90 \|\|*

*tasyopariṣṭātpraṇameccaiva lakṣmīṃ śvetadvīpākhyaṃ kanyake pūjayecca \|*  
*tasyopariṣṭātpraṇameccaiva lakṣmīṃ mahādivyāṃ maṇḍapasaṃjñakāṃ ca \|\| 91 \|\|*

90-91. Above that, cosmic Earth, the presiding deity of the universe. Above that, to Varuna, the lord of milky ocean. Above that, to Laksmi called Svetadvlpa, Above that, to divine Laksmi called Mandapa.

## \|\| 24.92 \|\|

*pīṭhasya madhye yamasaṃjñāṃ ca lakṣmīṃ samarcayedyamamadhye ca devīm \|*  
*yamasya devasya ca dakṣiṇe ca sūryaṃ nameddīparūpaṃ ca bhadre \|\| 92 \|\|*

92\. In the middle of Piths, to Laksmi called Yaml. By the side of Yam! to Devi. To the south of Yama, to Surya in the forth of an earthen lamp.

## \|\| 24.93 \|\|

*yamasya devasya ca vāmabhāge śriyaṃ nameddīparūpāṃ ca devīm \|*  
*yamasya devasya tu cāgrabhāge hutāśanaṃ dīparūpaṃ namecca \|\| 93 \|\|*

93\. To the left of God Yama, to Sri in the form of an earthen lamp. In front of Yama, to Agni also in the form of an earthern lamp.

## \|\| 24.94 \|\|

*devasyāgre bhūmināmnīṃ namecca tattvābhimānāṃ saṃsmareccaiva nityam \|*  
*paryaṅkarūpaṃ śrīnivāsasya viṣṇostamobhimānāṃ sannameccaiva durgām \|\| 94 \|\|*

94\. In front of the lord, he should bow to Bhumi the presiding deity of the elements, and to Durga, the presiding deity of tamas and the reclining couch of the lord.

## \|\| 24.95 \|\|

*pūrvādigaṃ pīṭhasopānarūpamātmānamekaṃ praṇamecca devi \|*  
*dakṣasthadikpīṭhasopānarūpaṃ jñānātmakaṃ praṇameccaiva kanye \|\| 95 \|\|*

95\. To the east, to the Atman in the form of steps of the pedestal. To the east, to knowledge (Jnana) in the form of steps of the pedestal.

## \|\| 24.96 \|\|

*padmasya pūrvasthadale ca devi strīrūpākhyaṃ vimalākhyāmimāṃ ca \|*  
*brahmādidevānpraṇamecca devi āgneyakoṇasthadale śṛṇutvam \|\| 96 \|\|*

96\. To the east, in the leaf of a lotus, to Vimala in the form of a female and to the deities, Brahma, etc.

## \|\| 24.97 \|\|

*utkarṣanāmnīṃ paramāṃ ca devīṃ namedramāṃ brahmavāyū ca śeṣam \|*  
*dakṣasthapadmasya dalāṣṭake ca nārāyaṇākāraśeṣādikānām \|\| 97 \|\|*

97\. In the lotus-leaf in the south-east corner you should bow to the excellent gods and goddesses Utkarsa, LaksmT, Brahma, Vayu and Shesa.

## \|\| 24.98 \|\|

*strīrūpebhyo namanaṃ kāryameva kanye mayā paścimasthe dale ca \|*  
*gopākhyanārāyaṇabrahmavāyuviprādikānāṃ śeṣarūdrādikānām \|\| 98 \|\|*

98\. In the eight-petalled lotus to the right you should worship Narayana, Sesa; etc, in the form of a female.

## \|\| 24.99 \|\|

*strīrūpebhyo namanaṃ kāryameva īśāna koṇasthadaleṣu caiva \|*  
*īśānanārāyaṇamāviriñcavāyurviyaccheṣasurādikānām \|\| 99 \|\|*

99\. In the lotus leaf in the north-east, to Isana, Narayana, Viranca, Vayu, ether, Sesa and Devas in the form of a female.

## \|\| 24.100-101 \|\|

*strīrūpebhyo namanaṃ kāryameva tathaiva padmasya ca madhyabhāge \|*  
*anugrahākhyā viṣṇulakṣmīśca devī vāyurviyaccheṣarudrādikānām \|\| 100 \|\|*

*strīrūpāṇāṃ namanaṃ kāryameva suyogapīṭhasya svarūpa bhūtam \|*  
*sadā namecchrīmadanantasaṃjñamevaṃ na mecchrīnivāsaṃ ca devam \|\| 101 \|\|*

100-101. In the middle of the lotus, to Visnu, Laksmi Anugraha and to Vayu, ether, Sesa and Rudra in the form of a female. To lord Ananta in the form of pedestal and to Srinivasa.

## \|\| 24.102 \|\|

*śrīnivāsasya vāme tu lakṣmīṃ ca praṇamedvudhaḥ \|*  
*śrīnivāsasya savye tu dharāyai praṇamecchubhe \|\| 102 \|\|*

102\. To the left of Srinivasa to LaksmT; in the middle of Srinivasa to Dhara.

## \|\| 24.103-104 \|\|

*pīṭhādvahiḥ pūrvabhāge kṛpolkaṃ praṇamecchubham \|*  
*maholkaṃ dakṣiṇe caiva vīrolkaṃ paścime namet \|\| 103 \|\|*

*uttare ca namaḥ kuryādyulkāya ca mahātmane \|*  
*caturṣvapi ca koṇeṣu sahasrolkaṃ nametsudhīḥ \|\| 104 \|\|*

103-104. Outside the pedestal, in the east, to Krpolka; in the south, to Maholka; in the west, to VIrolka; in the north, to Ulka; in the four corners,-to Sahasrolka.

## \|\| 24.105-112 \|\|

*pūrve tu vāsudevāya namaskuryācca dakṣiṇe \|*  
*saṃkarṣaṇāya devāya pradyumnāya ca paścime \|\| 105 \|\|*

*uttare hyaniruddhyā namaskuryādatandritaḥ \|*  
*āgneye ca namaskuryātkanye māyāṃ sadā śubhe \|\| 106 \|\|*

*jayāyai ca namaskuryānnairṛtye cāpi vāyave \|*  
*kṛtye caiva namaskuryādīśānye śāntisaṃjñakām \|\| 107 \|\|*

*keśavāya namaḥ pūrve tathā nārāyaṇāya ca \|*  
*mādhavāya namaskuryānnairṛtye cāpi vāyave \|\| 108 \|\|*

*āgneye kanyake nityaṃ bhaktyā tu prayataḥ śubhe \|*  
*govindāya namaskuryāddakṣiṇe viṣṇave tathā \|\| 109 \|\|*

*madhusūdanāya bhoḥ kanye namaskuryāttu nairṛtau \|*  
*paścime trivikramāya vāmanāya tathaiva ca \|\| 110 \|\|*

*viṣṇave śrīdharāyātha namaskuryācca bhāmiti \|*  
*uttare tu mahākanye hṛṣīkeśāya vai namaḥ \|\| 111 \|\|*

*tathā vai padmanābhāya namaskuryādatandritaḥ \|*  
*dāmodarāya caiśānye namaskuryācca bhāmini \|\| 112 \|\|*

105-112. In the east to Vasudeva, in the south to Sankara in the west to Pradyumna, in the north to Aniruddha; in the south-east to Maya, in the south-west to Jaya, in the northeast to Krtya. in the north-east to anti; in the east to Kesava in the south-west and north-west to Narayana and Madhava, in the south-east to Govinda, in the south to Visnu, in the southwest to Madhusudana, in the west to Trivikrama, Vamana, Visnu and Sridhara, in the north to Hrslkesa and Padmanabha, in the north-east to Damodara.

## \|\| 24.113-119 \|\|

*caturthāvaraṇe pūrve mahākūrmāya vai namaḥ \|*  
*varāhāya namaskuryādāgneye kanyake śubhe \|\| 113 \|\|*

*dakṣiṇe nārasiṃhāya vāmanāya namonamaḥ \|*  
*bhārgavāya namaskuryānnairṛtye śuddhacetasā \|\| 114 \|\|*

*paścime mādhavāyātha tathā kṛṣṇāya vai namaḥ \|*  
*buddhāya ca namaskuryādvāyavye kanyake śubhe \|\| 115 \|\|*

*uttare hyulkarūpāya anantāya namostu te \|*  
*īśānye viśvarūpāya namaskuryādatandritaḥ \|\| 116 \|\|*

*āgneye vāruṇīṃ caiva gāyatrīṃ caiva nairṛte \|*  
*vāyavye bhāratīṃ caiva īśānye girijāṃ namet \|\| 117 \|\|*

*girijāṃ vāmabhāge tu sauparṇai caiva saṃnamet \|*  
*prāgindrāya namaskuryātsāyudhāya tathaiva ca \|\| 118 \|\|*

*sa parigrahāya śrīviṣṇoḥ pārṣadāya namonamaḥ \|*  
*āgneyetyagnaye tubhyaṃ sāyudhāyeti pūrvavat \|\| 119 \|\|*

113-119. In the fourth enclosure, in the east to Mahakurma in the south-east to Varaha; in the south to Nrsimha and Vamana; in the southwest to Bhargava (Parasurama); in the west to Madhava Krsna; in the north-west to Buddha, in the north to Ananta in the form of Ulka and in the north-east to Visvarupa; in the south-east to Varuru; in the south-west to Gayatrl; in the north-west to Bharatl, in the north-east to Girija. To the left of Girija to SauparnI; in the east to Indra with weapons, in the south-east to Visnu with his attendants and assemblage with the formula, To you О Agni with weapons, as stated previously.

## \|\| 24.120-122 \|\|

*dakṣiṇe tu yamāyaiva nairṛtyāṃ nirṛtiṃ yajet \|*  
*paścime varuṇāyaiva vāyavye vāyave namaḥ \|\| 120 \|\|*

*uttare ca kuberāya īśānye ca śivāya ca \|*  
*īśānaśakrayormadhye brahmaṇe sāyudhāya ca \|\| 121 \|\|*

*nirṛtyapyatimadhye tu śeṣāya ca namonamaḥ \|*  
*evaṃ kṛtvā namaskāraṃ praṇamecca punaḥ punaḥ \|\| 122 \|\|*

120-122. In the south to Yama, in the south-west to Nirrti, in the west to Varuna, in the north-west, to Vayu, in the north to Kubera, in the north-east to diva, in the midst of north-east and east to Brahma with weapons, in the middle of north-west to Sesa. Thus you should pay homage again and again.

## \|\| 24.123 \|\|

*ityetatsarvamākhyātaṃ vidhipūrvaṃ tu darśanam \|*  
*itaḥ parantu gantavyaṃ darśanārthaṃ ramāpate \|\| 123 \|\|*

123\. Thus, I have narrated to you the prescribed way how one should have the vision of the lord when one goes to a shrine to pay visit.

Thus addressed, she went to the shrine together with companions.

## \|\| 24.124 \|\|

*evamuktvā tu sā devī taiḥ sārdhaṃ tu yayau mudā \|*  
*yaduktaḥ śrīnivāsasya darśanasya vidhiḥ khaga \|*  
*kasyacinnaiva vaktavyo gopyatvācca kadācana \|\| 124 \|\|*

124\. This prescribed way of paying visit to Srinivasa which I have told you just now should not be divulged to any one, О bird, since it is very, very secret.

## \|\| 24.125 \|\|

*samāgamo durghaṭa eva vīndra satāṃ ca sattattvavibodhakānām \|*  
*anekajanmārjitapuṇyasaṃcayādabhūdguroḥ saṃgama eva tasya \|\| 125 \|\|*

125\. О lord of birds, it is very rare to keep company the good and noble people who can throw light on the nature of tattvas. It is possible to have a preceptor only if one has In store the aggregate of merits accumulated in previous existences.

## \|\| 24.126 \|\|

*payo vikāraṃ ca nijaṃ jahāti śeṣasya śeṣaṃ nalinasya ṣaṅkajam \|*  
*bhāvaṃ calaṃ paṅkajanābhayogātsatsaṃgayogādaśubhāni na syuḥ \|\| 126 \|\|*

126\. In the company of holy pious people even unauspicious things turn into auspicious ones. In the company of lord Visnu the unsteady mind leaves its steadiness, as water changes its nature in association with the seashell or in contact with the lotus leaf.

## Chapter 25

## \|\| 25.1 \|\|

*sā dvāradeśe śrīnivāsasya devī svāmipuṣkariṇīṃ dadṛśe kaiśca sārdham \|*  
*svāminhare śrīnivāseti sā taṃ brahmādīnāṃ tārakaṃ sampradadhyau \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. At the gate of the temple of Srinivasa she and her companions saw SvamipuskarinI saying, "O lord Hari, Srinivasa", she meditated on the lord who takes Brahma and others across the ocean of existence.

## \|\| 25.2 \|\|

*devaiḥ sārdhaṃ pālanārthaṃ ca viṣṇurastyeva nityaṃ puṣkariṇyāṃ jaleṣu \|*  
*ataḥ svāmipuṣkariṇīti cāhustatra snānaṃ kanyakānyāśca cakruḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. There, in the pool, Visnu abides, together with other gods for the preservation of people. Hence, the pool is called Svanmipuskarinl. Therein, the maiden and her companions took bath.

## \|\| 25.3 \|\|

*śucirbhūtvā śrīnivāsaṃ ca devāstuptuṃ viviśuḥ śuddhabhaktyā khagendra \|*  
*yathopadiṣṭaṃ guruṇā tathaiva cakre kanyāśca sarvaṃ khagendra \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. О lord of birds, formerly, the gods purified themselves by taking bath and entered the temple of Srinivasa to practise penance with sincere devotion. As instructed by the preceptor, the maid too acted all accordingly, О lord of birds.

## \|\| 25.4 \|\|

*tadā hariṃ darśayāmāsa tasyai svakaṃ rūpaṃ supratīke supūrṇam \|*  
*sā kanyakā śrīnivāsasya rūpaṃ dadarśa bhaktyā svamanobhirāmam \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Then, the lord in his full and beautiful form appeared before her. The maid saw the beautiful form of Srinivasa and was delighted thereby.

## \|\| 25.5-7 \|\|

*suvarṇacitraṃ vasanaṃ vasānaṃ soṣṇīṣakaṃ kañcukaṃ saṃdadhānam \|\| 5 \|\|*

*mṛgotthamadagandhena surabhīkṛtadiṅmukham \|*  
*puṇḍarīkaviśālākṣaṃ kaṃbugrīvaṃ mahābhujam \|\| 6 \|\|*

*hemayajñopavītāṅgaṃ sākṣātkandarpasannibham \|*  
*jaganmohanasaindaryaṃ komalāṅgaṃ manoharam \|\| 7 \|\|*

5-7. She saw the lord wearing the gold- embroidered dress with an attractive head gear and bodice. His body was anointed with the paste of musk whereof the smell had made the quarters fragrant. The lord had lotuslike blooming eyes, neck as round as the conch, long arms, golden sacred thread and looked as the cupid incarnate. He possessed charms which could subjugate any people and who had a body pretty and tender.

## \|\| 25.8 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā ca kanyā mumude romāñcitasugātrakā \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. At the sight of the lord, the maid was extremely delighted. Her body showed a thrilling hair.

## \|\| 25.9 \|\|

*taddarśanāhlādapariplutāśayā premṇātha romāśrukulākulekṣaṇā \|*  
*nanarta devī puratastasya viṣṇoḥ sā dhvastadoṣā paramādareṇa \|*  
*ānanda māṃ pāhi sukhaṃ ca dattvā mukundā māṃ pāhi vimuktidānāt \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Her heart overflowed with pleasure at the sight of the lord. Her eyes were full of tears and her body showed joyful signs of thrilling hair. She danced in the presence of the lord. Her faults were destroyed with the feeling of devotion. In a tone full of emotion she addressed the lord thus.

## \|\| 25.10 \|\|

*māṃ pāhi nityaṃ hyaravindanetra prasannadṛṣṭyā karuṇāsudhārdra \|*  
*govinda govinda suduḥ khitāṃ māṃ jñānādidānena hi pāhi nityam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. "О blissful lord, save me from worldly affections, grant me your eternal bliss by releasing me from the shackles of Karman.

О lotus-eyed lord, save me by your sweet, charming glance full of ambrosial compassion. О lord, I am extremely distressed, save me by bestowing wisdom on me.

## \|\| 25.11 \|\|

*janārdana tvaṃ hi suduṣṭasaṃgānkāmādirūpānsatataṃ varjayitvā \|*  
*hare hare māṃ satataṃ pāhi daityānsamāhṛtya prabalānvighnarūpān \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. О lord, dissociate me from my bad companions-desire wrath and greed, and destroy my strong foes, viz., the obstacles.

## \|\| 25.12 \|\|

*rameśa māṃ pāhi caturmukheśa viśveśa māṃ pāhi sarasvatīśa \|*  
*rameśa māṃ pāhi nidānamūrte vṛndāravṛndairvanditapādapadma \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. О lord of Laksmi, Sarasvati, Brahma and the universe protect me; О lord of Laksmi, the primeval course of the Universe, whose lotus feet are bowed to by the gods, protect me."

## \|\| 25.13 \|\|

*evaṃ tu natvā paramādareṇa tuṣṭāva viṣṇuṃ paramaṃ purāṇam \|*  
*lakṣmyā sadā ye 'viditā guṇāśca asaṃkhyātāḥ saṃti viṣṇau ca vīśa \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Thus paying homage with reverence she praised the primeval lord. О lord of birds, there are innumerable merits n Visnu which are not known to Laksmi.

## \|\| 25.14 \|\|

*teṣāṃ sakāśādatibāhulyasaṃkhyā guṇā harau te 'viditā vai ramāyāḥ \|*  
*ato hare stavane kvāsti śaktistathāpi yatnaṃ stavane te kariṣye \|\| 14 \|\|*

14 \[She continued\] "Unknown even to Laksmi, there exist several qualities in Hari. I cannot praise you adequately. Still I shall try to praise you, О lord.

## \|\| 25.15 \|\|

*tava prasādācca ramāprasādādvidhiprasādātbhāratīśaprasādāt \|*  
*rudraprasādātstavanaṃ te kariṣye tathāpi viṣṇo mayi śāntiṃ kuruṣva \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. By the favour of Brahma, the lord of speech, by the favour of Rudra, above all by your favour, I praise you, О lord. Be kind enough to bestow peace on me.

## \|\| 25.16 \|\|

*yadi prasannosi mayi tvamīśa tvatpādamūle dehi bhaktiṃ sadaiva \|*  
*tvaddarśanāddeva śubhāśubhaṃ ca naṣṭaṃ madīyaṃ hyaśubhaṃ ca nityam \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. О lord, if you are pleased with me, bestow devotion on me for your feet. At your very sight, О lord, good and evil-both disappear for ever.

## \|\| 25.17 \|\|

*tvanmāyayā naṣṭamimaṃ ca lokaṃ madena mattaṃ badhiraṃ cāndhabhūtam \|*  
*aiśvaryayogena ca yo hi mūko jātaḥ sadā dīnāgurvādikeṣu \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. The world is destroyed by illusion indicated by pride, It has turned deaf and blind by the excess of wealth.

## \|\| 25.18 \|\|

*mā dehi aiśvaryamanuttamaṃ tvatpādāravindasya viruddhabhūtam \|*  
*tvaṃ deva me dehi satāṃ ca saṃgaṃ tava svarūpapratipādakānām \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. I do not seek for that wealth which would make me dumb to the entreaties of the poor or the preceptor and drive me off from the service of your lotus-feet.

О lord, grant me company of the good that may help me to realize my inner self.

## \|\| 25.19-20 \|\|

*putrādīnāmaihikaṃ vāsudeva dagdhvā ca me dehi pādāravinde \|*  
*sadvaṣṇave kriyamāṇaṃ ca kopaṃ dagdhvā ca me dehi pādaravinde \|\| 19 \|\|*

*dravyādike kriyamāṇaṃ ca lobhaṃ dagdhvā vai me dehi pādābjamūle \|*  
*putrādike kriyamāṇaṃ ca mohaṃ dagdhvā ca me dehi pādābjamūle \|\| 20 \|\|*

19-20. Please extinguish my desire for sons, etc. Grant me devotion for your lotus-feet. Take off my treasure and bestow the same among your devotees. Grant me devotion for your lotus-feet.

## \|\| 25.21 \|\|

*vidyāṃ putraṃ dravyajātaṃ madaṃ ca dagdhvā ca me dehi pādābjamūle \|*  
*sadvaiṣṇavāsahamānasvarūpaṃ dagdhvā mātsaryaṃ pāhi māṃ veṅkaṭeśa \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Please take off my learning, progeny, wealth and pride. Grant me devotion for your lotus-feet. Take off my jealousy and intolerance for the noble devotees of Visnu. О lord of Venkata grant me protection.

## \|\| 25.22 \|\|

*mantraṃ ca me dehi nidānamūrte yenaiva me syāttava saṃgaśca bhūyaḥ \|*  
*nānyaṃ vṛṇe tava pādābjasaṃgāttadeva me dehi mama prasannaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. O primeval cause of the universe, grant me that mantra which may enable me to meet you again. I ask for nothing more than my contact with your lotus-feet. Please grant me this all if you are so pleased with me."

## \|\| 25.23 \|\|

*itīritaḥ śrīnivāsaḥ prasanna uvāca devo hyamṛtastravaṃ ca \|*  
*atraiva kanye prajapasva mantraṃ sugopyarūpaṃ paramādareṇa \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Thus spoken to by her, Srinivasa was extremely pleased. Emitting nectar from his mouth he spoke to her.

"O maid, I give you the most secret mantra which you should keep repeating with devotion.

## \|\| 25.24-25 \|\|

*vakṣyāmi mantraṃ paramādareṇa śṛṇvadya bhaktyā paramādareṇa \|*  
*antaḥ sthamantyaṃ hyādyasaṃyuktameva sabindu tadvatsparśakādyena yuktam \|\| 24 \|\|*

*ekārayuktaṃ prathamāntaḥ sthayuktaṃ samatrikoṇe coṣmaṇā saṃyutaṃ ca \|*  
*takārasaktaṃ svarśamantaḥ sthayuktamādyanta oṃkārasamanvitaṃ ca \|\| 25 \|\|*

24-25. I tell you this mantra out of affection for you.

Please listen to the mantra with devotion, as I disclose it to you; Om Vyankatesaya namah Om.

## \|\| 25.26 \|\|

*anena mantreṇa tavepsitaṃ ca bhaveddhi kanye nāntra vicāryamasti \|*  
*evaṃ sa uktvā śrīnivāso haristu pratīkavaddarśayāmāsa rūpam \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. О maid, by this mantra you will achieve the object of your desire." Having addressed the maid thus, the lord appeared before her in a symbolic form.

## \|\| 25.27 \|\|

*natvā tu sā śrīnivāsaṃ ca devī uvāsa ha svāmisaraḥ samīpe \|*  
*tasmindine brāhmaṇādīṃśca sarvānsaṃtarpayāmāsa ca ṣaḍrasānnaiḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. The girl bowed to the lord and stayed in the vicinity of the lake named Svamisaras, On that very day she propitiated the Brahmanas and other castes with the cooked food of ingredients.

## \|\| 25.28-29 \|\|

*sāyaṅkāle śrīnivāsasya dṛṣṭvā utsāharūpaiḥ śrīnivāsapratīkaiḥ \|*  
*sākaṃ bhaktyā saṃpraṇamyātha devī pradakṣiṇaṃ śrīnivāsasya suṣṭhu \|\| 28 \|\|*

*nanarta devī supratīkasya cāgre lajjāṃ tyaktvā jaya deveti coktvā \|*  
*ānṛttakāle ca hareśca vaktraṃ dṛṣṭvā ca dṛṣṭyā tu paraṃ nanarta \|\| 29 \|\|*

28-29. In the evening she paid homage to the idol and other symbols of the lord. She circumambulated the image and danced without reservation, proclaiming victory of the lord. During this performance, she set her eyes on the image of the lord and danced over and over again.

## \|\| 25.30 \|\|

*mamādya gātraṃ pāvitaṃ śrīnivāsa mamādya netraṃ saphalaṃ saṃbabhūva \|*  
*mamādya pādau sārthakau caiva jātau pradakṣiṇaṃ śrīnivāseśa kṛtvā \|\| 30 \|\|*

30"To day, my body is purified, my eyes have borne fruit, my feet have gained purpose, since I have circumambulated you, my lord.

## \|\| 25.31 \|\|

*hastau ca me sārthakāvadya jātau agre kṛtvā hastaśabdaṃ murāreḥ \|*  
*evaṃ vadantī prīṇayantī ca devaṃ jagāmasā stotravacaḥ kadambaiḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. My hands have attained the fruits of their existence since they have clapped to glorify the lord." Thus she pleased the lord by these utterances which were full of praises for the lord.

## \|\| 25.32-33 \|\|

*devāstadā dunduṃbhayo vinedire tanmastake puṣpavṛṣṭiṃ ca cakruḥ \|*  
*tasminkāle ubhayoḥ pārśvayośca nṛtyaṃ cakrurdevatāvāranāryaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

*tathaiva tāstalaśabdaṃ ca kṛtvā tadā sarvā namanaṃ cāpi cakruḥ \|*  
*ānandaśaile sarvadā tvitthameva sā sarvadā nartayantī ca vīndra \|\| 33 \|\|*

32-33. The gods sounded drums, played on instruments and scattered flowers on her head. They danced around her, together with their consorts, clapped hands and paid homage. О lord of birds, she too danced before the lord on the Ananda mountain every day.

## \|\| 25.34 \|\|

*ānandamagnā sāpi devī jagāma svamāśramaṃ jaigiṣavyeṇa sārdham \|*  
*yātrāmevaṃ ye na kurvanti vīndra teṣāṃ ca sarvaṃ niṣphalaṃ cāhurāryāḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Immersed in bliss he returned to her hermitage together with Jaigisavya. О lord of birds, those who do not go on pilgrimage thus have all their pious activities come to naughty thus say the noble.

## \|\| 25.35 \|\|

*gatvāśramaṃ jaigiṣavyeṇa sārdhaṃ guruṃ tvapṛcchadveṅkaṭeśasya mantram \|*  
*mantrasyārthaṃ brūhi me jaigiṣavya mantrāvṛttiṃ kurvatāṃ vai phalāya \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. She went to the hermitage together with Jaigisavya. and asked the preceptor about the mantra of Vyankatesa. О sir, tell me the meaning of mantra so that I may perform Japa for the attainment of my desire.

## \|\| 25.36 \|\|

*jaigīṣvya uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇuṣva bhadre veṅkaṭe śasya nāmnastvarthaṃ śrutvā hṛdaye saṃnidhatsva \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Jaigisavya said: — О blessed one, listen to the meaning of the mantra of Venkatesa and then think upon it concentratedly.

## \|\| 25.37 \|\|

*viti hyuttamavācī syādyeti jñānamudāhṛtam \|*  
*kakāraḥ sukhavācī syāṭṭeti cittamudāhṛtam \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. The letter v denotes excellence, у denotes knowledge, к denotes happiness, t expresses consciousness.

## \|\| 25.38 \|\|

*īśatvamātmavāci syādevaṃ jñeyaṃ tu kanyake \|*  
*pūrṇajñānaṃ sukhaṃ vittaṃ vyāptatvādvyaṅkaṭābhidhaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. The word isa signifies the soul. Collectively, it is called Vyankata since it embodies supreme knowledge, undiluted pleasure and immense wealth.

## \|\| 25.39 \|\|

*vya (ve) mindriyādikaṃ proktaṃ vyaṅgabhūtaṃ harau yataḥ \|*  
*kaṭaśca samudāyārtho vyaṃ (veṃ) kaṭaścendriyaughakaḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Or v denotes the organs of senses which constitute the corpus of Hari. Kata means the aggregate of the organs of senses.

## \|\| 25.40 \|\|

*svasminprerayate yasmāttasmādvyaṅkaṭanāmakaḥ \|*  
*viṣaye preṣayennityamato vyaṅkaṭanāmakaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Since he directs these organs inward he is called Vyankata or since he directs them' outward he is called Vyankata.

## \|\| 25.41 \|\|

*viśiṣṭajñānarūpatvādvyeti muktāḥ sadā smṛtāḥ \|*  
*muktānāṃ ca samūhastu vyaṅkaṭeti prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Or vya means the released souls who have attained supreme knowledge.

## \|\| 25.42 \|\|

*sadā muktasamūhānāmīśatvādvyaṅkaṭābhidhaḥ \|*  
*liṅgadehamato jīvo vyaṅkaṭeti samāhṛtaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Vyankata would mean the aggregate of 'released souls'. Vyankatesa would mean the lord of released souls. The individual soul in the subtle body is called Vyankata.

## \|\| 25.43 \|\|

*liṅgānāṃ caiva svāmitvādvyaṅkaṭeśeti saṃjñitaḥ \|*  
*daityānāṃ ca samūhāstu jñānādividhurā yataḥ \|*  
*ato daityasamūhastu vyaṅkaṭeti prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Or the word Vyankata means the aggregate of Daityas, so called because they are without knowledge.

## \|\| 25.44 \|\|

*teṣāṃ saṃharaṇe īśastvato vyaṅkaṭanāmakaḥ \|*  
*ānandasya viruddhatvātkāmakrodhādayo guṇāḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. The lord Vyankatesa is capable of liquidating them, he is called Vyankatesa.

## \|\| 25.45 \|\|

*vyaṅkaṭā iti saṃproktāsteṣāṃ nāśayitā prabhuḥ \|*  
*atastu vyaṅkaṭeśākhya evaṃ jñātvā japaṃ kuru \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Desire, rage, etc are evils that are contrary to bliss, hence they are named Vyankata. Since he destroys these he is called Vyankatesa. О maiden, on knowing this you should perform japa.

## \|\| 25.46 \|\|

*evaṃ vyaṅkaṭāmāhātmyaṃ śrutvā devī khageśvara \|*  
*nidrāṃ cakāra tatraiva rātrau pitrā sahaiva ca \|*  
*brāhme muhūrte cotthayi hṛdi sasmāra kanyakā \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. О lord of birds, the maiden heard the glory of Vyarikata and passed the night in that asrama together with her father. She got up early in the morning, set her thought on Vyankatesa and praised the lord.

## \|\| 25.47 \|\|

*(vyaṅkaṭeśasya prātaḥ stutiḥ) \|*  
*śrīvyaṅkaṭeśaśca nṛsiṃhamūrtiḥ śrīvaradarājaśca varāhamūrtiḥ \|*  
*śrīraṅgaśāyī ca anantaśāyī kurvantu sarve mama suprabhātam \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Sri Vyankatesa, Sri Nrsirhha, Sri Varadaraja, Varaha, the god Sriranga, lord Visnu with Sesa for his repose — may all these deities make this morning auspicious for me.

## \|\| 25.48 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇamūrtiśca gadādharaśca śrīviṣṇupādastu prayāgavāsaḥ \|*  
*nārāyaṇaḥ śrībadarīnivāsaḥ kurvantu sarve mama suprabhātam \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Lord Srikrsna, Balarama, lord Visnu who resides at Prayaga, Narayana who abides in Badari — may all these deities make this morning auspicious for me.

## \|\| 25.49 \|\|

*dāmodaro vai trijagannivāsaḥ śrīpāṇḍuraṅgaśca nṛsiṃhadevaḥ \|*  
*śrīrāmadevaśca amoghavāsaḥ kudṛ \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Damodara, the abode of three worlds, lord Pandurariga, lord Nrsirhha, lord Ramadeva who resides at Amogha — may all these make this morning auspicious for me.

## \|\| 25.50 \|\|

*śrīdharmaputraśca nṛsiṃhamūrtiḥ śrīpippalasthaśca muhallavāsaḥ \|*  
*kolānṛsiṃhaḥ śūrpakārastha siṃhaḥ kurvantudṛ \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Sri Dharmaputra, of the form of Nrsimha, Sri Pippastha who resides at Muhalla, Kola Nrsirhha and Surpakara Nrsirhha — may all these deities make this morning auspicious for me

## \|\| 25.51 \|\|

*caturmukhaścārusarasvatī ca svabhāratī śarvasuparṇaśeṣāḥ \|*  
*amāmahedraśca śacīmukhāstāḥ kurvantu dṛ \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Lord Brahma, Sarasvatl, Bharatl, Sarva, Supana, Sesa, Uma, Mahendra, Saci and others — may all these deities make this morning auspicious for me.

## \|\| 25.52 \|\|

*dvārāvatī kāśikāvantikā ca prayāgakāñcyau mathurāpurī ca \|*  
*māyāvatī hastimatī purī ca kurvantu sa dṛ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Dvaravati, Kasika, Avantika, Pray — ga, Kanci, Mathura, Mayavati, Hastimafi — may all these cities make this morning auspicious for me.

## \|\| 25.53 \|\|

*bhāgīrathī caiva sarasvatī ca godāvarī siṃdhukṛṣṇa ca veṇī \|*  
*kalindakanyā yamunā ca narmadā kurva dṛ \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. Bhagirathi, Sarasvati, Godavari, Sindhu, Krsna, Veni, Kalindi Yamuna, Narmada — may all these rivers make this morning auspicious for me.

## \|\| 25.54 \|\|

*vitastikāverisatuṅgabhadrāḥ suvañjarā bhīmarathī vipāśā \|*  
*sutāmraparṇī ca pinākinī ca ku dṛ \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. Vitasta, Kaveri, Turigabhadra, Suvanjara, Bhima-rathl, Vipasa, TamraparnI, Pinakini — may all these rivers make this morning auspicious for me.

## \|\| 25.55 \|\|

*svāmipuṣkariṇī caiva suvarṇamukharī tathā \|*  
*śrīpāṇḍavī taubaruśca kapilā pāpanāśanī \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Svami Puskarini, Suvarnamukhan,Sripandavi, Taumbaru, Kapil; the destroyer of sins.

## \|\| 25.56 \|\|

*gururvasiṣṭhaḥ kraturaṅgirāśca manuḥ pulastyaḥ pulahaśca gautamaḥ \|*  
*raibhyo marīciścyavanaśca dakṣaḥ kurvantu sarve mama suprabhātam \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. Guru, Vasistha, Kratu, Arigiras, Manu, Pulastya, Pulaha, Gautama, Raibhya, Marici, Cyavana, Daksa — may all these sages make this morning auspicious for me.

## \|\| 25.57 \|\|

*saptārṇavāḥ sapta kulācalāśca dvīpāśca saptopavanāni sapta \|*  
*bhūrādikāni bhuvanāni sapta kurvantu sa dṛ \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Seven oceans, seven Kula-parvatas, seven dvlpas, seven pleasure gardens, seven regions such as Bhur etc., may all these make this morning auspicious for me.

## \|\| 25.58 \|\|

*māndhātrā nahuṣoṃbarīṣasagarau rājā nalo dharmarāṭ prahlādaḥ kraturāḍ vibhīṣaṇagayau vyāso hanūmānapi \|*  
*aśvatthāma kṛpāvumā drupadajā śrījānakī tārakā mandodaryakhilāḥ prabhātasumahaṃ kurvantu nityaṃ hare \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. Mandhatr, Nahusa, Ambarlsa, Sagara, Nala, Dharma-raja, Prahlada, Kraturaj, Vibhlsana, Gaya, Vyasa, Hanuman, Asvatthaman, Krpa, DraupadI, SIta, Taraka, Mandodarl — may all these make this morning auspicious for me.

## \|\| 25.59 \|\|

*aśvatthasya vanāni kiṃ ca tulasīdhātrīvanāni prabho punnāgasya vanāni caṃpakavanānyanyāni puṣpāṇi ca \|*  
*mandārasya vanāni yāni ca hareḥ saugandhikānyapyaho nityaṃ tāni diśantu matpramuditaṃ śrīveṅkaṭeśa prabho \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. The forests of holy fig, holy basil, amalaka, Naga-kesara, Campaka, coral trees and flowers of various hues and tragrancesmay all these make this morning auspicious for me.

## \|\| 25.60 \|\|

*evaṃ smṛtvā śrīnivāsasya devī kṛtvā śaucaṃ jaigiṣavyeṇa sākam \|*  
*snātuṃ yayau puṣkariṇīṃ hareśca snānaṃ samyak tatra cakāra deśe \|*  
*samyag japtvā vyaṅkaṭeśasya mantramuvāca sā jaigiṣavyaṃ guruṃ ca \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. Thus after meditating on Srinivasa in her heart, the maiden performed the daily necessaries and went to SvamipuskarinI together with Jaiglsavya and took bath in the prescribed way. She then repeated the mantra of Vyankatesa and spoke to Jaiglsavya and the preceptor.

## Chapter 26

## \|\| 26.1 \|\|

*kanyovāca \|*  
*śrīnivāsaḥ kimarthaṃ vai āgatotra vadasva me \|*  
*śeṣācalopi kutrā bhūtkadāyātaśca pāpahā \|*  
*svāmipuṣkariṇī cātra kimarthaṃ hyagatā vada \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. The Maiden said: — Why has Srinivasa come over here, please tell me, О sage? Where was the mountain Sesa, the destroyer of sins situated formerly? Please tell me, why has the lake SvamipuskarinI come over here?

## \|\| 26.2 \|\|

*jaigīṣavya uvāca śṛṇu bhadre mahābhāge vyaṅkaṭeśasya cāgamam \|*  
*āvayordevi pāpāni viṣamaṃ yānti bhāmini \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Jaigisvya said: — О fortunate one, О blessed one, hear how lord Vyankatesa came over here. О illustrious one, our sins are destroyed by narrating and listening to the tale.

## \|\| 26.3-5 \|\|

*āsītpurā hiraṇyākṣaḥ kāśyapo ditinandanaḥ \|*  
*sanakādeśca vāgdaṇḍāddvitīyadvārapālakaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

*babhūva daityayonau ca devānāṃ kaṇṭako balī \|*  
*saṃjīvo vijayaḥ prokto haribhakto mahāprabhuḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

*hariṇyākṣaḥ svayaṃ daityo haribhaktavidūṣakaḥ \|*  
*etādṛśo hiraṇyākṣastapastaptuṃ samudyataḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

3-5. In olden days, there was an asura named Hiranyaksa, the son of sage Kasyapa and Diti. He functioned as a keeper at the second gate of the palace of Visnu. But as cursed by Sanaka the son of Brahma, he became a daitya and began to trouble the devotees of lord Visnu.

Sanjiva, known as Vijaya was a devotee of Visnu. Hiranyaksa the daitya began to trouble the devotees. But, as influenced by Vijaya, he thought of practising penance. Thereupon his mother Diti spoke to him.

## \|\| 26.6-8 \|\|

*tadā mātā ditirdevī hiraṇyākṣamuvāca sā \|*  
*ditiruvāca \|*  
*vatsalastvaṃ mahābhāgamā tapasvāṣṭahāyanaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

*tvaṃ mā dadasva duḥ khaṃ me pālayiṣyati kovidaḥ \|*  
*kṣaṇamātraṃ na jīvāmi tvāṃ vinā jīvanaṃ na hi \|\| 7 \|\|*

*mā tapa tvaṃ mahābhāga mama jīvanahetave \|*  
*evamuktastu mātrā sa vijayovaśatobravīt \|\| 8 \|\|*

6-8. Diti said: — Dear son, do not take to penance. You are just eight years old. Do not trouble me. I cannot remain, even for a moment without you. Do not practice penance if you want me to remain alive. Though he was thus accosted by his mother, he being still under the spiritual influence of Vijaya said.

## \|\| 26.9 \|\|

*hiraṇyākṣo mātaraṃ prāha jālaṃ hitvā viṣṇorbhajane 'laṃ kuruṣva \|*  
*mayisnehaṃ putrahetorvirūḍhaṃ sukhaduḥ khe ceha loke paratra \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. "Dear mother, get rid of these worldly attachments. Direct your mind to the service of lord Visnu. The very fact that you love me as your son is the cause of your pain and pleasure.

## \|\| 26.10-11 \|\|

*yāvatsnehaṃ mayi mātaḥ karoṣi tāvatkleśaṃ śāśvataṃ yāsyasi tvam \|*  
*mātaśca te mayi putratvabuddhistvayyapyeṣā mātṛbuddhirmamāpi \|\| 10 \|\|*

*tāte pūjye pitṛbuddhirmamāsti tasmiṃstute bhartṛbuddhirhi mithyā \|*  
*nirmāti yasmāddharireva sarvaṃ samyak pātā niyato 'sau murāriḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

10-11. О mother, as long as you have love for me, you will remain in trouble. О mother, you regard me as your son, I regard you as your mother. I regard the venerable sire as my father, while you regard him as your husband. О mother, all this is an appearance, not reality, for Visnu alone is the creator as well as protector. This is a fact which cannot be controverted.

## \|\| 26.12 \|\|

*ato hi mātā harireva sarvadā tvanyāsāṃ vai mātṛtā copacārāt \|*  
*nirmātṛtvaṃ yadi mukhyaṃ tvayi syāddroṇādīnāṃ jananī kā vadasva \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Hence, Visnu alone is the real mother. The mother who gives birth is so only formally.

## \|\| 26.13 \|\|

*mātṛtvaṃ vai yadi mukhyaṃ tvayi syāddhātrādīnāṃ jananī kā vadasva \|*  
*yataḥ sadā yāti jagattatto hariḥ sadā pitā viṣṇurajaḥ purāṇaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. If motherhood accruing from giving birth were a principal factor, then you will also be the mother of a jar which you create. Or conversely, who will be the mother of Brahma, the uncreated god? Therefore, the uncreated primeval lord Visnu alone is the father, he being the real creator.

## \|\| 26.14-15 \|\|

*sadā pitā mukhyapitā yadi syādgarbhasthabāle pālakaḥ ko vadasva \|*  
*mātāpitroḥ pālakatvaṃ yadi syātkūrmādīnāṃ pālakau kau vadasva \|\| 14 \|\|*

*mātāpitroḥ pālakatvaṃ yadi syātkṛpādīnāṃ rakṣakau kau vadasva \|*  
*punnāmakānnārakāddeha bhajāntasmāttrātāputraviṣṇuḥ purāṇaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

14-15. If the generating parents be the principal cause, then who is the protector and preserver of the child in womb? If the protection of the child be confined to parents then who were the protectors of Kurma and Krpa in their sufferings? Tell me, О mother.

## \|\| 26.16 \|\|

*na tārakohaṃ narakācca subhrūrna vai bhartā nāpi pitrādayaśca \|*  
*na vai mātā nānujādiśca sarvaḥ sarvatrātā viṣṇurato na cānyaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The primeval lord Visnu alone is the son because he protects against the hell Pum which is none other than this body itself.

О mother, neither I your son nor your husband nor your parents nor brothers can ever be called your protectors. None other than Visnu is the protector.

## \|\| 26.17 \|\|

*māyāṃ madīyāṃ jñānaśastreṇa cchitvā bhaktyā hareḥ smaraṇaṃ tvaṃ kuruṣva \|*  
*yadbhaktirūpūrvaṃ smaraṇaṃ nāma viṣṇostatsarvathā pāpaharaṃ ca mātaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. О mother, cut off your illusion with the weapon of knowledge and fix up your mind in Hari. О mother, the devotional remembrance of the name of Hari alone can destroy sins for ever.

## \|\| 26.18 \|\|

*yo vā bhaktyā smaraṇaṃ nāma viṣṇoḥ karotyasau pāpaharo bhaviṣyati \|*  
*ayaṃ deho durllabhaḥ karmabhūmau tatrāpi madhye bhajanaṃ viṣṇumūrteḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. He who out of devotion remembers Visnu is purified. But in this land of activity (Bharata) it is rare to obtain a human form and rarer is the devotion for Visnu.

## \|\| 26.19 \|\|

*āyurgataṃ vyarthameva tvadīyaṃ śīghraṃ bhajeḥ śrīnivāsasya pādam \|*  
*upadiśyaivaṃ mātaraṃ putravaryo daityāveśātsobhavadvai tapasvī \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Your life is wasted in vain. О mother, you should soon begin worshipping the feet of Srinivasa". Having instructed the mother thus, the pious son adopted the life of an ascetic.

## \|\| 26.20 \|\|

*caturmukhaṃ prīṇayitvaiva bhaktyā hyavadhyatvaṃ prāpa tasmānmahātmā \|*  
*tato bhūmiṃ karavadveṣṭayitvā ninye tadā daityavaryo mahātmā \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. By his sincere devotion he pleased Brahma and got the boon of immortality. Then he, the best of daityas rounded the earth in his hand and took the same to Patala.

## \|\| 26.21 \|\|

*śrīmuṣṭadeśe prādurāsīddharistu vārāhaviṣṇustvajanaḥ purāṇaḥ \|*  
*bhittvācābdhiṃ viviśe taṃ mahātmā rasātale saṃsthitaṃ bhūtalaṃ ca \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Then Hari incarnated as boar in the country of Mustas. Making way through the ocean he entered Patala and discovered the earth.

## \|\| 26.22 \|\|

*svadaṃṣṭrāgre sthāpayitvā'jagāma tadāgamādāgato daityavaryaḥ \|*  
*taṃ karṇamūle tāḍayitvā jaghāna prasādayāmāsa ca pūrvavadbhuvam \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Putting the earth at the tip of his teeth he lifted it up from the waters. Hiranyaksa, when he saw the earth lifted up, came upon Visnu but was struck at the ears and killed by Visnu who established the earth in the previous fashion.

## \|\| 26.23 \|\|

*sudiggajānsthāpayitvā ca viṣṇuḥ śrīmuṣṭe vai saṃsthitaḥ śrīvarāhaḥ \|*  
*tadā hariścintayāmāsa viṣṇurbhaktyā madīyaṃ mānuṣaṃ dehamadya \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Visnu, as a boar, set up the elephants in all quarters and settled in Srimusta Then Visnu thought to himself. "Where shall the people propitiate me in my human form? Where shall I favour them, with my blessings."

## \|\| 26.24 \|\|

*ārādhayiṣyanti ca māṃ kva ete teṣāṃ dayāṃ kutra vāhaṃ kariṣye \|*  
*evaṃ hariścintayitvā sukanye vaikuṇṭhalokādacalaṃ śeṣa saṃjñam \|*  
*vīndraskandhe sthāpayitvā svayaṃ ca samāgatobhūdbhūtalaṃ bhūtaleśaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. О good girl, thus Hari thought to himself. Then from Vaikuntha he came down to the earth, riding on Garuda and brought the mountain Sesa along with him.

## \|\| 26.25 \|\|

*suvarṇamukharītīramārabhya garuḍadhvajaḥ \|*  
*śrīkṛṣṇaveṇīparyantaṃ sthāpayā māsa taṃ girim \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Then lord Visnu established the mountain Sesa over the region extending from Suvarnamukhan to Srikrsna Veni.

## \|\| 26.26 \|\|

*gireḥ pucche tu śrīśailaṃ madhyame 'hobalaṃ smṛtam \|*  
*mukhaṃ ca śrīnivāsasya kṣetraṃ ca samudāhṛtam \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. He settled Srisaila at the tail of the mountain, Ahobala at the middle and Srinivasa at the mouth.

## \|\| 26.27 \|\|

*alpena tapasābhīṣṭaṃ sidhyatyasminnahobale \|*  
*gaṅgādisarvatīrthāni puṇyāni hyatra saṃti vai \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. By practising even a little of penance at Ahobala, one can fulfil one's desire. The holy rivers such as Ganga and others are always present here.

## \|\| 26.28-29 \|\|

*ya enaṃ sevate nityaṃ śraddhābhaktisamanvitaḥ \|*  
*jñānārthī jñānamāpnoti dravyārthī dravyamāpruyāt \|\| 28 \|\|*

*putrārthī putramāpnoti nṛpo rājyaṃ ca vindati \|*  
*yaṃyaṃ kāmayate martyastantamāpnoti sarvathā \|\| 29 \|\|*

28-29. Whoever abides here, full of faith and devotion obtains knowledge, wealth, progeny, kingdom, whatever be his needs.

Whatever desire he entertains he obtains the fulfilment of it. Whatever is desired to be aceomplished the same is accomplished there. The mountain is therefore called Cintamani.

## \|\| 26.30-31 \|\|

*cintitaṃ sādhyate yasmāttasmāccintāmaṇiṃ viduḥ \|*  
*puṣkariṇyāśca bāhulyādgirāvasminsaraḥ su ca \|*  
*puṣkarādririti prāhurevaṃ tattvārthavedinaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

*śātakuṃbhasvarūpatvātkanakādriṃ ca taṃ viduḥ \|*  
*vaikuṇṭhādāgatenaiva vaikuṇṭhādririti smṛtaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

30-31. The mountain contains many lakes, hence it is called Puskaradri. It is golden in hue, hence it is named Kanakadri. It has arrived from Vaikuntha, so it is called Vaikuntha. It contains the wealth of nectar, so it is named Vyarikatadri.

## \|\| 26.32-33 \|\|

*amṛtaiśvaryasaṃyukto vyaṅkaṭādririti smṛtaḥ \|*  
*vyaṅkaṭeśasya śailasya māhātmyaṃ yāvadasti hi \|\| 32 \|\|*

*tāvadvaktuṃ samagreṇa na samarthaścaturmukhaḥ \|*  
*vyaṅkaṭādrau parāṃ bhaktiṃ ye kurvanti dinedine \|*  
*paṅgarjaṅghāla eva syādacakṣuḥ padmalocanaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

32-33. The glory of Vyankata mountain is indeed very great. Even lord Brahma of four faces cannot describe it. Those who propitiate the mountain every day receive immense reward. The lame are cured of their lameness, the blind are restored to their eye-sight.

## \|\| 26.34 \|\|

*mūko vāgmī bhavedeva badhiraḥ śrāvako bhavet \|*  
*vandhyā syādbahuputrā ca nirdhanaḥ sadhano bhavet \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. The dumb gain speech; the deaf are restored to hearing. The barren become fertile and are blessed with sons; the poor become rich.

## \|\| 26.35 \|\|

*etatsarvaṃ girau bhaktimātreṇaiva bhaveddhruvam \|*  
*tattvato vyaṅkaṭādrestu svarūpaṃ vetti ko bhuvi \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Mere devotion for this mountain can bring about these benefits. In fact, no one can realize the true nature of this mountainlord.

## \|\| 26.36 \|\|

*yasmādasya gireḥ puṇyaṃ māhātmyaṃ vetti yaḥ pumān \|*  
*māyāvī paramānandaṃ tyaktvā vaikuṇṭhamuttamam \|*  
*svāmipuṣkariṇītīre ramayā sahamodate \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. He who understands the true nature of this mountain is blessed with pleasure. Lord Visnu who knew the glory of this mountain left Vaikuntha and made his abode on this mountain on the bank of Svamipuskarim together with his consort LaksmI.

## \|\| 26.37 \|\|

*kalyāṇādbhutagātrāya kāmitārthapdāyine \|*  
*śrīmadvyaṅkaṭanāthāya śrīnivāsāya te namaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Homage to Srinivasa, lord of Vyankata, of charming and auspicious body and the bestower of desired objects.

## \|\| 26.38 \|\|

*śrīsvāmipuṣkariṇyāśca māhātmyaṃ śṛṇu kanyake \|*  
*svāmipuṣkariṇīmadhye śrīnivāsosti sarvadā \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. О maid, now hear the glory of Svamipuskarim. Lord Srinivasa abides in the midest of this lake.

## \|\| 26.39 \|\|

*snānaṃ kurvanti ye tatra teṣāṃ muktiḥ kare sthitā \|*  
*tisraḥ koṭyordhakoṭiśca tīrthāni bhuvanatraye \|*  
*tāni sarvāṇi tatraiva saṃti tīrthe hareḥ sadā \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Those who take bath herein attain liberation very easily and very quickly. In this holy place of pilgrimage there abide three and a half crore of TIrthas that constitute the entire lot of holy places in the three worlds.

## \|\| 26.40 \|\|

*tattīrthaṃ śrīnivāsākhyaṃ sarvadevanamaskṛtam \|*  
*tadeva śrīnivāsasya mandiraṃ parikīrtitam \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. This sacred place of pilgrimage called Srinivasa is visited by all gods. This is what they declare to be the abode of Srinivasa.

## \|\| 26.41 \|\|

*taddarśanādeva kanye yānti pāpāni bhasmasāt \|*  
*ekaikasnānamātreṇa satsaṃgo bhavati dhruvam \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. The very sight of Srinivasa destroys sins. At each and every visit the devotee is associated with the good.

## \|\| 26.42 \|\|

*satsaṃgājjñānamāsādya jñānānmokṣaṃ ca vindati \|*  
*adhikāriṇāṃ bhavedevaṃ viparītamayoginām \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Association with the good leads to knowledge, knowledge leads to liberation. But the benefit is available only to those who are initiated in the cult of Visnu.

## \|\| 26.43 \|\|

*tīrthānāṃ snānamātreṇa mokṣaṃ yāntīti ye viduḥ \|*  
*te sarve asurā jñeyāste yānti hyadhamāṃ gatim \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Mere ablution in the TIrthas, without initiation into the Vaisnava cult, cannot bring about liberation. Those who think otherwise are asums, condemned to five in a wretched State.

## \|\| 26.44 \|\|

*śrīnivāsasya tīrthesminvāyukoṇe ca kanyake \|*  
*āste vāyuḥ sadā viṣṇoḥ pūjāṃ kartumanuttamām \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. О maiden, in the north-west of this holy place, there dwells Vayu always engaged in the service of the lord.

## \|\| 26.45 \|\|

*vāyutīrthaṃ ca tatproktaṃ hastadvādaśakāntaram \|*  
*hastaṣaṭkapramāṇaṃ ca paścime samudāhṛtam \|*  
*uttare hastaṣaṭkaṃ tu vāyutīrthamudāhṛtam \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. This is called Vayutirtha measuring twelve betas,, six in the west and six in the north.

## \|\| 26.46 \|\|

*ye veṣṇavā vaiṣṇavadāsavaryāḥ snānaṃ suryustatra pūrvaṃ sukanye \|*  
*madhvāntasthāḥ śrīnivāsastu nityamatra snānātprīyatāṃ me dayāluḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. The Vaisnavas (the devotees of Visnu) and their devotees too while they take bath in this Vayu tlrtha should mutter prayers thus: "May the compassionate lord Srinivasa bepleased with me while I take bath here.

## \|\| 26.47 \|\|

*ye madhvatīrthe snātumicchanti devi rudrādayo vāyubhaktā mahāntaḥ \|*  
*sadā snānaṃ tatra kurvanti devi prātaḥ kāle codayātpūrvameva \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. The god Rudra and other gods take bath in this Maddhva-Tirtha. Those others who desire to take bath should do likewise early in the morning before sun-rise.

## \|\| 26.48 \|\|

*ye vāyutīrthe visṛjanti dehajaṃ malaṃ mūtraṃ vamanaṃ śleṣmakaṃ ca \|*  
*ye 'pānaśuddhiṃ liṅgaśuddhiṃ ca kanye kurvanti te hyasurā rākṣasāśca \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Those who evacuate, urinate, vomit or spit or wash their anus or penis at his holy tlrtha become asuras or raksasas.

## \|\| 26.49-50 \|\|

*śṛṇvanti ye bhāgavataṃ purāṇaṃ kiṃ varṇaye tasya puṇyaṃ tu devi \|*  
*ye kṛṣṇamantraṃ tu japanti devi hyaṣṭā kṣaraṃ mantravaraṃ sugopyam \|\| 49 \|\|*

*teṣāṃ hariḥ prīyate keśavolaṃ madhvāntastho nātra vicāryamasti \|*  
*evaṃ dānaṃ tatra kurvanti ye vai dvijāgryāṇāṃ vaiṣṇavānāṃ vidāṃ ca \|\| 50 \|\|*

49-50. Those who listen to the recitation of the Bhagavata Purana attain indescribable merit. Lord Hari, Kesava who abides in this Madhvatlrtha is pleased with those who repeat the eight-syllabled Mantra, the best and the most confidential of all mantras.

## \|\| 26.51 \|\|

*teṣāṃ puṇyaṃ naiva jānanti devā jānātyevaṃ śrīnivāso haristu \|*  
*śālagrāmaṃ vāyutīrthe dadante teṣāṃ puṇyaṃ vetti sa vyaṅkaṭeśaḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. The gods do not know the merit that accrues to those who give salagrama in charity at the Vayutirtha. That merit is known only to Srinivasa, the lord of Vyankata who bestows the same upon his devotees.

## \|\| 26.52 \|\|

*sudurlabho vāyutīrthe 'bhiṣeko niṣkāmabuddhyā vaiṣṇavānāṃ ca devi \|*  
*tatrāpi tīrthe labhyate bhāgyayogādbhāgavatasya śravaṇaṃ viṣṇudāsaiḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Ablution in the pool at Vayutirtha without any desire of fruit is highly meritorious. It is a sheer luck if a devotee happens to hear the recitation of the Bhagavata Purana.

## \|\| 26.53-54 \|\|

*tathaiva tīrthe durlabhaṃ tatra devi śālagrāmasya dvijavarye ca dānam \|*  
*jaṃbūphalākārasunīlavarṇaṃ mukhadvayaṃ cakracatuṣṭayānvitam \|\| 53 \|\|*

*sukesaraiḥ saṃyutaṃ svarṇacihnadhvajāṃ kuśairvajracihnairyavaiśca \|*  
*jānārdanīṃ mūrtimāhurmahānto dānaṃ tasyā durlabhaṃ tatra tīrthe \|\| 54 \|\|*

53-54. Highly meritorious is the gift of salagrama to a Brahmana and the gift of Visnu's image of the colour of Jambu fruit, possessed of two faces, wielding four wheels, with the body anointed with saffron and adorned with goldembroidered flag, good thunderbolt and the golden seeds of barley corn.

## \|\| 26.55 \|\|

*atyuttamaṃ mūrtidānaṃ tu bhadre sudurllabhaṃ paramaṃ nātra lobhaḥ \|*  
*sudurlabhaṃ bahudogdhyāśca gṛṣṭerdānaṃ tathā vastraratnādikānām \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. О auspicious one, the gift of the idol of a deity is excellent. More excellent or meritorious is the gift of a cow that yields abundant milk Similarly, excellent are the gifts of precious clothes and jewels.

## \|\| 26.56 \|\|

*atyuttamaṃ dravyadānaṃ ca devi svāpekṣitaṃ dānamāhurmahāntaḥ \|*  
*svasyānapekṣaṃ phaladānaṃ ca vastrādānaṃ tasya vyarthamāhurmahāntaḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. The gift of wealth in coins that are extremely precious to the donor is the best of all gifts, they say. To gift cloth etc. which are discarded by the donor do not yield any fruitful result.

## \|\| 26.57 \|\|

*atyuttamaṃ gṛṣṭidānaṃ ca puṇyaṃ naivāpyate dugdhadohāśca gāvaḥ \|*  
*atyuttame vastradāne subuddhiḥ sudurghaṭā paramā vai janānām \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. The gift of milk-yielding cow is most excellent. The cows giving abundant milk are rare. Moreover, the donors do not generally give excellent gifts in charity.

## \|\| 26.58 \|\|

*atyuttamaṃ bhāgavatasya pustakaṃ sudurghaṭaṃ vāyutīrthaṃ ca kanye \|*  
*atyuttamaṃ dravyadānaṃ ca devi sudurghaṭaṃ vāyutīrthaṃ nṛṇāṃ hi \|*  
*sudurlabho vaiṣṇavaistattvavidbhirharervicāro vāyutīrthe ca kanye \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. О maiden, the gift of the Bhagavata Purana at the Vayutlrtha is most excellent, though very rare. The gift of wealth in coins too is very excellent, though this too is very rare. Discourse with the learned Vaisnavas who possess the essence of knowledge is indeed rare, very rare.

## \|\| 26.59 \|\|

*śrīnivāsasya tīrthasya uttarasyāṃ diśi sthitam \|*  
*candratīrtha miti proktaṃ tatrāste candramāḥ sadā \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. In the north of Srinivasa there is Candratlrtha where the moon shines always.

## \|\| 26.60-61 \|\|

*śrīnivāsasya pūjāṃ ca tatra sthitvā karotyayam \|*  
*tatra snānaṃ prakurvanti puṇyadeśe ca kanyake \|\| 60 \|\|*

*gurutalpādipāpebhyo mucyante nātra saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*tatra snātvā pūrvabhāge śālagrāmaṃ dadāti yaḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

60-61. The moon worships lord Srinivasa at this Titha. О maiden, those who take bath here are absolved of sins such as the sin of going to bed with the wife of the preceptor.

## \|\| 26.62 \|\|

*jñānadvārā mokṣameti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|*  
*dadhivāmanamūrteśca dānaṃ tatra sudurlabham \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. Не who after taking bath donates ^alagrama while sitting in the eastern direction attains knowledge that gets him release. He who makes the gift of Dadhivamana derives immense fruit.

## \|\| 26.63-66 \|\|

*badarīphalamātraṃ tu vatulaṃ nīlavarṇakam \|*  
*prasannavadanaṃ sūkṣmaṃ susnigdhaṃ kanyake śubhe \|\| 63 \|\|*

*cakradvayasamāyuktaṃ gaupūraiḥ pañcabhiryutam \|*  
*cāpabāṇasamāyuktamanataṃ kuṇḍalākṛtim \|\| 64 \|\|*

*vanamāla sukhayutaṃ mūrdhnasāhasrasaṃyutam \|*  
*raupyabindusamāyuktaṃ savye bhadrārdhamātrakam \|\| 65 \|\|*

*candreṇa sahitaṃ devi dadhivāmanamucyate \|*  
*etādṛśaṃ kalau nṝṇāṃ durlabhaṃ bahubhāgyadam \|*  
*lakṣmīnārāyaṇasamāṃ tāṃ mūrtiṃ viddhi bhāmini \|\| 66 \|\|*

63-66. The idol of dadhivamana should have the size of barley corn. It should be mund, blue in hue, slender in proportion and of pleasant aspect. It should wield a pair of wheels, five gopuras, bow and arrow. It should be straight and round and adorned with a garland of wild flowers. It should contain a thousand heads, a silvery dot on the right side of the forehead and an auspicious semicircular mark on the left andthefullmooninthemiddle. Such auspicious gifts are attended by good fortune to the donor. They are very rare in the Kali age. О maiden, know that this idol is equal in merit to the idol of Laksmi Narayana.

## \|\| 26.67 \|\|

*sudurlabhaṃ tasya mūrteśca dānaṃ taccandratīrthe śravaṇaṃ durghaṭaṃ ca \|*  
*samyak svarūpaṃ dadhivāmanasya sudurghaṭaṃ śravaṇaṃ vaiṣṇavācca \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. The gift of this idol is very rare, listening to the narrative of this idol is also rare. Listening to the real nature of the lord, that too from the mouth of a devotee of lord Visnu, is rarer still.

## \|\| 26.68 \|\|

*tatra snātvā vāmanasya svarūpaśravaṇādvidurdānaphalaṃ samaṃ ca \|*  
*daśahastapramāṇaṃ tu candratīrthamudāhṛtam \|\| 68 \|\|*
