68\. Taking bath thereat and listening to the true nature of Vamana со-relate in equal' merit

## \|\| 26.69 \|\|

*madhyāhne durlabhaṃ snānaṃ nṛṇāṃ tatra sumaṅgale \|*  
*tatra sthitvā dhanyanaraḥ sadā bhajati vai harim \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. Takingbath in this auspicious pool is highly meritorious. Lucky indeed is the person who takes bath at noon in this pool.

## \|\| 26.70 \|\|

*varāhamūrtidānaṃ tu śālagrāmasya durlabham \|*  
*jaṃbūphalapramāṇaṃ tu etadvai kukkuṭāṇḍavat \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. Greatly meritorious is the gift of an idol of Visnu who had formerly assumed the form of a pig. The idol should measure in size as the Jambu fruit or should be circular as the egg of a hen.

## \|\| 26.71-72 \|\|

*vadanaṃ valayākāraṃ pramāṇaṃ caṇakādivat \|*  
*devasya vāmabhāge ca madhyadeśaṃ vihāya ca \|\| 71 \|\|*

*cakradvayasamāyuktaṃmūrdhadeśe ca bhāmini \|*  
*suvarṇabindunā yuktaṃ bhūvarāhākhyamucyate \|\| 72 \|\|*

71-72. The idol should have the size of chick-pea and a pleasant aspect too. It should have two wheels one on each side, with the middle left blank. In the middle of the forehead it should have a golden dot.

## \|\| 26.73 \|\|

*pūjāṃ kṛtvā bhūvarāhasya marterdānaṃ dattvā śravaṇaṃ cāpi kṛtvā \|*  
*tatra sthitaṃ bhūvarāhaṃ ca dṛṣṭvā sa vai naraḥ kṛtakṛtyo hi loke \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. He should worship the idol of Bhuvaraha, give the idol to a Brahmana in charity, listen to the merits of his narrative and have a full view of the idol of ^rlvaraha and thus achieve the aim of his life.

## \|\| 26.74 \|\|

*tatra snātvā bhūvarāhasya marteḥ śṛṇoti yo lakṣaṇaṃ samyageva \|*  
*sa tena puṇyaṃ samupaiti devi sa muktibhāṅ nātra vicāryamasti \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. By taking bath at the pool, listening to the auspicious narrative about the idol of Bhuvaraha, he shall attain worldly prosperity and then final beatitude.

## \|\| 26.75 \|\|

*īśānakoṇe śrīnivāsasya devi raudraṃ tīrthaṃ paramaṃ pāvanaṃ ca \|*  
*tatra sthitvā rudradevo mahātmā pūjāṃ karoti śrīnivāsasya nityam \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. In the north-east of Srinivasa, О maiden, there is the most sacred TIrtha of Rudra. Being stationed there, god Rudra worships Srinivasa.

## \|\| 26.76-78 \|\|

*hastāṣṭakaṃ tatpramāṇaṃ vadanti tatra snānaṃ vaiṣṇavaiḥ kāryameva \|*  
*tatra snātvā prayato vai murāreḥ kathāṃ divyāṃ śṛṇuyādādareṇa \|*  
*snānaṃ pānaṃ tatra dānaṃ ca kuryāllakṣmīnṛsiṃhaprīyate devi nityam \|\| 76 \|\|*

*badarīphalamātraṃ ca vartulaṃ bindusaṃyutam \|\| 77 \|\|*

*devasya vāmabhāge tu cakradvayasamanvitam \|*  
*suvarṇarekhāsaṃyuktaṃ kiñcidraktasamanvitam \|\| 78 \|\|*

76-78. This Tirtha is spread ova an area of eight pastas. The devotee of lord Visnu must take bath here. After taking bath concentratedly and attentively he should listen to the divine tale of lord Visnu. О maiden, while staying there he should perform all activities — bathing, eating, drinking and donating gifts — for the propitiation of goddess LaksmI and lord Nrsimha every day.

## \|\| 26.79 \|\|

*vaiśyavarṇaṃ savadanaṃ padmarekhādicihnitam \|*  
*lakṣmīnṛsiṃhaṃ taṃ viddhi bhuktimuktipradāyakam \|\| 79 \|\|*

79\. The idol of LaksmI Nrsirhha is of the size of a berry fruit. It is round, marked with dot, adorned with a pair of wheel on the left side. It bears two streaks of gold and some marks of fluid of blood. It is yellow, of amiable disposition and marked with the lines of the lotus, etc. You should know that it gives prosperity to the devotee in this world and salvation hereafter.

## \|\| 26.80 \|\|

*etā dṛśaṃ gaṇḍikāyāḥ śilāyā mūrterdānaṃ durghaṭaṃ viddhi vīndra \|*  
*tatra snātvā śrīnṛsiṃhasvarūpaṃ lakṣmīpateḥ śṛṇuyādbhaktiyuktaḥ \|\| 80 \|\|*

80\. О lord of bird, the gift of such an idol made of Gandika stone is highly commendable, though it is very rare. (If unable to gift the idol) the devotee should take bath of purification, listen to the tale of Nrsimha, the lord of LaksmI.

## \|\| 26.81 \|\|

*mūrterdānātphalamāpnoti devi satyaṃsatyaṃ nātra vicāryamasti \|\| 81 \|\|*

81\. О maiden, by giving the idol of Shrinrsiihha, the devotee achieves the fulfilment of his wishes.

## \|\| 26.82-83 \|\|

*īśānaśakrayormadhye brahmatīrthamudāhṛtam \|*  
*durlabhaṃ mānuṣāṇāṃ tu snānaṃ sarvārthasādhakam \|\| 82 \|\|*

*śālagrāmasya dānaṃ tu durlabhaṃ tatra vai nṛṇām \|*  
*lakṣmīnārāyaṇasyaiva mūrterdānaṃ sudurlabham \|\| 83 \|\|*

82-83. In the midst of the north-eastern and eastern directions, there is Brahma-Tirtha. A bath therein is highly meritorious. It accomplishes all undertakings. As the gift of Salagrama is highly meritorious, so is the gift of LaksmI Narayana.

## \|\| 26.84-88 \|\|

*sthalamauduṃbarasamaṃ tatpramāṇamudāhṛtam \|*  
*chattrākāraṃ vartulaṃ ca prasannavadanaṃ śubham \|\| 84 \|\|*

*caṇakapradeśamātraṃ ca vadanaṃ samudāhṛtam \|*  
*savye dakṣiṇapārśve ca samayoḥ puṣkalānvitam \|\| 85 \|\|*

*goyūthavatsavarṇaṃ ca catuścakrasamanvitam \|*  
*gokhuraiśca samāyuktaṃ suvarṇakiṇasaṃyutam \|\| 86 \|\|*

*vanamālābhisaṃyuktaṃ vajrapuṅkhaiśca saṃyutam \|*  
*etādṛśīṃ darermūrti lakṣmīnārāyaṇaṃ viduḥ \|\| 87 \|\|*

*kalau nṛṇāṃ tasya lābho durlabhaḥ saṃsmṛto bhuvi \|*  
*dānaṃ ca sutarāṃ devi darlabhaṃ kiṃ vadāmi te \|\| 88 \|\|*

84-88. This idol is of the size of Udumbara. In complexion it is as white as the cow. It bears four wheels, marks of gokhura and suvarnakina. Adorned with the garlands of wild flowers and Vajrapunkha, such an idol of LaksmI Narayana is rare to obtain, specially in the Kali age. The gift of such an idol is highly meritorious.

## \|\| 26.89-90 \|\|

*brahmatīrthe ca saṃsnāya śrotavyā vai hareḥ kathā \|*  
*gaṇḍikāyāḥ śilāyāśca lakṣmīnārāyaṇasya tu \|\| 89 \|\|*

*lakṣaṇaṃ yo vijānāti tadā tatsadṛśaṃ phalam \|*  
*prāpnotyeva na saṃdeho nātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|\| 90 \|\|*

89-90. A devotee should take ablution at the Brahma-tlrtha and listen to the tale of LaksmI Narayana. He who understands the merit of Salagrama stone and of the idol of LaksmI Narayana obtains the fruit of that knowledge equal to that of merit.

## \|\| 26.91 \|\|

*śrīnivāsasya tīrthasya pūrve syādindratīrthakam \|*  
*śrīnivāsasya pūjāṃ tu kartumāste śacīpatiḥ \|\| 91 \|\|*

91\. Indra-Tirtha is situated to the east of Srinivasa, facts, the lord of SacI, is stationed there to worship Srinivasa.

## \|\| 26.92 \|\|

*śālagrāmaśilādānaṃ kartavyaṃ śrotriyāyavai \|*  
*śālagrāmaśilādānaṃ hatyākoṭivināśanam \|\| 92 \|\|*

92\. One should donate the holy stone of Salagrama to a Brahmin well versed in the Vedas. The gift of Salagrama destroys all sins, even those accruing from the slaughter of a Brahmin.

## \|\| 26.93 \|\|

*tasmiṃstīrthe tu yo devi sītārāmaśilābhidhām \|*  
*dadāti bhūtale bhadre bhūpateḥ sadṛśo bhavet \|\| 93 \|\|*

93\. О maiden, he who donates, at this TIrtha, the idol of Salagrama to a worthy Brahmin attains a status equal to the status of the lord.

## \|\| 26.94-95 \|\|

*sītārāmaśilā devi dvividhā saṃprakīrtitā \|*  
*pañcacakrayutā kācitṣaṭracakreṇa ca saṃyutā \|\| 94 \|\|*

*tatrāpi ṣaṭracakrayutā hyuttamā saṃprakīrtitā \|*  
*pañcacakrayutāyāśca phalaṃ dviguṇamīritam \|\| 95 \|\|*

94-95. The idol of Sitarama is twofold: The one carrying five wheels and the other possessed of six wheels. There too, the one possessed of six wheels is superior to the other. The one that carries five wheels yields double fruit.

## \|\| 26.96-97 \|\|

*kukkuṭāṇḍapramāṇaṃ ca susigdhaṃ nīlavarṇakam \|*  
*vadanatrayasaṃyuktaṃ saṭcakraiḥ kesarairyutam \|\| 96 \|\|*

*svarṇarekhāsamāyuktaṃ dhvajavajrāṅkuśairyutam \|*  
*etādṛśaṃ tu vai bhadre sītārāmābhidhaṃ smṛtam \|\| 97 \|\|*

96-97. The idol of sitarama is of the size of a hen's egg. It is smooth, of blue complexion, possessed of three faces, six wheels, adorned with the pollen of lotus, streaks of gold, flag, diamond, etc.

## \|\| 26.98 \|\|

*vadanevandane devi sītārāmasya kośakam \|*  
*durlabhaṃ tu kalau nṝṇāṃ svasāmrājyapradaṃ śubham \|\| 98 \|\|*

98\. The auspicious idol of SItarama, capable of bestowing freedom upon the devotee is indeed very rare in this Kali age.

## \|\| 26.99 \|\|

*indratīrthe mahādevi sītārāma bhidhāśilā \|*  
*yā taddānaṃ durlabhaṃ tannālpasya tapasaḥ phalam \|\| 99 \|\|*

99\. О maiden, at this Indra-TIrha, there is a holy stone called SItarama. Donating gifts thereat is highly meritorious. But that is possible only to a person who has performed a great penance.

## \|\| 26.100 \|\|

*dānasya śaktyabhāve tu śrotavyaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ hareḥ \|*  
*śālagrāma śilādānādyatphalaṃ tatphalaṃ labhet \|\| 100 \|\|*

100\. If a devotee is unable to donate anything in charity, he should at least hear the glorious narrative of the lord. Thereby, he can derive as much fruit as is available by the gift of salagrama.

## \|\| 26.101 \|\|

*āgneyakoṇe śrīnivāsasya devi tīrthaṃ tvāste vahnisaṃjñaṃ suśastam \|*  
*sa vahnidevaḥ śrīnivāsasya pūjāṃ kartuṃ hyāste sarvadā tīrthamadhye \|\| 101 \|\|*

101\. О maid, to the south-east of Srinivasa there is a holy Tlrtha called Vahni. The god Vahni is always stationed there to offer worship to Srinivasa.

## \|\| 26.102 \|\|

*yo vā tīrthe vahnisaṃjñe ca devi bhaktyā snānaṃ kurute 'jaṃ smaranhi \|*  
*jñānadvārā mokṣamāpnoti devi tatra snānaṃ durllabhaṃ vai nṛṇāṃ ca \|\| 102 \|\|*

102\. He who takes bath at Vahnitirtha and remembers lord Visnu with devotion attains supreme knowledge that confers final release. A bath at this Tlrtha is highly meritorious.

## \|\| 26.103 \|\|

*jñātvā snānaṃ duṣkaraṃ tīrtharāje bhaktistasmindurllabhā caiva devi \|*  
*śālagrāme tacchilāyāśca dānaṃ sudurlabhaṃ vāsudevābhidhāyāḥ \|\| 103 \|\|*

103\. As the bath at this place is highly meritorious, so is devotion highly meritorious. Similarly meritorious is the gift of Shalagrama or Vasudeva stone.

## \|\| 26.104 \|\|

*hrasvaṃ tathā vartulaṃ nīlavarṇaṃ sūkṣmaṃ mukhaṃ mukhacakraṃ suśuddham \|*  
*suveṇuyuktaṃ vāsudevābhidheyaṃ dānaṃ kalau durlabhaṃ tasya bhadre \|\| 104 \|\|*

104\. The idol of Vasudeva is short, round, blue, auspicious, of pleasant aspect and possessed of Venu. The gift thereof is highly meritorious in the Kali age, О maid.

## \|\| 26.105 \|\|

*dāne tasyāḥ śaktya bhāve ca devi snātvā tīrthe vāsudevābhidhasya \|*  
*samyak śrāvyaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ vai śilāyāstayostulyaṃ phalamāhurmahāntaḥ \|\| 105 \|\|*

105\. If a devotee is unable to gift the idol, he should at least take bath at this Tlrtha and listen to the glory of Vasudeva Shila. The wise declare the merit of the one at par with the merit of the other.

## \|\| 26.106 \|\|

*dakṣiṇe śrīnivāsasya yamatīrthaṃ ca saṃsmṛtam \|*  
*tatrāste yamarājastu pūjāṃ kartuṃ hareḥ sadā \|\| 106 \|\|*

106\. То the south of Srinivasa, there is Yams Tlrtha. Lord Yama is stationed there to offer worship to the supreme lord Visnu.

## \|\| 26.107 \|\|

*tatra snānaṃ ca dānaṃ cāpyakṣayaṃ paramaṃ smṛtam \|*  
*śālagrāmaśilādānaṃ kāryaṃ tatra mahāmune \|\| 107 \|\|*

107\. Taking bath and giving gift thereat is attended by indestructible award. The gift of salagrama Shila is what they recommend highly at this Tlrtha.

## \|\| 26.108 \|\|

*paṭṭābhirāmasaṃjñāyāḥ śilāyā dānamiṣyate \|*  
*taccūtaphalavatsthūlaṃ vadanatrayasaṃyutam \|\| 108 \|\|*

108\. The gift of Pattabhirama Sita is also meritorius. As big as the mango fruit, ft has three faces.

## \|\| 26.109 \|\|

*śiraścakreṇa rahitaṃ saptacakraiḥ samanvitam \|*  
*nīlavarṇaṃ svarṇarekhaṃ gośurādyaiḥ samanvitam \|\| 109 \|\|*

109\. It bears no wheel on the head, though otherwise it bears seven wheels. It is blue in hue, bears golden streaks, gopura, etc.

## \|\| 26.110 \|\|

*paṭṭavardhanarāmaṃ tu durlabhaṃ bahubhāgyadam \|*  
*paṭṭavardhanarāmaṃ tu yo dadāti ca tatra vai \|*  
*paṭṭābhiṣikto bhavati nātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|\| 110 \|\|*

110\. The idol of Pattavardhana Rama is highly meritorious and blissful. He who donates Pattavardhana attains sovereignty. No doubt should be entertained on this point.

## \|\| 26.111 \|\|

*śrīnivāsasya nairṛtye nairṛtaṃ tīrthamuttamam \|*  
*āste hi nirṛtistatra pūjāṃ kurtuṃ ca sarvadā \|\| 111 \|\|*

111\. In the Nairrta Kona, there is a Nairrta tlrtha. God Nirrta is stationed to worship lord Srinivasa.

## \|\| 26.112 \|\|

*tatra snānaṃ prakartavyaṃ punarjanma na vidyate \|*  
*śālagrāmaśilāyāścaḥ puruṣottamasaṃjñikām \|\| 112 \|\|*

112\. A devotee should take bath thereat — an act which absolves him from rebirth.

## \|\| 26.113 \|\|

*mūrtiṃ dadāti yo martyaḥ sa yāti paramāṃ gatim \|*  
*auduṃbaraphalākāraṃ prasannavadanaṃ śubham \|\| 113 \|\|*

113\. He who donates the idol of Purusottama in the form of Shalagrama stone attains the supreme position.

## \|\| 26.114 \|\|

*cakradvyasamāyuktaṃ śiraścakrasamanvitam \|*  
*suvarṇabindusaṃyuktaṃ vajrāṅkuśasamānvatam \|\| 114 \|\|*

114\. The idol of Purusottama is of the size of Audumbara fruit auspicious and pleasant in appearance. It carries a pair of wheel beside the head-wheel. It has a golden dot, a diamond and a goad.

## \|\| 26.115 \|\|

*tanmūrtidānaṃ durlabhaṃ tatra devaḥ prīṇāti yasmācchrīnivāso mahātmā \|*  
*yadā dānaṃ durghaṭaṃ syācca devi tadā śrotavyaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ tasya mūrteḥ \|\| 115 \|\|*

115\. The gift of the idol is highly meritorious and pleasing to Srinivasa. If it is not possible to make gift of that idol, О maid, he should listen to its traits. (One is on par in merit with the

## \|\| 26.116 \|\|

*pāśinairṛtayormadhye śeṣatīrthaṃ paraṃ smṛtam \|*  
*tatra snātvā śeṣamūrtiṃ pradadāti dvijātaye \|\| 116 \|\|*

116\. In the midst of southern and south-west comer is situated Sesa-tlrtha. If a devotee takes bath here and donates the idol of Shesa to a Brahmin he goes to the highest regions whence them is no return to this earth.

## \|\| 26.117-118 \|\|

*sa yāti paramaṃ lokaṃ punarāvṛttivarjitam \|*  
*auduṃbaraphalākāraṃ kuṇḍalākṛtimeva ca \|\| 117 \|\|*

*śeṣavadvadanaṃ tasya tasmiṃścakradvayaṃ smṛtam \|*  
*phalaṃ tamekacakreṇa saṃyutaṃ valmikānvitam \|\| 118 \|\|*

117-118. The idol of Shesa is of the Udumbara fruit, circular as the ear-ornament, with the face resembling that of the serpent chief Sesa, carrying a pair of wheels with a fruit in one of the wheels. The idol has a faint colour though the form is quite distinct.

## \|\| 26.119 \|\|

*kiñcidvarṇasamāyuktaṃ śeṣamūrti matisphuṭam \|*  
*suptā prabuddhā dvividhā śeṣamūrtirudāhṛtā \|\| 119 \|\|*

119\. The idol of Sesa is twofold: awakened and asleep.

## \|\| 26.120 \|\|

*phaṇonnatā prabuddhā syātsaptalakṣaphaṇānvitā \|*  
*tatrāpi durlabhā suptā mahābhāgyakarīsmṛtā \|\| 120 \|\|*

120\. The awakened form is the one with rising hoods which number seven lacs. The sleeping form is rare to behold. It is one that bestows fortune in this world and liberation hereafter.

## \|\| 26.121-122 \|\|

*iha loke paratrāpi mokṣadā nātra saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*navacakrādupakramya viṃśatyantaṃ ca yatra saḥ \|\| 121 \|\|*

*ananta iti vijñeyo hyanantaphaladāyakaḥ \|*  
*viśvaṃbharaḥ sa vijñeyo viṃśatyūrdhvaṃ varānane \|\| 122 \|\|*

121-122. If the idol carries nine to twenty wheels it is called Ananta. It confers endless fruits to the worshipper. If it carries more than twenty wheels it is called Visvaambhara.

## \|\| 26.123 \|\|

*tatrāpi kesaraiścaikrarlakṣaṇaiśca samanvitam \|*  
*kalau tu durlabhaṃ naṇāṃ taddānaṃ cātidurlabham \|\| 123 \|\|*

123\. The gift of an idol of gesa with saffron, wheels, and other traits is highly meritorious, though the idol with such traits is very rare in the Kali age.

## \|\| 26.124 \|\|

*snānaṃ kṛtvā śeṣatīrthe viśuddhenaiva cetasā \|*  
*eteṣāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ śrutvā prayāti paramāṃ gatim \|\| 124 \|\|*

124\. If he is unable to gift the idol, he should take bath in the Shesa-tirtha and listen to the traits of the lord with a clear mind. He too attains the highest position.

## \|\| 26.125 \|\|

*tataḥ paraṃ mahābhāge vāruṇaṃ tīrthamuttamam \|*  
*tatrāste varuṇo devaḥ pūjāṃ kartuṃ hareḥ sadā \|\| 125 \|\|*

125\. О lucky one, beyond the Shesa-tirtha, there lies a Varuna Tlrtha. The god Varuna is stationed there to worship the lord.

## \|\| 26.126 \|\|

*tatra snānaṃ prakartavyaṃ dātavyaṃ dānamuttamam \|*  
*śiśumāraṃ ca matsyaṃ ca trivikramamathāpi vā \|*  
*dātavyaṃ bhūtikāmena tīrthesminviravarṇini \|\| 126 \|\|*

126\. He should take bath at this TIrtha and donate the gift of Shumara, Matsya or Trivlkrama, if he desires prosperity.

## \|\| 26.127-128 \|\|

*jaṃbūphalasamākārā pucche sūkṣmā sabindukā \|*  
*cakratrayā ca vadane pucchopari sacakrakā \|\| 127 \|\|*

*śrīvatsabindumālāḍhyā matsyamūrtirudāhṛtā \|*  
*pucchādadhaścakrayutaṃ śiśumāramudāhṛtam \|\| 128 \|\|*

127-128. The idol of Matsya is of the form of jambu fruit. It is slender in tail, adorned with dot, with three wheels on the face and a single wheel on the tail. It bears the mark of Srivatsa and a garland of flowers. The idol of Simsumara has the symbol of wheel below the tail.

## \|\| 26.129 \|\|

*vakracakrayutaścetsyāttrivikrama udāhṛtaḥ \|*  
*eteṣāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ śrutvā vāruṇe tīrtha uttame \|\| 129 \|\|*

129\. The idol of Trivikrama bears a wheel on the face. (If he is unable to gift these idols) he should listen to their traits and the fruit of gifting them at the excellent Varuna TIrtha. He goes to Visnu's region where he rejoices in the company of the gods.

## \|\| 26.130 \|\|

*etaddānaphalaṃ prāpya modate viṣṇumandire \|*  
*pūrvauktā mūrtayo yasmin gṛhe tiṣṭhanti bhāmini \|*  
*bhāgīrathī tīrthavarā saṃnidhatte na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 130 \|\|*

130\. At the place where the above idols are stationed, Ganga, the beat of the sacred rivers, is indeed present there.

## \|\| 26.131 \|\|

*svāmi puṣkariṇīsnānaṃ durghaṭaṃ tu kalau nṛṇām \|*  
*tatra sthitānāṃ tīrthānāṃ snānaṃ cāpyatidurghaṭam \|\| 131 \|\|*

131\. A bath at the Svami Puskarani is highly meritorious and a bath at the sacred pools in the area of Svami Puskarani is still more highly meritorious.

## \|\| 26.132 \|\|

*śālagrāmaśilādānaṃ durghaṭaṃ ca tathā smṛtām \|*  
*svāmipuṣkariṇītīre kanyādānaṃ sudurghaṭam \|\| 132 \|\|*

132\. The donation of ^alagrama stone on the bank of the lake Svami Puskarini is highly meritorious. The gift of one's daughter (in marriage to a bride-groom) is still more meritorious.

## \|\| 26.133 \|\|

*durghaṭaṃ kapilādānaṃ bhakṣyadānaṃ sudurghaṭam \|*  
*svāmipuṣkariṇītīrthe tīrtheṣvanyeṣu bhāmini \|\| 133 \|\|*

133\. Meritorious is the gift of a Kapila cow; meritorious is the gift of eatables.

## \|\| 26.134 \|\|

*snānaṃ kuru yathānyā yaṃ śayyādānaṃ tathā kuru \|*  
*jaigīṣavyena muninā tvevamuktā ca kanyakā \|\| 134 \|\|*

134\. О maid, you should take bath in the prescribed way in the Svami Puskarini lake and other sacred pools and donate a bed to a worthy Brahmin.

## \|\| 26.135 \|\|

*svāmipuṣkariṇīsnānaṃ sā cakāra dhṛtavratā \|*  
*tīrtheṣveteṣu susnātā dānaṃ cakre subhāminī \|\| 135 \|\|*

135\. Thus addressed by the sage Jaiglsavya, the maid took bath in the Svami Puskarin lake and observed fast She took bath at other holy places too. She offered gifts to the Brahmanas and halted there for twentyone days.

## \|\| 26.136 \|\|

*uvāsa tatra sā davī triḥ saptakandināni ca \|*  
*svāmipuṣkaraṇītīramahimānaṃ śṛṇoti yaḥ \|*  
*sa yāti paramāṃ bhaktiṃ śrīnivāse jaganmaye \|\| 136 \|\|*

136\. He who hears the glory of Svami PuskarnI becomes highly devoted to lord Srinivasa who is identical with the universe.

## Chapter 27

## \|\| 27.1 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*sā gatā snātukāmātha nandāṃ pāpanivāriṇīm \|*  
*papraccha taṃ guruṃ vipraṃ vinayāvanatā sudhīḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Shri Krsna said Desirous of taking bath the maid went to the Nanda river that wards off sins. She asked the Brahmana preceptor there.

## \|\| 27.2-3 \|\|

*kinnāmeyaṃ nadī vipra kiṃ kāryaṃ cātra me vada \|*  
*jaigīṣavyastvevamukto vākyametaduvāca ha \|\| 2 \|\|*

*jaigīṣavya uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu bhadre pravakṣyāmi māhātmyaṃ pāpanāśanam \|*  
*iyaṃ nadī mahābhāge sadā pāpavināśinī \|\| 3 \|\|*

2-3. Revered preceptor, what is the name of this river. What rite shall I perform here. Thus addressed, Jaiglsavya, the Brahmana preceptor,, spoke to her in reply.

О good one, listen I shall tell you the glory of this river — the glory which destroys sins altogether.

## \|\| 27.4 \|\|

*brahmahatyādipāpaugho yatra snānena naśyati \|*  
*pratyakṣaṃ dṛśyate hyatra snānaṃ kartuṃ samudyataiḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. О fortunate one, this river is the destroyer of sins. Multitudes of sins, including the Brahmin-slaughter are destroyed by taking bath щ. this river. Those who take bath here can perceive its glory directly.

## \|\| 27.5 \|\|

*jalaṃ cāśubhrarūpeṇa pāpaiśca paridṛśyate \|*  
*yāvacchubhrodakaṃ devi tāvatsanānaṃ ca kārayet \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The sinners, when they plunge into the river perceive the waters as dark They should continue taking bath so long as the waters do not look bright to them.

## \|\| 27.6 \|\|

*yāvacchubhrodakaṃ naiva tāvatpāpaṃ na naśyati \|*  
*śuddhodake samāyāte pāpaṃ naṣṭamiti dhruvam \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Until the waters become bright, the sins arenotwiped off. When the waters become bright, one should know for certain that their sins are wiped off.

## \|\| 27.7 \|\|

*kalāvitthaṃ viśālākṣi mahimā dṛśyate bhuvi \|*  
*atra snānaṃ prakartavyaṃ dātavyaṃ dāna muttamam \|*  
*tataśca jñānamāsādya viviṣṇulokaṃ sa gacchati \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. О wide-eyed one, such-like glory of this river is perceptible on this earth even in this Kali age.

One should take bath in this river and bestow valuable gifts on the deserving. One attains knowledge thereby and goes to the world of Visnu.

## \|\| 27.8-9 \|\|

*gurustrīgamanāccandra ahalyāyāṃ gato hariḥ \|*  
*surāpānācca śukrastu suvarṇaharaṇādbaliḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

*brahmahatyāyāśca rudro nāgo dattāpahārakaḥ \|*  
*sūtasya hananādrāmo nirmukto hyatra bhāmini \|\| 9 \|\|*

8-9. By taking bath in this river, themoon was released from the sin of copulating with his preceptor's wife, Indra was released from the sin of incest with Ahalya, Sukra from the sin of drinking wine, Bali from the sin of stealing gold, Rudra from the sin of Brahmana-slaughter, Naga from the sin of taking back what was given in charity, Balarama from the sin of slaying Suta.

## \|\| 27.10 \|\|

*nānena sadṛśaṃ tīrthaṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati \|*  
*snānaṃ kuru mahābhāge tena siddhiṃ hyavāpsyasi \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. There has notbeen nor shall everbe a place of pilgrimage equal to it in merit. О blessed one, by taking bath herein make your life fruitful.

## \|\| 27.11-18 \|\|

*jaigīṣavyeṇa muninā pitrā saha ca kanyakā \|*  
*snānaṃ cakāra vidhivadudatiṣṭhacca bhāmini \|\| 11 \|\|*

*yāvacca pauruṣaṃ sūktaṃ tāvatkālaṃ hi tiṣṭhati \|*  
*paścājjaptvā mahāmantraṃ veṅkaṭeśābhidhaṃ param \|\| 12 \|\|*

*dvijātīnprīṇayitvā sā vastradravyādibhūṣaṇaiḥ \|*  
*tasmācca prayayau devī kamārītīrthamuttamam \|\| 13 \|\|*

*kumārīmahimānaṃ ca śrutvā snānaṃ cakāra sā \|*  
*punarāvṛtya sā devī hyantarā virajānadīm \|\| 14 \|\|*

*dṛṣṭvā papraccha sā devī jaigīṣavyaṃ guruṃ prabhum \|*  
*kiṃ saṃjñikeyaṃ viprendra kiṃ kāryaṃ hyatra me vada \|\| 15 \|\|*

*jaigīṣavyaḥ pṛṣṭa eva muvāca karuṇānidhiḥ \|*  
*iyaṃ bhāgīrathī kanye āyāti hyantareṇa tu \|\| 16 \|\|*

*ataḥ sā procyate hyantargaṅgeti paramarṣibhiḥ \|*  
*kanye tvasyāstu salilaṃ śrīninivāsapriyaṃ sadā \|\| 17 \|\|*

*atra snānaṃ yaḥ karoti sa yāti paramāṃ gatim \|*  
*snānaṃ cakāra sā kanyā jale paramapāvane \|\| 18 \|\|*

11-18. Together with the sage Jaiglsavya and her father, the maid took bath in the river in the prescribed manner and emerged out of waters. She repeated the sacred mantra of Vyankatesa and the Purusa hymn as long as she remained in the waters. She propitiated the Brahmins, with gifts of cloth, money and ornaments. Then she proceeded to Kumar! Tirtha and took bath. She dressed up herself and stepped on forward, journey. She came across the Viraja river and asked the preceptor Jaigisavya. О best of Brahmins, what is the name of this, sacred river. What rite shall I perform here.

## \|\| 27.19-20 \|\|

*dānādikaṃ tathā jñātvā jajāpa paramaṃ manum \|*  
*śrīnivāsasamīpaṃ tu punarāgatya bhāminī \|\| 19 \|\|*

*aṅgapradakṣiṇaṃ cakre bhaktyā veṅkaṭanāyakam \|*  
*brāhmaṇādīnprīṇayitvā vastragandhādibhūṣaṇaiḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

Thus asked, Jaigisavya, who felt compassion for her said: "O maid, this river, called Ganga, flows under the earth. Hence, the sages declare her as antaraganga. О maid, the water of this, river is pleasing to Srinivasa. One who takes bath here attains the highest place". On hearing the glory of the sacred river the-girl took bath in the most purifying waters.

19-20. She offered gifts to the Brahmanas and repeated, the sacred mantra. She returned to Srinivasa the lord of Venkata and circumambulated him with devotion. She propitiated the brahmanas with fragrance, ornaments and cloth

## \|\| 27.21 \|\|

*punaḥ paradine prātaḥ svāmipuṣkariṇījale \|*  
*snānaṃ kṛtvā mahābhāgā yayau tuṃburusaṃjñikām \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Next morning, she went to Svamlpuskarinl and took bath there. She then went to the river Tumburu and asked the preceptor again./'Sir, please tell me what is the name of this river."

## \|\| 27.22-23 \|\|

*papraccha taṃ guruṃ devī nāthaṃ kinnāmikā tvayam \|*  
*jaigīṣavya uvāca \|*  
*iyaṃ tuṃbarukābhijñā nārī vai varavarṇinī \|\| 22 \|\|*

*purā tuṃ buruṇā sākaṃ nāradastapasi sthitaḥ \|*  
*atra prādurabhūdviṣṇurnāradasya hitāya ca \|\| 23 \|\|*

22-23. Jaglsavya said: — This chanting river is called Tumburu. Once upon a time, the sages Narada and Tumburu practised penance here, when lord Visnu appeared and showered blessings on them.

## \|\| 27.24 \|\|

*snānaṃ yaḥ kurute hyatra sa yāti paramāṃ gatim \|*  
*atra snānaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ durlabhaṃ kalau \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Не who takes bath in this river attains the highest place. But it is not open to all to have a bath here.

## \|\| 27.25-27 \|\|

*atra snānaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ nālpasya tapasaḥ phalam \|*  
*tatra snātvā ca pītvā ca dattvā dānānyakeśaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

*punarāgatya sā devī śrīnivāsaṃ nanāma ha \|*  
*tasmimandine brāhmaṇāṃśca tarpayāmāsa bhamini \|\| 26 \|\|*

*svāmipuṣkariṇīṃ prāpya dīpānprājvālayatsatī \|*  
*sopāneṣu mahābhāgā dīpāvalibhirañjasā \|*  
*prīṇayāmāsa deveśaṃ śrīnivāsaṃ jagadgurum \|\| 27 \|\|*

25-27. A few privileged persons who have performed hard penance can have a plunge into this holy river. She took bath, drank waters, offered gifts and returned to Srinivasa. She worshipped the god, propitiated the Brahmanas and lighted the earthen lamps on the banks of the SvamI lake. She placed the earthen lamps in a row on the steps of the lake. Thus, she propitiated the great lord Srinivasa, the preceptor of the universe.

## \|\| 27.28 \|\|

*punaḥ paradine prāpte śakratīrthamanuttamam \|*  
*kapilākhyordhvadeśe tu tattīrthaṃ pāvanaṃ smṛtam \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Next day she reached the holy place of pilgrimage sacred to Indra on the peak of the mountain Kapila.

## \|\| 27.29 \|\|

*tatra snātvā mahābhāgā tadūrdhvaṃ snāpayetsvayam \|*  
*viṣvasenasarastatra sarvapāpavināśanam \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. She took bath in the pool and bathed the holy idol of lord Srinivasa with the sacred waters of the lake Visvaksena which is purifying and which destroys all sins.

## \|\| 27.30 \|\|

*tata ūrdhvaṃ mahābhāgā yayau tatra dadarśa sā \|*  
*pañcāyudhānāṃ tīrthāni teṣu snānaṃ cakāra sā \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Shemounted the summitand saw theholy places of pancayudhas and bathed there.

## \|\| 27.31-33 \|\|

*tadūrdhvaṃ cāgnikuṇḍaṃ syāddurārohaṃ tatogrataḥ \|*  
*tasyopari brahmatīrthaṃ brahmahatyāvimocanam \|\| 31 \|\|*

*saptarṣīṇāṃ tadūrdhvaṃ tu puṇyatīrthaṃ ca satphalam \|*  
*daśādhikaphalaṃ teṣā tīrthānāmuttarottaram \|\| 32 \|\|*

*eteṣāṃ caiva māhātmyaṃ ko vā vaktumihārhati \|*  
*ṛṣitīrtheṣu sā kanyā cacāra tapa uttamam \|\| 33 \|\|*

31-33. There, at the place, difficult to ascend she saw a fire-pit. High upon a place, she saw Brahma-tirtha which releases one from the sin of Brahma-hatya (slaughter of a Brahmana). Higher still, she reached the holy place of seven sages. These sacred places impart ten per cent additional gain in merit in their serial order. Who can describe the glory of these sacred places?

## \|\| 27.34-36 \|\|

*mamāvatāraparyantaṃ caritvā tapa uttamam \|*  
*yogadhāraṇa yā dehaṃ tyaktvā jāṃbavato gṛhe \|\| 34 \|\|*

*jātā jāṃbavatī nāma vavṛdhe tasya veśmani \|*  
*tasyāḥ pitā jāṃbavānsa samādātkanyakāṃ tadā \|*  
*rukmyā anaṃ tarā saiṣā mama bhāryā khageśvara \|\| 35 \|\|*

*idaṃ hi paramākhyānaṃ veṅkaṭādrermahāgireḥ \|*  
*ko vā varṇayituṃ śakto madanyaḥ puruṣo bhuvi \|\| 36 \|\|*

34-36. The maid practised penance at these holy places. She continued the practice till I descended on earth. She gave up her body by way of yoga and was born in the house of Jambavat. She was called Jambavat!. Her.father Jambavat gave her in marriage to me. I married her and gave her a rank next to RukminL Who else than myself can describe the glory of Mount Venkata.

## \|\| 27.37 \|\|

*veṅkaṭeśasya naivedyaṃ sadā lakṣmīḥ karoti vai \|*  
*brahmā pūjayate nityamevaṃ śāstrasya nirṇayaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. LaksmI offers Naivedya to the lord of Venkata. Brahma perfomffs worship regularly everyday. This is what. Sastras have declared.

## \|\| 27.38 \|\|

*naivedyabhakṣiṇāṃ puṃsāmupahāsaṃ na kārayet \|*  
*svasya prāśastyabhāve tu naivedyādi guḍādikam \|*  
*grāhyameva na saṃdeho anyathā nārakī bhavet \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. One should not mock at the people who are given to the eating of Naivedya. For ones own welfare, one shall partake of Naivedya. One shall not hesitate in accepting Naivedya. Otherwise one falls in hell.

## \|\| 27.39 \|\|

*śrīnivāsātparo devo na bhūto na bhaviṣyati \|*  
*svayaṃ ca pācayitvātvaṃ ghṛtapakvādikaṃ tathā \|*  
*śrīnivāsasya naivedyaṃ dattvā bhojanamācaret \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. There has been no god higher than Srinivasa nor there shag ever be. One should cook one's food in ghee, offer a portion to Srinivasa and partake of the rest himself.

## \|\| 27.40 \|\|

*idaṃ tu paramaṃ gopyaṃ tavoktaṃ ca khageśvara \|*  
*na kasyāpi ca vaktavyaṃ gopyatvātkhagasattama \|*  
*itaḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi tāratamyaṃ śṛṇu prabho \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. This secret of secrets I have declared to you, О lord of birds. This should not be divulged to any, since it is very confidential. О lord, now I shall tell you the rest of the story in continuation.

## Chapter 28

## \|\| 28.1 \|\|

*yā pūrvasarge dakṣaputrī satī tu rudrasya patnī dakṣayajñe svadeham \|*  
*visṛjya sā menakāyāṃ ca jajñe dharādharāddhemavato vai sakāśāt \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Parvati, in her previous birth, was the daughter of Daksa. (known as San) she was married to Rudra (lord Siva). But she gave up her fife by throwing herself in the sacrifice of Daksa. She was born of Menaka and Himavat, the lord of the mountains.

## \|\| 28.2 \|\|

*sā pārvatā rudrapatnī khagendra yā śeṣapatnī vāruṇī nāma pūrvā \|*  
*saivāgatā balabhadreṇa rantuṃ dvirūpamāsthāya mahāpativratā \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Parvati became the consort of Rudra. That Varum, the wife of Sesa, became the wife of Balabhadra in her second birth as RevatL

## \|\| 28.3 \|\|

*śrīrityākhyā indirāveśayuktā tasyā dvitīyā pratimā megharūpā \|*  
*śeṣaṇa rūpeṇa yadā hi vīndra tapaścacāra viṣṇunā sārdhameva \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Her second form was pervaded by Shri (LaksmI) as a cloud is pervaded by water.

## \|\| 28.4 \|\|

*tadaiva devī vāruṇī śeṣapatnī tapaśca kre indirāprītaye ca \|*  
*tadā prītā indirā suprasannā uvāca tāṃ vāruṇīṃ śeṣapatnīm \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Then Varuni, the wife of Sesa, practised penancein order toplease LaksmI. LaksmI being pleased spoke to VarunI, the consort of Sesa.

## \|\| 28.5-6 \|\|

*yadā rāmo vaiṣṇavāṃśena yuktaḥ saṃpatsyate bhūtale rauhiṇeyaḥ \|*  
*mayyāveśātsaṃyutā tvaṃ tu bhadre śrīrityākhyā valabhadrasya rantum \|\| 5 \|\|*

*saṃpatsyase nātra vicāryamastītyuktvā sā vai prayayau viṣṇuloke \|*  
*śrīlakṣmyaṃśācchrīritīḍyāṃ samākhyāṃ labdhvā loke śeṣapatnī babhūva \|\| 6 \|\|*

5-6. "When Rama (Balarama) constituted of a part of Visnu will be born as the son of RohinI, pervaded by me you will be born as srl to be united with Balabhadra. Do not be worded over this. So saying LaksmI went to the region of Visnu. Being a part of LaksmI she obtained the name LaksmI.

## \|\| 28.7 \|\|

*yadāhīśo vipulāmuddharecca tadā rāmaḥ śrībhidāsaṃgame ca \|*  
*karoti toṣatsarvadā vai ramāyāstasyāpyāveśo vyaṃstritamonasaṃgam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. When Shesa, the lord of serpents, lifts up the earth on his hoods, the lord Visnu is pleased to have copulation with her.

## \|\| 28.8 \|\|

*yā revatī raivatasyaiva putrī sā vāruṇī balabhadrasya patnī \|*  
*sauparṇanāmnī balapatnī khagendra yāstāstisraḥ ṣaḍviṣṇośca strībhyaḥ \|*  
*dviguṇādhamā rudraśeṣādikebhyo daśādhamā tvaṃ vijānīhi pautra \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Revati, the daughter of Raivata became the wife of Balabhadra along with Varuni and Saupama. The three out of the six are twice inferior to the wives of Visnu and ten times inferior to Rudra, Sesa and others.

## \|\| 28.9 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*rāmeṇa rantuṃ sarvadā vāruṇī tu putrītvamāpe revatasyaiva subhrūḥ \|*  
*evaṃ trirūpā vāruṇī śeṣapatnī dvirūpabhūtā pārvatī rudrapatnī \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Garuda said: — Varuni was born as the daughter of Revata who became the wife of Balabhadra, (the brother of lord Krsna VarunI, the wife of Sesa has three forms.

## \|\| 28.10 \|\|

*nīcāyā jāṃbavatyāśca śeṣasāmyaṃ ca kutracit \|*  
*śrūyate ca mayā kṛṣṇa nimittaṃ brūhi me prabho \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Parvati, the wife of Rudra has two forms: Nila and Jambavati are at par with Shesa. This is what I hear.

## \|\| 28.11 \|\|

*umāyāśca tathā rudraḥ sadā bahuguṇādhikaḥ \|*  
*evaṃ tvayoktaṃ bhagavanniścayārthaṃ mama prabho \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Please tell me the reason thereof, О lord. You have also said that Rudra is superior to Uma in several qualities. Please let me know your conclusive opinion on this point.

## \|\| 28.12-13 \|\|

*revatī śrīyutā śrīśca śeṣarūpā ca vāruṇī \|*  
*sauparṇi pārvatī caiva tisraḥ śeṣāśato varāḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

*ityapi śrūyate kṛṣṇa kutracinmadhusūdana \|*  
*nimittaṃ brūhi me kṛṣṇa tavaśiṣyāya suvrata \|\| 13 \|\|*

12-13. (I have also heard that) Revati is pervaded by Shri. She is VarunI. the wife of Sera. The I three — Revati, Supana and Parvatl are superior' to Sesa. О slayer of Madhu, this too I have heard in some quarters. О lord of good vows, tell me the reason thereof. I am your disciple.

## \|\| 28.14-15 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*vijñāya jāṃbavatyāśca tadanyeṣāṃ khagādhipa \|*  
*uttamānāṃ ca sāmyaṃ tu uttamāveśato bhavet \|\| 14 \|\|*

*avarāṇāṃ guṇasyāpi hyuttamānāmadhīnatā \|*  
*astīti dyotanāyaiva śatāṃśādhikamucyate \|\| 15 \|\|*

14-15. Sri Krsna said: — О lord of birds, to express the mutual superiority, inferiority or equality of Jambavati and others, I have used the term 'hundred per cent superior, hundred per cent inferior.'

## \|\| 28.16 \|\|

*yathā mayocyate vīndra tathā jānīhi nānyathā \|*  
*tadanantarajānvakṣye śṛṇu kāśyapajottama \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. О lord of birds, as I tell you, you should know it accordingly. О the noblest son of Kasyapa, now I shall tell you the lineage of Indra.

## \|\| 28.17-18 \|\|

*caturdaśasu cendreṣu saptamo yaḥ purandaraḥ \|*  
*vṛtrādīnāṃ śarīraṃ tu puramityucyate budhaiḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

*taṃ dārayati vajreṇa yasmāttasmātpurandaraḥ \|*  
*caturdaśasu cendreṣu mantradyumnastu ṣaṣṭhakaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

17-18. Among the fourteen Indras, the seventh was called Purandara. The body of Vrtra and those of his associates were caged Рига. Because he cleft their bodies with Vajra, Indra was called Purandara. Among the fourteen Indras Mantradyumna is the sixth.

## \|\| 28.19 \|\|

*mantrānaṣṭa mahāvīndra devo dyotayate yataḥ \|*  
*mantradyumnastato loke ubhāvapyeka eva tu \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Because he explained the eight Vedic mantras and shed light on their meaning he was caged Mantradyumna.

## \|\| 28.20-21 \|\|

*mantradyumnāvatārobhūtkuntīputrorjuno bhuvi \|*  
*viṣṇorvāyoranantasya cendrasya khagasattama \|\| 20 \|\|*

*pārthaścaturbhiḥ saṃyukta indra eva prakīrtitaḥ \|*  
*caturthepi ca vāyośca viśeṣosti sadārjuna \|\| 21 \|\|*

20-21. Arjuna, the son of Komi was the incarnation of Mantradyumna (Indra), Visnu and Ananta. Among the four, Vayu was pronmentm Arjuna.

## \|\| 28.22-23 \|\|

*vālirnāmā vānarastu purandara iti smṛtaḥ \|*  
*candravaṃśe samutpanno gādhirājo vicakṣaṇaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

*mantradyumnāvatāraḥ sa viśvāmitrapitā smṛtaḥ \|*  
*vedoktamantrā gāḥ proktā dhiyā saṃdhārayedyataḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

22-23. The monkey lord Ball is also called Purandara. King Gadhi of the lunar race was also the incarnation of Mantradyumna. I He was the father of ViSvamitra.

## \|\| 28.24 \|\|

*ato gādhiriti proktastadarthaṃ bhūtale hyabhūt \|*  
*ikṣvākuputro vīndra vikukṣiriti viśrutaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. The Vedic mantras are called gah. Since he understood them by his sharp intellect he was called Gadhi. lie incarnated on earth for preserving the Vedas.

## \|\| 28.25 \|\|

*sa evendrāvatārobhūddharisevārthameva ca \|*  
*viśeṣeṇa hariṃ kukṣau vijñānācca hariḥ sadā \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Vikuksi, the son of Iksvaku was the incarnation of Indra. He incarnated on earth for propitiating Hari. Hari was known as Vikuksi, for he realized Had in his heart.

## \|\| 28.26-27 \|\|

*ato vikukṣināmāsau bhūloke viśrutaḥ sadā \|*  
*rāmaputraḥ kuśaḥ prokta indra eva prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

*vālmīkiṛṣiṇā yasmātkuśenaiva vinirmitaḥ \|*  
*ataḥ kuśa iti prokto jānakīnandanaḥ prabhuḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

26-27. Kusa, the son of Rama Dasarathi was also Indra. Since he was created by sage Valmlki by means of Kusa grass he was named Kusa the son of SIta.

## \|\| 28.28 \|\|

*indradyumnaḥ puredrastu gādhī vālī tathārjunaḥ \|*  
*vikukṣiḥ kuśa evaite sapta cendrāḥ prakīrtitāḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Indradyumna, Purandara, Gadhi, Bali, Arjuna, Vikuksi and Kusa — these seven are Indras.

## \|\| 28.29 \|\|

*yaḥ kṛṣṇaputtraḥ pradyumnaḥ kāma eva prakīrtitaḥ \|*  
*prakṛṣṭaprakāśarūpatvātpradyumna iti nāmavān \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Pradyumna, the son of Krsna was the incarnation of Kama (the cupid). He was called Pradyumna because he possessed excellent brilliance.

## \|\| 28.30 \|\|

*yā rāmabhrātā bharataḥ kāma evābhavadbhuvi \|*  
*rāmājñāṃ bharate yasmāttasmādbharatanāmakaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Bharata, the younger brother of Rama was also the incarnation of cupid. He was called rata because he travelled on the footprints of king Bharata.

## \|\| 28.31-33 \|\|

*cakrābhimāni kāmastu sudarśana iti smṛtaḥ \|*  
*brahmaiva kṛṣṇaputrastu sāṃbo jāmbavatīsutaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

*kāmāvatāro vijñeyaḥ saṃdeho nātra vidyate \|*  
*yo rudraputraḥ skandastu kāma eva prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

*ripūnāskaṃ date nityamataḥ skanda iti smṛtaḥ \|*  
*yo vā sanatkumārastu brahmaputraḥ khagādhipa \|*  
*kāmāvatāro vijñeyo nātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|\| 33 \|\|*

31-33. Kama, the presiding deity of the disc, was homes Sudarsana. He was the son of Krsna and Jambavatl. Samba the son of lord Krsna was also the incarnation of Kama. Skanda, the son of Rudra was also Kama. Since he overcame the enemies he is called Skanda. Sanatkumara, the son of Brahma, О lord of birds is the incarnation of Kama.

## \|\| 28.34 \|\|

*sudarśanaśca paramaḥ pradyumnaḥ sāṃba eva ca \|*  
*sanatkumāraḥ sāṃbaścaṣaḍete kāmarūpakāḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Sudarsana, Pradyumna, liberate, Samba, Sanatkumara, and Skanda these six are the incarnations of Kama (cupid).

## \|\| 28.35 \|\|

*tataśca indrakāmāvapyumādibhyo daśāvarau \|*  
*tayormadhye tu garuḍa kāma indrādhamaḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Indra and Kama are inferior to Uma, etc., by ten per cent. Between the two Kama is inferior to Indra.

## \|\| 28.36 \|\|

*prāṇastvahaṅkāra eva ahaṅkārakasaṃjñakaḥ \|*  
*garutmadaṃśo vijñeyaḥ kāmendrābhyāṃ daśādhamaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Prana (vital airs) is called ahahkara; it is apart of Garutmat. It is inferior to Kama and Indra by ten per cent.

## \|\| 28.37 \|\|

*tadanantarajānvakṣye śṛṇu vīndra samāhitaḥ \|*  
*śravaṇānmokṣamāpnoti mahāpāpādvimucyate \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. I shall now tell you about thesuccessors of Kama, О lord of birds, hear attentively; the knowledge thereof leads to liberation from sins and effects the final release.

## \|\| 28.38 \|\|

*kāmaputroniruddho 'pi hareranyaḥ prakīrtitaḥ \|*  
*sa evābhūddhareḥ sevāṃ kartuṃ rāmānujo bhuvi \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Aniruddha, the son of Pradyumna the Kama incarnate, was born to attend upon lord Krsna. The younger brother of Rama was called Shatrughna. He was so-called because he smote his enemies.

## \|\| 28.39-40 \|\|

*śatrughna iti vikhyātaḥ śatrūnsūdayate yataḥ \|*  
*aniruddhaḥ kṛṣṇaputro pradyumnādyo 'janiṣṭa ha \|\| 39 \|\|*

*saṃkarṣaṇādirūpaistu tribhirāviṣṭa eva saḥ \|*  
*evaṃ dvirūpo vijñeyo hyaniruddho mahāmatiḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

39-40. Aniruddha was born of Pradyumna the son of srlkrsna. Aniruddha was pervaded by the two, out of the three forms of Sankarsana.

## \|\| 28.41 \|\|

*kāmabhāryā ratiryā tu dvirūpā saṃprakīrtitā \|*  
*rugmaputrī rugmavatī kāmabhāryā prakīrtitā \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Rati, the wife of Kama consists of two forms. Rukmavati, the daughter of Rukma was the wife of Kama.

## \|\| 28.42 \|\|

*atiprakāśayuktatvāttasmādrugmavatī smṛtā \|*  
*duryodhanasya yā putrī lakṣaṇā sā ratiḥ smṛtā \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. By virtue of excessive brilliance she was called Rukmavati. Laksana, the daughter of Duryodhana was Rati incarnate.

## \|\| 28.43 \|\|

*kāṣṭhā sāṃbasya bhāryā sā lakṣaṇaṃ saṃyunaktyataḥ \|*  
*lakṣaṇābhidhayābhūmau duṣṭa vīryodbhavā hyapi \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Kastha, the wife of Samba, was called Laksana, for though born of wicked parents she possessed good qualities. Rati, the wife of Kama had two forms: one as the wife of Kama and the other as the wife of Samba.

## \|\| 28.44 \|\|

*evaṃ dvirūpā vijñeyā kāmabhāryā ratiḥ smṛtā \|*  
*svāyaṃbhuvo brahmaputro manustvādyo gurau samaḥ \|*  
*rājadharmeṇa viṣṇośca jātaḥ prīṇayituṃ hareḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Svayambhuva Manu, at par with Brhaspati the preceptor of Devas, was the first son.of the creator. He was born to propitiate lord Visnu. He formulated laws of the state.

## \|\| 28.45 \|\|

*bṛhaspatirdevāgururmahātmā tasyāvatārāstraya āsan khagendra \|*  
*rāmāvatāre bharatākhyo babhūva hyaṃbhojajāveśayuto bṛhaspatiḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Brhaspati. the preceptor of gods had three forms. О lord of birds. When Rama incarnated on earth he was born as Bharata. As such he was pervaded by Brahma.

## \|\| 28.46 \|\|

*devāvatārānvānarāṃstārayitvā śrīrāmadivyā'caritānyavādīt \|*  
*ato hyasau nāranāmā babhūva hyaṅgatvamāptuṃ rāmadevasya bhūmyām \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. He took monkeys, the incarnations of Devas, across the ocean of life and death. He narrated the mighty exploits of lord Rama. He was known as Nara who incarnated to become absorbed in Rama.

## \|\| 28.47 \|\|

*kṛṣṇāvatāre droṇanāmā babhūva aṃbhojajāveśayuto bṛhasyapatiḥ \|*  
*yasmāddoṇātsaṃbhabhūva guruśca tasmādasau droṇasaṃjño babhūva \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. When lord Krsna incarnated on earth, Brhaspati, the preceptor of gods, incarnated as Drona. He was pervaded by the creator. As Brhaspati was born of Drona he was called Drona.

## \|\| 28.48 \|\|

*bhūbhārabhūtādyuddhṛtau hyaṅgabhūto viṣṇoḥ sevāṃ kartumevāsa bhūmau \|*  
*bṛhaspatiḥ pavanāveśapuktā sa uddhavaścetyamidhānamāpa \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Brhaspati incarnated as Uddhava. He was. pervaded by wind god in order to help the lord to relieve the earth of its burden and mankind of their suffering.

## \|\| 28.49 \|\|

*yasmādutkṛṣṭo hariratra samyagato hyasau budhavannāma cāpa \|*  
*sakhā hyabhūtkṛṣṇadevasya nityaṃ mahāmatiḥ sarvalokeṣu pujvaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. As lord Hari is excellent among all, he obtained the name Budha. He became the friend of lord Krsna. The wise one was worshipped by all people.

## \|\| 28.50 \|\|

*dakṣiṇāṅguṣṭhajo dakṣo brahmaputro mahāmatiḥ \|*  
*kanyāṃ sṛṣṭvā hareḥ prīṇannāsa bhūmā prajāpatiḥ \|*  
*putrānudapādayaddakṣastvato dakṣa iti smṛtaḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. From the right thumb of Brahma was born Daksa. To him was born Laksmi who became the wife of lord Visnu. Since he, the Patriarch, gave birth to sons he was called Daksa.

## \|\| 28.51 \|\|

*śacīṃ bharyāṃ devarājasya viddhi tasyā hyavatāraṃ śṛṇu samyak khagendra \|*  
*rāmāvatāre nāma tārā babhūva sā vālipatnī śacīsajakā ca \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Know that Saci is the wife of India. Listen to her incarnations. When Rama descended on earth she was born as Tara. She became the wife of Bali and was called Saci.

## \|\| 28.52-54 \|\|

*rāmānmṛte vālisaṃjñe patau hi sugrīvasaṃgaṃ sā cakārātha tārā \|*  
*ato nāgātsvargalokaṃ ca tārā kva vā yāyādantarikṣe na pāpā \|\| 52 \|\|*

*kṛṣṇāvatāre saiva tārā ca vīndra babhūva bhūmau vijayasya patnī \|*  
*piśaṅgadeti hyabhidhā syācca tasyāḥ sāmīpyamasyāstvajuṃnaveva cāsīt \|\| 53 \|\|*

*utpādayitvā babhruvāhaṃ ca putraṃ tasyāṃ tyaktvā hyarjuno vai mahātmā \|*  
*ataścobhe vāracitrāṅgade ca śacīrūpe nātra vivāryamasti \|\| 54 \|\|*

52-54. When Rama killed Bali, she became the wife of Sugriva. Thus she was a sinner. Hence, she could not go to heaven.

She continued moving in the ethr till when the lord incarnated as Krsna on earth. She was born as Tara and became the wife of Vijaya. She was known as Pisangada. She wedded Pandava Arjuna to whom she bore a son Babhruvahana when Arjuna left her company. Tara and Citrangada are the incarnations of Shacl.

## \|\| 28.55 \|\|

*pulomajā mantradyumnasya bhāryā yā kāśikā gādhirājasya bhāryā \|*  
*vikukṣibhāryā sumatiśceti saṃjñā kuśasya patnī kāntimatīti saṃjñā \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Pulomaja was the wife of Mantradyumna. Kasika was the wife of Gadhiraja. Sumati was the wife of Vikuksl. Kantimatl was the wife of Kusa. These seven are inferior to Saci.

## \|\| 28.56 \|\|

*etā hi sapta hyavarāśca śacyā jānīhi vai nāsti vicāraṇātra \|*  
*śacī ratiścāniruddho manurdakṣo bṛhaspatiḥ \|*  
*ṣaḍanyonyasamāḥ proktā ahaṅkārāddaśādhamāḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. Saci, Rati, Aniruddha, Manu, Daksa, Brhaspati — these six are equal to each other. These are inferior to ahamkara by ten per rent.

## \|\| 28.57 \|\|

*atha yaḥ pravaho vāyurmukhyavāyoḥ suto balī \|*  
*sa vāyuṣu mahānadya sa vai koṇādhipastathā \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Pravaha (wind) is the son of principal Vayu. He, the strong one, is the strongest among Vayus. He is the lord of all directions.

## \|\| 28.58-59 \|\|

*nāsikāsu sa evokto bhautikastulya eva ca \|*  
*ativāhaḥ sa evoktaḥ yato gamyo mumukṣubhiḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

*dakṣādibhyaḥ pañcaguṇādadhamaḥ saṃprakīrtitaḥ \|*  
*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*pravahaśceti saṃjñāṃ sa kimarthaṃ prāpa tadvada \|\| 59 \|\|*

58-59. This Vayu breathes through nostrils. It is material and uniform in nature. It is also called Ativaha as it is resorted to by those who desire liberation. It is inferior to Daksa and others by five per cent.

## \|\| 28.60 \|\|

*arthaḥ kaścāsti tannāmnaḥ pratītastaṃ vadasva me \|*  
*garuḍenaivamuktastu bhagavāndevakīsutaḥ \|*  
*uvāca paramaprītaḥ saṃstūya garuḍaṃ hariḥ \|\| 60 \|\|*

Garuda said: — О lord, please tell me how he obtained the epithet Pravaha. What is the significance of the name.

60\. Thus addressed by Garuda, lord Krsna, the son of DevakI — was highly pleased. He appreciated Garuda and spoke in reply.

## \|\| 28.61 \|\|

*kṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*praharṣeṇa harestulyānsarvadā vahate yataḥ \|*  
*ataḥ pravahanāmāsau kīrtitaḥ pakṣisattama \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. Krsna said: — О best of birds, as he ever takes the devotees of Hari across the ocean of existence, he is called Pravaha.

## \|\| 28.62-63 \|\|

*sarvottamo viṣṇurevāsti nāmnā brahmādayastadadhīnāḥ sadāpi \|*  
*mayoktametattu satyaṃ na mithyā gṛhṇāmi hastenoragaṃ kopayuktam \|\| 62 \|\|*

*sarvaṃ nu satyaṃ yadi mithyā bhavettu tadā tvasau māṃ daśatuhyahīndraḥ \|*  
*evaṃ bruvannuragaṃ kopayuktaṃ samagrahīnnādaśatsopyuraṅgaḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

62-63. Visnu is the best of all gods. Brahma and the rest are dependent on him. Whatever I state is the truth.

I take hold of an infuriated serpent. If alt what I have stated proves to be untrue then let the lord of snakes sting me.

So saying, he caught hold of an infuriated serpent but the serpent did not sting him.

## \|\| 28.64-65 \|\|

*etasya saṃdhāraṇādeva vīndra sa vāyuputraḥ pravahetyāpa saṃjñām \|*  
*yo vā loke viṣṇumūrtiṃ vihāya daityasvarūpā reṇukādyāḥ kudevāḥ \|\| 64 \|\|*

*teṣāṃ tathā matpitṝṇāṃ ca pūjā vyarthā satyaṃ satyametadbravīmi \|*  
*etatsarvaṃ yadi mithyā bhavettu tadā tvasau māṃ daśatu hyahīndraḥ \|\| 65 \|\|*

64-65. Because he held the serpent by hand, О lord of birds, the son of Vayu got the designation Pravaha.

Those who do not worship the idol of Visnu but worship the idols of inferior gods or goddesses Renuka, $tc., of the form of Daityas, purposeless is their worship of Visnu as well as of the manes. This is true very true, I say. If all what have said is proved to be false and untrue, then let the lord of serpents sting me mortally.

## \|\| 28.66 \|\|

*pitryaṃ nayāmi pravihāyaiva ye tu pitruddeśātkevalaṃ yaḥ karoti \|*  
*sa pāpātmā narakānvai prayātītyetadvākyaṃ satyametadbravīmi \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. He who performs sraddhas to the manes without offering gifts to Visnu, that sinner goes to bell. What I say is indeed true.

## \|\| 28.67 \|\|

*na śrīḥ svatantrā nāpi vidhiḥ svatantro na vāyudevo nāpi śivaḥ svatantraḥ \|*  
*tadanye no gauripuloma jādyāḥ kiṃ vaktavyaṃ nātra loke svatantraḥ \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. Neither LaksmI is sovereign nor Brahma nor Vayu nor Shiva nor Parvatlnor Sacl the daughter of Puloman and the wife of Indra.

## \|\| 28.68 \|\|

*bravīmi satyaṃ puruṣo viṣṇureva satyaṃ satya bhujamuddhṛtya satyam \|*  
*etatsarvaṃ yadi mithyā bhavettu tadā tvasau māṃ daśatu hyahīndra \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. The primeval being Visnu alone is real. I declare this truth by my uplifted arms. If all this proves to be false then let the lord of snakes sting me.

## \|\| 28.69-70 \|\|

*jīvaśca satyaḥ paramātmā ca satyastayorbhedaḥ satye e tatsadāpi \|*  
*jaḍaścasatyo jīvajaḍayośca bhedo bhedaḥ satyaḥ kiṃ ca jaḍaiśayorbhidā \|\| 69 \|\|*

*bhedaḥ satyaḥ sarvajīveṣu nityaṃ satyā jaḍānāṃ ca bhedā sadāpi \|*  
*etatsarvaṃ yadi mithyā bhavettu tadā tvasau daśatu māṃ hyahīndraḥ \|\| 70 \|\|*

69-70. The supreme soul is real, so is the personal soul. The difference between the two is real, so also between the animate and the inanimate, similarly between the inanimate and the lord. The difference between one soul and the other is also real. If all this is proved to be false then let the lord of snakes sting me mortally.

## \|\| 28.71 \|\|

*evaṃ bruvannuragaṃ kopayuktaṃ samagrahīnnādaśatsopyuraṅgaḥ \|*  
*etasya saṃdhāraṇādevavīdre sā vāyuputraḥ pravahetyāpa saṃjñām \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. So saying, he caught hold of the infuriated snake but the snake did not sting him at all. By catching hold of the infuriated snake, this son of Vayu obtained the designation Pravaha.

## \|\| 28.72 \|\|

*dvayaṃ svarūpaṃ praviditvaiva pūrvaṃ tvaṃ svīkuruṣva dvayameva nityam \|*  
*snānādikaṃ ca prakaroti nityaṃ pāpī sa ātmā naiva mokṣaṃ prayāti \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. Accepting dual nature of the lord, you should accept the two as real. He who disregards this maxim, remains a\* sinner through out life. Though he may be taking plunge in the holy rivers daily without fail, he does not obtain release.

## \|\| 28.73 \|\|

*tasmāddvayaṃ pravicāryaiva nityaṃ sukhī bhavennātra vicāryamasti \|*  
*etatsarvaṃ yadi mithyā bhavettu tadā tvasau māṃ daśatu hyahīndraḥ \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. By accepting duality as a matter of fact, one feels quite happy. If all this is proved to be false then let the lord of serpents sting me mortally.

## \|\| 28.74 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*kiṃ taddvayaṃ devadeveśa kiṃ vā tatkāraṇaṃ kīdṛśaṃ me vadasva \|*  
*dvayostyāgaṃ kīdṛśaṃ me vadasva tyāgātsukhaṃ kīdṛśaṃ me vadasva \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. Garuda said: — О supreme god, please tell me what duality is, of what nature? What is the source of this concept? How shall a person forsake duality. What bliss is derivable from renunciation.

## \|\| 28.75 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*dvayaṃ cāhustvindriye dve baliṣṭhe dehe hyasmiñ śrotranetre susṛṣṭe \|*  
*avāntare śrotranetre khagendra dvayaṃ cāhustatsvarūpaṃ ca vakṣye \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. Krsna said: — In this body two organs are the strongest of all. They are two cars and two eyes. The two are interrelated. О lord of birds, I shall tell you the real nature of the two.

## \|\| 28.76 \|\|

*śrotrasvabhāvo loka vārtāśrutau ca hyatīva modastvādarāsvādanena \|*  
*harervārtāśravaṇe duḥ khajālaṃ śrotrasvabhāvo jaḍatā damaśca \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. Ears are prone to hearing gossips and enjoying them with pleasure. They are naturally averse to hearing the ambrosial tale of lord Visnu. They have twofold nature dullness and control.

## \|\| 28.77 \|\|

*netrasvabhāvo darśane strīnarāṇāṃ hyatyādarānnāsti nidrādikaṃ ca \|*  
*harerbhaktānāṃ darśane duḥ kharūpo viṣṇoḥ pūjādarśane duḥ khajālam \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. Eyes are prone to gaze at men and women. Excessive doting takes away sleep. They are averse to seeing the devotees and their worship of the lord.

## \|\| 28.78 \|\|

*tayoḥ svarūpaṃ praviditvaiva pūrvaṃ punaḥ punaḥ svīkarotyeva mūḍhaḥ \|*  
*śiśraṃ maurkhyāccaiva kutrāpi yonau praveśayetsarvadā hyādareṇa \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. Even the stupid person is aware of their dual nature. He rather accepts indulgence as a matter of course. In his stupidity he takes delight in entering his penis in the vagina of my female.

## \|\| 28.79 \|\|

*bhayaṃ ca lajjā naiva cāste vadhūnāṃ tathā nṛṇāṃ vanitānāṃ yatīnām \|*  
*svasāraṃ te hyaviditvā dinepi suvāma yajñena svābhāvaśca vīndra \|\| 79 \|\|*

79\. Neither men and women nor ascetics have any dread or shame in this respect. Men can copulate even with their sisters, that too at day time, just as the priests do with the women at the soma sacrifice.

## \|\| 28.80-82 \|\|

*rasāsvabhāvo bhakṣaṇe sarvadāpi hyanarpitasyānnabhakṣyasya viṣṇoḥ \|*  
*tatho pahārasya ca tatsvabhāvaḥ abhakṣyāṇāṃ bhakṣaṇe tatsvabhāvaḥ \|\| 80 \|\|*

*alehyalehasya ca tatsvabhāvaḥ pātuṃ tvapeyasya ca tatsvabhāvaḥ \|*  
*dvayoḥ svarūpaṃ ca vihāya mūḍhaḥ punaḥ punaḥ svīkarotyeva nityam \|\| 81 \|\|*

*tasya snānaṃ vyarthamāhuśca yasmāttasmāttyājyaṃ na dvayoḥ kāryameva \|*  
*abhiprāyaṃ hyetamevaṃ khagendra jānīhi tvaṃ prahasyaiva nityam \|\| 82 \|\|*

80-82. This is the nature of the tongue that it eats what is not to be eaten, that it eats without offering it to the lord. This is the nature of the tongue to eat, lick or drink all that is not worthy to be eaten, licked or drunk, whether received as a gift or otherwise. The stupid person does not understand the true nature of the senseorgans, accepts the one and forsakes the other. If he acts otherwise his bath at the sacred place of pilgrimage (tlrthas) becomes fruitless. О lord of birds, be pleased to know what is the essence of my statement.

## \|\| 28.83-84 \|\|

*bhāryādvayaṃ hyaviditvā svarūpaṃ svīkṛtya caikāṃ pravihāyaiva caikām \|*  
*snānādikaṃ kurute mūḍhabūddhiḥ vyarthaṃ cāhurmokṣabhogau ca naiva \|*  
*etatsarvaṃ yadi mithyā bhavettu tadā tvasau māṃ daśatu hyahīndraḥ \|\| 83 \|\|*

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*bhāryādvayaṃ kiṃ vada tvaṃ mamāpi tayoḥ svarūpaṃ kiṃ vada tvaṃ murāre \|*  
*tayormadhye grāhyabhāryāṃ vada tvamagrāhyabhāryāṃ cāpi samyagvada tvam \|\| 84 \|\|*

83-84. A person who bathes at the holy places should note the differing nature of the two. If without understanding the true nature of the two mistresses, the ignorant fool accepts the one and abandons the other his ablution, etc. are rendered fruitless. He attains neither prosperity here nor liberation hereafter. If all what I have said is proved to be false then let that lord of snake sting me.

## \|\| 28.85 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*buddhiḥ patnī sā dvirūpā khagendra duṣṭā caikā tvaparā suṣṭhurūpā \|*  
*tayormadhye duṣṭarūpā kaniṣṭhā jyeṣṭhā tu yā suṣṭhubuddhisvarūpā \|\| 85 \|\|*

85\. Sri Krsna said: — Intellect is the wife of Purusa. She has twofold nature О lord of birds. One is wicked; the other is pious. Of the two the younger one is wicked, the elder oneispious.

## \|\| 28.86 \|\|

*kaniṣṭhayā naṣṭatāṃ yāti jīvaḥ sutiṣṭhantyā yāti yogyāṃ pratiṣṭhām \|*  
*kaniṣṭhāyāḥ śṛṇu vakṣye svarūpaṃ śrutvā tasyāstyāgabuddhiṃ kuruṣva \|\| 86 \|\|*

86\. Through the younger one, the soul succumbs to destruction Through the elder one he attains position. I shall now tell you about the nature of the younger. Listen and after listening try to abandon her.

## \|\| 28.87 \|\|

*jīvaṃ yaṃ vai prerayantī kaniṣṭhā kāmyaṃ dharmaṃ kurute sarvadāpi \|*  
*kva brāhmaṇāḥ kva ca viṣṇurmahātmā kva vai kathā kva ca yajñāḥ kvagāvaḥ \|\| 87 \|\|*

87\. The younger one encourages the soul (her husband) to indulge In activities for the fulfilment of worldly desires. She disregards the sanctity of the Brahmanas, of lord Visnu and his narratives, of sacrifices, cows, the holy fig tree, of bath and purity. She misdirects to worship their stupid husbands and goddess Renuka and waive fights to Mayadevi.

## \|\| 28.88-91 \|\|

*kva cāśvatthaḥ kva ca snānaṃ kva śaucametatsarvaṃ nāma nāśaṃ karoti \|*  
*mūḍhaṃ patiṃ reṇukāṃ pūjayasva māyādevyā dīpadānaṃ kuruṣva \|\| 88 \|\|*

*subhairavādīn bhaja mūḍha tvamandha hāridracūrṇandhārayeḥ sarvadāpi \|*  
*jyeṣṭhāṣṭamyāṃ jyeṣṭhadevīṃ bhajasva bhaktyā sūtraṃ galabandhaṃ kuruṣva \|\| 89 \|\|*

*marigandhāṣṭamyāṃ marigandhaṃ bhajasva tathā sūtraṃ svagale dhārayasva \|*  
*dīpastaṃbhaṃ sudine pūjayasva tatsūtrameva svagale dhārayasva \|\| 90 \|\|*

*mahālakṣmīṃ cādyalakṣmīṃ ca samyak pūjāṃ kuru tvaṃ hi bhaktyātha jīva \|*  
*lakṣmīsūtraṃ svāgale dhārayasva mahālakṣmīvān bhavasītyuttaratra \|\| 91 \|\|*

88-91. She encourages the stupid and ignorant husband to worship Bhairava and others and to put on yellow marks of turmeric powder on the forehead, to worship goddess Jyestha on the eighth day of Jyestha, to put on sacred thread roimd the neck with devotion, to worship god Marigandhi on the Marigandhi astaml, to raise a column of earthen lamps on the auspicious day before MahalaksmI (Supreme Nature) and Adya-laksmi (Primordial Nature) to put on LaksmI thread on the neck with a temptation that thereby he will attain riches.

## \|\| 28.92-93 \|\|

*vihāya mauñjīdivase bhāgyakāmaḥ suguggulāndhārayasvātibhaktyā \|*  
*suvāsinīḥ pūjayasvāśu bhaktyā gandhaiḥ puṣpairdhūpadīpaiḥ pratoṣya \|\| 92 \|\|*

*varārtikyaṃ kāṃsyapātre nidhāya kurvārtikyaṃ devatādevatānām \|*  
*picumandapatrāṇi vitatya bhūmau namasva tvaṃ kṣamyatāṃ ceti coktvā \|\| 93 \|\|*

92-93. On the day when they change old girdles for the new ones she encourages them to give up girdles and put on chains. She encourages them to propitiate the women of fashion, to worship gods and goddesses with scents, flowers and incense and to waive fights from the vessels of brass, spread leaves of Neem on the ground, pay homage and seek forgiveness for their past misdeeds.

## \|\| 28.94 \|\|

*mahādevīṃ pūjayasvādya bhaktyā sadvaiṣṇavānāṃ mā dadasvāpyathānnam \|*  
*sadvaiṣṇavānāṃ yadi vānnaṃ dadāsi bhāgyaṃ ca te paśyato nāśameti \|\| 94 \|\|*

94\. She encourages men to worship goddess Mahadevi and forbids them feed the pious devotees of lord Visnu. She warns her husband (the soul) against feeding the Vaisnavas and threatens himwith thedireconsequences of total destruction and addresses him thus.

## \|\| 28.95 \|\|

*svavāmahaste veṇupātre nidhāya dīpaṃ dhṛtvā savyahaste pate tvam \|*  
*uttiṣṭha bhoḥ pañcagṛheṣu bhikṣāṃ kuruṣva samyak pravihāyaiva lajjām \|\| 95 \|\|*

95\. О husband, now put a vessel of bamboo wood on the left hand and an earthen lamp on the right and move about for begging food at the five neighbouring houses, casting your sense of shame aside.

## \|\| 28.96-97 \|\|

*ādau gṛhe ṣaḍrasānnaṃ ca kutvā jagadgopyaṃ bhojanaṃ tvaṃ kuruṣva \|*  
*taccheṣānnaṃ bhojayitvā pate tvaṃ tāsāṃ ca re śaraṇaṃ tvaṃ kuruṣva \|\| 96 \|\|*

*tāsaṃ hastaṃ pustake stāpayitvā trāhityevaṃ tanmukhairvācayasva \|*  
*tvaṃ khaḍgadevaṃ pūjayasvādyabhartastatsevakānpūjayasvādya samyak \|\| 97 \|\|*

96-97. At first, prepare food of six varieties, cat the same without letting the people know. Distribute the residue among your womanfolk, asking them to putthetrhand on the sacred book and prodaim/O save us/ О husband, worship a good sword together with the devotees.

## \|\| 28.98 \|\|

*taiḥ sārdhaṃ tvaṃ śvānaśabdaṃ kuruṣva haridrācūrṇaṃsarvadā tvaṃ dadhasva \|*  
*kuruṣva tvaṃ bhīmasenasya pūjāṃ pañcāmṛtaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiścopacāraiḥ \|\| 98 \|\|*

98\. Make a barking sound together with them. Keep always in your possession the powder of orpiment. Worship Bhlmasena with five ambrosial articles.

## \|\| 28.99 \|\|

*tatkaupīnaṃ raupyajaṃ kārayitvā samarpayitvā dīpamālāṃ kuruṣva \|*  
*taddāsavaryān bhojayasvādya bhaktyā garjasva tvaṃ bhīmabhīmeti suṣṭhu \|\| 99 \|\|*

99\. Make a present of silver-girdle to lord Bhlmadeva and make a row of earthen lamps in front of the idol of the lord, feed the best of his devotees with devotion and make a thundering sound Bhlma, Bhima at the top of your voice.

## \|\| 28.100 \|\|

*taddāsavaryānmodayasva svavastrairmadyairmāṃsadravyajālena nityam \|*  
*mahādevaṃ pūjayasvādya samyag mahārudrairatirudraiśca samyak \|\| 100 \|\|*

100\. Propitiate the best of the devotees of Bhlma with garments, wine and meat. Worship Mahadeva in a proper way with the mantras of Maharudra and Atirudra.

## \|\| 28.101 \|\|

*haretyuktvā jaṅgamānpūjayasvaśaivāgame nipuṇāñchūdrajātān \|*  
*śākaṃbharīṃ vivisaḥ sarvaśākānsupācayitvā ca gṛhe gṛhe ca \|\| 101 \|\|*

101\. Worship even those devotees of lord Shiva who though expert in the knowledge of Shaiva scriptures belong to the sudra class.

## \|\| 28.102 \|\|

*dadasva bhaktyā paramādareṇa svalaṅkṛtya prāstuvaṃstadguṇāṃśca \|*  
*kulādevaṃ pūjayasvādya bhaktyā tvaṃ dṛgbhyāṃ vai taddine śaṃbhubuddhyā \|\| 102 \|\|*

102\. Prepare vegetable dishes of different variety and propitiate the goddess Shakambhari with devotion and interest. Decorate her idol and praise her traits. Fix up your mind on the creator Brahma and eyes full of devotion at his idol and offer worship.

## \|\| 28.103 \|\|

*tadbhaktavaryānpūjayasvādya samyak tatpādamūle vandanaṃ tvaṃ kuruṣva \|*  
*supañcamyāṃ mṛnmayīṃ śeṣamūrtiṃ pūjāṃ kuruṣva kṣīralājādikaiśca \|\| 103 \|\|*

103\. Worship the best of his devotees, pay homage at his feet, and bow your head to him. On the fifth day of each fortnight of the month, make an earthen idol of lord Sesa and worship him with milk, fried rice grains and such-like other articles used in worship.

## \|\| 28.104 \|\|

*sunāgapāśaṃ hi gale ca baddhvā taccheṣānnaṃ bhojayerbhoḥ punastvam \|*  
*dine caturthe bhoja yasvādya bhaktyā naivedyānnaṃ bhojayasvādya suṣṭhu \|\| 104 \|\|*

104\. Wear a naga-pasa around your neck; eat whatever is left of the food that has been offered to the deity. Make an offering of foodstuff on each fourth day of the two fortnights of every month.

## \|\| 28.105 \|\|

*ityādikaṃ prerayitvā patiṃ sā jīvena naṣṭaṃ prikarotyeva nityam \|*  
*tasyāḥ saṃgājjīvarūpaḥ patistvāṃ samyagdaṣṭāmihaloke paratra \|\| 105 \|\|*

105\. By these instructions, О lord of birds, the wicked intellect vitiates her husband, the soul. As he associates with her he the husband in the form of the soul, is stung by her.

## \|\| 28.106 \|\|

*tasyāḥ saṃgaṃ suvidūraṃ visṛjyaceṣṭvā samagraṃ kuru sarvadā tvam \|*  
*subuddhirūpā tvīrayantī jagāda bhajasva viṣṇuṃ paramādareṇa \|\| 106 \|\|*

106\. Therefore, do not associate with her. Resorting to sacrifice make your life a complete success.

The second wife, the noble intellect, encourages the husband in the following way:

## \|\| 28.107 \|\|

*hariṃ vinānyaṃ na bhajasva nityaṃ sā reṇukā tvāṃ tu na pālayiṣyati \|*  
*adṛṣṭanāmā haririve hi nityaṃ phalaprado yadi na syātkhagendra \|\| 107 \|\|*

107\. О my lord, do not propitiate any god or goddess except lord Visnu. Let not that (deceitful) Renuka attend on you. If lord Hari, of unmanifest form is unable toprotect you who else can do the same.

## \|\| 28.108 \|\|

*jugupsitāṃ śrutyanuktāṃ ca devīṃ patidruhāṃ sarvadā sevayitvā \|*  
*tasyāḥ prasādātkuṣṭhabhagandarādyairbhuktvā duḥ khaṃ saṃyaminīṃ prayāhi \|\| 108 \|\|*

108\. If you propitiate her who is deceitful and an a-vedic deity, always inimical to you (though outwardly pleasing), you will by that very act contract diseases such as leprosy, fistula, etc., and you will go to hell. This, your bad wife, will not help you at all. Hence, О lord, do not follow upon the bad advice of that wretched lady.

## \|\| 28.109 \|\|

*tadā kudavī kutra gatā vadasvame hyataḥ pate tvaṃ na bhajasva devīm \|*  
*pate bhaja tvaṃ brāhmaṇānvaiṣṇavāṃśca saṃsāraduḥ khāttāransuṣṭhurūpān \|\| 109 \|\|*

109\. О lord, propitiate the Brahmanas, the devotees of Visnu, who alone can help you to cross the ocean of suffering in this world. Therefore give up the worship of gods or goddesses (other than Visnu). Is there any gain in propitiating illusion.

## \|\| 28.110 \|\|

*sevādikaṃ pravīhāyaiva svacchaṃ māyādevyā bhajanātkiṃ vadasva \|*  
*jyeṣṭhāṣṭamyāṃ jyeṣṭhadevīṃ hyalakṣmīṃ lakṣmīti buddhyā pūjayitvā ca samyak \|\| 110 \|\|*

110\. О my lord, if on the eight day of each fortnight of the month of Jyestha, you worship Alaksmi taking her for LaksmI and wear the sacred thread round your neck as an insignia of devotion you will suffer an immense lot of trouble.

## \|\| 28.111 \|\|

*tasyāḥ sūtraṃ galabaddhaṃ ca kṛtvā nānāduḥ khaṃ hyanubhūyāḥ pate tvam \|*  
*yadā pate yamādūtaiśca pāśairbaddhvā ca samyak tāḍyamānaiḥ kaśābhiḥ \|\| 111 \|\|*

111\. When the emissaries of Yama tie you up with nooses and whip you severely, then Alaksnu (whom you propitiated during your lifetime) will not be present there to help you. Hence, О lord, abandon your contact with Alaksmi and seek shelter at die feet of lord Hari.

## \|\| 28.112-113 \|\|

*tadā hyalakṣmīḥ kutra palāyate 'sāvato mūlaṃ viṣṇupādaṃ bhajasva \|*  
*pate bhaja tvaṃ sarvadā vāyutattvaṃ na cāśrayestvaṃ sūkṣmaskandaṃ ca mūḍha \|\| 112 \|\|*

*tadvattaṃ tvaṃ navanītaṃ ca bhaktyā taducchiṣṭaṃ bhakṣayitvā pate hi \|*  
*tasyāśca sūtraṃ galabaddhaṃ ca kṛtvā ihaiva duḥ khānyanubhūyāḥ pate tvam \|\| 113 \|\|*

112-113. О ignorant lord, do always resort to Vayu- and not to Skanda-tattva. Fat fresh butter and not the one that has been left as refuge after god Skanda has partaken of his share. Wearing the thread of protection around your neck do not suffer any more in this world.

## \|\| 28.114 \|\|

*yadā pate yamadūtaiśca pāśairbaddhvā ca samyak tāḍyamānaḥ kaśābhiḥ \|*  
*tadā skandaḥ kutra palāyate 'sāvato mūlaṃ viṣṇupādaṃ bhajasva \|\| 114 \|\|*

114\. When the emissaries of Yama tie you with their nooses and beat you with whips, lord Skanda will not be present there to help you. О lord, give him up and seek the shelter of Lord Hari's feet.

## \|\| 28.115-116 \|\|

*dīpastaṃbhaṃ dāpayitvā pate tvaṃ sūtraṃ ca baddhvā svagale ca bhaktyā \|*  
*tadā baddhvā yamadūtaiśca pāśairdīpastaṃbhaistāḍyamānastu samyak \|\| 115 \|\|*

*dīpastaṃbhaḥ kutra palāyitobhūdato mūlaṃ viṣṇupādaṃ bhajasva \|*  
*lakṣmīdine pūjayitvā ca lakṣmīṃ sūtraṃ tasyāḥ svagale dhāraya tvam \|\| 116 \|\|*

115-116. О husband, you raised a pillar of earthen lamps for propitiating the lord and wore a holy thread around your neck for your protection. But when the emissaries of Yama tie you with their noose and whip you severely, the pillar of light and the holy thread will not be there to render help. Take resort at the feet of Visnu therefore.

## \|\| 28.117-119 \|\|

*yadā pate yamadūtaiśca pāśairbadhvā samyak tāḍyamānaḥ kaśābhiḥ \|*  
*tadā lakṣmīḥ kutra palāyate 'sāvato mūlaṃ viṣṇupādaṃ bhajasva \|\| 117 \|\|*

*vivāhamaiñjīdivase mūḍhabuddhe jugusitāndhārayitvā subhaktyā \|*  
*varārārtikaṃ kāṃsyapātre nidhāya kṛtvārtikyaṃ udaudaiti śabdam \|\| 118 \|\|*

*tathaiva daṣṭvā picumandasya patraṃ sunartayitvā paramādareṇa \|*  
*yadā tadā yamadūtaiśca pāśairbaddhvābaddhvā tāḍyamānaśca samyak \|\| 119 \|\|*

117-119. On the day of your marriage, you wore a fresh girdle, waived lights patio the vessel of brass. You made a peculiar soimd to propitiate your family god Siva. Chewing betel in the mouth you danced before the lord. But when after death, the emissaries of Yama tie you with their noose, your family god will not be there to help you. Where has he run away, tell me, О lord.

## \|\| 28.120-121 \|\|

*tava svāminkuladevo mahātmanpalāyitaḥ kutra me tadvadasva \|*  
*svadehānāṃ pūjayitvā ca samyakkaṇṭhābharaṇairvidhurāṇāṃ ca keśaiḥ \|\| 120 \|\|*

*saṃtiṣṭhamāne yamadūtā baliṣṭhā saṃtāḍyamāne musalairbhindipālaiḥ \|*  
*yadā tadā kutra palāyitā sā keśairvihīnā laṃbakarṇaṃ ca kṛtvā \|\| 121 \|\|*

120-121. You propitiated your family goddess, by gifting your ear ornaments or in the absence of ornaments with the hair of yourbody. But when the fierce emissaries of Yama beat you with iron pestles or mace, your family goddess will not be there to help you. Riding on a donkey she would have fled away long before.

## \|\| 28.122-123 \|\|

*svavāmahaste veṇupātraṃ nidhāya dīpaṃ dhṛtvā savyahaste ca mūḍhaḥ \|*  
*gṛhegṛhe bhaikṣacaryāṃ ca kṛtvā saṃtiṣṭhamāne svagṛhaṃ caiva devī \|\| 122 \|\|*

*yadā tadā yamadūtaiśca mūḍha dīpaiḥ sahasrairdahyamānaśca samyak \|*  
*nirnāsikā reṇukā mūḍhabuddhe palāyitā kutra sā me vadasva \|\| 123 \|\|*

122-123. You held a vessel of bamboo wood by your left hand and an earthen lamp by your right and begged О stupid person from door to door when you had a facility of a settled life at home. When after death, the emissaries of Yama beat you severely and put you on the fire of thousands of earthem lamps that snub-nosed Renuka will not be there to offer protection. Where would she have fled away, tell me, О lord.

## \|\| 28.124 \|\|

*sadā mūḍhaṃ khaḍgadevaṃ ca bhaktyā taṃ bhaktavatpūjayitvā ca samyak \|*  
*taiḥ sārdhaṃ tvaṃ śvānavadgarjayitvā saṃtiṣṭhamāne svagṛhe caiva nityam \|\| 124 \|\|*

124\. That lifeless object, the sword you worshipped with devotion as a devotee would worship the idol. You barked like a dog. You discarded home when you had the facility of a settled life.

## \|\| 28.125 \|\|

*yadā tadā yamadūtaiśca samyak saṃtāḍyamānastatra śabdaṃ prakurvan \|*  
*saṃtiṣṭhamāne bhaktavaryaṃ vihāya tadā devaḥ kutra palāyitobhūt \|\| 125 \|\|*

125\. When after death, the emissaries of Yama beat you up and you cried, then where did your family deity run away leaving you, the best of his devotees, all alone.

## \|\| 28.126 \|\|

*sa pārthakyādbhīmasenapratīkaṃ pañcāmṛtaiḥ pūjayitvā ca samyak \|*  
*suvyañjane cānnakaupīnameva dattvā mūḍhastiṣṭhamāne svagehe \|\| 126 \|\|*

126\. You worshipped the idol of Bhimasena adorned with five jewels. You offered sweet dishes, loin cloth, etc., to the devotees. О silly one, you wandered when you had the facility of a settled life at home.

## \|\| 28.127 \|\|

*yadā tadā yamadūtaiśca samyak saṃtāḍyamāne yamamārge ca mūḍhaḥ \|*  
*bhīmaḥ sa vai kutra palāyitobhūto mūlaṃ viṣṇupādaṃ bhajasva \|\| 127 \|\|*

127\. When the emissaries of Yama beat you on the way to Yama's region, where did the deity Bhima run away leaving you all alone? О ignorant one, take shelterat the feet of lord Visnu, О ignorant one.

## \|\| 28.128 \|\|

*mahādevaṃ pūjayitvā ca samyak haretyuktvā svagṛhe vidyamāne \|*  
*yadā gṛhaṃ dahyate vahninā tu tadā haraḥ kutra palāyitobhūt \|\| 128 \|\|*

128\. You offered worship to lord Siva, repeating the name Нага which means the remover of all suffering. When you had the facility of a settled life at home, you wandered. When your house was on fire, where did your lord Нага fly away (tell me, О lord).

## \|\| 28.129 \|\|

*śākaṃ bharīdivase sarvameva śākaṃbharī sā ca devī mahātman \|*  
*palāyitā kutra me tvaṃ vadasva kulāladevaṃ pūjayitvā ca bhaktyā \|\| 129 \|\|*

129\. At the festival of goddess Sakambhari, you worshipped Sakambhari. Where did Sakam- bharl fly away, when the emissaries of Yama beat you severely.

## \|\| 28.130 \|\|

*kārpāsaṃ vai tena dattaṃ gṛhītvā saṃtiṣṭhamāne yamadūtaiśca samyak \|*  
*saṃhanyamānastīkṣaṇadhāraiḥ kuṭhāraiḥ kulāladevaṃ ca sudaṃṣṭranetram \|*  
*vihāya vai kutra palāyitobhūnna jñāyate 'nveṣaṇāccāpi kena \|\| 130 \|\|*

130\. With concentrated devotion you worshipped Brahma and wearing a piece of cloth received in gilt when you had tire facility of a settled life at home. When after death the emmismasaries of Yam beat you with axes of sharp edge where did that Kulaladeva of bright teeth and shining eyes fly away loving you, a true devotee, in wretched plight Kulaladeva cannot be traced by any.

## \|\| 28.131 \|\|

*yadā pañcamyāṃ mṛnmayīṃ śeṣamūrtiṃ saṃpūjya bhaktyā vidyamāne svagehe \|*  
*tadā baddhvā yamadūtāśca samyak saṃnahyamāne nāgapāśaiścabaddhvā \|\| 131 \|\|*

131\. On the fifth day of each fortnight you worshipped the earthen idol of Sesa with devotion. You wandered when you had the' facility of a setteld life at home. When after death, the emmissaries of Yama tied you with a serpent noose, then where did Nagaraja run away leaving you, the best of his devotees, all alone. I Tell me, that.

## \|\| 28.132-133 \|\|

*svabhaktavaryaṃ pravihāya nāgaḥ palāyitaḥ kutra vai saṃvada tvam \|*  
*dūrvāṅkurairmodakaiḥ pūjayitvā vināyakaṃ pañcakhādyaistathaiva \|\| 132 \|\|*

*saṃtiṣṭhaṃmāne yamadūtaiśca samyak saṃtāḍyamāne taptadaṇḍaiśca mūḍha \|*  
*dantaṃ vihāyaiva ca vighnarājaḥ palāyitaḥ kutra me taṃ vadatvam \|\| 133 \|\|*

132-133. You worshipped Vinayaka with the tiny shoots of Dmva grass or with the fivefold variety of dishes. But when after death you were' beaten up with the heated rods of iron by the emissaries of Yams, where did lord Vinayak fly away leaving his single tusk behind Tell me, О lord

## \|\| 28.134 \|\|

*vivāhakāle piṣṭadevīṃ subhaktyā saṃpūjayitvā vidyamāno gṛhe sve \|*  
*yadā tadā yamadūtaiśca baddhvā saṃpīḍyamāno yamamārge sa mūḍhaḥ \|\| 134 \|\|*

134\. You worshipped Visthadevi (the goddess of faeces) with devotion on the occasion of marriage. You wandered when you had the facility of a settled life at home. When after death the emissaries of Yama tied you with a noose and beat you on the way to Yama's region where did the goddess of faeces fly away leaving you, a sincere devotee, all alone.

## \|\| 28.135 \|\|

*viṣṭhādevī pīḍyamānaṃ ca bhaktaṃ vihāya sā kutra palāyitābhūt \|*  
*vivāhakāle rajakasya gehaṃ gatvā samyak prārthayitvā ca mūḍhaḥ \|\| 135 \|\|*

135\. At the wedding ceremony you went to a washer' washerman's house, begged for the auspicious thread which you put on the earthen jar and worshipped.

## \|\| 28.136 \|\|

*yastaṃbhasūtraṃ kalaśe parītya pūjāṃ kṛtvā vidyamāno gṛhe sve \|*  
*yadā tadā yamadūtaśca samyak taṃ staṃbhasūtraṃ tasya mukhe nidhāya \|\| 136 \|\|*

136\. When after death the emissaries of Yama put the holy thread in your very mouth and beat you up, then where did the goddess Stambhasutra run away. Tell me, О lord.

## \|\| 28.137 \|\|

*saṃtāḍyamāne saṃtabhasūtrasthadevī palāyitā kutra me saṃvadasva \|*  
*vivāhakāle pūjayitvā ca samyak caṇḍāladevīṃ bhaktavaśyāṃ ca tasyāḥ \|\| 137 \|\|*

137\. At the wedding ceremony you worshipped Goddess Caula who fulfils the wishes of his devotees. You carried her in the winnowing basket to the bank of a river and propitiated her together with the devotees. You wandered when, you had the facility of a settled fife at home.

## \|\| 28.138 \|\|

*tadbhaktavaryaiḥ śūrpamadhye ca tīre saṃsevayitvā vidyamāno gṛhesve \|*  
*yadā tadā yamadūtaiśca baddhvā saṃtāḍyamāno yamamārge mahadbhiḥ \|\| 138 \|\|*

138\. When after death the emissaries of Yama tied you with the noose and beat you on the way to Yama, where did goddess Caula fly away. О stupid one, take shelter at the feet of lord Visnu.

## \|\| 28.139 \|\|

*cūledavī kva palāyitābhūtsumūḍhabuddhe viṣṇupādaṃ bhajasva \|*  
*jvarādibhiḥ pīḍyamāne svaputre gṛhe sthitaṃ brahmadevaṃ ca samyak \|\| 139 \|\|*

139\. When your son was suffering from fever you worshipped Brahmadeva at home with m cense, earthemlamps, flowers and tasty dishes. You accepted Brahmadeva as your family deity.

## \|\| 28.140 \|\|

*dhūrpairdīpairbhakṣyabhojyaiśca puṣpaiḥ pūjāṃ kṛtvā vidyamānaśca gehe \|*  
*yadā tadā yamadūtaiśca baddhvā saṃtāḍyamāne veṇupāśādibhiśca \|\| 140 \|\|*

140\. And when after death the emissaries of Yama tied you with a noose and beat you with the cane, where did Brahmadeva run away. О silly one, take shelter at the feet of lord Visnu.

## \|\| 28.141 \|\|

*sa brahmadevaḥ kva palāyitobhūtsumūḍhabuddhe viṣṇupādaṃ bhajasva \|*  
*santānārthaṃ bṛhatīṃ pūjayitvā galena baddhvā bṛhatīṃ vai phalaṃ ca \|\| 141 \|\|*

141\. You worshipped Brhati for obtaining offspring and kept the fruit of Brhati hanging around your neck You wandered when you had the facility of a settled life at home.

## \|\| 28.142 \|\|

*saṃtiṣṭhamāne yamadūtaiśca baddhvā saṃtāḍyamāne bṛhatīkaṇṭakaiśca \|*  
*tadā devī bṛhatī mūḍhabuddhe palāyitā kutra me tadvada tvam \|\| 142 \|\|*

142\. When after death, the emissaries of Yama tied you with the noose and pricked you with the thorns of Brhati, О ignorant one, then where did Brhati run away. Tell me that.

## \|\| 28.143 \|\|

*bhajasva mūḍha paradaivataṃ ca nārāyaṇaṃ tārakaṃ sarvaduḥ khāt \|*  
*sukṣudradeveṣu matiṃ ca mā kuru na ca śṛṇu tvaṃ phalguvākyaṃ tathaiva \|\| 143 \|\|*

143\. О ignorant one, take shelter at the feet of supreme lord Narayana, one who takes you across all sufferings. Do not worship the minor deities and do not hear their wasteful tales.

## \|\| 28.144 \|\|

*sukṣudradevān bhindipāle nidhāya visarjayitvā dūradeśe mahātman \|*  
*saṃdhārya tvaṃ svakulācāradharmaṃ saṃpātane narakaṃ hetubhūtam \|\| 144 \|\|*

144\. Put all minor deities together in a tiny basket and throw them off at a distant place. Put aside those customs of your family that are likely to occasion your fall into terrible hells.

## \|\| 28.145 \|\|

*punīhi gātraṃ sarvadā mūḍhabuddhe mantrāṣṭakairjanmatīrthe pavitre \|*  
*hṛdi sthitāṃmārairvyamudrāṃ vihāya kṛtvābhūṣāṃ viṣṇumudrābhiragryām \|\| 145 \|\|*

145\. О ignorant one, purge your soul by reciting the eight mantras (of purification) at this holy sight Cast off all emblems of stupidity that have entered your heart and adorn your self with the emblems of lord Visnu.

## \|\| 28.146 \|\|

*sadā mūḍho harivārtāṃ bhajasva hyāyurgataṃ vyarthamevaṃ kubuddhyā \|*  
*sadvaiṣṇavānāṃ saṃgamo durlabhaśca kṣubdhaṃ jñānaṃ tāratamyasvarūpam \|\| 146 \|\|*

146\. You are ignorant from the very beginning (and have remained as such to this very day). Listen to the tale of Visnu. You have wasted your life being led by vicious ideas. You should know that the association with the pious devotees of lord Visnu is difficult to achieve. Rarer is the knowledge of Reality — straight and simple.

## \|\| 28.147 \|\|

*hariṃ guruṃ hyanusṛtyaiva satyaṃ gatiṃ svakīyāṃ tena jānīhi mūḍha \|*  
*dagdhvā duṣṭāṃ buddhimevaṃ ca mūḍha subuddhirūpaṃ mā bhajasvaiva nityam \|\| 147 \|\|*

147\. Think of Visnu as your preceptor and the support of your existence. О ignorant one, throw off your wicked ideas. Resort to me, for I generate noble ideas in you.

## \|\| 28.148 \|\|

*mayā sārdhaṃ sadguruṃ prāpya samyagvairāgyapūrvaṃ tattvamātraṃ viditvā \|*  
*tenaiva mokṣaṃ prāpnumo nārjavairyattāryā viṣṇoḥ saṃprasādācca lakṣmyāḥ \|\| 148 \|\|*

148\. "Having obtained me as your noble preceptor, evolve feelings of detachment from your kith and kin. Know that knowledge of Reality leads to liberation. The grace of Visnu and her consort LaksmI can alone take you across the ocean of life."

## \|\| 28.149 \|\|

*ityāśayaṃ manasā sannidhāya tathā coktaṃ bhaktavaryo madīyaḥ \|*  
*ato bhaktaḥ pravahetyeva saṃjñāmavāpa vīndra prakṛtaṃ taṃ śṛṇu tvam \|\| 149 \|\|*

149\. With this thought in mind I addressed the best of my devotees thus. Hence I got the designation Pravaha, О lord of birds. Now hear what is relevant to the context.

## Chapter 29

## \|\| 29.1 \|\|

*pravahānantarānvakṣye śṛṇu pakṣīndrasattama \|*
