*yo dharmo brahmaṇaḥ putro hyādisṛṣṭau tvagudbhavaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. О best and noblest of birds, I shall now tell you the narrative as sequel to Pravaha, hear attentively. At the beginning of creation Dharma was born of the body of Brahma.

## \|\| 29.2 \|\|

*sajjanānsaumyarūpeṇa dhāraṇāddharmanāmakaḥ \|*  
*sa eva sūryaputrobhūdyamasaṃjñāmavāpa saḥ \|*  
*pāpināṃ śikṣakattvātsa yama ityucyate budhaiḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. He came to be known as Dharma because he sustained the noble by gentle treatment. He became the son of the solar deity and attained the designation of Yama.

Since he admonished the sinners the learned call him Yama.

## \|\| 29.3-4 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*prahlādānantaraṃ gaṅgā bhāryā vai varuṇasya ca \|*  
*prahlādādadhamā jñeyā mahimnā varuṇādhikā \|\| 3 \|\|*

*svarūpādadhamā jñeyā nātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|*  
*jñānasvarūpadaṃ viṣṇuṃ yamo jānāti sarvadā \|\| 4 \|\|*

3-4. Sri Krsna said: — After Prahlada came Ganga who became the wife of Varuna. She was inferior to Prahlada. She was superior to Varuna in glory, though in form she was inferior to him.

To Visnu who bestows supreme knowledge on men Yama is friendly.

## \|\| 29.5 \|\|

*ato gaṅgeti sā jñeyā sarvadā lokapāvanī \|*  
*bhaktyā viṣṇupadītyeva kīrtitā nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Ganga is so called because she purifies the world by her waters. The devotees call her Visnupadi out of devotion for her.

## \|\| 29.6 \|\|

*yā pūrvakāle yajñaliṅgasya viṣṇoḥ sākṣāddharervikramataḥ khagendra \|*  
*vāmasya pādasya nakhāgrataśca nirbhidya cordhvāṇḍakaṭāhakhaṇḍam \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Formerly, she forced her way out of the nails of the left feet of Visnu whose symbol is sacrifice. In the beginning she broke through the upper part of the cosmic egg.

## \|\| 29.7 \|\|

*tadudaramativegātsampraviśyāvahantīṃ jagadaghatatihantuḥ pādakiñjalkaśuddhām \|*  
*nikhilamalanihantrīṃ darśanātsparśanācca sakṛdavagahanādvā bhaktidāṃ viṣṇupāde \|*  
*śaśikaravaragaurāṃ mīnanetrāṃ supūjyāṃ smarati haripadotthāṃ mokṣameti krameṇa \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. She rushed into the belly of the lord the destroyer of the sins of the people and purified herself by contact with die filament of his lotusfeet She destroys impurities by her very sight, touch, bath and creates devotion for the feet of Visnu. She is as white in complexion as the rays of the moon. Her eyes resemble those of the fish. She is worthy of worship. Whosoever remembers her as rising from the feet of Hari attains liberation.

## \|\| 29.8 \|\|

*indropi vāyukaramarditavāyukūṭabinduṃ ca prāśya śirasi hyasahiṣṇumānaḥ \|*  
*bhāgīrathī haripadāṅkamiti sma nityaṃ jānanmahāparamabhāgavatapradhānaḥ \|*  
*bhaktyā ca khinnahṛdayaḥ paramādareṇa dhṛtvā svamūrdhni paramo hyaśivaḥ śivo 'bhūt \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Indra too, who drank in the heap of breath pressed, with hands, by the wind-god was not able to bear her upon his head,Knowing that Ganga had emerged from the feet of lord Hari, Shiva, the chief among the devotees of Visnu moved by devotion at heart, tookher up with reverence upon his head which though inauspicious became auspicious by her touch.

## \|\| 29.9 \|\|

*bhāgīrathyāśca catvāri rūpāṇyāsankhageśvara \|*  
*mahābhiṣagjanendrasya bhāryā tu hyabhiṣecanī \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. О lord of birds, Ganga had four forms. As the wife of chief physician Janendra she was called Abhisecani.

## \|\| 29.10 \|\|

*dvitīyenaiva rūpeṇa gaṅgā bhāryā ca śantanoḥ \|*  
*suṣeṇā vai suṣeṇasya bhāryā sā vānarī smṛtā \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. In her second form she became the wife of Santanu. As the wife of Susena, a monkey-chief, she was called Susena.

## \|\| 29.11 \|\|

*maṇḍūkabhāryā gaṅgā tu saiva maṇḍūkinī smṛtā \|*  
*evaṃ catvārī rūpāṇi gaṅgāyā iti kirtitamam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Being the wife of Manduka she was called Mandiikim.

## \|\| 29.12 \|\|

*ādityāccaiva gaṅgātaḥ parjanyaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ \|*  
*pravarṣati suvairāgyaṃ hyataḥ parjanyanāmakam \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Parjanya was born to Ganga from Aditya. He is called Parjanya (a cloud) since the rains (release of water) are symbols of detachment.

## \|\| 29.13 \|\|

*śaraṃvarāya pañcajanyācca pañca hitvā jagdhvā garvakaṃ ṣaṭkrameṇa \|*  
*svabāṇasya svahṛdi saṃsthitasya bhajetsadā naiva bhaktiṃ viṣaṃ ca \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. The word parjanya is derivable from Pancajanya meaning a conch by omitting the word pafica and retaining janya. It is symbolical of the idea that one should expel the six aberrations Kama, Krodha etc. from his interior and meditate upon atman with detachment not impaired by affection or hatred.

## \|\| 29.14 \|\|

*liṅgaṃ puṣṭaṃ naiva kāryaṃ sadaiva liṅgaṃ puṣṭaṃ kāryamevaṃ sadāpi \|*  
*yonau saktirnaiva kāryā sadāpi yonau mukte 'saṃgato yāti muktim \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. One should never nourish his own sexual organ but should ever devote oneself to the phallic emblem of diva. One should not attach oneself to the vagina of a woman. If he gives up his thought on vagina he becomes detached and attains liberation.

## \|\| 29.15 \|\|

*vairāgyamevaṃ prakārotyeva nityamataḥ parjanyastvantakaḥ pakṣivarya \|*  
*etāvatā śarabhākhyo mahātmā sa cāntaro sa tu parjanya eva \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Parjanya is always detached. О best of birds, Parjanya is, therefore, Yama. In this way sage Sarabha too is called antaka. He too is Yama.

## \|\| 29.16 \|\|

*śaśvatkeśā yasya gātre khagendra prabhāsyante śarabhākhyo payotaḥ \|*  
*yamasya bhāryā śyāmalā yā khagendra yasmātsadā kalibhāryāpiyā ca \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. O lord of birds, Sarabha is one on whose body the hair glisten brightly. Sarabha is a cloud. The wife of Yama is fSyamala. She is also the wife of Kali.

## \|\| 29.17-18 \|\|

*matvā samyak mānasaṃ yā karoti hyataśca sā śyāmalāsaṃjñakābhūt \|*  
*malaṃ vakṣye haribhaktervirodhī sulohapātre sannidhānaṃ ca tasya \|\| 17 \|\|*

*cikitsitaṃ paraduḥ khaṃ khagendra darerbhaktaistyājyamevaṃ sadaiva \|\| 18 \|\|*

17-18. She who with an awareness makes up her mind is called Syamala.

I shall now tell you aboutobstacles to ones devotion for Visnu. One should keep them aside, throw them away just as one puts excretion in an iron vessel and throws it away or just as one throws off cloth burnt by fire or abandons battle which has no aim or the treatment of a physician which occasions pain to the patient.

## \|\| 29.19 \|\|

*noccāśca te haribhaktervihīnāsteṣāṃ saṃgo naiva kāryaḥ sadāpi \|*  
*purāṇasaṃparkavisarjinaṃ ca purāṇatālaṃ ca purāṇavastram \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. The devotees of Had should dissociate from those who are devoid of devotion for Visnu and as such are considered to be inferior in society. He should avoid their company.

## \|\| 29.20-21 \|\|

*sujīrṇakanthājinamekhalaṃ ca yajñopavītaṃ ca kalipriyaṃ ca \|*  
*priyaṃ gṛhaṃ corṇavitā nakaṃ ca samitkuśaiḥ pūritaṃ kutsitaṃ ca \|\| 20 \|\|*

*sarvaṃ cetkalibhāryāpriyaṃ ca naiva priyaṃ śārṅgapāṇeḥ kadācit \|*  
*kāṃsye supakvaṃ yāvanālasya cānnaṃ tuṣaḥ piṇyākaṃ tumbabilve palāṇḍuḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

20-21. The following are not dear to the bow-wielding lord Krsna but they are deer to the mistress of Kali /those who are averse to the reciters of the Puranas, a delapidated pond, a wome-out garment, an old girdle, an old blanket, an old sacred thread, a house of which the inmates are always engaged in quarrel, or a house which contains a woollen canopy or a house which is full of woods and grass and is therefore shabby in appearance. All these are not dear to the bow-wielding lord but they are dear to the consort of Kali.

Grains of com — Yavanala cooked in a vessel of bell-metal, husks of corn, oil-cake, gourd wood-apple, a great quantity of butter-milk devoid of taste, pungent and hot-all these are dear to the consort of Kali.

## \|\| 29.22-23 \|\|

*dīrghaṃ takraṃ svāduhīnaṃ kaḍūṣṭaṇamete sarve kalibhāryāpriyāśca \|*  
*sudurmukhaṃ nindanaṃ cāryajānāṃ satovamatyātmajānāṃ prasahya \|\| 22 \|\|*

*supīḍanaṃ sarvadā bhartṛvarge gṛhasthitavrīhivastrādicauryāt \|*  
*prakīrṇabhūtānmūrdhajānsaṃdadhānaṃ karairyutaṃ devakalipriyaṃ ca \|\| 23 \|\|*

22-23. Bad face, censure of the noble, disrespect for the good, torture of children, stealing of rice, cloth, etc. at the house of one's husband, or wearing loose hair on the head or addiction to gambling and quarrels — all this is endearing to the consort of Kali.

## \|\| 29.24-25 \|\|

*ityādi sarvaṃ kalibhāryāpriyañca sunirmalaṃ prikarotyeva sarvam \|*  
*ataśca sā śyāmaleti svasaṃjñāmavāpa sā devakī saṃbabhūva \|\| 24 \|\|*

*yudhiṣṭhirasyaiva babhūva patnīsaṃbhāvitā tatra ca devakī sā \|*  
*candrasya bhāryā rohiṇī vai tadeyamaśvinyādibhyo 'hyadhikā sarvadaiva \|\| 25 \|\|*

24-25. Since she avoided all these, she was called Syamala. She became the consort of Vasudeva known as Devakl. The consort of the moon was called Rohini who was superior to Asvini and others.

## \|\| 29.26 \|\|

*roṇīṃ dhṛtvā rohati yogyasthānaṃ tasmācca sā rohiṇīti prasiddhā \|*  
*ādityabhāryā nāma saṃjñā khagendra jñeyā sā nārāyaṇasya svarūpā \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Holding rope in her hand, she could ascend a place, she was called RohinT. Samjna was the consort of Aditya. She was of the very nature of Narayana.

## \|\| 29.27 \|\|

*saṃjānātītyeva saṃjñāmavāpa saṃjñeti loke sūrya bhāryā khagendra \|*  
*brahmaṇḍasya hyabhimānī tu devo virāḍiti hyabhidhāmāpa tena \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Because she knowing made up her mind she was called Samjna, the consort of the solar deity. Virat is the presiding deity of the universe, therefore, he is called Virat.

## \|\| 29.28 \|\|

*gaṅgādiṣaṭkaṃ samameva nityaṃ parasparaṃ nottamaṃ nādhamaṃ ca \|*  
*pradhānāgneḥ pāvikānyaiva gaṅgā sadā śubhā nātra vicāryamasti \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. The six rivers Ganga, etc., are equal in merit to one another, each one of the group being neither superior nor inferior to the other. They purify the principal Agni. The river Ganga u always auspicious.

## \|\| 29.29 \|\|

*āsāṃ jñānatpuṇyamāpnoti nityaṃ sadā hariḥ prīyate keśavolam \|*  
*gaṅgādibhyo hyavarāhyagnijāyā svāhāsaṃjñādhiguṇā naiva hīnā \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Even by the knowledge of the sanctity of these purifying streams a devotee attains merit. Lord Kesava is extremely pleased thereby. Svaha, the consort of Agni is inferior to Ganga but superior to Samjna.

## \|\| 29.30 \|\|

*svāhākāro mantrarūpābhimānī svāheti saṃjñāmāpa sadaiva vīndra \|*  
*agnerbhāryāto buddhimān saṃbabhūva brahmābhimānī candraputro budhaśca \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Svaha is identical with Svahakara, the presiding deity of the mantras.

## \|\| 29.31 \|\|

*buddhyāharadvai rāṣṭrajātaṃ ca sarvaṃ dhṛtaṃ tvato budhasaṃjñāmavāpa \|*  
*evaṃ cābhūdabhimanyurmahātmā subhadrāyā jaṭhare hyarjunācca \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Budha, theson of the lunar deity was more intelligent than Svaha, die consort of Agni. He became the presiding deity of the Vedas. Since he established Kingdom with great intelligence he was called Budha.

The noble Abhimanyu was born of Arjuna and Subhadra He contains the amsas of Krsna, moon, Yama, Alvins and Нага.

## \|\| 29.32 \|\|

*kṛṣṇasya candrasya yamasya cāṃśaiḥ sa saṃyutastvaśvinorvai harasya \|*  
*svāhādhamaścandraputro budhastu pādāravinde viṣṇudevasya bhaktaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Budha, the son of the moon is inferior to Svaha. He is devoted to the lotus feet of Visnu.

## \|\| 29.33 \|\|

*nāmātmikā tvaśvibhāryā uṣā nāma prakīrtitā \|*  
*budhādhamā sā vijñeyā svāhā daśaguṇādhamā \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Nama, the wife of Asvins, is also called Usa. She is inferior to Budha and to Svaha (the wife of Agni) by ten, per cent virtues.

## \|\| 29.34 \|\|

*nakulasya bhāryā māgadhasyaiva putrī śalyātmajā sahadevasya bhāryā \|*  
*ubhe hyete aśvibhāryā hyuṣāpi upāsate ṣaḍguṇaṃ viṣṇumādyam \|*  
*ato 'pyuṣāsaṃjñakā sā khagendra anantarāñchṛṇu vakṣye mahātman \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. The wife of Nakula was the daughter of the king of Magadha and the wife of Sahadeva was the daughter of Salya — the two together with Usa, the wife of Asvins wait upon Visnu possessed of sixfold virtue. О lord of birds, hence, also she is called Usa. О noble bird, now I shag tell you about those which come after.

## \|\| 29.35 \|\|

*tataḥ śaktiḥ pṛthivyātmā śanaiścarati sarvadā \|*  
*ataḥ śanaiścaro nāma uṣāyāśca daśādhamāḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. A spiritual power (energy) in the form of earth moves slowly but ceaselessly. That power is called Sanaiscara. It is inferior to Usa by ten per cent virtues.

## \|\| 29.36 \|\|

*karmātmā puṣkaro jñeyaḥ śanaratha yamo mataḥ \|*  
*nayābhimānī puruṣaḥ kiñcinnamno daśāvaraḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Puskala or Puskara of the form of activity is the twin brother of Sari. He presides over law and order. He is comparatively gentle and inferior to Sanaiscara by ten per cent virtues.

## \|\| 29.37 \|\|

*hariprītikaro nityaṃ puṣkare krīḍate yataḥ \|*  
*atastu puṣkalo nāma loke sa parikīrtitaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Since the delights Visnu by his devotion and sports in the Puskara lake he is known as Puskala among the people.

## \|\| 29.38 \|\|

*hari prītikarāndharmānvakṣye śṛṇu khagādhipa \|*  
*prātaḥ kāle samutthāya smarennārāyaṇaṃ harim \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. О lord of birds, now I shall tell you about those activities which are delightful to the lord. A person should rise early in the morning and remember lord Hari Narayana.

## \|\| 29.39 \|\|

*tulasīvandanaṃ kuryācchrīviṣṇuṃ saṃsmaretkhaga \|*  
*viṇmūtrotsargakāle ca hyapānātmakakeśavam \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. He should bow to Tulasi and remember Visnu and his consort LaksmI. At the call of nature, while evacuating bowels he should remember Kesava of the form of apana.

## \|\| 29.40 \|\|

*trivikramaṃ śaucakāle gaṅgāpānakaraṃ harim \|*  
*dantadhāvanakāle tu candrāntaryāmiṇaṃ harim \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. At the time of purification he should remember Trivikrama, sipping waters of the Ganges. At the time of cleansing the teeth, he should remember Hari pervading the moon.

## \|\| 29.41 \|\|

*mukhaprakṣālane kāle mādhavaṃ saṃsmaretkhaga \|*  
*gavāṃ kaṇḍūyane caiva smaredgovardhanaṃ harim \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. At the washing of the mouth he should remember Madhava. At the itching of cows he should remember Govardhana.

## \|\| 29.42 \|\|

*sadā godohane kāle smaredgopālavallabham \|*  
*anantapuṇyārjitajanmakarmaṇāṃ supakvakāle ca khagendrasattama \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. At the milking of cows, or when the fruits of meritorious actions accomplished in the several previous births are ripened or at the touch of cows he should remember Gopala(the beloved of cowherds-lord Shri Krsna.)

## \|\| 29.43-44 \|\|

*sparśe gavāṃ caiva sadā nṛṇāṃ vai bhavatyato nātra vicāryamasti \|*  
*yasmin gṛhe nāsti sadottamā ca gauryaṅgaṇe śrītulasī ca nāsti \|\| 43 \|\|*

*yasmin gṛhe devamahotsavaśca yasmin gṛhe śravaṇaṃ nāsti viṣṇoḥ \|*  
*tatsaṃsargādyāti duḥ khādikaṃ ca tasya sparśo naiva kāryaḥ kadāpi \|\| 44 \|\|*

43-44. In the house where there is no cow or the holy Basil plant in the courtyard where the inmates do not celebrate any festival for the Gods, where there is no recitation of the narrative of Visnu, one should never stay even for a moment, for association with die inmates of that house will lead to misery.

## \|\| 29.45 \|\|

*gosparśanavihīnasya godohanamajānataḥ \|*  
*gopoṣaṇavihīnasya prāhurjanma nirarthakam \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. He who does not keep a cow at home, is unaware of the art of milking it or is averse to nourishing it, passes his life in vain.

## \|\| 29.46 \|\|

*gogrāsamapradātuśca gopuṣṭiṃ cāpyakurvataḥ \|*  
*gatirnāstyeva nāstyeva grāmacāṇḍālavatsmṛtaḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. He who does not give mouthful of food to the cow or does not tend it properly, can never pass a, good, comfortable life. He is like an outcaste in the village.

## \|\| 29.47 \|\|

*vatsyasya stanapāne ca bālakṛṣṇaṃ tu saṃsmaret \|*  
*dadhinirmanthane caiva manthādhāraṃ smareddharim \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. When the calf is sucking the breast he should remember the boy Krsna; when the milk is being churned he should remember Hari sucking.

## \|\| 29.48 \|\|

*mṛttikāsnāna kāle tu varāhaṃ saṃsmareddharim \|*  
*puṇḍrāṇāṃ dhāraṇe caiva keśavādīṃśca dvādaśa \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. When he bathes in waters mixed with the particles of earth from the sacred places of pilgrimage he should also remember Hari. When he puts sect marks on the forehead he should remember the twelve names of lord Visnu beginning with Kesava.

## \|\| 29.49 \|\|

*mudrāṇāṃ dhāraṇe caiva śaṅkhacakragadādharam \|*  
*padmaṃ nārāyaṇīṃ mudrāṃ kruddholkādīṃśca saṃsmaret \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. In practising certain positions (mudras) of his fingers he should remember lord Visnu adorned with conch, disc, mace, lotus, meteor, etc.

## \|\| 29.50 \|\|

*śrīrāmasaṃsmṛtiṃ caiva saṃdhyākāle khagottama \|*  
*acyutānantagovindāñchrāddhakāle ca saṃsmaret \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. In the evening when he performs Sandhya he should remember Rama. While performing a Sraddha he should remember Acyuta, Ananta and Govinda.

## \|\| 29.51 \|\|

*prāṇādikapañcahomecānirūddhādīṃśca saṃsmaret \|*  
*annādyarpaṇakāle tu vāsudevaṃ ca saṃsmaret \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. In the performance of five sacrifices Prana, etc., he should remember Aniruddha. When he offers oblations to the fire he should remember Vasudeva.

## \|\| 29.52 \|\|

*apośanasya kāle tu vāyorantargataṃ harim \|*  
*bastradhāraṇakākāle tu upendraṃ saṃsmareddharim \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. When he goes on fast he should remember Hari pervading Vayu. When he wears dress he should remember Upendra.

## \|\| 29.53 \|\|

*yajñopavītasya ca dhāraṇe tu nārāyaṇaṃ vāmanākhyaṃ smarettu \|*  
*ārtikyakāle ca tathaiva viṣṇoḥ samyak smaretparśurāmākhyaviṣṇum \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. When he puts on new sacred thread he should remember Vamana Narayana. When he waves lights before an idol he should remember ParaSurama.

## \|\| 29.54 \|\|

*apośanevaiśvadevasya kāle tadanyahomādiṣu bhasmadhāraṇe \|*  
*smarettu bhaktyā paramādareṇa nārāyaṇaṃ jāmadagnyākhyarāmam \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. When he goes on fast or at the time of VaiSvadeva or other sacrifices or at the lime of besmearing body with the holy ashes he should remember Narayana, Parasurama and Jamadagnya.

## \|\| 29.55 \|\|

*trivāratīrthagrahaṇasya kāle kṛṣṇaṃ rāmaṃ vyāsadevaṃ krameṇa \|*  
*śaṅkhodakasyoddharaṇe caiva kāle mukundarūpaṃ saṃsmaretsarvadaiva \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. When he thrice utters the name of sacred places of pilgrimage he should remember Krsna, Rama and Vyasa respectively. When he Pours water out of the conch he should remember Mukunda.

## \|\| 29.56 \|\|

*grāsegrāse smaraṇaṃ caiva kāryaṃ govindasaṃjñasya viśuddhamannam \|*  
*ekaikabhakṣyagrahaṇasya kāle samyak smaredacyutaṃ vai khagendra \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. When he offers undiluted food to Govinda, at each mouthful of food he should remember Govinda. When he partakes of food, at each core of the eatable he should remember Acyuta concentratedly.

## \|\| 29.57 \|\|

*śākādīnāṃ bhakṣaṇe caiva kāle dhanvantariṃ smareccaiva nityam \|*  
*tathā parānnasya ca bhogakāle smarecca samyak pāṇḍuraṅgaṃ ca viṣṇum \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Whan he partakes of Vegetables he should remember Dhanvantari. When he cats food offered by another he should remember Panduranga.

## \|\| 29.58 \|\|

*haiyaṅgavīnasya ca bhakṣaṇe vai samyak smarettāṇḍavākhyaṃ ca kṛṣṇam \|*  
*dadhyannabhakṣe paramaṃ purāṇaṃ gopālakṛṣṇaṃ saṃsmareccaiva nityam \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. When he eats butter he should remember the dancing Krsna. When he eats curd he should remember the premeval lord.

## \|\| 29.59 \|\|

*dugdhānnabhoge ca tathaiva kāle samyak smarecchrīnivāsaṃ hariṃ ca \|*  
*sutailasarpiḥ ṣu vipakvabhakṣasaṃbhojane saṃsmaredvyaṅkaṭeśam \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. When he drinks milk he should remember Gopala Krsna, lord Srinivasa, Visnu. When he eats food (tied in the oil or ghee he should remember Vyankatesa.

## \|\| 29.60 \|\|

*drākṣāsujambūkadalīrasālanāriṅgadāḍimbaphalāni cāru \|*  
*smarettu rambhottamanārikeladhātrīsubhoge khalu bālakṛṣṇam \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. When he partakes of grapes, pomegranates, banana, mango, orange, cocoanut or other fruits he should remember Balakrsna. When he drinks a good drink he should remember Nrsimha.

## \|\| 29.61 \|\|

*supānakasyaiva ca pānakāle samyak smarennārasiṃhākhyaviṣṇum \|*  
*gaṅgāmṛtasyaiva ca pānakāle gaṅgātātaṃ saṃsmaredviṣṇumeva \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. When he drinks the ambrosial waters of the Ganges he should remember Visnu, the source of Ganga.

## \|\| 29.62 \|\|

*prayāṇakāle saṃsmarettārkṣyavāhaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ nirguṇaṃ viśvamūrtim \|*  
*putrādīnāṃ cuṃbane caiva kāle suveṇuhastaṃ saṃsmaretkṛṣṇameva \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. When he is on he verge of death he should remember the attributeless and the omnipresent Narayana and his Vehicle Garuda. When he kisses his son and other boys he should remember Krsna holding Venu in his hands.

## \|\| 29.63 \|\|

*sukhaṅgakāle svastriyaścaiva nityaṃ gopi kucadvandvavilāsinaṃ harim \|*  
*tāṃbūlakāle saṃsmaraiccaiva nityaṃ pradyumnākhyaṃ vāsudevaṃ hariṃ ca \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. When indulging in intercourse with his consort he should remember Visnu playing with the Gopis' pair of breasts.

## \|\| 29.64 \|\|

*śayyākāle saṃsmareccaiva nityaṃ saṃkarṣaṇākhyaṃ viṣṇurūpaṃ hariṃ ca \|*  
*nidrākāle saṃsmaretpadmanāmaṃ kathākāle vyāsarūpaṃ hariṃ ca \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. When he is going to sleep he should remember Hari as the incarnation of Vyasa.

## \|\| 29.65 \|\|

*sugānakāle saṃsmaredveṇugītaṃ hariṃ hariṃ pravadetsarvadaiva \|*  
*śrīmattulasyāśchedane caiva kāle śrīrāmarāmeti ca saṃsmarettu \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. When he sings devotional songs he should remember Hari playing upon Venu. He should always utter the name Hari.

## \|\| 29.66 \|\|

*puṣpādīnāṃ chedane caiva kāle samyaka smaredetkapilākhyaṃ hariṃ ca \|*  
*pradakṣiṇegāruḍāntargataṃ ca hariṃ smaretsarvadā vai khagendra \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. When he plucks the leaves of TulasI he should utter 'Rama, Rama'. When he cuts off a leaf he should remember Kapila, an incarnation of Hari. When he circumambulates Hari pervading Garuda he should remember Hari with concentration.

## \|\| 29.67 \|\|

*praṇamakāle devadevasya viṣṇoḥ śeṣāntasthaṃ saṃsmareccaiva viṣṇum \|*  
*sunītikāle saṃsmarennārasiṃhaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ saṃsaṃmaretsarvadāpi \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. When he bows to Visnu, the lord of lords, he should remember Visnu pervading Sesa. When he is in council with his ministers he should remember Narasimha Narayana.

## \|\| 29.68-69 \|\|

*pūrtiryadā kriyate karmaṇāṃ ca samyak smaredvāsudevaṃ hariṃ ca \|*  
*evaṃ kṛtāni karmāṇi hariprītikarāṇi ca \|\| 68 \|\|*

*samyak prakurvannetāni puṣkaro harivallabhaḥ \|\| 69 \|\|*

68-69. When an imdertaking is accomplished he should remember lord Vasudeva Hari. When he has accomplished activities as are endearing to lord Puskara, he secures the affection of Hari.

## \|\| 29.70 \|\|

*etasmādeva pakṣīśa karma yatsamudāhṛtam puṣkarākhyānamatulaṃ śṛṇoti śraddhayānvitaḥ \|*  
*hariprītikare dharme prītiyukto bhavetsadā \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. Therefore, О lord of birds, he who hears with faith this incomparable narrative of Puskara, in all its details, is attracted m Dharma which creates devotion for the lord.
